=== FILE: C:\Users\Almas\Documents\QuietReader\src\app\autoscroll.rs ===
use crate::settings::AutoScrollSettings;
use eframe::egui;

#[derive(Debug, Default, Clone)]
pub struct AutoscrollController {
    pub is_active: bool,
    pub phase_elapsed: f32,
    pub in_pause: bool,
    pub step_start_scroll_y: f32,
    pub target_step_scroll_y: f32,
    pub is_middle_dragging: bool,
}

impl AutoscrollController {
    pub fn toggle(&mut self, current_scroll_y: f32, max_scroll_y: f32, step_distance: f32) {
        self.is_active = !self.is_active;
        self.phase_elapsed = 0.0;
        self.in_pause = false;
        self.step_start_scroll_y = current_scroll_y;
        self.target_step_scroll_y = (current_scroll_y + step_distance).min(max_scroll_y);
    }

    pub fn is_active(&self) -> bool {
        self.is_active
    }

    pub fn stop(&mut self) {
        self.is_active = false;
    }

    pub fn update(
        &mut self,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        settings: &AutoScrollSettings,
        current_scroll_y: f32,
        max_scroll_y: f32,
        _total_chapters: usize,
        current_chapter: &mut usize,
        pending_scroll_y: &mut Option<f32>,
        last_rendered_chapter: &mut usize,
        next_non_empty_chapter: Option<usize>,
    ) {
        if !self.is_active {
            return;
        }

        let dt = ctx.input(|i| i.stable_dt).clamp(0.001, 0.1);
        ctx.request_repaint();

        let at_bottom = current_scroll_y + 4.0 >= max_scroll_y;

        if at_bottom {
            if let Some(next_idx) = next_non_empty_chapter {
                *current_chapter = next_idx;
                *pending_scroll_y = Some(0.0);
                *last_rendered_chapter = *current_chapter;
                self.phase_elapsed = 0.0;
                self.in_pause = true;
            } else {
                self.is_active = false;
            }
        } else if !settings.pause_mode {
            let speed = settings.continuous_speed.clamp(5.0, 400.0);
            let next_y = (current_scroll_y + speed * dt).min(max_scroll_y);
            *pending_scroll_y = Some(next_y);
        } else {
            let pause_dur = settings.pause_duration.max(0.2);
            let move_dur = settings.step_duration.max(0.1);

            self.phase_elapsed += dt;

            if self.in_pause {
                if self.phase_elapsed >= pause_dur {
                    self.in_pause = false;
                    self.phase_elapsed = 0.0;
                    self.step_start_scroll_y = current_scroll_y;
                    self.target_step_scroll_y =
                        (current_scroll_y + settings.step_distance).min(max_scroll_y);
                }
            } else {
                let progress = (self.phase_elapsed / move_dur).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
                let smooth_t = progress * progress * (3.0 - 2.0 * progress);
                let current_target = self.step_start_scroll_y
                    + (self.target_step_scroll_y - self.step_start_scroll_y) * smooth_t;
                *pending_scroll_y = Some(current_target);

                if self.phase_elapsed >= move_dur {
                    self.in_pause = true;
                    self.phase_elapsed = 0.0;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn update_continuous(
        &mut self,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        settings: &AutoScrollSettings,
        current_scroll_y: f32,
        max_scroll_y: f32,
        pending_scroll_y: &mut Option<f32>,
    ) {
        if !self.is_active {
            return;
        }

        let dt = ctx.input(|i| i.stable_dt).clamp(0.001, 0.1);
        ctx.request_repaint();

        let at_bottom = current_scroll_y + 4.0 >= max_scroll_y;

        if at_bottom {
            self.is_active = false;
        } else if !settings.pause_mode {
            let speed = settings.continuous_speed.clamp(5.0, 400.0);
            let next_y = (current_scroll_y + speed * dt).min(max_scroll_y);
            *pending_scroll_y = Some(next_y);
        } else {
            let pause_dur = settings.pause_duration.max(0.2);
            let move_dur = settings.step_duration.max(0.1);

            self.phase_elapsed += dt;

            if self.in_pause {
                if self.phase_elapsed >= pause_dur {
                    self.in_pause = false;
                    self.phase_elapsed = 0.0;
                    self.step_start_scroll_y = current_scroll_y;
                    self.target_step_scroll_y =
                        (current_scroll_y + settings.step_distance).min(max_scroll_y);
                }
            } else {
                let progress = (self.phase_elapsed / move_dur).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
                let smooth_t = progress * progress * (3.0 - 2.0 * progress);
                let current_target = self.step_start_scroll_y
                    + (self.target_step_scroll_y - self.step_start_scroll_y) * smooth_t;
                *pending_scroll_y = Some(current_target);

                if self.phase_elapsed >= move_dur {
                    self.in_pause = true;
                    self.phase_elapsed = 0.0;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn update_paged(
        &mut self,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        settings: &AutoScrollSettings,
        current_page: usize,
        total_pages: usize,
    ) -> bool {
        if !self.is_active {
            return false;
        }

        let dt = ctx.input(|i| i.stable_dt).clamp(0.001, 0.1);
        ctx.request_repaint();

        if current_page + 1 >= total_pages {
            self.is_active = false;
            return false;
        }

        let dur = if settings.pause_mode {
            settings.pause_duration.max(1.0)
        } else {
            (60.0 / (settings.continuous_speed.max(10.0) / 10.0)).clamp(1.0, 15.0)
        };

        self.phase_elapsed += dt;
        if self.phase_elapsed >= dur {
            self.phase_elapsed = 0.0;
            true
        } else {
            false
        }
    }

    pub fn handle_middle_click_scroll(
        &mut self,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        show_toc: bool,
        current_scroll_y: f32,
        max_scroll_y: f32,
        pending_scroll_y: &mut Option<f32>,
    ) {
        let (pressed, down, delta_y, over_toc) = ctx.input(|i| {
            let p = &i.pointer;
            let over_toc = show_toc && p.latest_pos().map(|pos| pos.x < 280.0).unwrap_or(false);
            (
                p.button_pressed(egui::PointerButton::Middle),
                p.button_down(egui::PointerButton::Middle),
                p.delta().y,
                over_toc,
            )
        });

        if pressed && !over_toc {
            self.is_middle_dragging = true;
            self.is_active = false;
        }

        if self.is_middle_dragging {
            if down {
                ctx.set_cursor_icon(egui::CursorIcon::Grabbing);
                if delta_y.abs() > 0.0 {
                    let base_y = pending_scroll_y.unwrap_or(current_scroll_y);
                    let new_scroll = (base_y - delta_y).clamp(0.0, max_scroll_y);
                    *pending_scroll_y = Some(new_scroll);
                }
            } else {
                self.is_middle_dragging = false;
            }
        }
    }
}

use crate::document::{Document, DocumentViewKind};
use eframe::egui::{self, Panel, RichText};

pub struct TopBarActions {
    pub open_file: bool,
    pub open_folder: bool,
    pub toggle_recent: bool,
    pub toggle_toc: bool,
    pub toggle_fullscreen: bool,

pub fn render_top_bar(
    ui: &mut egui::Ui,
    has_document: bool,
    is_fullscreen: bool,
    show_toc: bool,

        open_file: false,
        open_folder: false,
        toggle_recent: false,
        toggle_toc: false,
        toggle_fullscreen: false,
        toggle_settings: false,
    };

    Panel::top("top_bar").show(ui, |ui| {
        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            if ui.button("рџ“‚ РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ...").on_hover_text("РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РѕРґРёРЅ РёР»Рё РЅРµСЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ РІ РЅРѕРІС‹С… РІРєР»Р°РґРєР°С… (PDF, FB2, EPUB, TXT, MD, LOG, CBZ, CBR, CB7, ZIP, RAR, 7Z)").clicked() {
                actions.open_file = true;
            }

                actions.open_folder = true;
            }
            if ui.button("рџ“љ РќРµРґР°РІРЅРёРµ...").on_hover_text("РЎРїРёСЃРѕРє РЅРµРґР°РІРЅРѕ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚С‹С… РєРЅРёРі").clicked() {
                actions.toggle_recent = true;

            if has_document {
                ui.separator();
                let toc_text = if show_toc { "рџ“– РЎРєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РѕРіР»Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ" } else { "рџ“– РћРіР»Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ" };
                if ui.button(toc_text).clicked() {
                    actions.toggle_toc = true;
                }
            }
            ui.with_layout(egui::Layout::right_to_left(egui::Align::Center), |ui| {
                let fs_text = if is_fullscreen { "рџ—— РћРєРѕРЅРЅС‹Р№ (F)" } else { "в›¶ РќР° РІРµСЃСЊ СЌРєСЂР°РЅ (F)" };
                if ui.button(fs_text).on_hover_text("РџРѕР»РЅРѕСЌРєСЂР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ СЂРµР¶РёРј С‡С‚РµРЅРёСЏ (РіРѕСЂСЏС‡Р°СЏ РєР»Р°РІРёС€Р°: F, РІС‹С…РѕРґ: F РёР»Рё Esc)").clicked() {
                    actions.toggle_fullscreen = true;

                    actions.toggle_settings = true;

}

pub fn render_bottom_bar(
    ui: &mut egui::Ui,
    document: &Option<Arc<dyn Document>>,
    theme: &ReaderTheme,
    status_message: Option<&str>,
    view_kind: DocumentViewKind,
    pdf_page: (usize, usize),
    comic_page: (usize, usize),
    text_chapter: (usize, usize, f32, f32),
) {
    Panel::bottom("bottom_bar").show(ui, |ui| {
        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            if let Some(doc) = document {
                let meta = doc.metadata();
                let book_info = if meta.author.is_empty() {
                    meta.title.clone()
                } else {
                    format!("{} вЂ” {}", meta.author, meta.title)
                };
                ui.label(RichText::new(format!("рџ“– {book_info}")).strong());
                if let Some(err) = crate::settings::AppSettings::last_save_error() {
                    ui.separator();
                    ui.label(RichText::new(format!("вљ  {err}")).color(egui::Color32::RED));
                } else if let Some(msg) = status_message {
                    ui.separator();
                    ui.label(RichText::new(msg).color(theme.accent_color()));
                }
            } else if let Some(err) = crate::settings::AppSettings::last_save_error() {
                ui.label(RichText::new(format!("вљ  {err}")).color(egui::Color32::RED));
            } else if let Some(msg) = status_message {
                ui.label(msg);
            } else {
                ui.label("Р“РѕС‚РѕРІ Рє С‡С‚РµРЅРёСЋ");
            }

            let (cur_str, pct_str) = match view_kind {
                DocumentViewKind::Pdf => {
                    let (cur_idx, total) = pdf_page;
                    let cur = cur_idx + 1;
                    let pct = if total > 0 { (cur as f32 / total as f32 * 100.0).clamp(0.0, 100.0) } else { 0.0 };
                    let fmt = document.as_ref().map(|d| d.format_name()).unwrap_or("PDF");
                    (format!("РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {fmt} {cur} РёР· {total}"), format!("{pct:.1}%"))
                }
                DocumentViewKind::Comic => {
                    let (cur_idx, total) = comic_page;
                    let cur = cur_idx + 1;
                    let pct = if total > 0 { (cur as f32 / total as f32 * 100.0).clamp(0.0, 100.0) } else { 0.0 };
                    (format!("РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {cur} РёР· {total}"), format!("{pct:.1}%"))
                }
                DocumentViewKind::Text => {
                    if document.is_some() {
                        let (ch_idx, total, cur_scroll, max_scroll) = text_chapter;
                        let scroll_frac = if max_scroll > 1.0 { (cur_scroll / max_scroll).clamp(0.0, 1.0) } else { 1.0 };
                        let pct = if total > 0 { ((ch_idx as f32 + scroll_frac) / total as f32 * 100.0).clamp(0.0, 100.0) } else { 0.0 };
                        (format!("Р Р°Р·РґРµР» {} РёР· {total}", ch_idx + 1), format!("{pct:.1}%"))
                    } else {
                        (String::new(), String::new())
                    }
                }
            };
            if !cur_str.is_empty() {
                ui.with_layout(egui::Layout::right_to_left(egui::Align::Center), |ui| {
                    ui.label(RichText::new(format!("{cur_str}  вЂў  {pct_str}")).strong());
                });
            }
        });
    });
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\Almas\Documents\QuietReader\src\app\comic_view.rs ===
use super::document_loader::format_document_error;
use super::theme::ReaderTheme;
use crate::document::{DecodedImage, Document, DocumentError};
use crate::settings::ComicSettings;
use eframe::egui::{self, RichText, ScrollArea, Vec2};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::sync::mpsc::{self, Receiver, Sender};

struct LoadRequest {
    doc_generation: u64,
    doc: Arc<dyn Document>,
    page_idx: usize,
    ctx: egui::Context,
}

struct LoadResponse {
    doc_generation: u64,
    page_idx: usize,
    result: Result<DecodedImage, DocumentError>,
}

pub struct ComicViewState {
    pub current_page: usize,
    pub textures: HashMap<usize, (egui::TextureHandle, u64)>,
    pub pending_pages: HashSet<usize>,
    pub failed_pages: HashMap<usize, String>,
    pub access_counter: u64,
    pub doc_generation: u64,
    pub pan_offset: Vec2,
    pub is_middle_dragging: bool,
    pub page_input: String,
    req_tx: Sender<LoadRequest>,
    resp_rx: Receiver<LoadResponse>,
}

fn spawn_comic_worker() -> (Sender<LoadRequest>, Receiver<LoadResponse>) {
    let (req_tx, req_rx) = mpsc::channel::<LoadRequest>();
    let (resp_tx, resp_rx) = mpsc::channel::<LoadResponse>();

    std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("comic-page-worker".to_string())
        .spawn(move || {
            while let Ok(req) = req_rx.recv() {
                let doc = req.doc.clone();
                let page_idx = req.page_idx;
                let load_res = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
                    if let Some(page_content) = doc.page_content() {
                        page_content.load_page_image(page_idx)
                    } else {
                        Err(DocumentError::Unsupported(
                            "Р”РѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚ РЅРµ РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РёРІР°РµС‚ РїРѕСЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС‡РЅС‹Р№ СЂРµР¶РёРј".to_string(),
                        ))
                    }
                }));

                let result = match load_res {
                    Ok(res) => res,
                    Err(_) => Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(
                        "РђРІР°СЂРёР№РЅС‹Р№ СЃР±РѕР№ РїСЂРё РґРµРєРѕРґРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРё СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ РєРѕРјРёРєСЃР°".to_string(),
                    )),
                };

                let _ = resp_tx.send(LoadResponse {
                    doc_generation: req.doc_generation,
                    page_idx,
                    result,
                });
                req.ctx.request_repaint();
            }
        })
        .ok();

    (req_tx, resp_rx)
}

impl Default for ComicViewState {
    fn default() -> Self {
        let (req_tx, resp_rx) = spawn_comic_worker();

        Self {
            current_page: 0,
            textures: HashMap::new(),
            pending_pages: HashSet::new(),
            failed_pages: HashMap::new(),
            access_counter: 0,
            doc_generation: 0,
            pan_offset: Vec2::ZERO,
            is_middle_dragging: false,
            page_input: String::new(),
            req_tx,
            resp_rx,
        }
    }
}

impl ComicViewState {
    pub fn clear_cache(&mut self) {
        self.textures.clear();
        self.pending_pages.clear();
        self.failed_pages.clear();
        self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
        self.doc_generation = self.doc_generation.wrapping_add(1);
    }

    pub fn page_count(&self, document: &Option<Arc<dyn Document>>) -> usize {
        document
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|d| d.page_content())
            .map(|c| c.page_count())
            .unwrap_or(0)
    }

    pub fn is_effective_manga(
        &self,
        document: &Option<Arc<dyn Document>>,
        settings: &ComicSettings,
    ) -> bool {
        let is_manga_doc = document
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|d| d.page_content())
            .map(|c| c.is_manga())
            .unwrap_or(false);

        match settings.reading_mode.as_str() {
            "Manga" => true,
            "Comic" => false,
            "Webtoon" => false,
            _ => is_manga_doc,
        }
    }

    pub fn next_page(&mut self, total_pages: usize, settings: &ComicSettings) {
        if total_pages == 0 {
            return;
        }
        let is_double = settings.page_layout == "Double" && settings.reading_mode != "Webtoon";
        let step = if is_double { 2 } else { 1 };
        self.current_page = (self.current_page + step).min(total_pages.saturating_sub(1));
        self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
    }

    pub fn prev_page(&mut self, settings: &ComicSettings) {
        let is_double = settings.page_layout == "Double" && settings.reading_mode != "Webtoon";
        let step = if is_double { 2 } else { 1 };
        self.current_page = self.current_page.saturating_sub(step);
        self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
    }

    fn poll_responses(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context, status_message: &mut Option<String>) {
        loop {
            match self.resp_rx.try_recv() {
                Ok(resp) => {
                    if resp.doc_generation != self.doc_generation {
                        continue;
                    }
                    self.pending_pages.remove(&resp.page_idx);
                    match resp.result {
                        Ok(img) => {
                            self.access_counter += 1;
                            let counter = self.access_counter;
                            let color_image = egui::ColorImage::from_rgba_unmultiplied(
                                [img.width as usize, img.height as usize],
                                &img.rgba,
                            );
                            let texture = ctx.load_texture(
                                format!("comic_page_tex_{}_{}", self.doc_generation, resp.page_idx),
                                color_image,
                                egui::TextureOptions::LINEAR,
                            );

                            const MAX_CACHED_TEXTURES: usize = 16;
                            if self.textures.len() >= MAX_CACHED_TEXTURES
                                && let Some((&oldest_key, _)) =
                                    self.textures.iter().min_by_key(|(_, (_, acc))| *acc)
                            {
                                self.textures.remove(&oldest_key);
                            }

                            self.textures.insert(resp.page_idx, (texture, counter));
                        }
                        Err(e) => {
                            let msg = format_document_error(&e);
                            *status_message = Some(format!(
                                "РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}: {msg}",
                                resp.page_idx + 1
                            ));
                            self.failed_pages.insert(resp.page_idx, msg);
                        }
                    }
                }
                Err(mpsc::TryRecvError::Empty) => break,
                Err(mpsc::TryRecvError::Disconnected) => {
                    for page_idx in self.pending_pages.drain() {
                        let msg = "РџРѕС‚РѕРє РґРµРєРѕРґРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ Р°РІР°СЂРёР№РЅРѕ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€РёР»СЃСЏ".to_string();
                        self.failed_pages.insert(page_idx, msg.clone());
                        *status_message = Some(format!(
                            "РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}: {msg}",
                            page_idx + 1
                        ));
                    }
                    let (tx, rx) = spawn_comic_worker();
                    self.req_tx = tx;
                    self.resp_rx = rx;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn request_page_texture(
        &mut self,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        document: &Option<Arc<dyn Document>>,
        page_idx: usize,
    ) -> Option<egui::TextureHandle> {
        self.access_counter += 1;
        let counter = self.access_counter;

        if let Some(entry) = self.textures.get_mut(&page_idx) {
            entry.1 = counter;
            return Some(entry.0.clone());
        }

        if self.pending_pages.contains(&page_idx) || self.failed_pages.contains_key(&page_idx) {
            return None;
        }

        let doc = document.as_ref()?.clone();
        self.pending_pages.insert(page_idx);
        let _ = self.req_tx.send(LoadRequest {
            doc_generation: self.doc_generation,
            doc,
            page_idx,
            ctx: ctx.clone(),
        });
        None
    }

    pub fn render_toolbar(
        &mut self,
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        page_count: usize,
        settings: &mut ComicSettings,
        theme: &ReaderTheme,
        is_manga: bool,
    ) {
        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            ui.spacing_mut().item_spacing.x = 6.0;

            let prev_enabled = self.current_page > 0;
            if ui
                .add_enabled(prev_enabled, egui::Button::new("в—Ђ"))
                .on_hover_text("РџСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰Р°СЏ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р°")
                .clicked()
            {
                self.prev_page(settings);
            }

            if self.page_input.is_empty() {
                self.page_input = (self.current_page + 1).to_string();
            }
            let cur_display = self.current_page + 1;
            ui.label(RichText::new("РЎС‚СЂ.").size(13.0));
            let text_edit = ui.add(
                egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut self.page_input)
                    .desired_width(38.0)
                    .font(egui::TextStyle::Body),
            );
            if text_edit.lost_focus() && ui.input(|i| i.key_pressed(egui::Key::Enter)) {
                if let Some(target) = super::paged_common::parse_page_input(&self.page_input, page_count) {
                    self.current_page = target;
                    self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
                }
                self.page_input = (self.current_page + 1).to_string();
            } else if !text_edit.has_focus() {
                self.page_input = cur_display.to_string();
            }

            ui.label(RichText::new(format!("РёР· {page_count}")).size(13.0));

            let next_enabled = self.current_page + 1 < page_count;
            if ui
                .add_enabled(next_enabled, egui::Button::new("в–¶"))
                .on_hover_text("РЎР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰Р°СЏ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р°")
                .clicked()
            {
                self.next_page(page_count, settings);
            }

            ui.separator();

            let is_manga_active = settings.reading_mode == "Manga";
            if ui
                .selectable_label(is_manga_active, "рџЌ™ РњР°РЅРіР° (RTL)")
                .on_hover_text("Р РµР¶РёРј С‡С‚РµРЅРёСЏ СЃРїСЂР°РІР°-РЅР°Р»РµРІРѕ (СЏРїРѕРЅСЃРєР°СЏ РјР°РЅРіР°)")
                .clicked()
            {
                settings.reading_mode = "Manga".to_string();
            }

            let is_comic_active = settings.reading_mode == "Comic";
            if ui
                .selectable_label(is_comic_active, "рџ“– РљРѕРјРёРєСЃ (LTR)")
                .on_hover_text("Р РµР¶РёРј С‡С‚РµРЅРёСЏ СЃР»РµРІР°-РЅР°РїСЂР°РІРѕ (РµРІСЂРѕРїРµР№СЃРєРёРµ Рё Р°РјРµСЂРёРєР°РЅСЃРєРёРµ РєРѕРјРёРєСЃС‹)")
                .clicked()
            {
                settings.reading_mode = "Comic".to_string();
            }

            ui.separator();

            let is_single = settings.page_layout == "Single" && settings.reading_mode != "Webtoon";
            if ui
                .selectable_label(is_single, "1 СЃС‚СЂ.")
                .on_hover_text("РћРґРёРЅРѕС‡РЅР°СЏ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р°")
                .clicked()
            {
                settings.page_layout = "Single".to_string();
                if settings.reading_mode == "Webtoon" {
                    settings.reading_mode = "Auto".to_string();
                }
                self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
            }

            let is_double = settings.page_layout == "Double" && settings.reading_mode != "Webtoon";
            if ui
                .selectable_label(is_double, "2 СЃС‚СЂ.")
                .on_hover_text("Р”РІСѓС…СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС‡РЅС‹Р№ СЂР°Р·РІРѕСЂРѕС‚")
                .clicked()
            {
                settings.page_layout = "Double".to_string();
                if settings.reading_mode == "Webtoon" {
                    settings.reading_mode = "Auto".to_string();
                }
                self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
            }

            let is_webtoon = settings.reading_mode == "Webtoon";
            if ui
                .selectable_label(is_webtoon, "РЎРІРёС‚РѕРє")
                .on_hover_text("РќРµРїСЂРµСЂС‹РІРЅР°СЏ РІРµСЂС‚РёРєР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ Р»РµРЅС‚Р° (Webtoon / РјР°РЅС…РІР°)")
                .clicked()
            {
                settings.reading_mode = "Webtoon".to_string();
                self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
            }

            ui.separator();

            if ui
                .button("в€’")
                .on_hover_text("РЈРјРµРЅСЊС€РёС‚СЊ РјР°СЃС€С‚Р°Р± (Ctrl -)")
                .clicked()
            {
                super::paged_common::zoom_out(&mut settings.zoom_percent, &mut settings.fit_mode);
            }

            let zoom_lbl = format!("{:.0}%", settings.zoom_percent);
            if ui
                .button(RichText::new(zoom_lbl).strong())
                .on_hover_text("РЎР±СЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ РјР°СЃС€С‚Р°Р± РЅР° 100% (Ctrl 0)")
                .clicked()
            {
                super::paged_common::zoom_reset(
                    &mut settings.zoom_percent,
                    &mut settings.fit_mode,
                    &mut self.pan_offset,
                );
            }

            if ui
                .button("+")
                .on_hover_text("РЈРІРµР»РёС‡РёС‚СЊ РјР°СЃС€С‚Р°Р± (Ctrl +)")
                .clicked()
            {
                super::paged_common::zoom_in(&mut settings.zoom_percent, &mut settings.fit_mode);
            }

            let fit_win_active = settings.fit_mode == "FitWindow";
            if ui
                .selectable_label(fit_win_active, "РџРѕ РѕРєРЅСѓ")
                .on_hover_text("Р’РјРµСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёРµ С†РµР»РёРєРѕРј РІ РѕРєРЅРѕ")
                .clicked()
            {
                settings.fit_mode = "FitWindow".to_string();
                self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
            }

            let fit_w_active = settings.fit_mode == "FitWidth";
            if ui
                .selectable_label(fit_w_active, "РџРѕ С€РёСЂРёРЅРµ")
                .on_hover_text("Р Р°СЃС‚СЏРЅСѓС‚СЊ РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёРµ РїРѕ С€РёСЂРёРЅРµ РѕРєРЅР°")
                .clicked()
            {
                settings.fit_mode = "FitWidth".to_string();
                self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
            }

            let fit_h_active = settings.fit_mode == "FitHeight";
            if ui
                .selectable_label(fit_h_active, "РџРѕ РІС‹СЃРѕС‚Рµ")
                .on_hover_text("Р Р°СЃС‚СЏРЅСѓС‚СЊ РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёРµ РїРѕ РІС‹СЃРѕС‚Рµ РѕРєРЅР°")
                .clicked()
            {
                settings.fit_mode = "FitHeight".to_string();
                self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
            }

            ui.with_layout(egui::Layout::right_to_left(egui::Align::Center), |ui| {
                let badge_txt = if is_manga {
                    "рџЌ™ РњР°РЅРіР°"
                } else {
                    "рџЋЁ РљРѕРјРёРєСЃ"
                };
                ui.label(
                    RichText::new(badge_txt)
                        .size(11.0)
                        .strong()
                        .color(theme.accent_color()),
                );
            });
        });
    }

    pub fn render(
        &mut self,
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        document: &Option<Arc<dyn Document>>,
        settings: &mut ComicSettings,
        theme: &ReaderTheme,
        pending_scroll_y: &mut Option<f32>,
        current_scroll_y: &mut f32,
        viewport_height: &mut f32,
        max_scroll_y: &mut f32,
        status_message: &mut Option<String>,
    ) {
        self.poll_responses(ctx, status_message);

        let page_count = self.page_count(document);
        if page_count == 0 {
            ui.centered_and_justified(|ui| {
                ui.label(RichText::new("Р’ СЌС‚РѕРј РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Рµ РЅРµС‚ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†").color(theme.text_color()));
            });
            return;
        }

        self.current_page = self.current_page.min(page_count.saturating_sub(1));
        let is_manga = self.is_effective_manga(document, settings);

        ui.add_space(2.0);
        self.render_toolbar(ui, page_count, settings, theme, is_manga);
        ui.add_space(4.0);
        ui.separator();

        let is_webtoon = settings.reading_mode == "Webtoon";

        if is_webtoon {
            let mut scroll_area = ScrollArea::vertical()
                .id_salt(("webtoon_scroll", self.doc_generation))
                .auto_shrink([false, false]);
            if let Some(offset) = pending_scroll_y.take() {
                scroll_area = scroll_area.vertical_scroll_offset(offset);
            }

            let avail_w = ui.available_width().max(200.0);
            let zoom_factor = (settings.zoom_percent / 100.0).clamp(0.3, 4.0);
            let target_w = match settings.fit_mode.as_str() {
                "FitWidth" => (avail_w - 32.0).clamp(200.0, 4000.0),
                "Custom" => (avail_w * 0.85 * zoom_factor).clamp(200.0, 6000.0),
                _ => (avail_w * 0.85).clamp(320.0, 1200.0),
            };
            let est_row_h = target_w * 1.4;

            let scroll_output =
                scroll_area.show_rows(ui, est_row_h, page_count, |ui, row_range| {
                    ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                        if let Some(first_visible) = row_range.clone().next() {
                            self.current_page = first_visible.min(page_count.saturating_sub(1));
                        }
                        for p in row_range {
                            if let Some(tex) = self.request_page_texture(ctx, document, p) {
                                let tex_size = tex.size_vec2();
                                let h = target_w * (tex_size.y / tex_size.x);
                                let img_widget = egui::Image::from_texture(&tex)
                                     .fit_to_exact_size(Vec2::new(target_w, h));
                                ui.add(img_widget);
                                ui.add_space(8.0);
                            } else if let Some(err) = self.failed_pages.get(&p) {
                                ui.label(
                                    RichText::new(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}: {err}", p + 1))
                                        .color(theme.accent_color()),
                                );
                            } else {
                                ui.add_space(24.0);
                                ui.label(
                                    RichText::new(format!("вЏі Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}...", p + 1))
                                        .color(theme.text_color()),
                                );
                                ui.add_space(24.0);
                            }
                        }
                    });
                });

            *current_scroll_y = scroll_output.state.offset.y;
            *viewport_height = scroll_output.inner_rect.height();
            *max_scroll_y = (scroll_output.content_size.y - *viewport_height).max(0.0);

            super::paged_common::handle_middle_mouse_scroll(
                ctx,
                *current_scroll_y,
                *max_scroll_y,
                pending_scroll_y,
            );
        } else {
            let avail_size = ui.available_size();
            let is_double = settings.page_layout == "Double" && self.current_page + 1 < page_count;

            let pages_to_show: Vec<usize> = if is_double {
                if is_manga {
                    vec![self.current_page + 1, self.current_page]
                } else {
                    vec![self.current_page, self.current_page + 1]
                }
            } else {
                vec![self.current_page]
            };

            let max_pan_x = (avail_size.x * 2.0).max(400.0);
            let max_pan_y = (avail_size.y * 2.0).max(400.0);
            super::paged_common::handle_paged_pan(ctx, &mut self.pan_offset, max_pan_x, max_pan_y);

            let num_pages = pages_to_show.len() as f32;
            let target_w_per_page = (avail_size.x - 24.0) / num_pages;
            let zoom_factor = (settings.zoom_percent / 100.0).clamp(0.3, 4.0);

            let mut prev_clicked = false;
            let mut next_clicked = false;

            let (container_rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(avail_size, egui::Sense::hover());
            let panned_rect = container_rect.translate(self.pan_offset);

            let mut child_ui = ui.new_child(
                egui::UiBuilder::new()
                    .max_rect(panned_rect)
                    .layout(egui::Layout::left_to_right(egui::Align::Center)),
            );
            child_ui.set_clip_rect(container_rect);

            child_ui.horizontal_centered(|ui| {
                ui.add_space(8.0);

                for &p in &pages_to_show {
                    if let Some(tex) = self.request_page_texture(ctx, document, p) {
                        let tex_size = tex.size_vec2();
                        let (display_w, display_h) = match settings.fit_mode.as_str() {
                            "FitHeight" => {
                                let h = (avail_size.y - 20.0).max(100.0);
                                let w = h * (tex_size.x / tex_size.y);
                                (w, h)
                            }
                            "FitWidth" => {
                                let w = (target_w_per_page - 12.0).max(100.0);
                                let h = w * (tex_size.y / tex_size.x);
                                (w, h)
                            }
                            "Original" => (tex_size.x, tex_size.y),
                            "Custom" => {
                                let w = (target_w_per_page - 12.0) * zoom_factor;
                                let h = w * (tex_size.y / tex_size.x);
                                (w, h)
                            }
                            _ => {
                                let max_w = (target_w_per_page - 12.0).max(100.0);
                                let max_h = (avail_size.y - 20.0).max(100.0);
                                let scale = (max_w / tex_size.x).min(max_h / tex_size.y);
                                (tex_size.x * scale, tex_size.y * scale)
                            }
                        };

                        let img_widget = egui::Image::from_texture(&tex)
                            .fit_to_exact_size(Vec2::new(display_w, display_h))
                            .sense(egui::Sense::click());
                        let img_resp = ui.add(img_widget);

                        if img_resp.clicked()
                            && let Some(pos) = img_resp.interact_pointer_pos()
                        {
                            let is_left = pos.x < img_resp.rect.center().x;
                            if (is_manga && is_left) || (!is_manga && !is_left) {
                                next_clicked = true;
                            } else {
                                prev_clicked = true;
                            }
                        }
                    } else if let Some(err) = self.failed_pages.get(&p) {
                        ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                            ui.add_space(avail_size.y * 0.4);
                            ui.label(
                                RichText::new(format!("вќЊ РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}: {err}", p + 1))
                                    .size(16.0)
                                    .color(theme.accent_color()),
                            );
                        });
                    } else {
                        ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                            ui.add_space(avail_size.y * 0.45);
                            ui.label(
                                RichText::new(format!("вЏі Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}...", p + 1))
                                    .size(18.0)
                                    .color(theme.text_color()),
                            );
                        });
                    }
                    ui.add_space(12.0);
                }
            });

            if self.current_page + 1 < page_count {
                let _ = self.request_page_texture(ctx, document, self.current_page + 1);
            }
            if self.current_page > 0 {
                let _ = self.request_page_texture(ctx, document, self.current_page - 1);
            }

            if next_clicked {
                self.next_page(page_count, settings);
            } else if prev_clicked {
                self.prev_page(settings);
            }
        }
    }
}

use crate::app::QuietReaderApp;
use crate::core::{Command, ReaderCore};
use crate::document::DocumentViewKind;
use eframe::egui;

impl QuietReaderApp {
    pub(crate) fn execute_command(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context, cmd: &Command, view_kind: DocumentViewKind) {
        match cmd {
            Command::CopyChapterText => {
                if let Some(doc) = &self.document {
                    let text_opt = if view_kind == DocumentViewKind::Pdf {
                        doc.page_content()
                            .and_then(|c| c.extract_page_text(self.pdf_view.current_page))
                    } else {
                        ReaderCore::extract_chapter_text(doc.as_ref(), self.current_chapter)
                    };
                    if let Some(text) = text_opt {
                        ctx.copy_text(text);
                        let msg = if view_kind == DocumentViewKind::Pdf {
                            format!(
                                "РўРµРєСЃС‚ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {} СЃРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ РІ Р±СѓС„РµСЂ РѕР±РјРµРЅР° (Ctrl+A)",
                                self.pdf_view.current_page + 1
                            )
                        } else {
                            "Р’РµСЃСЊ СЂР°Р·РґРµР» СЃРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ РІ Р±СѓС„РµСЂ РѕР±РјРµРЅР° (Ctrl+A)".to_string()
                        };
                        self.status_message = Some(msg);
                    }
                }
            }
            Command::ZoomIn | Command::ZoomOut | Command::ResetZoom => {
                self.font_size = match cmd {
                    Command::ZoomIn => (self.font_size + 1.0).clamp(12.0, 36.0),
                    Command::ZoomOut => (self.font_size - 1.0).clamp(12.0, 36.0),
                    _ => 19.0,
                };
                self.mark_settings_dirty();
            }
            Command::ToggleAutoscroll => {
                if self.document.is_some() {
                    self.autoscroll.toggle(
                        self.current_scroll_y,
                        self.max_scroll_y,
                        self.settings.auto_scroll.step_distance,
                    );
                }
            }
            Command::ToggleFullscreen => {
                self.set_fullscreen_mode(ctx, !self.is_fullscreen);
            }
            Command::ExitFullscreen => {
                if self.autoscroll.is_active() {
                    self.autoscroll.stop();
                } else if self.is_fullscreen {
                    self.set_fullscreen_mode(ctx, false);
                } else if self.show_settings_dialog {
                    self.show_settings_dialog = false;
                }
            }
            nav_cmd => {
                if matches!(
                    nav_cmd,
                    Command::PageDown
                        | Command::PageUp
                        | Command::LineDown
                        | Command::LineUp
                        | Command::NextChapter
                        | Command::PrevChapter
                ) {
                    self.autoscroll.stop();
                }
                match view_kind {
                    DocumentViewKind::Pdf => {
                        let total_pages = self.pdf_view.page_count(&self.document);
                        match nav_cmd {
                            Command::LineDown => {
                                if self.settings.pdf.layout == "Continuous" {
                                    let cur = self.pending_scroll_y.unwrap_or(self.current_scroll_y);
                                    self.pending_scroll_y = Some((cur + 60.0).clamp(0.0, self.max_scroll_y));
                                } else if self.settings.pdf.fit_mode != "FitWindow" && self.pdf_view.pan_offset.y > -400.0 {
                                    self.pdf_view.pan_offset.y -= 60.0;
                                } else {
                                    self.pdf_view.next_page(total_pages, &self.settings.pdf);
                                }
                                self.mark_settings_dirty();
                            }
                            Command::LineUp => {
                                if self.settings.pdf.layout == "Continuous" {
                                    let cur = self.pending_scroll_y.unwrap_or(self.current_scroll_y);
                                    self.pending_scroll_y = Some((cur - 60.0).clamp(0.0, self.max_scroll_y));
                                } else if self.settings.pdf.fit_mode != "FitWindow" && self.pdf_view.pan_offset.y < 400.0 {
                                    self.pdf_view.pan_offset.y += 60.0;
                                } else {
                                    self.pdf_view.prev_page(&self.settings.pdf);
                                }
                                self.mark_settings_dirty();
                            }
                            Command::PageDown | Command::NextPage => {
                                if self.settings.pdf.layout == "Continuous" {
                                    let cur = self.pending_scroll_y.unwrap_or(self.current_scroll_y);
                                    self.pending_scroll_y = Some((cur + self.viewport_height * 0.85).clamp(0.0, self.max_scroll_y));
                                } else {
                                    self.pdf_view.next_page(total_pages, &self.settings.pdf);
                                }
                                self.mark_settings_dirty();
                            }
                            Command::PageUp | Command::PrevPage => {
                                if self.settings.pdf.layout == "Continuous" {
                                    let cur = self.pending_scroll_y.unwrap_or(self.current_scroll_y);
                                    self.pending_scroll_y = Some((cur - self.viewport_height * 0.85).clamp(0.0, self.max_scroll_y));
                                } else {
                                    self.pdf_view.prev_page(&self.settings.pdf);
                                }
                                self.mark_settings_dirty();
                            }
                            _ => {}
                        }
                    }
                    DocumentViewKind::Comic => {
                        if ReaderCore::apply_paged_command(
                            nav_cmd,
                            &self.document,
                            &self.settings.comic,
                            &mut self.comic_view.current_page,
                            self.current_scroll_y,
                            self.max_scroll_y,
                            self.viewport_height,
                            &mut self.pending_scroll_y,
                        ) {
                            self.mark_settings_dirty();
                        }
                    }
                    DocumentViewKind::Text => {
                        if let Some(doc) = &self.document {
                            ReaderCore::apply_text_command(
                                nav_cmd,
                                doc.as_ref(),
                                &mut self.current_chapter,
                                &mut self.last_rendered_chapter,
                                self.current_scroll_y,
                                self.max_scroll_y,
                                self.viewport_height,
                                &mut self.pending_scroll_y,
                            );
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

        }

=== FILE: C:\Users\Almas\Documents\QuietReader\src\app\document_loader.rs ===
use crate::document::{Document, DocumentError};
use crate::settings::AppSettings;
use eframe::egui;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::mpsc::{self, Receiver, Sender};
use std::sync::Arc;

pub fn format_document_error(err: &DocumentError) -> String {
    match err {
        DocumentError::Io(msg) => format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° РІРІРѕРґР°-РІС‹РІРѕРґР°: {msg}"),
        DocumentError::Unsupported(msg) => format!("РќРµРїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РёРІР°РµРјС‹Р№ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚: {msg}"),
        DocumentError::Corrupted(msg) => format!("РџРѕРІСЂРµР¶РґС‘РЅРЅС‹Р№ РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚: {msg}"),
        DocumentError::LimitExceeded(msg) => format!("РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚ Р±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё: {msg}"),
    }
}

pub struct DocumentLoadRequest {
    pub request_id: u64,
    pub path: PathBuf,
    pub ctx: Option<egui::Context>,
}

pub struct DocumentLoadResponse {
    pub request_id: u64,
    pub path: PathBuf,
    pub result: Result<Arc<dyn Document>, String>,
}

pub struct LoadedBookState {
    pub document: Arc<dyn Document>,
    pub file_path: PathBuf,
    pub chapter: usize,
    pub scroll_y: f32,
    pub comic_page: usize,
    pub status_message: String,
}

pub struct DocumentLoader {
    req_tx: Sender<DocumentLoadRequest>,
    resp_rx: Receiver<DocumentLoadResponse>,
    request_counter: u64,
    ctx: Option<egui::Context>,
}

fn spawn_loader_worker(
    req_rx: Receiver<DocumentLoadRequest>,
    resp_tx: Sender<DocumentLoadResponse>,
) {
    std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("document-loader".to_string())
        .spawn(move || {
            while let Ok(req) = req_rx.recv() {
                let path = req.path.clone();
                let load_res = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
                    crate::document::open_document(&path)
                        .map(Arc::from)
                        .map_err(|e| format_document_error(&e))
                }));

                let result = match load_res {
                    Ok(res) => res,
                    Err(_) => Err("РђРІР°СЂРёР№РЅС‹Р№ СЃР±РѕР№ РїСЂРё РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РёРё РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°".to_string()),
                };

                let _ = resp_tx.send(DocumentLoadResponse {
                    request_id: req.request_id,
                    path: req.path,
                    result,
                });
                if let Some(ctx) = req.ctx {
                    ctx.request_repaint();
                }
            }
        })
        .ok();
}

impl Default for DocumentLoader {
    fn default() -> Self {
        let (req_tx, req_rx) = mpsc::channel::<DocumentLoadRequest>();
        let (resp_tx, resp_rx) = mpsc::channel::<DocumentLoadResponse>();
        spawn_loader_worker(req_rx, resp_tx);

        Self {
            req_tx,
            resp_rx,
            request_counter: 0,
            ctx: None,
        }
    }
}

impl DocumentLoader {
    pub fn update_ctx(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context) {
        if self.ctx.is_none() {
            self.ctx = Some(ctx.clone());
        }
    }

    pub fn request_load(&mut self, path: PathBuf) -> u64 {
        self.request_counter = self.request_counter.wrapping_add(1);
        let request_id = self.request_counter;
        let _ = self.req_tx.send(DocumentLoadRequest {
            request_id,
            path,
            ctx: self.ctx.clone(),
        });
        request_id
    }

    pub fn poll_responses(&mut self) -> Vec<DocumentLoadResponse> {
        let mut responses = Vec::new();
        loop {
            match self.resp_rx.try_recv() {
                Ok(resp) => responses.push(resp),
                Err(mpsc::TryRecvError::Empty) => break,
                Err(mpsc::TryRecvError::Disconnected) => {
                    let (req_tx, req_rx) = mpsc::channel::<DocumentLoadRequest>();
                    let (resp_tx, resp_rx) = mpsc::channel::<DocumentLoadResponse>();
                    spawn_loader_worker(req_rx, resp_tx);
                    self.req_tx = req_tx;
                    self.resp_rx = resp_rx;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        responses
    }

    pub fn finalize_loaded_document(
        doc: Arc<dyn Document>,
        path: PathBuf,
        settings: &mut AppSettings,
    ) -> LoadedBookState {
        let title = doc.metadata().title.clone();
        let author = doc.metadata().author.clone();
        let total_chapters = doc.chapters().len();
        let is_comic = doc.page_content().is_some();
        let comic_page_count = doc.page_content().map(|c| c.page_count()).unwrap_or(0);

        let (ch, scroll_y) =
            if let Some((saved_ch, saved_scroll)) = settings.get_book_progress(&path) {
                (saved_ch, saved_scroll)
            } else {
                (0, 0.0)
            };

        let comic_page = if is_comic && comic_page_count > 0 {
            ch.min(comic_page_count.saturating_sub(1))
        } else {
            0
        };

        let effective_ch = if is_comic {
            0
        } else {
            ch.min(total_chapters.saturating_sub(1))
        };

        settings.record_book_progress(
            &path,
            &title,
            &author,
            if is_comic { comic_page } else { effective_ch },
            scroll_y,
        );
        let fmt = doc.format_name();
        let status = if is_comic {
            if comic_page > 0 {
                format!(
                    "РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚ {fmt}: {title} (СЃС‚СЂ. {}/{})",
                    comic_page + 1,
                    comic_page_count
                )
            } else {
                format!("РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚ {fmt}: {title} (РІСЃРµРіРѕ {} СЃС‚СЂ.)", comic_page_count)
            }
        } else if effective_ch > 0 || scroll_y > 10.0 {
            format!(
                "РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚Р° РєРЅРёРіР°: {title} (РїСЂРѕРґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅРёРµ СЃ СЂР°Р·РґРµР»Р° {})",
                effective_ch + 1
            )
        } else if !author.is_empty() {
            format!("РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚Р° РєРЅРёРіР°: {author} вЂ” {title}")
        } else {
            format!("РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚Р° РєРЅРёРіР°: {title}")
        };

        LoadedBookState {
            document: doc,
            file_path: path,
            chapter: effective_ch,
            scroll_y,
            comic_page,
            status_message: status,
        }
    }

    pub fn load_file_sync(
        path: PathBuf,
        settings: &mut AppSettings,
    ) -> Result<LoadedBookState, String> {
        let doc: Arc<dyn Document> = Arc::from(
            crate::document::open_document(&path).map_err(|e| format_document_error(&e))?,
        );
        Ok(Self::finalize_loaded_document(doc, path, settings))
    }
}

use eframe::egui;

pub fn apply_font_family(ctx: &egui::Context, font_name: &str) {
    let mut fonts = egui::FontDefinitions::default();

    if let Some(bytes) = crate::platform::load_system_font_bytes(font_name) {
        fonts.font_data.insert(
            font_name.to_owned(),
            std::sync::Arc::new(egui::FontData::from_owned(bytes)),
        );
        fonts
            .families
            .entry(egui::FontFamily::Proportional)
            .or_default()
            .insert(0, font_name.to_owned());
    }

    ctx.set_fonts(fonts);
}

    pub id: &'static str,
    pub name: &'static str,
    pub badge: &'static str,
    pub description: &'static str,
}

use crate::core::Command;
use eframe::egui;

pub fn process_keyboard_events(
    wants_keyboard_input: bool,
    key_pressed: impl Fn(egui::Key) -> bool,
    modifiers: egui::Modifiers,
    is_manga: bool,
) -> Vec<Command> {
    if wants_keyboard_input {
        return Vec::new();
    }

    let mut cmds = Vec::new();
    let ctrl = modifiers.ctrl || modifiers.command || modifiers.alt;
    let cmd_mod = modifiers.ctrl || modifiers.command;
    let shift = modifiers.shift;

    if cmd_mod && key_pressed(egui::Key::A) {
        cmds.push(Command::CopyChapterText);
    } else if !ctrl && key_pressed(egui::Key::A) {
        cmds.push(Command::ToggleAutoscroll);
    }
    if cmd_mod && (key_pressed(egui::Key::Plus) || key_pressed(egui::Key::Equals)) {
        cmds.push(Command::ZoomIn);
    } else if cmd_mod && key_pressed(egui::Key::Minus) {
        cmds.push(Command::ZoomOut);
    } else if cmd_mod && key_pressed(egui::Key::Num0) {
        cmds.push(Command::ResetZoom);
    }
    if !ctrl && key_pressed(egui::Key::F) {
        cmds.push(Command::ToggleFullscreen);
    } else if key_pressed(egui::Key::Escape) {
        cmds.push(Command::ExitFullscreen);
    }
    if ctrl && (key_pressed(egui::Key::ArrowRight) || key_pressed(egui::Key::ArrowDown)) {
        cmds.push(Command::NextChapter);
    } else if ctrl && (key_pressed(egui::Key::ArrowLeft) || key_pressed(egui::Key::ArrowUp)) {
        cmds.push(Command::PrevChapter);
    }

    if key_pressed(egui::Key::PageDown) || (!shift && key_pressed(egui::Key::Space)) {
        cmds.push(Command::PageDown);
    } else if key_pressed(egui::Key::PageUp) || (shift && key_pressed(egui::Key::Space)) {
        cmds.push(Command::PageUp);
    } else if key_pressed(egui::Key::ArrowDown) {
        cmds.push(Command::LineDown);
    } else if key_pressed(egui::Key::ArrowUp) {
        cmds.push(Command::LineUp);
    } else if key_pressed(egui::Key::Home) {
        cmds.push(Command::Home);
    } else if key_pressed(egui::Key::End) {
        cmds.push(Command::End);
    } else if !ctrl && key_pressed(egui::Key::ArrowLeft) {
        cmds.push(if is_manga {
            Command::NextPage
        } else {
            Command::PrevPage
        });
    } else if !ctrl && key_pressed(egui::Key::ArrowRight) {
        cmds.push(if is_manga {
            Command::PrevPage
        } else {
            Command::NextPage
        });
    }
    cmds
}

pub fn poll_keyboard_commands(ctx: &egui::Context, is_manga: bool) -> Vec<Command> {
    if ctx.egui_wants_keyboard_input() {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    ctx.input(|i| process_keyboard_events(false, |k| i.key_pressed(k), i.modifiers, is_manga))
}

        Self::from_settings(AppSettings::load())

impl Drop for QuietReaderApp {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        crate::platform::set_keep_screen_on(false);
        self.flush_settings_now();
    }
