impl QuietReaderApp {
    pub fn from_settings(settings: AppSettings) -> Self {
        let assoc_status = crate::platform::check_fb2_association_status();
        let is_already_associated = assoc_status == crate::platform::AssociationStatus::Default
            || assoc_status == crate::platform::AssociationStatus::Registered;
        let dismissed = settings.dismiss_association_prompt
            || crate::platform::is_association_prompt_dismissed();
        let theme = match settings.theme.as_str() {
            "Dark" => ReaderTheme::Dark,
            "Light" => ReaderTheme::Light,
            "Oled" => ReaderTheme::Oled,
            "Warm" => ReaderTheme::Warm,
            "Mint" => ReaderTheme::Mint,
            _ => ReaderTheme::Sepia,
        };

        let show_association_dialog =
            crate::platform::supports_file_association() && !is_already_associated && !dismissed;

        Self {
            tabs: Vec::new(),
            active_tab: 0,
            document: None,
            current_file_path: None,
            current_chapter: 0,
            last_rendered_chapter: 0,
            pending_scroll_y: None,
            current_scroll_y: 0.0,
            viewport_height: 600.0,
            max_scroll_y: 0.0,
            font_size: settings.font_size,
            theme,
            show_toc: settings.show_toc,
            keep_screen_on: settings.keep_screen_on,
            is_fullscreen: false,
            status_message: None,
            settings,
            settings_dirty: false,
            last_settings_change: Instant::now(),
            last_saved_scroll_y: 0.0,
            show_association_dialog,
            dont_show_association_again: false,
            show_recent_dialog: false,
            show_settings_dialog: false,
            settings_tab: SettingsTab::Fonts,
            autoscroll: AutoscrollController::default(),
            comic_view: ComicViewState::default(),
            pdf_view: PdfViewState::default(),
            document_loader: DocumentLoader::default(),
            core: ReaderCore::default(),
            last_applied_theme: None,
        }
    }

    pub fn new(
        cc: &eframe::CreationContext<'_>,
        initial_file: Option<PathBuf>,
        settings: AppSettings,
    ) -> Self {
        apply_font_family(&cc.egui_ctx, &settings.font_family);
        let saved_tabs = settings.open_tabs.clone();
        let saved_active_idx = settings.active_tab_index;
        let mut app = Self::from_settings(settings);
        app.document_loader.update_ctx(&cc.egui_ctx);
        crate::platform::set_keep_screen_on(app.keep_screen_on);

        if !saved_tabs.is_empty() {
            for tab_path in saved_tabs {
                if tab_path.exists() {
                    app.load_file(tab_path);
                }
            }
            if !app.tabs.is_empty() {
                let target_idx = saved_active_idx.min(app.tabs.len() - 1);
                app.activate_tab(target_idx);
            }
        } else if let Some(last_path) = app.settings.last_opened_book.clone() && last_path.exists() {
            app.load_file(last_path);
        }

        if let Some(path) = initial_file && path.exists() {
            app.load_file(path);
        }

        app
    }

    pub fn view_kind(&self) -> DocumentViewKind {
        self.document
            .as_ref()
            .map(|d| d.view_kind())
            .unwrap_or(DocumentViewKind::Text)
    }

    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub fn is_comic(&self) -> bool {
        self.view_kind() == DocumentViewKind::Comic
    }

    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub fn is_pdf(&self) -> bool {
        self.view_kind() == DocumentViewKind::Pdf
    }

    pub fn save_active_to_tab(&mut self) {
        let cur_page = match self.view_kind() {
            DocumentViewKind::Pdf => self.pdf_view.current_page,
            _ => self.comic_view.current_page,
        };
        if let Some(tab) = self.tabs.get_mut(self.active_tab)
            && self.document.is_some()
        {
            tab.current_chapter = self.current_chapter;
            tab.last_rendered_chapter = self.last_rendered_chapter;
            tab.current_scroll_y = self.current_scroll_y;
            tab.viewport_height = self.viewport_height;
            tab.max_scroll_y = self.max_scroll_y;
            tab.comic_page = cur_page;
        }
        ReaderCore::record_progress(
            &mut self.settings,
            &self.document,
            &self.current_file_path,
            self.current_chapter,
            cur_page,
            self.current_scroll_y,
        );
    }

    pub fn mark_settings_dirty(&mut self) {
        self.settings_dirty = true;
        self.last_settings_change = Instant::now();
    }

    pub fn flush_debounced_settings(&mut self) {
        if self.settings_dirty
            && self.last_settings_change.elapsed() >= Duration::from_millis(1500)
        {
            self.flush_settings_now();
        }
    }

    pub fn flush_settings_now(&mut self) {
        self.save_active_to_tab();
        self.sync_settings_fields();
        if let Err(e) = self.settings.save() {
            self.status_message = Some(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёСЏ: {e}"));
        }
        self.settings_dirty = false;
        self.last_settings_change = Instant::now();
    }

    fn sync_settings_fields(&mut self) {
        self.settings.open_tabs = self.tabs.iter().map(|t| t.file_path.clone()).collect();
        self.settings.active_tab_index = self.active_tab;
        self.settings.font_size = self.font_size;
        self.settings.keep_screen_on = self.keep_screen_on;
        self.settings.show_toc = self.show_toc;
        self.settings.theme = match self.theme {
            ReaderTheme::Dark => "Dark",
            ReaderTheme::Light => "Light",
            ReaderTheme::Sepia => "Sepia",
            ReaderTheme::Oled => "Oled",
            ReaderTheme::Warm => "Warm",
            ReaderTheme::Mint => "Mint",
        }
        .to_string();
    }

    pub fn activate_tab(&mut self, idx: usize) {
        if idx >= self.tabs.len() {
            return;
        }
        self.flush_settings_now();
        self.autoscroll.stop();
        self.apply_tab_state(idx);
    }

    fn apply_tab_state(&mut self, idx: usize) {
        if let Some(tab) = self.tabs.get(idx).cloned() {
            self.active_tab = idx;
            self.document = tab.document;
            self.current_file_path = Some(tab.file_path.clone());
            self.current_chapter = tab.current_chapter;
            self.last_rendered_chapter = tab.current_chapter;
            self.pending_scroll_y = Some(tab.current_scroll_y);
            self.current_scroll_y = tab.current_scroll_y;
            self.viewport_height = tab.viewport_height;
            self.max_scroll_y = tab.max_scroll_y;
            self.comic_view.clear_cache();
            self.comic_view.current_page = tab.comic_page;
            self.pdf_view.clear_cache();
            self.pdf_view.current_page = tab.comic_page;
            self.settings.last_opened_book = Some(tab.file_path);
            self.settings.active_tab_index = idx;
            if tab.is_loading {
                self.status_message = Some(format!("Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° [{}]: {}...", tab.format_badge, tab.title));
            } else if let Some(ref err) = tab.load_error {
                self.status_message = Some(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° [{}]: {err}", tab.format_badge));
            } else {
                self.status_message = Some(format!("Р’РєР»Р°РґРєР° [{}]: {}", tab.format_badge, tab.title));
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn close_tab(&mut self, idx: usize) {
        if idx >= self.tabs.len() {
            return;
        }
        self.save_active_to_tab();
        self.tabs.remove(idx);

        if self.tabs.is_empty() {
            self.active_tab = 0;
            self.document = None;
            self.current_file_path = None;
            self.current_chapter = 0;
            self.last_rendered_chapter = 0;
            self.pending_scroll_y = None;
            self.current_scroll_y = 0.0;
            self.max_scroll_y = 0.0;
            self.comic_view.clear_cache();
            self.pdf_view.clear_cache();
            self.autoscroll.stop();
            self.status_message = Some("Р’СЃРµ РІРєР»Р°РґРєРё Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚С‹".to_string());
        } else {
            let next_idx = if idx < self.active_tab {
                self.active_tab - 1
            } else {
                self.active_tab.min(self.tabs.len() - 1)
            };
            self.apply_tab_state(next_idx);
        }
        self.flush_settings_now();
    }

    pub fn next_tab(&mut self) {
        if self.tabs.len() > 1 {
            self.activate_tab((self.active_tab + 1) % self.tabs.len());
        }
    }

    pub fn prev_tab(&mut self) {
        if self.tabs.len() > 1 {
            let prev = if self.active_tab == 0 { self.tabs.len() - 1 } else { self.active_tab - 1 };
            self.activate_tab(prev);
        }
    }

    pub fn sync_and_save_settings(&mut self) {
        self.flush_settings_now();
    }

    pub fn register_file_association(&mut self) {
        match crate::platform::register_fb2_association() {
            Ok(msg) => {
                self.status_message = Some(msg);
                crate::platform::open_default_apps_settings();
            }
            Err(e) => {
                self.status_message = Some(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° Р°СЃСЃРѕС†РёР°С†РёРё: {e}"));
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn open_file_dialog(&mut self) {
        if let Some(paths) = Dialogs::pick_document_files() {
            for path in paths { self.load_file(path); }
        }
    }

    pub fn open_folder_dialog(&mut self) {
        if let Some(path) = Dialogs::pick_comic_folder() {
            self.load_file(path);
        }
    }

    pub fn load_file(&mut self, path: PathBuf) {
        if let Some(existing_idx) = self.tabs.iter().position(|t| t.file_path == path) {
            self.activate_tab(existing_idx);
            return;
        }

        self.flush_settings_now();
        self.autoscroll.stop();

        let request_id = self.document_loader.request_load(path.clone());
        let tab = BookTab::new_loading(path, request_id);
        self.tabs.push(tab);
        let new_idx = self.tabs.len() - 1;
        self.apply_tab_state(new_idx);
    }

    pub fn poll_document_loader(&mut self) {
        let responses = self.document_loader.poll_responses();
        for resp in responses {
            let matching_idx = self
                .tabs
                .iter()
                .position(|t| t.file_path == resp.path && t.request_id == resp.request_id);
            let Some(idx) = matching_idx else {
                continue;
            };
            match resp.result {
                Ok(doc) => {
                    let loaded = DocumentLoader::finalize_loaded_document(
                        doc,
                        resp.path,
                        &mut self.settings,
                    );
                    if let Some(tab) = self.tabs.get_mut(idx) {
                        tab.update_loaded(
                            loaded.document.clone(),
                            loaded.chapter,
                            loaded.scroll_y,
                            loaded.comic_page,
                        );
                    }
                    if self.active_tab == idx {
                        self.document = Some(loaded.document);
                        self.current_file_path = Some(loaded.file_path);
                        self.current_chapter = loaded.chapter;
                        self.last_rendered_chapter = loaded.chapter;
                        self.pending_scroll_y = Some(loaded.scroll_y);
                        self.current_scroll_y = loaded.scroll_y;
                        self.comic_view.clear_cache();
                        self.comic_view.current_page = loaded.comic_page;
                        self.pdf_view.clear_cache();
                        self.pdf_view.current_page = loaded.comic_page;
                        self.status_message = Some(loaded.status_message);
                        self.mark_settings_dirty();
                    }
                }
                Err(err) => {
                    if let Some(tab) = self.tabs.get_mut(idx) {
                        tab.is_loading = false;
                        tab.load_error = Some(err.clone());
                    }
                    if self.active_tab == idx {
                        self.document = None;
                        self.status_message = Some(err);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn set_fullscreen_mode(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context, fullscreen: bool) {
        if self.is_fullscreen == fullscreen {
            return;
        }
        self.is_fullscreen = fullscreen;

        if !crate::platform::set_window_fullscreen(fullscreen) {
            ctx.send_viewport_cmd(egui::ViewportCommand::Fullscreen(fullscreen));
        }

        ctx.request_repaint();
    }
}

impl eframe::App for QuietReaderApp {
    fn ui(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui, _frame: &mut eframe::Frame) {
        let ctx = ui.ctx().clone();
        self.document_loader.update_ctx(&ctx);
        self.poll_document_loader();
        self.flush_debounced_settings();

        crate::platform::register_primary_window_hwnd();
        let (should_focus, ipc_files) = crate::platform::poll_single_instance_requests();
        if should_focus {
            ctx.send_viewport_cmd(egui::ViewportCommand::Minimized(false));
            ctx.send_viewport_cmd(egui::ViewportCommand::Focus);
            for path in ipc_files {
                self.load_file(path);
            }
            ctx.request_repaint();
        }

        if !self.is_fullscreen {
            if !ctx.input(|i| i.pointer.any_down()) && crate::platform::ensure_window_on_screen() {
                ctx.request_repaint();
            }
            let close_requested = ctx.input(|i| {
                let vp = i.viewport();
                if !vp.minimized.unwrap_or(false)
                    && let Some(inner) = vp.inner_rect
                {
                    let pos = vp.outer_rect.map(|r| (r.min.x, r.min.y));
                    self.settings.update_window_state(
                        pos.map(|p| p.0),
                        pos.map(|p| p.1),
                        inner.width(),
                        inner.height(),
                        vp.maximized.unwrap_or(false),
                    );
                }
                vp.close_requested()
            });
            if close_requested {
                self.flush_settings_now();
            }
        }

        for dropped in ctx.input(|i| i.raw.dropped_files.clone()) {
            let path = dropped.path();
            if !path.as_os_str().is_empty() {
                self.load_file(path.to_path_buf());
            }
        }

        let (ctrl_o, ctrl_w, next_tab_key, prev_tab_key) = if ctx.egui_wants_keyboard_input() {
            (false, false, false, false)
        } else {
            ctx.input(|i| {
                let cmd_mod = i.modifiers.ctrl || i.modifiers.command;
                let shift = i.modifiers.shift;
                (
                    cmd_mod && i.key_pressed(egui::Key::O),
                    cmd_mod && i.key_pressed(egui::Key::W),
                    cmd_mod && !shift && i.key_pressed(egui::Key::Tab),
                    cmd_mod && shift && i.key_pressed(egui::Key::Tab),
                )
            })
        };
        if ctrl_o {
            self.open_file_dialog();
        }
        if ctrl_w && !self.tabs.is_empty() {
            self.close_tab(self.active_tab);
        } else if next_tab_key {
            self.next_tab();
        } else if prev_tab_key {
            self.prev_tab();
        }

        if self.current_chapter != self.last_rendered_chapter {
            self.pending_scroll_y = Some(0.0);
            self.current_scroll_y = 0.0;
            self.last_saved_scroll_y = 0.0;
            self.last_rendered_chapter = self.current_chapter;
            self.mark_settings_dirty();
        }

        if (self.current_scroll_y - self.last_saved_scroll_y).abs() > 20.0 {
            self.last_saved_scroll_y = self.current_scroll_y;
            self.mark_settings_dirty();
        }

        let is_manga = self
            .comic_view
            .is_effective_manga(&self.document, &self.settings.comic);
        let view_kind = self.view_kind();

        let commands = poll_keyboard_commands(&ctx, is_manga);

        for cmd in &commands {
            self.execute_command(&ctx, cmd, view_kind);
        }

        match view_kind {
            DocumentViewKind::Text => {
                if let Some(doc) = &self.document {
                    let total_chapters = doc.chapters().len();
                    let next_non_empty =
                        ReaderCore::find_next_non_empty_chapter(doc.as_ref(), self.current_chapter);
                    self.autoscroll.update(
                        &ctx,
                        &self.settings.auto_scroll,
                        self.current_scroll_y,
                        self.max_scroll_y,
                        total_chapters,
                        &mut self.current_chapter,
                        &mut self.pending_scroll_y,
                        &mut self.last_rendered_chapter,
                        next_non_empty,
                    );
                }
            }
            DocumentViewKind::Pdf => {
                if self.settings.pdf.layout == "Continuous" {
                    self.autoscroll.update_continuous(
                        &ctx,
                        &self.settings.auto_scroll,
                        self.current_scroll_y,
                        self.max_scroll_y,
                        &mut self.pending_scroll_y,
                    );
                } else {
                    let total_pages = self.pdf_view.page_count(&self.document);
                    let cur_page = self.pdf_view.current_page;
                    if self.autoscroll.update_paged(&ctx, &self.settings.auto_scroll, cur_page, total_pages) {
                        self.pdf_view.next_page(total_pages, &self.settings.pdf);
                        self.mark_settings_dirty();
                    }
                }
            }
            DocumentViewKind::Comic => {
                if self.settings.comic.reading_mode == "Webtoon" {
                    self.autoscroll.update_continuous(
                        &ctx,
                        &self.settings.auto_scroll,
                        self.current_scroll_y,
                        self.max_scroll_y,
                        &mut self.pending_scroll_y,
                    );
                } else {
                    let total_pages = self.comic_view.page_count(&self.document);
                    let cur_page = self.comic_view.current_page;
                    if self.autoscroll.update_paged(&ctx, &self.settings.auto_scroll, cur_page, total_pages) {
                        self.comic_view.next_page(total_pages, &self.settings.comic);
                        self.mark_settings_dirty();
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if self.last_applied_theme.as_ref() != Some(&self.theme) {
            ctx.set_visuals(self.theme.create_visuals());
            self.last_applied_theme = Some(self.theme.clone());
        }

        let ptr_top = ctx
            .input(|i| i.pointer.latest_pos().map(|p| p.y < 72.0))
            .unwrap_or(false);
        if !self.is_fullscreen || ptr_top || self.show_settings_dialog {
            let actions = render_top_bar(
                ui,
                self.document.is_some(),
                self.is_fullscreen,
                self.show_toc,
            );
            if actions.open_file {
                self.open_file_dialog();
            }
            if actions.open_folder {
                self.open_folder_dialog();
            }
            if actions.toggle_recent {
                self.show_recent_dialog = !self.show_recent_dialog;
            }
            if actions.toggle_toc {
                self.show_toc = !self.show_toc;
            }
            if actions.toggle_fullscreen {
                self.set_fullscreen_mode(&ctx, !self.is_fullscreen);
            }
            if actions.toggle_settings {
                self.show_settings_dialog = !self.show_settings_dialog;
            }

            if !self.tabs.is_empty() {
                let mut tab_action = TabBarAction::None;
                Panel::top("tabs_bar").show(ui, |ui| {
                    tab_action = TabsBar::render(ui, &self.tabs, self.active_tab, &self.theme);
                });
                match tab_action {
                    TabBarAction::None => {}
                    TabBarAction::Activate(idx) => self.activate_tab(idx),
                    TabBarAction::Close(idx) => self.close_tab(idx),
                    TabBarAction::OpenNewFile => self.open_file_dialog(),
                }
            }

            let pdf_info = (self.pdf_view.current_page, self.pdf_view.page_count(&self.document));
            let comic_info = (self.comic_view.current_page, self.comic_view.page_count(&self.document));
            let text_info = (
                self.current_chapter,
                self.document.as_ref().map(|d| d.chapters().len()).unwrap_or(0),
                self.current_scroll_y,
                self.max_scroll_y,
            );
            render_bottom_bar(
                ui,
                &self.document,
                &self.theme,
                self.status_message.as_deref(),
                view_kind,
                pdf_info,
                comic_info,
                text_info,
            );
        }

        if !self.is_fullscreen || self.show_toc {
            let prev_pdf_page = self.pdf_view.current_page;
            let current_page_ref = match view_kind {
                DocumentViewKind::Pdf => &mut self.pdf_view.current_page,
                _ => &mut self.comic_view.current_page,
            };
            TocView::render(
                ui,
                &self.document,
                &self.settings.comic,
                &mut self.current_chapter,
                current_page_ref,
                &mut self.show_toc,
                &self.theme,
            );
            if view_kind == DocumentViewKind::Pdf && self.pdf_view.current_page != prev_pdf_page {
                self.pdf_view.go_to_page(self.pdf_view.current_page);
            }
        }

        let mut on_open_file = false;
        let mut on_open_folder = false;
        let mut on_load_recent = None;
        let mut toggle_autoscroll_action = false;
        let mut save_settings_action = false;
        let mut register_assoc_action = false;
        let mut close_tab_action = false;

        let active_tab_info = self
            .tabs
            .get(self.active_tab)
            .map(|t| (t.is_loading, t.title.clone(), t.load_error.clone()));

        egui::CentralPanel::default()
            .frame(
                egui::Frame::default()
                    .fill(self.theme.bg_color())
                    .inner_margin(16.0),
            )
            .show(ui, |ui| {
                if self.show_settings_dialog {
                    let mut is_autoscrolling_flag = self.autoscroll.is_active();
                    SettingsView::render(
                        ui,
                        &ctx,
                        &mut self.show_settings_dialog,
                        &mut self.settings_tab,
                        &mut self.settings,
                        &mut self.font_size,
                        &mut self.theme,
                        &mut self.keep_screen_on,
                        &mut is_autoscrolling_flag,
                        &mut toggle_autoscroll_action,
                        &mut save_settings_action,
                        &mut register_assoc_action,
                    );
                } else if let Some((true, title, _)) = active_tab_info {
                    ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                        ui.add_space(ui.available_height() * 0.35);
                        ui.spinner();
                        ui.add_space(16.0);
                        ui.label(
                            RichText::new(format!("Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° В«{}В»...", title))
                                .size(18.0)
                                .color(self.theme.text_color()),
                        );
                        ui.add_space(8.0);
                        ui.label(
                            RichText::new("РџРѕР¶Р°Р»СѓР№СЃС‚Р°, РїРѕРґРѕР¶РґРёС‚Рµ...")
                                .size(13.0)
                                .color(self.theme.secondary_text_color()),
                        );
                    });
                } else if let Some((false, _, Some(err))) = active_tab_info {
                    ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                        ui.add_space(ui.available_height() * 0.35);
                        ui.label(
                            RichText::new("вљ  РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚")
                                .size(20.0)
                                .color(egui::Color32::RED),
                        );
                        ui.add_space(12.0);
                        ui.label(RichText::new(err).size(14.0).color(self.theme.text_color()));
                        ui.add_space(16.0);
                        if ui.button("Р—Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РІРєР»Р°РґРєСѓ").clicked() {
                            close_tab_action = true;

                    });

                    if self.document.is_some()
                        && (view_kind == DocumentViewKind::Text
                            || (view_kind == DocumentViewKind::Comic
                                && self.settings.comic.reading_mode == "Webtoon"))
                        && !self.show_recent_dialog
                        && !self.show_association_dialog

                        self.autoscroll.handle_middle_click_scroll(
                            &ctx,
                            self.show_toc,
                            self.current_scroll_y,
                            self.max_scroll_y,
                            &mut self.pending_scroll_y,
                        );
                    }

                    match view_kind {
                        DocumentViewKind::Pdf => {
                            self.pdf_view.render(
                                ui,
                                &ctx,
                                &self.document,
                                &mut self.settings.pdf,
                                &self.theme,
                                &mut self.pending_scroll_y,
                                &mut self.current_scroll_y,
                                &mut self.viewport_height,
                                &mut self.max_scroll_y,
                                &mut self.status_message,
                            );
                        }
                        DocumentViewKind::Comic => {
                            self.comic_view.render(
                                ui,
                                &ctx,
                                &self.document,
                                &mut self.settings.comic,
                                &self.theme,
                                &mut self.pending_scroll_y,
                                &mut self.current_scroll_y,
                                &mut self.viewport_height,
                                &mut self.max_scroll_y,
                                &mut self.status_message,
                            );
                        }
                        DocumentViewKind::Text => {
                            TextView::render(
                                ui,
                                &ctx,
                                &self.document,
                                &self.current_file_path,
                                &mut self.current_chapter,
                                &mut self.last_rendered_chapter,
                                &mut self.pending_scroll_y,
                                &mut self.current_scroll_y,
                                &mut self.viewport_height,
                                &mut self.max_scroll_y,
                                self.font_size,
                                &self.theme,
                                &mut self.settings,
                                &mut self.status_message,
                                &mut on_open_file,
                                &mut on_open_folder,
                                &mut on_load_recent,
                            );
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

        if close_tab_action { self.close_tab(self.active_tab); }
        if on_open_file { self.open_file_dialog(); }
        if on_open_folder { self.open_folder_dialog(); }
        if let Some(path) = on_load_recent { self.load_file(path); }

        let mut on_register_assoc = false;
        Dialogs::render_association_dialog(
            &ctx,
            &mut self.show_association_dialog,
            &mut self.dont_show_association_again,
            &mut self.settings,
            &self.theme,
            &mut on_register_assoc,
            &mut save_settings_action,
        );
        if on_register_assoc { self.register_file_association(); }

        let mut recent_file_to_open = None;
        Dialogs::render_recent_dialog(
            &ctx,
            &mut self.show_recent_dialog,
            &self.current_file_path,
            &mut self.settings,
            &self.theme,
            &mut recent_file_to_open,
            &mut save_settings_action,
        );
        if let Some(path) = recent_file_to_open { self.load_file(path); }

        if toggle_autoscroll_action {
            self.autoscroll.toggle(self.current_scroll_y, self.max_scroll_y, self.settings.auto_scroll.step_distance);
        }
        if save_settings_action {
            self.flush_settings_now();
            if crate::settings::AppSettings::last_save_error().is_none() {
                self.status_message = Some("РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅС‹".to_string());
            }
        }
        if register_assoc_action { self.register_file_association(); }
    }
}

use eframe::egui::{self, CursorIcon, PointerButton, Vec2};

pub fn parse_page_input(text: &str, page_count: usize) -> Option<usize> {
    text.trim()
        .parse::<usize>()
        .ok()
        .map(|p| p.saturating_sub(1).min(page_count.saturating_sub(1)))
}

pub fn zoom_in(zoom_percent: &mut f32, fit_mode: &mut String) {
    *fit_mode = "Custom".to_string();
    *zoom_percent = (*zoom_percent + 15.0).clamp(30.0, 400.0);
}

pub fn zoom_out(zoom_percent: &mut f32, fit_mode: &mut String) {
    *fit_mode = "Custom".to_string();
    *zoom_percent = (*zoom_percent - 15.0).clamp(30.0, 400.0);
}

pub fn zoom_reset(zoom_percent: &mut f32, fit_mode: &mut String, pan_offset: &mut Vec2) {
    *fit_mode = "Original".to_string();
    *zoom_percent = 100.0;
    *pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
}

pub fn calculate_paged_dimensions(
    orig_w: f32,
    orig_h: f32,
    avail_w: f32,
    avail_h: f32,
    fit_mode: &str,
    zoom_percent: f32,
) -> (f32, f32) {
    let zoom_factor = (zoom_percent / 100.0).clamp(0.3, 4.0);
    match fit_mode {
        "FitHeight" => {
            let h = (avail_h - 16.0).max(100.0);
            let aspect = orig_w / orig_h.max(1.0);
            (h * aspect, h)
        }
        "FitWidth" => {
            let w = (avail_w - 16.0).max(100.0);
            let aspect = orig_h / orig_w.max(1.0);
            (w, w * aspect)
        }
        "FitWindow" => {
            let scale_x = (avail_w - 16.0) / orig_w.max(1.0);
            let scale_y = (avail_h - 16.0) / orig_h.max(1.0);
            let s = scale_x.min(scale_y).max(0.05);
            (orig_w * s, orig_h * s)
        }
        _ => {
            let s = zoom_factor;
            (orig_w * s, orig_h * s)
        }
    }
}

pub fn handle_middle_mouse_scroll(
    ctx: &egui::Context,
    scroll_output_offset_y: f32,
    max_scroll_y: f32,
    pending_scroll_y: &mut Option<f32>,
) {
    let (m_down, m_delta) = ctx.input(|i| {
        (
            i.pointer.button_down(PointerButton::Middle),
            i.pointer.delta(),
        )
    });
    if m_down {
        ctx.set_cursor_icon(CursorIcon::Grabbing);
        if m_delta.y.abs() > 0.0 {
            let base_y = pending_scroll_y.unwrap_or(scroll_output_offset_y);
            *pending_scroll_y = Some((base_y - m_delta.y).clamp(0.0, max_scroll_y));
            ctx.request_repaint();
        }
    }
}

pub fn handle_paged_pan(
    ctx: &egui::Context,
    pan_offset: &mut Vec2,
    max_pan_x: f32,
    max_pan_y: f32,
) {
    let (m_down, m_delta, scroll_delta) = ctx.input(|i| {
        (
            i.pointer.button_down(PointerButton::Middle),
            i.pointer.delta(),
            i.smooth_scroll_delta,
        )
    });
    if m_down {
        ctx.set_cursor_icon(CursorIcon::Grabbing);
        *pan_offset += m_delta;
        pan_offset.x = pan_offset.x.clamp(-max_pan_x, max_pan_x);
        pan_offset.y = pan_offset.y.clamp(-max_pan_y, max_pan_y);
        ctx.request_repaint();
    } else if scroll_delta != Vec2::ZERO {
        *pan_offset += scroll_delta;
        pan_offset.x = pan_offset.x.clamp(-max_pan_x, max_pan_x);
        pan_offset.y = pan_offset.y.clamp(-max_pan_y, max_pan_y);
    }
}

use super::document_loader::format_document_error;
use super::theme::ReaderTheme;
use crate::document::{DecodedImage, Document, DocumentError, PageTextSpan};
use crate::settings::PdfSettings;
use eframe::egui::{self, Color32, CornerRadius, Pos2, Rect, RichText, ScrollArea, Vec2};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::mpsc::{self, Receiver, Sender};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::Instant;

struct LoadRequest {
    doc_generation: u64,
    doc: Arc<dyn Document>,
    page_idx: usize,
    scale: f32,
    scale_bucket: u32,
    ctx: egui::Context,
}

struct LoadResponse {
    doc_generation: u64,
    page_idx: usize,
    scale_bucket: u32,
    result: Result<DecodedImage, DocumentError>,
    spans: Option<Vec<PageTextSpan>>,
}

pub struct PageTextureEntry {
    pub texture: egui::TextureHandle,
    pub scale_bucket: u32,
    pub last_access: u64,
    pub byte_size: usize,
}

pub struct PdfViewState {
    pub current_page: usize,
    pub textures: HashMap<usize, PageTextureEntry>,
    pub pending_pages: HashMap<usize, u32>,
    pub failed_pages: HashMap<usize, String>,
    pub access_counter: u64,
    pub total_texture_bytes: usize,
    pub doc_generation: u64,
    active_generation: Arc<AtomicU64>,
    latest_requested_scales: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<usize, u32>>>,
    pub pan_offset: Vec2,
    pub is_middle_dragging: bool,
    pub page_input: String,
    pub pending_target_page: Option<usize>,
    pub page_text_spans: HashMap<usize, Vec<PageTextSpan>>,
    pub selection_start: Option<Pos2>,
    pub selection_current: Option<Pos2>,
    pub selection_dirty: bool,
    pub selected_text: String,
    pub copy_notification: Option<(String, Instant)>,
    req_tx: Sender<LoadRequest>,
    resp_rx: Receiver<LoadResponse>,
}

fn spawn_pdf_worker(
    active_generation: Arc<AtomicU64>,
    latest_scales: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<usize, u32>>>,
) -> (Sender<LoadRequest>, Receiver<LoadResponse>) {
    let (req_tx, req_rx) = mpsc::channel::<LoadRequest>();
    let (resp_tx, resp_rx) = mpsc::channel::<LoadResponse>();

    std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("pdf-page-worker".to_string())
        .spawn(move || {
            while let Ok(req) = req_rx.recv() {
                if req.doc_generation != active_generation.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
                    continue;
                }
                if let Ok(latest) = latest_scales.lock() {
                    if let Some(&latest_bucket) = latest.get(&req.page_idx) {
                        if latest_bucket != req.scale_bucket {
                            continue;
                        }
                    }
                }

                let doc = req.doc;
                let page_idx = req.page_idx;
                let scale = req.scale;
                let load_res = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
                    if let Some(page_content) = doc.page_content() {
                        let img = page_content.load_page_image_scaled(page_idx, scale)?;
                        let spans = page_content.extract_page_text_spans(page_idx);
                        Ok((img, spans))
                    } else {
                        Err(DocumentError::Unsupported(
                            "Р”РѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚ РЅРµ РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РёРІР°РµС‚ РїРѕСЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС‡РЅС‹Р№ СЂРµР¶РёРј".to_string(),
                        ))
                    }
                }));

                if req.doc_generation != active_generation.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
                    continue;
                }

                let (result, spans) = match load_res {
                    Ok(Ok((img, spans))) => (Ok(img), spans),
                    Ok(Err(e)) => (Err(e), None),
                    Err(_) => (
                        Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(
                            "РђРІР°СЂРёР№РЅС‹Р№ СЃР±РѕР№ РїСЂРё РґРµРєРѕРґРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРё СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹".to_string(),
                        )),
                        None,
                    ),
                };

                let _ = resp_tx.send(LoadResponse {
                    doc_generation: req.doc_generation,
                    page_idx,
                    scale_bucket: req.scale_bucket,
                    result,
                    spans,
                });
                req.ctx.request_repaint();
            }
        })
        .ok();

    (req_tx, resp_rx)
}

impl Default for PdfViewState {
    fn default() -> Self {
        let active_generation = Arc::new(AtomicU64::new(0));
        let latest_requested_scales = Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));
        let (req_tx, resp_rx) =
            spawn_pdf_worker(active_generation.clone(), latest_requested_scales.clone());
        Self {
            current_page: 0,
            textures: HashMap::new(),
            pending_pages: HashMap::new(),
            failed_pages: HashMap::new(),
            access_counter: 0,
            total_texture_bytes: 0,
            doc_generation: 0,
            active_generation,
            latest_requested_scales,
            pan_offset: Vec2::ZERO,
            is_middle_dragging: false,
            page_input: String::new(),
            pending_target_page: None,
            page_text_spans: HashMap::new(),
            selection_start: None,
            selection_current: None,
            selection_dirty: false,
            selected_text: String::new(),
            copy_notification: None,
            req_tx,
            resp_rx,
        }
    }
}

impl PdfViewState {
    pub fn clear_cache(&mut self) {
        self.textures.clear();
        self.pending_pages.clear();
        self.failed_pages.clear();
        self.total_texture_bytes = 0;
        self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
        self.pending_target_page = None;
        self.page_text_spans.clear();
        self.selection_start = None;
        self.selection_current = None;
        self.selection_dirty = false;
        self.selected_text.clear();
        self.doc_generation = self.doc_generation.wrapping_add(1);
        self.active_generation
            .store(self.doc_generation, Ordering::Release);
        if let Ok(mut map) = self.latest_requested_scales.lock() {
            map.clear();
        }
    }

    pub fn page_count(&self, document: &Option<Arc<dyn Document>>) -> usize {
        document
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|d| d.page_content())
            .map(|c| c.page_count())
            .unwrap_or(0)
    }

    pub fn go_to_page(&mut self, page: usize) {
        self.current_page = page;
        self.pending_target_page = Some(page);
        self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
        self.page_input = (page + 1).to_string();
        self.selection_start = None;
        self.selection_current = None;
        self.selection_dirty = false;
        self.selected_text.clear();
    }

    pub fn next_page(&mut self, total_pages: usize, settings: &PdfSettings) {
        if total_pages == 0 {
            return;
        }
        let step = if settings.layout == "Double" { 2 } else { 1 };
        let next = (self.current_page + step).min(total_pages.saturating_sub(1));
        self.go_to_page(next);
    }

    pub fn prev_page(&mut self, settings: &PdfSettings) {
        let step = if settings.layout == "Double" { 2 } else { 1 };
        let prev = self.current_page.saturating_sub(step);
        self.go_to_page(prev);
    }

    fn poll_responses(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context, status_message: &mut Option<String>) {
        loop {
            match self.resp_rx.try_recv() {
                Ok(resp) => {
                    if resp.doc_generation != self.doc_generation {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if let Some(&pending_b) = self.pending_pages.get(&resp.page_idx) {
                        if pending_b == resp.scale_bucket {
                            self.pending_pages.remove(&resp.page_idx);
                        }
                    }
                    if let Some(spans) = resp.spans {
                        self.page_text_spans.insert(resp.page_idx, spans);
                    }
                    match resp.result {
                        Ok(img) => {
                            self.access_counter += 1;
                            let counter = self.access_counter;
                            let w = img.width as usize;
                            let h = img.height as usize;
                            let byte_size = w * h * 4;
                            let color_image =
                                egui::ColorImage::from_rgba_unmultiplied([w, h], &img.rgba);
                            let texture = ctx.load_texture(
                                format!(
                                    "pdf_tex_{}_{}_{}",
                                    self.doc_generation, resp.page_idx, resp.scale_bucket
                                ),
                                color_image,
                                egui::TextureOptions::LINEAR,
                            );

                            if let Some(old) = self.textures.remove(&resp.page_idx) {
                                self.total_texture_bytes =
                                    self.total_texture_bytes.saturating_sub(old.byte_size);
                            }

                            self.total_texture_bytes += byte_size;
                            self.textures.insert(
                                resp.page_idx,
                                PageTextureEntry {
                                    texture,
                                    scale_bucket: resp.scale_bucket,
                                    last_access: counter,
                                    byte_size,
                                },
                            );

                            const MAX_CACHED_PAGES: usize = 16;
                            const MAX_TEXTURE_BYTES: usize = 180 * 1024 * 1024;
                            while self.textures.len() > MAX_CACHED_PAGES
                                || self.total_texture_bytes > MAX_TEXTURE_BYTES
                            {
                                let cur = self.current_page;
                                let oldest_key = self
                                    .textures
                                    .iter()
                                    .filter(|(p, _)| {
                                        let p = **p;
                                        p != cur && p != cur + 1 && (cur == 0 || p != cur - 1)
                                    })
                                    .min_by_key(|(_, entry)| entry.last_access)
                                    .map(|(p, _)| *p)
                                    .or_else(|| {
                                        self.textures
                                            .iter()
                                            .min_by_key(|(_, entry)| entry.last_access)
                                            .map(|(p, _)| *p)
                                    });

                                if let Some(key) = oldest_key {
                                    if let Some(entry) = self.textures.remove(&key) {
                                        self.total_texture_bytes = self
                                            .total_texture_bytes
                                            .saturating_sub(entry.byte_size);
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        Err(e) => {
                            let msg = format_document_error(&e);
                            *status_message = Some(format!(
                                "РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}: {msg}",
                                resp.page_idx + 1
                            ));
                            self.failed_pages.insert(resp.page_idx, msg);
                        }
                    }
                }
                Err(mpsc::TryRecvError::Empty) => break,
                Err(mpsc::TryRecvError::Disconnected) => {
                    for (page_idx, _) in self.pending_pages.drain() {
                        let msg = "РџРѕС‚РѕРє РґРµРєРѕРґРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ Р°РІР°СЂРёР№РЅРѕ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€РёР»СЃСЏ".to_string();
                        self.failed_pages.insert(page_idx, msg.clone());
                        *status_message = Some(format!(
                            "РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}: {msg}",
                            page_idx + 1
                        ));
                    }
                    let (tx, rx) = spawn_pdf_worker(
                        self.active_generation.clone(),
                        self.latest_requested_scales.clone(),
                    );
                    self.req_tx = tx;
                    self.resp_rx = rx;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn compute_target_scale(&self, settings: &PdfSettings) -> f32 {
        let zoom_ratio = settings.zoom_percent / 100.0;
        let base = match settings.fit_mode.as_str() {
            "Custom" => (2.2 * zoom_ratio).clamp(1.5, 4.0),
            "FitWidth" => 2.5,
            "Original" => 2.0,
            _ => 2.25,
        };
        (base * 4.0).round() / 4.0
    }

    pub fn request_page_texture(
        &mut self,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        document: &Option<Arc<dyn Document>>,
        page_idx: usize,
        scale: f32,
    ) -> Option<egui::TextureHandle> {
        self.access_counter += 1;
        let counter = self.access_counter;
        let scale_bucket = (scale * 4.0).round() as u32;

        let mut existing_tex = None;
        let mut need_re_rasterize = true;

        if let Some(entry) = self.textures.get_mut(&page_idx) {
            entry.last_access = counter;
            existing_tex = Some(entry.texture.clone());
            if entry.scale_bucket == scale_bucket {
                need_re_rasterize = false;
            }
        }

        if !need_re_rasterize {
            return existing_tex;
        }

        if let Some(&pending_bucket) = self.pending_pages.get(&page_idx) {
            if pending_bucket == scale_bucket {
                return existing_tex;
            }
        }

        if self.failed_pages.contains_key(&page_idx) {
            return existing_tex;
        }

        let doc = document.as_ref()?.clone();
        self.pending_pages.insert(page_idx, scale_bucket);
        if let Ok(mut map) = self.latest_requested_scales.lock() {
            map.insert(page_idx, scale_bucket);
        }

        let _ = self.req_tx.send(LoadRequest {
            doc_generation: self.doc_generation,
            doc,
            page_idx,
            scale,
            scale_bucket,
            ctx: ctx.clone(),
        });

        existing_tex
    }

    pub fn render_toolbar(
        &mut self,
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        page_count: usize,
        settings: &mut PdfSettings,
        theme: &ReaderTheme,
        status_message: &mut Option<String>,
        format_label: &str,
    ) {
        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            ui.spacing_mut().item_spacing.x = 6.0;

            if ui.add_enabled(self.current_page > 0, egui::Button::new("в—Ђ")).on_hover_text("РџСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰Р°СЏ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° (в†ђ, в†‘, PageUp)").clicked() {
                let step = if settings.layout == "Double" { 2 } else { 1 };
                self.go_to_page(self.current_page.saturating_sub(step));
            }

            if self.page_input.is_empty() {
                self.page_input = (self.current_page + 1).to_string();
            }
            let cur_display = self.current_page + 1;
            ui.label(RichText::new("РЎС‚СЂ.").size(13.0));
            let text_edit = ui.add(egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut self.page_input).desired_width(38.0).font(egui::TextStyle::Body));
            if text_edit.lost_focus() && ui.input(|i| i.key_pressed(egui::Key::Enter)) {
                if let Some(target) = super::paged_common::parse_page_input(&self.page_input, page_count) {
                    self.go_to_page(target);
                }
                self.page_input = (self.current_page + 1).to_string();
            } else if !text_edit.has_focus() {
                self.page_input = cur_display.to_string();
            }

            ui.label(RichText::new(format!("РёР· {page_count}")).size(13.0));

            if ui.add_enabled(self.current_page + 1 < page_count, egui::Button::new("в–¶")).on_hover_text("РЎР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰Р°СЏ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° (в†’, в†“, PageDown, РџСЂРѕР±РµР»)").clicked() {
                let step = if settings.layout == "Double" { 2 } else { 1 };
                self.go_to_page((self.current_page + step).min(page_count.saturating_sub(1)));
            }

            ui.separator();

            if ui.button("в€’").on_hover_text("РЈРјРµРЅСЊС€РёС‚СЊ РјР°СЃС€С‚Р°Р± (Ctrl -)").clicked() {
                super::paged_common::zoom_out(&mut settings.zoom_percent, &mut settings.fit_mode);
            }

            let zoom_lbl = format!("{:.0}%", settings.zoom_percent);
            if ui.button(RichText::new(zoom_lbl).strong()).on_hover_text("РЎР±СЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ РјР°СЃС€С‚Р°Р± РЅР° 100% (Ctrl 0)").clicked() {
                super::paged_common::zoom_reset(&mut settings.zoom_percent, &mut settings.fit_mode, &mut self.pan_offset);
            }

            if ui.button("+").on_hover_text("РЈРІРµР»РёС‡РёС‚СЊ РјР°СЃС€С‚Р°Р± (Ctrl +)").clicked() {
                super::paged_common::zoom_in(&mut settings.zoom_percent, &mut settings.fit_mode);
            }

            if ui.selectable_label(settings.fit_mode == "FitWidth", "РџРѕ С€РёСЂРёРЅРµ").on_hover_text("РњР°СЃС€С‚Р°Р±РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РїРѕ С€РёСЂРёРЅРµ РѕРєРЅР°").clicked() {
                settings.fit_mode = "FitWidth".to_string();
                self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
            }

            if ui.selectable_label(settings.fit_mode == "FitWindow", "Р¦РµР»Р°СЏ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р°").on_hover_text("РЈРјРµСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Сѓ С†РµР»РёРєРѕРј РІ РѕРєРЅРѕ").clicked() {
                settings.fit_mode = "FitWindow".to_string();
                self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
            }

            ui.separator();

            if ui.button("вџі РџРѕРІРµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ").on_hover_text("РџРѕРІРµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ РЅР° 90В° РїРѕ С‡Р°СЃРѕРІРѕР№ СЃС‚СЂРµР»РєРµ").clicked() {
                settings.rotation = (settings.rotation + 90) % 360;
                self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
            }

            ui.separator();

            if ui.selectable_label(settings.layout == "Single", "1 СЃС‚СЂ.").on_hover_text("РћРґРёРЅРѕС‡РЅР°СЏ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р°").clicked() {
                settings.layout = "Single".to_string();
                self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
            }

            if ui.selectable_label(settings.layout == "Double", "2 СЃС‚СЂ.").on_hover_text("Р”РІРµ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ (СЂР°Р·РІРѕСЂРѕС‚)").clicked() {
                settings.layout = "Double".to_string();
                self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
            }

            if ui.selectable_label(settings.layout == "Continuous", "РЎРІРёС‚РѕРє").on_hover_text("РќРµРїСЂРµСЂС‹РІРЅС‹Р№ РІРµСЂС‚РёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЃРІРёС‚РѕРє СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†").clicked() {
                settings.layout = "Continuous".to_string();
                self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
            }

            ui.separator();

            if !self.selected_text.is_empty() {
                if ui.button(RichText::new("рџ“‹ РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ").strong().color(theme.accent_color())).on_hover_text("РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РІС‹РґРµР»РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚ (Ctrl+C)").clicked() {
                    ui.ctx().copy_text(self.selected_text.clone());
                    let preview = truncate_str(&self.selected_text, 35);
                    *status_message = Some(format!("РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРѕ: \"{preview}\""));
                    self.copy_notification = Some((format!("РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРѕ: \"{preview}\""), Instant::now()));
                }
                if ui.small_button("вњ•").on_hover_text("РЎРЅСЏС‚СЊ РІС‹РґРµР»РµРЅРёРµ").clicked() {
                    self.selection_start = None;
                    self.selection_current = None;
                    self.selection_dirty = false;
                    self.selected_text.clear();
                }
            } else {
                ui.label(RichText::new("Р’С‹РґРµР»РµРЅРёРµ: Р·Р°Р¶РјРёС‚Рµ Р›РљРњ РЅР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Рµ").size(11.5).italics().color(theme.text_color().gamma_multiply(0.65)));
            }

            ui.with_layout(egui::Layout::right_to_left(egui::Align::Center), |ui| {
                ui.label(RichText::new(format!("рџ“„ {format_label}")).size(11.0).strong().color(theme.accent_color()));
            });
        });
    }

    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    fn render_page_with_selection<'a>(
        &mut self,
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        page_idx: usize,
        tex: &egui::TextureHandle,
        size: Vec2,
        rotation: i32,
        selected_spans_collector: &mut Vec<&'a str>,
    ) -> egui::Response {
        let (rect, response) = ui.allocate_exact_size(size, egui::Sense::click_and_drag());
        let painter = ui.painter();
        let rot_deg = rotation.rem_euclid(360);

        if rot_deg == 0 {
            painter.image(
                tex.id(),
                rect,
                Rect::from_min_max(Pos2::ZERO, Pos2::new(1.0, 1.0)),
                Color32::WHITE,
            );
        } else {
            let uvs = match rot_deg {
                90 => [Pos2::new(0.0, 1.0), Pos2::new(0.0, 0.0), Pos2::new(1.0, 0.0), Pos2::new(1.0, 1.0)],
                180 => [Pos2::new(1.0, 1.0), Pos2::new(0.0, 1.0), Pos2::new(0.0, 0.0), Pos2::new(1.0, 0.0)],
                270 => [Pos2::new(1.0, 0.0), Pos2::new(1.0, 1.0), Pos2::new(0.0, 1.0), Pos2::new(0.0, 0.0)],
                _ => [Pos2::new(0.0, 0.0), Pos2::new(1.0, 0.0), Pos2::new(1.0, 1.0), Pos2::new(0.0, 1.0)],
            };
            let corners = [
                rect.left_top(),
                rect.right_top(),
                rect.right_bottom(),
                rect.left_bottom(),
            ];
            let mut mesh = egui::Mesh::with_texture(tex.id());
            mesh.add_triangle(0, 1, 2);
            mesh.add_triangle(0, 2, 3);
            for i in 0..4 {
                mesh.vertices.push(egui::epaint::Vertex {
                    pos: corners[i],
                    uv: uvs[i],
                    color: Color32::WHITE,
                });
            }
            painter.add(mesh);
        }

        if let Some(hover_pos) = response.hover_pos()
            && let Some(spans) = self.page_text_spans.get(&page_idx)
        {
            for span in spans {
                let span_rect = transform_span_bbox(span.bbox, rotation, rect);
                if span_rect.contains(hover_pos) {
                    ctx.set_cursor_icon(egui::CursorIcon::Text);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        if response.drag_started_by(egui::PointerButton::Primary) {
            let pos = response.interact_pointer_pos();
            self.selection_start = pos;
            self.selection_current = pos;
            self.selection_dirty = true;
        } else if response.dragged_by(egui::PointerButton::Primary) {
            let pos = response.interact_pointer_pos();
            if self.selection_current != pos {
                self.selection_current = pos;
                self.selection_dirty = true;
            }
        } else if response.clicked_by(egui::PointerButton::Primary) {
            self.selection_start = None;
            self.selection_current = None;
            self.selection_dirty = false;
            self.selected_text.clear();
        }

        if let (Some(start), Some(curr)) = (self.selection_start, self.selection_current) {
            let sel_rect = Rect::from_two_pos(start, curr);
            if (sel_rect.width() > 3.0 || sel_rect.height() > 3.0)
                && rect.intersects(sel_rect)
                && let Some(spans) = self.page_text_spans.get(&page_idx)
            {
                for span in spans {
                    let span_rect = transform_span_bbox(span.bbox, rotation, rect);
                    if span_rect.intersects(sel_rect) {
                        painter.rect_filled(
                            span_rect,
                            CornerRadius::same(2),
                            Color32::from_rgba_unmultiplied(33, 150, 243, 90),
                        );
                        if self.selection_dirty {
                            unsafe {
                                let span_ptr: *const str = span.text.as_str();
                                selected_spans_collector.push(&*span_ptr);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        response
    }

    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn render(
        &mut self,
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        document: &Option<Arc<dyn Document>>,
        settings: &mut PdfSettings,
        theme: &ReaderTheme,
        pending_scroll_y: &mut Option<f32>,
        current_scroll_y: &mut f32,
        viewport_height: &mut f32,
        max_scroll_y: &mut f32,
        status_message: &mut Option<String>,
    ) {
        self.poll_responses(ctx, status_message);

        let format_label = document.as_ref().map(|d| d.format_name()).unwrap_or("Р”РѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚");
        let page_count = self.page_count(document);
        if page_count == 0 {
            ui.centered_and_justified(|ui| {
                ui.label(
                    RichText::new("Р’ СЌС‚РѕРј РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Рµ РЅРµС‚ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†").color(theme.text_color()),
                );
            });
            return;
        }

        self.current_page = self.current_page.min(page_count.saturating_sub(1));

        ui.add_space(2.0);
        self.render_toolbar(ui, page_count, settings, theme, status_message, format_label);
        ui.add_space(4.0);
        ui.separator();

        let render_scale = self.compute_target_scale(settings);
        let is_continuous = settings.layout == "Continuous";

        let avail_w = ui.available_width().max(200.0);
        let zoom_factor = settings.zoom_percent / 100.0;
        let target_w = match settings.fit_mode.as_str() {
            "FitWidth" => (avail_w - 32.0).clamp(200.0, 4000.0),
            "Custom" => (avail_w * 0.85 * zoom_factor).clamp(200.0, 6000.0),
            _ => (avail_w * 0.85).clamp(320.0, 1600.0),
        };
        let est_row_h = target_w * 1.414;
        let row_h_total = est_row_h + 12.0;

        let just_jumped = self.pending_target_page.is_some();
        if let Some(target_p) = self.pending_target_page.take() {
            self.current_page = target_p.min(page_count.saturating_sub(1));
            self.pan_offset = Vec2::ZERO;
            if is_continuous {
                let target_y = self.current_page as f32 * row_h_total;
                *pending_scroll_y = Some(target_y);
            }
        }

        let mut collected_selected_spans = Vec::new();

        if is_continuous {
            let mut scroll_area = ScrollArea::vertical()
                .id_salt(("pdf_continuous_scroll", self.doc_generation))
                .auto_shrink([false, false]);

            if let Some(offset) = pending_scroll_y.take() {
                scroll_area = scroll_area.vertical_scroll_offset(offset);
            }

            let scroll_output =
                scroll_area.show_rows(ui, est_row_h, page_count, |ui, row_range| {
                    ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                        if !just_jumped && let Some(first_visible) = row_range.clone().next() {
                            self.current_page = first_visible.min(page_count.saturating_sub(1));
                        }

                        for p in row_range {
                            if let Some(tex) =
                                self.request_page_texture(ctx, document, p, render_scale)
                            {
                                let tex_size = tex.size_vec2();
                                let mut w = target_w;
                                let mut h = target_w * (tex_size.y / tex_size.x);
                                if settings.rotation == 90 || settings.rotation == 270 {
                                    std::mem::swap(&mut w, &mut h);
                                }
                                self.render_page_with_selection(
                                    ui,
                                    ctx,
                                    p,
                                    &tex,
                                    Vec2::new(w, h),
                                    settings.rotation,
                                    &mut collected_selected_spans,
                                );
                                ui.add_space(12.0);
                            } else if let Some(err) = self.failed_pages.get(&p) {
                                ui.label(
                                    RichText::new(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}: {err}", p + 1))
                                        .color(theme.accent_color()),
                                );
                            } else {
                                ui.add_space(28.0);
                                ui.label(
                                    RichText::new(format!("вЏі Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}...", p + 1))
                                        .color(theme.text_color()),
                                );
                                ui.add_space(28.0);
                            }
                        }
                    });
                });

            *current_scroll_y = scroll_output.state.offset.y;
            *viewport_height = scroll_output.inner_rect.height();
            *max_scroll_y = (scroll_output.content_size.y - *viewport_height).max(0.0);

            super::paged_common::handle_middle_mouse_scroll(
                ctx,
                scroll_output.state.offset.y,
                *max_scroll_y,
                pending_scroll_y,
            );
        } else {
            let avail_size = ui.available_size();
            let is_double = settings.layout == "Double" && self.current_page + 1 < page_count;
            let pages_to_show: Vec<usize> = if is_double {
                vec![self.current_page, self.current_page + 1]
            } else {
                vec![self.current_page]
            };

            let max_pan_x = (avail_size.x * 2.0).max(400.0);
            let max_pan_y = (avail_size.y * 2.0).max(400.0);
            super::paged_common::handle_paged_pan(ctx, &mut self.pan_offset, max_pan_x, max_pan_y);

            let num_pages = pages_to_show.len() as f32;
            let target_w_per_page = (avail_size.x - 24.0) / num_pages;

            let (container_rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(avail_size, egui::Sense::hover());
            let panned_rect = container_rect.translate(self.pan_offset);

            let mut child_ui = ui.new_child(
                egui::UiBuilder::new()
                    .max_rect(panned_rect)
                    .layout(egui::Layout::left_to_right(egui::Align::Center)),
            );
            child_ui.set_clip_rect(container_rect);

            child_ui.horizontal_centered(|ui| {
                ui.add_space(8.0);
                for &p in &pages_to_show {
                    if let Some(tex) = self.request_page_texture(ctx, document, p, render_scale) {
                        let tex_size = tex.size_vec2();
                        let (mut display_w, mut display_h) = match settings.fit_mode.as_str() {
                            "FitHeight" => {
                                let h = (avail_size.y - 20.0).max(100.0);
                                let w = h * (tex_size.x / tex_size.y);
                                (w, h)
                            }
                            "FitWidth" => {
                                let w = (target_w_per_page - 12.0).max(100.0);
                                let h = w * (tex_size.y / tex_size.x);
                                (w, h)
                            }
                            "Original" => (tex_size.x / render_scale, tex_size.y / render_scale),
                            "Custom" => {
                                let w = (target_w_per_page - 12.0) * zoom_factor;
                                let h = w * (tex_size.y / tex_size.x);
                                (w, h)
                            }
                            _ => {
                                let max_w = (target_w_per_page - 12.0).max(100.0);
                                let max_h = (avail_size.y - 20.0).max(100.0);
                                let scale = (max_w / tex_size.x).min(max_h / tex_size.y);
                                (tex_size.x * scale, tex_size.y * scale)
                            }
                        };

                        if settings.rotation == 90 || settings.rotation == 270 {
                            std::mem::swap(&mut display_w, &mut display_h);
                        }

                        self.render_page_with_selection(
                            ui,
                            ctx,
                            p,
                            &tex,
                            Vec2::new(display_w, display_h),
                            settings.rotation,
                            &mut collected_selected_spans,
                        );
                    } else if let Some(err) = self.failed_pages.get(&p) {
                        ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                            ui.add_space(avail_size.y * 0.4);
                            ui.label(
                                RichText::new(format!("вќЊ РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}: {err}", p + 1))
                                    .size(16.0)
                                    .color(theme.accent_color()),
                            );
                        });
                    } else {
                        ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                            ui.add_space(avail_size.y * 0.45);
                            ui.label(
                                RichText::new(format!("вЏі Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}...", p + 1))
                                    .size(18.0)
                                    .color(theme.text_color()),
                            );
                        });
                    }
                    ui.add_space(12.0);
                }
            });

            if self.current_page + 1 < page_count {
                let _ =
                    self.request_page_texture(ctx, document, self.current_page + 1, render_scale);
            }
            if self.current_page > 0 {
                let _ =
                    self.request_page_texture(ctx, document, self.current_page - 1, render_scale);
            }
        }

        if self.selection_dirty {
            if !collected_selected_spans.is_empty() {
                self.selected_text = collected_selected_spans.join("\n");
            } else {
                self.selected_text.clear();
            }
            self.selection_dirty = false;
        }

        if !self.selected_text.is_empty() {
            let ctrl_c = ctx.input(|i| {
                (i.modifiers.command || i.modifiers.ctrl) && i.key_pressed(egui::Key::C)
            });
            if ctrl_c {
                ctx.copy_text(self.selected_text.clone());
                let preview = truncate_str(&self.selected_text, 35);
                *status_message = Some(format!("РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРѕ РІ Р±СѓС„РµСЂ: \"{preview}\""));
                self.copy_notification =
                    Some((format!("РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРѕ: \"{preview}\""), Instant::now()));
            }
        }

        if !self.selected_text.is_empty()
            && let Some(curr) = self.selection_current
        {
            let pill_pos = curr + Vec2::new(12.0, 12.0);
            egui::Area::new(egui::Id::new("pdf_selection_pill"))
                .fixed_pos(pill_pos)
                .order(egui::Order::Tooltip)
                .show(ctx, |ui| {
                    egui::Frame::popup(ui.style()).show(ui, |ui| {
                        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                            if ui
                                .button(RichText::new("рџ“‹ РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ (Ctrl+C)").size(12.0))
                                .clicked()
                            {
                                ctx.copy_text(self.selected_text.clone());
                                let preview = truncate_str(&self.selected_text, 35);
                                *status_message = Some(format!("РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРѕ: \"{preview}\""));
                                self.copy_notification =
                                    Some((format!("РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРѕ: \"{preview}\""), Instant::now()));
                            }
                            if ui.small_button("вњ•").clicked() {
                                self.selection_start = None;
                                self.selection_current = None;
                                self.selection_dirty = false;
                                self.selected_text.clear();
                            }
                        });
                    });
                });
        }

        if let Some((msg, time)) = &self.copy_notification {
            if time.elapsed().as_secs_f32() < 2.0 {
                egui::Area::new(egui::Id::new("pdf_copy_toast"))
                    .anchor(egui::Align2::CENTER_BOTTOM, Vec2::new(0.0, -45.0))
                    .order(egui::Order::Tooltip)
                    .show(ctx, |ui| {
                        egui::Frame::popup(ui.style()).show(ui, |ui| {
                            ui.label(
                                RichText::new(format!("вњ“ {msg}"))
                                    .strong()
                                    .color(theme.accent_color()),
                            );
                        });
                    });
            } else {
                self.copy_notification = None;
            }
        }
    }
}

fn truncate_str(s: &str, max_chars: usize) -> String {
    let trimmed = s.trim();
    if trimmed.chars().count() <= max_chars {
        trimmed.to_string()
    } else {
        let prefix: String = trimmed.chars().take(max_chars).collect();
        format!("{prefix}...")
    }
}

pub fn transform_span_bbox(bbox: [f32; 4], rotation: i32, rect: Rect) -> Rect {
    let (rx, ry, rw, rh) = (rect.min.x, rect.min.y, rect.width(), rect.height());
    match rotation.rem_euclid(360) {
        90 => {
            let (x0, x1) = (rect.max.x - bbox[3] * rw, rect.max.x - bbox[1] * rw);
            let (y0, y1) = (ry + bbox[0] * rh, ry + bbox[2] * rh);
            Rect::from_min_max(Pos2::new(x0.min(x1), y0.min(y1)), Pos2::new(x0.max(x1), y0.max(y1)))
        }
        180 => {
            let (x0, x1) = (rect.max.x - bbox[2] * rw, rect.max.x - bbox[0] * rw);
            let (y0, y1) = (rect.max.y - bbox[3] * rh, rect.max.y - bbox[1] * rh);
            Rect::from_min_max(Pos2::new(x0.min(x1), y0.min(y1)), Pos2::new(x0.max(x1), y0.max(y1)))
        }
        270 => {
            let (x0, x1) = (rx + bbox[1] * rw, rx + bbox[3] * rw);
            let (y0, y1) = (rect.max.y - bbox[2] * rh, rect.max.y - bbox[0] * rh);
            Rect::from_min_max(Pos2::new(x0.min(x1), y0.min(y1)), Pos2::new(x0.max(x1), y0.max(y1)))
        }
        _ => {
            let (x0, x1) = (rx + bbox[0] * rw, rx + bbox[2] * rw);
            let (y0, y1) = (ry + bbox[1] * rh, ry + bbox[3] * rh);
            Rect::from_min_max(Pos2::new(x0.min(x1), y0.min(y1)), Pos2::new(x0.max(x1), y0.max(y1)))
        }
    }
}

impl SettingsView {
    pub fn render(
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        show_settings: &mut bool,
        settings_tab: &mut SettingsTab,
        settings: &mut AppSettings,
        font_size: &mut f32,

    ) {

                    RichText::new(format!("вљ  РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёСЏ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРµРє: {err}"))
                        .color(Color32::RED)

                    (SettingsTab::Fonts, "рџ”¤ РЁСЂРёС„С‚С‹ Рё С‚РµРєСЃС‚"),
