            RichText::new("РџРѕРєР° РѕРєРЅРѕ QuietReader Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅРѕ, РґРёСЃРїР»РµР№ РЅРµ РіР°СЃРЅРµС‚ Р°РІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРё.")
                .size(12.0)
                .color(text_color.gamma_multiply(0.7)),
        );
    }

    fn render_autoscroll_tab(
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        settings: &mut AppSettings,
        is_autoscrolling: &mut bool,
        on_toggle_autoscroll: &mut bool,
        theme: &ReaderTheme,
        on_save_settings: &mut bool,
    ) {
        let text_color = theme.text_color();

        ui.label(
            RichText::new("РђРІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєР°СЏ РїСЂРѕРјРѕС‚РєР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†")
                .size(17.0)
                .strong()
                .color(text_color),
        );
        ui.label(
            RichText::new("Р§С‚РµРЅРёРµ Р±РµР· РїРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРЅС‹С… РєР°СЃР°РЅРёР№ РєРѕР»РµСЃРёРєР° РјС‹С€Рё:")
                .size(13.0)
                .color(text_color.gamma_multiply(0.75)),
        );
        ui.add_space(8.0);

        let scroll_btn_text = if *is_autoscrolling {
            "вЏё РћСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ Р°РІС‚РѕРїСЂРѕРєСЂСѓС‚РєСѓ (РєР»Р°РІРёС€Р° A)"
        } else {
            "в–¶ Р—Р°РїСѓСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ Р°РІС‚РѕРїСЂРѕРєСЂСѓС‚РєСѓ (РєР»Р°РІРёС€Р° A)"
        };
        if ui
            .button(RichText::new(scroll_btn_text).size(14.0).strong())
            .clicked()
        {
            *on_toggle_autoscroll = true;
        }

        ui.add_space(10.0);
        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            ui.label(RichText::new("РўРёРї РґРІРёР¶РµРЅРёСЏ:").strong().color(text_color));
            if ui
                .radio_value(
                    &mut settings.auto_scroll.pause_mode,
                    true,
                    "РЎ РїР°СѓР·Р°РјРё (С€Р°Рі + РѕСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєР°)",
                )
                .clicked()
            {
                *on_save_settings = true;
            }
            if ui
                .radio_value(
                    &mut settings.auto_scroll.pause_mode,
                    false,
                    "РќРµРїСЂРµСЂС‹РІРЅС‹Р№ (РїР»Р°РІРЅС‹Р№ СЃРєСЂРѕР»Р»)",
                )
                .clicked()
            {
                *on_save_settings = true;
            }
        });

        ui.add_space(8.0);
        if settings.auto_scroll.pause_mode {
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.label("Р’СЂРµРјСЏ РїР°СѓР·С‹ РґР»СЏ С‡С‚РµРЅРёСЏ:");
                let resp = ui.add(
                    egui::Slider::new(&mut settings.auto_scroll.pause_duration, 0.5..=15.0)
                        .suffix(" СЃРµРє"),
                );
                if resp.drag_stopped() || resp.lost_focus() {
                    *on_save_settings = true;
                }
            });
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.label("РЁР°Рі СЃРґРІРёРіР°:");
                let resp = ui.add(
                    egui::Slider::new(&mut settings.auto_scroll.step_distance, 30.0..=350.0)
                        .suffix(" px"),
                );
                if resp.drag_stopped() || resp.lost_focus() {
                    *on_save_settings = true;
                }
            });
        } else {
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.label("РЎРєРѕСЂРѕСЃС‚СЊ РїСЂРѕРєСЂСѓС‚РєРё:");
                let resp = ui.add(
                    egui::Slider::new(&mut settings.auto_scroll.continuous_speed, 10.0..=250.0)
                        .suffix(" px/СЃРµРє"),
                );
                if resp.drag_stopped() || resp.lost_focus() {
                    *on_save_settings = true;
                }
            });
        }
    }

    fn render_comics_tab(
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        settings: &mut AppSettings,
        theme: &ReaderTheme,
        on_save_settings: &mut bool,
    ) {
        let text_color = theme.text_color();
        ui.label(
            RichText::new("РџР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂС‹ РєРѕРјРёРєСЃРѕРІ, РјР°РЅРіРё Рё PDF")
                .size(17.0)
                .strong()
                .color(text_color),
        );
        ui.add_space(6.0);

        let modes = [
            (
                "Auto",
                "РђРІС‚Рѕ (СЏРїРѕРЅСЃРєРёР№ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє R2L РґР»СЏ РјР°РЅРіРё, РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Р№ РґР»СЏ РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹С…)",
                "РћРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏРµС‚ РѕСЂРёРµРЅС‚Р°С†РёСЋ РїРѕ РјРµС‚Р°РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рј Р°СЂС…РёРІР°",
            ),
            (
                "Manga",
                "РўРѕР»СЊРєРѕ РјР°РЅРіР° (СЃРїСЂР°РІР°-РЅР°Р»РµРІРѕ)",
                "РџСЂРёРЅСѓРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЏРїРѕРЅСЃРєРёР№ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС† R2L",
            ),
            (
                "Comic",
                "РћР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Р№ РєРѕРјРёРєСЃ (СЃР»РµРІР°-РЅР°РїСЂР°РІРѕ)",
                "Р•РІСЂРѕРїРµР№СЃРєРёР№ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС† L2R",
            ),
            (
                "Webtoon",
                "Р’РµР±С‚СѓРЅ (РІРµСЂС‚РёРєР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ Р»РµРЅС‚Р°)",
                "РќРµРїСЂРµСЂС‹РІРЅР°СЏ РІРµСЂС‚РёРєР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ РїСЂРѕРєСЂСѓС‚РєР°",
            ),
        ];

        for (mode_key, title, desc) in modes {
            let is_sel = settings.comic.reading_mode == mode_key;
            let fill = if is_sel {
                theme.panel_bg_color()
            } else {
                theme.bg_color()
            };
            let stroke = if is_sel {
                egui::Stroke::new(1.5, theme.accent_color())
            } else {
                egui::Stroke::new(1.0, Color32::from_gray(120).gamma_multiply(0.3))
            };
            egui::Frame::new()
                .fill(fill)
                .stroke(stroke)
                .corner_radius(6.0)
                .inner_margin(egui::Margin::symmetric(12, 8))
                .show(ui, |ui| {
                    if ui
                        .radio(is_sel, RichText::new(title).strong().color(text_color))
                        .clicked()
                    {
                        settings.comic.reading_mode = mode_key.to_string();
                        *on_save_settings = true;
                    }
                    ui.label(
                        RichText::new(desc)
                            .size(11.5)
                            .color(text_color.gamma_multiply(0.75)),
                    );
                });
            ui.add_space(3.0);
        }

        ui.add_space(6.0);
        ui.separator();
        ui.add_space(6.0);
        ui.label(
            RichText::new("Р Р°Р·РІРѕСЂРѕС‚ Рё РјР°СЃС€С‚Р°Р± СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†:")
                .strong()
                .color(text_color),
        );
        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            let s1 = ui.radio_value(
                &mut settings.comic.page_layout,
                "Single".to_string(),
                "РћРґРёРЅРѕС‡РЅР°СЏ (1)",
            );
            let s2 = ui.radio_value(
                &mut settings.comic.page_layout,
                "Double".to_string(),
                "Р Р°Р·РІРѕСЂРѕС‚ (2)",
            );
            if s1.clicked() || s2.clicked() {
                *on_save_settings = true;
            }
        });
        ui.horizontal_wrapped(|ui| {
            for (val, label) in [
                ("FitWindow", "РџРѕ РѕРєРЅСѓ"),
                ("FitWidth", "РџРѕ С€РёСЂРёРЅРµ"),
                ("FitHeight", "РџРѕ РІС‹СЃРѕС‚Рµ"),
                ("Original", "100%"),
            ] {
                if ui
                    .radio_value(&mut settings.comic.fit_mode, val.to_string(), label)
                    .clicked()
                {
                    *on_save_settings = true;
                }
            }
        });
    }

    fn render_system_tab(
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        settings: &mut AppSettings,
        on_register_association: &mut bool,
        on_save_settings: &mut bool,
        theme: &ReaderTheme,
    ) {
        let text_color = theme.text_color();
        let card_stroke = egui::Stroke::new(1.0, theme.accent_color().gamma_multiply(0.25));

        ui.label(
            RichText::new("РРЅС‚РµРіСЂР°С†РёСЏ СЃ СЃРёСЃС‚РµРјРѕР№ Рё С…СЂР°РЅРёР»РёС‰Рµ")
                .size(17.0)
                .strong()
                .color(text_color),
        );
        ui.add_space(6.0);

        if crate::platform::supports_file_association() {
            egui::Frame::new()
                .fill(theme.bg_color())
                .stroke(card_stroke)
                .corner_radius(6.0)
                .inner_margin(egui::Margin::symmetric(12, 10))
                .show(ui, |ui| {
                    ui.label(RichText::new("РђСЃСЃРѕС†РёР°С†РёСЏ С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ FB2 РІ Windows:").strong().color(text_color));
                    ui.add_space(4.0);

                    match crate::platform::check_fb2_association_status() {
                        crate::platform::AssociationStatus::Default => {
                            ui.label(RichText::new("вњ” QuietReader РЅР°Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅ С‡РёС‚Р°Р»РєРѕР№ РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ РґР»СЏ С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ .fb2").color(Color32::from_rgb(46, 139, 87)));
                        }
                        crate::platform::AssociationStatus::Registered => {
                            ui.label("QuietReader Р·Р°СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ РІ СЃРёСЃС‚РµРјРµ, РЅРѕ РІС‹Р±РѕСЂ РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРёС‚СЊ РІ РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂР°С… Windows.");
                        }
                        _ => {
                            ui.label("QuietReader РµС‰Рµ РЅРµ Р°СЃСЃРѕС†РёРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ СЃ С„Р°Р№Р»Р°РјРё .fb2.");
                        }
                    }

                    ui.add_space(6.0);
                    if ui.button(RichText::new("рџ“Њ Р—Р°СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ Рё РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂС‹ Windows").strong()).clicked() {
                        *on_register_association = true;
                    }

                    ui.add_space(8.0);
                    if ui.checkbox(&mut settings.dismiss_association_prompt, "РќРµ РїРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°С‚СЊ РґРёР°Р»РѕРі Р°СЃСЃРѕС†РёР°С†РёРё РїСЂРё СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Рµ РїСЂРѕРіСЂР°РјРјС‹").changed() {
                        *on_save_settings = true;
                    }
                });
            ui.add_space(8.0);
        }

        egui::Frame::new()
            .fill(theme.bg_color())
            .stroke(card_stroke)
            .corner_radius(6.0)
            .inner_margin(egui::Margin::symmetric(12, 10))
            .show(ui, |ui| {
                ui.label(
                    RichText::new("РҐСЂР°РЅРёР»РёС‰Рµ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРµРє:")
                        .strong()
                        .color(text_color),
                );
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                let path_str = AppSettings::get_settings_path().display().to_string();
                ui.label(
                    RichText::new(path_str)
                        .size(12.0)
                        .color(text_color.gamma_multiply(0.7)),
                );
                ui.add_space(6.0);
                if ui
                    .button(RichText::new("рџ’ѕ РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё СЃРµР№С‡Р°СЃ").strong())
                    .clicked()
                {
                    *on_save_settings = true;
                }
            });
    }

=== FILE: C:\Users\Almas\Documents\QuietReader\src\app\tabs.rs ===
use super::theme::ReaderTheme;
use crate::document::Document;
use eframe::egui::{self, Color32, RichText, ScrollArea, Stroke};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct BookTab {
    pub file_path: PathBuf,
    pub title: String,
    pub format_badge: String,
    pub document: Option<Arc<dyn Document>>,
    pub is_loading: bool,
    pub load_error: Option<String>,
    pub request_id: u64,
    pub current_chapter: usize,
    pub last_rendered_chapter: usize,
    pub current_scroll_y: f32,
    pub viewport_height: f32,
    pub max_scroll_y: f32,
    pub comic_page: usize,
}

impl BookTab {
    pub fn new_loading(file_path: PathBuf, request_id: u64) -> Self {
        let format_badge = Self::detect_format_badge(&file_path);
        let title = file_path
            .file_name()
            .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "Р”РѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚".to_string());

        Self {
            file_path,
            title,
            format_badge,
            document: None,
            is_loading: true,
            load_error: None,
            request_id,
            current_chapter: 0,
            last_rendered_chapter: 0,
            current_scroll_y: 0.0,
            viewport_height: 600.0,
            max_scroll_y: 0.0,
            comic_page: 0,
        }
    }

    pub fn new(
        file_path: PathBuf,
        document: Arc<dyn Document>,
        current_chapter: usize,
        current_scroll_y: f32,
        comic_page: usize,
    ) -> Self {
        let meta_title = document.metadata().title.trim().to_string();
        let title = if !meta_title.is_empty() {
            meta_title
        } else {
            file_path
                .file_name()
                .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "Р”РѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚".to_string())
        };
        let format_badge = document.format_name().to_string();

        Self {
            file_path,
            title,
            format_badge,
            document: Some(document),
            is_loading: false,
            load_error: None,
            request_id: 0,
            current_chapter,
            last_rendered_chapter: current_chapter,
            current_scroll_y,
            viewport_height: 600.0,
            max_scroll_y: 0.0,
            comic_page,
        }
    }

    pub fn update_loaded(
        &mut self,
        document: Arc<dyn Document>,
        chapter: usize,
        scroll_y: f32,
        comic_page: usize,
    ) {
        let meta_title = document.metadata().title.trim().to_string();
        if !meta_title.is_empty() {
            self.title = meta_title;
        }
        self.format_badge = document.format_name().to_string();
        self.document = Some(document);
        self.is_loading = false;
        self.load_error = None;
        self.current_chapter = chapter;
        self.last_rendered_chapter = chapter;
        self.current_scroll_y = scroll_y;
        self.comic_page = comic_page;
    }

    pub fn detect_format_badge(path: &Path) -> String {
        if path.is_dir() {
            return "DIR".to_string();
        }
        let name_lower = path
            .file_name()
            .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_lowercase())
            .unwrap_or_default();
        if name_lower.ends_with(".fb2.zip") {
            return "FB2".to_string();
        }
        match path
            .extension()
            .and_then(|e| e.to_str())
            .map(|e| e.to_lowercase())
            .as_deref()
        {
            Some("pdf") => "PDF".to_string(),
            Some("djvu") | Some("djv") => "DJVU".to_string(),
            Some("fb2") | Some("xml") => "FB2".to_string(),
            Some("epub") => "EPUB".to_string(),
            Some("txt") => "TXT".to_string(),
            Some("md") | Some("markdown") => "MD".to_string(),
            Some("log") => "LOG".to_string(),
            Some("cbz") | Some("zip") => "CBZ".to_string(),
            Some("cbr") | Some("rar") => "CBR".to_string(),
            Some("cb7") | Some("7z") => "CB7".to_string(),
            Some(ext) => ext.to_uppercase(),
            None => "DOC".to_string(),
        }
    }

    pub fn short_title(&self, max_chars: usize) -> String {
        let count = self.title.chars().count();
        if count <= max_chars {
            self.title.clone()
        } else {
            let truncated: String = self
                .title
                .chars()
                .take(max_chars.saturating_sub(1))
                .collect();
            format!("{truncated}вЂ¦")
        }
    }
}

pub enum TabBarAction {
    None,
    Activate(usize),
    Close(usize),
    OpenNewFile,
}

pub struct TabsBar;

impl TabsBar {
    pub fn render(
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        tabs: &[BookTab],
        active_tab: usize,
        theme: &ReaderTheme,
    ) -> TabBarAction {
        let mut action = TabBarAction::None;

        ScrollArea::horizontal()
            .id_salt("open_books_tab_bar")
            .scroll_bar_visibility(egui::scroll_area::ScrollBarVisibility::AlwaysHidden)
            .show(ui, |ui| {
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.spacing_mut().item_spacing.x = 4.0;

                    for (idx, tab) in tabs.iter().enumerate() {
                        let is_active = idx == active_tab;
                        let bg_fill = if is_active {
                            theme.accent_color().gamma_multiply(0.22)
                        } else if theme.is_dark() {
                            Color32::from_rgba_unmultiplied(255, 255, 255, 12)
                        } else {
                            Color32::from_rgba_unmultiplied(0, 0, 0, 10)
                        };

                        let border_stroke = if is_active {
                            Stroke::new(1.5, theme.accent_color())
                        } else {
                            Stroke::new(1.0, Color32::TRANSPARENT)
                        };

                        let frame = egui::Frame::default()
                            .fill(bg_fill)
                            .stroke(border_stroke)
                            .corner_radius(6.0)
                            .inner_margin(egui::Margin::symmetric(8, 3));

                        frame.show(ui, |ui| {
                            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                                ui.spacing_mut().item_spacing.x = 6.0;

                                let badge_text = RichText::new(format!("[{}]", tab.format_badge))
                                    .size(11.0)
                                    .strong()
                                    .color(theme.accent_color());
                                ui.label(badge_text);

                                let label_str = if tab.is_loading {
                                    format!("вЏі {}", tab.short_title(22))
                                } else if tab.load_error.is_some() {
                                    format!("вљ пёЏ {}", tab.short_title(22))
                                } else {
                                    tab.short_title(26)
                                };
                                let title_rich = if is_active {
                                    RichText::new(label_str)
                                        .size(13.0)
                                        .strong()
                                        .color(theme.text_color())
                                } else {
                                    RichText::new(label_str)
                                        .size(13.0)
                                        .color(theme.text_color())
                                };

                                let tab_btn = ui.add(
                                    egui::Button::new(title_rich)
                                        .frame(false)
                                        .min_size(egui::Vec2::new(40.0, 20.0)),
                                );

                                let tooltip = format!(
                                    "{}\nР¤РѕСЂРјР°С‚: {}\nРџСѓС‚СЊ: {}\n(РЎРљРњ РїРѕ РІРєР»Р°РґРєРµ РёР»Рё Г— вЂ” Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ)",
                                    tab.title,
                                    tab.format_badge,
                                    tab.file_path.display()
                                );
                                let tab_btn = tab_btn.on_hover_text(tooltip);

                                if tab_btn.clicked() && !is_active {
                                    action = TabBarAction::Activate(idx);
                                } else if tab_btn.middle_clicked() {
                                    action = TabBarAction::Close(idx);
                                }

                                let (close_rect, close_resp) = ui.allocate_exact_size(
                                    egui::Vec2::new(18.0, 18.0),
                                    egui::Sense::click(),
                                );
                                if ui.is_rect_visible(close_rect) {
                                    let is_hovered = close_resp.hovered();
                                    if is_hovered {
                                        ui.painter().rect_filled(
                                            close_rect,
                                            4.0,
                                            theme.accent_color().gamma_multiply(0.25),
                                        );
                                    }
                                    let cross_color = if is_hovered {
                                        theme.accent_color()
                                    } else {
                                        theme.text_color().gamma_multiply(0.75)
                                    };
                                    let c = close_rect.center();
                                    let r = 3.8;
                                    let stroke = Stroke::new(1.6, cross_color);
                                    ui.painter().line_segment(
                                        [
                                            egui::pos2(c.x - r, c.y - r),
                                            egui::pos2(c.x + r, c.y + r),
                                        ],
                                        stroke,
                                    );
                                    ui.painter().line_segment(
                                        [
                                            egui::pos2(c.x - r, c.y + r),
                                            egui::pos2(c.x + r, c.y - r),
                                        ],
                                        stroke,
                                    );
                                }
                                let close_resp =
                                    close_resp.on_hover_text("Р—Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РІРєР»Р°РґРєСѓ (Ctrl+W)");
                                if close_resp.clicked() {
                                    action = TabBarAction::Close(idx);
                                }
                            });
                        });
                    }

                    let add_btn = ui
                        .add(
                            egui::Button::new(RichText::new("+").size(15.0).strong())
                                .min_size(egui::Vec2::new(26.0, 24.0)),
                        )
                        .on_hover_text("РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РµС‰С‘ РѕРґРЅСѓ РєРЅРёРіСѓ РІ РЅРѕРІРѕР№ РІРєР»Р°РґРєРµ (Ctrl+O)");
                    if add_btn.clicked() {
                        action = TabBarAction::OpenNewFile;
                    }
                });
            });

        action
    }
}

use super::theme::ReaderTheme;
use crate::document::Document;
use crate::settings::AppSettings;
use eframe::egui::{self, Color32, RichText, ScrollArea, Vec2};
use std::path::PathBuf;

pub struct TextView;

impl TextView {
    pub fn render(
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        _ctx: &egui::Context,
        document: &Option<std::sync::Arc<dyn Document>>,
        current_file_path: &Option<PathBuf>,
        current_chapter: &mut usize,
        last_rendered_chapter: &mut usize,
        pending_scroll_y: &mut Option<f32>,
        current_scroll_y: &mut f32,
        viewport_height: &mut f32,
        max_scroll_y: &mut f32,
        font_size: f32,
        theme: &ReaderTheme,
        settings: &mut AppSettings,
        _status_message: &mut Option<String>,
        on_open_file: &mut bool,
        on_open_folder: &mut bool,
        on_load_recent: &mut Option<PathBuf>,
    ) {
        if let Some(doc) = document {
            let total_chapters = doc.chapters().len();
            let chapter = doc.chapters().get(*current_chapter);

            let allow_select = !ui.input(|i| i.pointer.button_down(egui::PointerButton::Middle));

            let mut scroll_area = ScrollArea::vertical()
                .id_salt(("text_scroll", current_file_path, *current_chapter))
                .auto_shrink([false, false]);
            if let Some(offset) = pending_scroll_y.take() {
                scroll_area = scroll_area.vertical_scroll_offset(offset);
            }

            let scroll_output = scroll_area.show(ui, |ui| {
                ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                    ui.set_max_width(760.0);

                    if let Some(ch) = chapter {
                        ui.add_space(20.0);
                        if !ch.title.is_empty() {
                            let title_heading = RichText::new(&ch.title)
                                .size(font_size * 1.35)
                                .strong()
                                .color(theme.text_color());
                            let resp =
                                ui.add(egui::Label::new(title_heading).selectable(allow_select));
                            resp.context_menu(|ui| {
                                if ui.button("рџ“‹ РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ").clicked() {
                                    ui.ctx().copy_text(ch.title.clone());
                                    ui.close();
                                }
                                if ui.button("рџ”І Р’С‹Р±СЂР°С‚СЊ РІСЃС‘ (РєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ СЂР°Р·РґРµР»)").clicked() {
                                    if let Some(full) =
                                        crate::core::ReaderCore::extract_chapter_text(
                                            doc.as_ref(),
                                            *current_chapter,
                                        )
                                    {
                                        ui.ctx().copy_text(full);
                                    }
                                    ui.close();
                                }
                            });
                            ui.add_space(24.0);
                        }

                        let clip_rect = ui.clip_rect();
                        let margin = 400.0;
                        let view_top = clip_rect.min.y - margin;
                        let view_bot = clip_rect.max.y + margin;

                        let line_height = font_size * 1.45;
                        let para_spacing = font_size * 0.4;
                        let chars_per_line = (760.0 / (font_size * 0.52)).max(10.0);

                        for paragraph in &ch.paragraphs {
                            if paragraph.is_empty() {
                                ui.add_space(font_size * 0.7);
                                continue;
                            }

                            let char_count = paragraph.chars().count();
                            let num_lines = (char_count as f32 / chars_per_line).ceil().max(1.0);
                            let est_height = num_lines * line_height + para_spacing;

                            let top_y = ui.cursor().min.y;
                            let bot_y = top_y + est_height;

                            if bot_y < view_top || top_y > view_bot {
                                ui.add_space(est_height);
                            } else {
                                let text_content = format!("    {paragraph}");
                                let text = RichText::new(&text_content)
                                    .size(font_size)
                                    .color(theme.text_color());
                                let resp =
                                    ui.add(egui::Label::new(text).wrap().selectable(allow_select));
                                resp.context_menu(|ui| {
                                    if ui.button("рџ“‹ РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ").clicked() {
                                        ui.ctx().copy_text(paragraph.clone());
                                        ui.close();
                                    }
                                    if ui.button("рџ”І Р’С‹Р±СЂР°С‚СЊ РІСЃС‘ (РєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ СЂР°Р·РґРµР»)").clicked() {
                                        if let Some(full) =
                                            crate::core::ReaderCore::extract_chapter_text(
                                                doc.as_ref(),
                                                *current_chapter,
                                            )
                                        {
                                            ui.ctx().copy_text(full);
                                        }
                                        ui.close();
                                    }
                                });
                                ui.add_space(para_spacing);
                            }
                        }

                        ui.add_space(40.0);

                        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                            let prev_chapter_idx = (0..*current_chapter).rev().find(|&idx| {
                                doc.chapters()
                                    .get(idx)
                                    .map(|c| !c.is_empty())
                                    .unwrap_or(false)
                            });

                            let next_chapter_idx =
                                ((*current_chapter + 1)..total_chapters).find(|&idx| {
                                    doc.chapters()
                                        .get(idx)
                                        .map(|c| !c.is_empty())
                                        .unwrap_or(false)
                                });

                            if let Some(prev_idx) = prev_chapter_idx
                                && ui.button("В« РџСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰РёР№ СЂР°Р·РґРµР»").clicked()
                            {
                                *current_chapter = prev_idx;
                                *pending_scroll_y = Some(0.0);
                                *current_scroll_y = 0.0;
                                *last_rendered_chapter = *current_chapter;
                            }

                            ui.with_layout(
                                egui::Layout::right_to_left(egui::Align::Center),
                                |ui| {
                                    if let Some(next_idx) = next_chapter_idx
                                        && ui.button("РЎР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РёР№ СЂР°Р·РґРµР» В»").clicked()
                                    {
                                        *current_chapter = next_idx;
                                        *pending_scroll_y = Some(0.0);
                                        *current_scroll_y = 0.0;
                                        *last_rendered_chapter = *current_chapter;
                                    }
                                },
                            );
                        });

                        ui.add_space(30.0);
                    }
                });
            });

            *current_scroll_y = scroll_output.state.offset.y;
            *viewport_height = scroll_output.inner_rect.height();
            *max_scroll_y =
                (scroll_output.content_size.y - scroll_output.inner_rect.height()).max(0.0);

            if let Some(path) = current_file_path {
                let meta = doc.metadata();
                settings.record_book_progress(
                    path,
                    &meta.title,
                    &meta.author,
                    *current_chapter,
                    *current_scroll_y,
                );
            }
        } else {
            ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                ui.add_space(80.0);
                ui.heading(
                    RichText::new("рџ“– QuietReader")
                        .size(36.0)
                        .color(theme.accent_color()),
                );
                ui.add_space(10.0);
                ui.label(
                    RichText::new("РњРёРЅРёРјР°Р»РёСЃС‚РёС‡РЅР°СЏ, РїРѕСЂС‚Р°С‚РёРІРЅР°СЏ С‡РёС‚Р°Р»РєР° РєРЅРёРі, РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚РѕРІ Рё РєРѕРјРёРєСЃРѕРІ")
                        .size(18.0)
                        .color(theme.text_color()),
                );
                ui.add_space(30.0);

                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    let open_btn = egui::Button::new(
                        RichText::new("рџ“‚ Р’С‹Р±СЂР°С‚СЊ РєРЅРёРіСѓ / PDF / РєРѕРјРёРєСЃ").size(17.0),
                    )
                    .min_size(Vec2::new(280.0, 46.0));

                    if ui
                        .add(open_btn)
                        .on_hover_text("PDF, FB2, EPUB, TXT, MD, LOG, CBZ, CBR, CB7, ZIP, RAR, 7Z")
                        .clicked()
                    {
                        *on_open_file = true;
                    }

                    let open_dir_btn = egui::Button::new(
                        RichText::new("рџ“Ѓ РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РїР°РїРєСѓ РјР°РЅРіРё").size(17.0),
                    )
                    .min_size(Vec2::new(240.0, 46.0));

                    if ui.add(open_dir_btn).on_hover_text("РљР°С‚Р°Р»РѕРі СЃ РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏРјРё (JPG, PNG, WebP)").clicked() {
                        *on_open_folder = true;
                    }
                });

                ui.add_space(16.0);
                ui.label(
                    RichText::new("РёР»Рё РїРµСЂРµС‚Р°С‰РёС‚Рµ С„Р°Р№Р» (PDF, FB2, EPUB, TXT, MD, LOG, CBZ, CBR, CB7) РёР»Рё РїР°РїРєСѓ СЃ РјР°РЅРіРѕР№ РІ СЌС‚Рѕ РѕРєРЅРѕ")
                        .size(14.0)
                        .color(Color32::from_gray(140)),
                );

                if !settings.recent_books.is_empty() {
                    ui.add_space(28.0);
                    ui.separator();
                    ui.add_space(12.0);
                    ui.label(RichText::new("РќРµРґР°РІРЅРёРµ РєРЅРёРіРё:").strong().size(16.0));
                    ui.add_space(10.0);

                    for book in settings.recent_books.iter().take(4) {
                        let label = if book.author.is_empty() {
                            format!("рџ“– {} (Р Р°Р·РґРµР» {})", book.title, book.chapter + 1)
                        } else {
                            format!("рџ“– {} вЂ” {} (Р Р°Р·РґРµР» {})", book.author, book.title, book.chapter + 1)
                        };
                        if ui.button(RichText::new(label).size(14.0)).clicked() {
                            *on_load_recent = Some(book.path.clone());
                        }
                        ui.add_space(4.0);
                    }
                }
            });
        }
    }
}

use eframe::egui::Color32;

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Clone, Copy)]
pub enum ReaderTheme {
    Dark,
    Light,
    Sepia,
    Oled,
    Warm,
    Mint,
}

impl ReaderTheme {
    pub fn is_dark(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, ReaderTheme::Dark | ReaderTheme::Oled)
    }

    pub fn bg_color(&self) -> Color32 {
        match self {
            ReaderTheme::Dark => Color32::from_rgb(26, 26, 28),
            ReaderTheme::Light => Color32::from_rgb(248, 249, 250),
            ReaderTheme::Sepia => Color32::from_rgb(244, 237, 222),
            ReaderTheme::Oled => Color32::from_rgb(0, 0, 0),
            ReaderTheme::Warm => Color32::from_rgb(250, 240, 220),
            ReaderTheme::Mint => Color32::from_rgb(238, 245, 238),
        }
    }

    pub fn text_color(&self) -> Color32 {
        match self {
            ReaderTheme::Dark => Color32::from_rgb(220, 220, 220),
            ReaderTheme::Light => Color32::from_rgb(33, 37, 41),
            ReaderTheme::Sepia => Color32::from_rgb(60, 48, 36),
            ReaderTheme::Oled => Color32::from_rgb(205, 205, 205),
            ReaderTheme::Warm => Color32::from_rgb(70, 50, 30),
            ReaderTheme::Mint => Color32::from_rgb(38, 55, 42),
        }
    }

    pub fn secondary_text_color(&self) -> Color32 {
        self.text_color().gamma_multiply(0.65)
    }

    pub fn panel_bg_color(&self) -> Color32 {
        match self {
            ReaderTheme::Dark => Color32::from_rgb(35, 35, 38),
            ReaderTheme::Light => Color32::from_rgb(238, 240, 244),
            ReaderTheme::Sepia => Color32::from_rgb(235, 227, 210),
            ReaderTheme::Oled => Color32::from_rgb(10, 10, 12),
            ReaderTheme::Warm => Color32::from_rgb(242, 230, 208),
            ReaderTheme::Mint => Color32::from_rgb(226, 236, 226),
        }
    }

    pub fn accent_color(&self) -> Color32 {
        match self {
            ReaderTheme::Dark => Color32::from_rgb(100, 160, 255),
            ReaderTheme::Light => Color32::from_rgb(0, 102, 204),
            ReaderTheme::Sepia => Color32::from_rgb(140, 90, 40),
            ReaderTheme::Oled => Color32::from_rgb(85, 145, 255),
            ReaderTheme::Warm => Color32::from_rgb(180, 95, 25),
            ReaderTheme::Mint => Color32::from_rgb(45, 125, 80),
        }
    }

    pub fn create_visuals(&self) -> eframe::egui::Visuals {
        use eframe::egui;
        let mut visuals = if self.is_dark() {
            egui::Visuals::dark()
        } else {
            egui::Visuals::light()
        };
        visuals.panel_fill = self.panel_bg_color();
        visuals.window_fill = self.panel_bg_color();
        visuals.window_stroke = egui::Stroke::new(1.0, self.accent_color().gamma_multiply(0.4));
        visuals.override_text_color = Some(self.text_color());

        match self {
            ReaderTheme::Oled => {
                visuals.extreme_bg_color = Color32::from_rgb(0, 0, 0);
                visuals.widgets.noninteractive.bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(10, 10, 12);
                visuals.widgets.noninteractive.bg_stroke =
                    egui::Stroke::new(1.0, Color32::from_rgb(30, 30, 35));
                visuals.widgets.inactive.weak_bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(14, 14, 18);
                visuals.widgets.inactive.bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(14, 14, 18);
            }
            ReaderTheme::Sepia => {
                visuals.widgets.hovered.fg_stroke.color = Color32::from_rgb(20, 15, 10);
                visuals.widgets.active.fg_stroke.color = Color32::from_rgb(0, 0, 0);
                visuals.widgets.noninteractive.bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(228, 220, 203);
                visuals.widgets.noninteractive.bg_stroke =
                    egui::Stroke::new(1.0, Color32::from_rgb(210, 200, 180));
                visuals.widgets.inactive.weak_bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(228, 220, 203);
                visuals.widgets.inactive.bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(228, 220, 203);
            }
            ReaderTheme::Warm => {
                visuals.widgets.hovered.fg_stroke.color = Color32::from_rgb(30, 20, 10);
                visuals.widgets.active.fg_stroke.color = Color32::from_rgb(10, 5, 0);
                visuals.widgets.noninteractive.bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(236, 224, 200);
                visuals.widgets.noninteractive.bg_stroke =
                    egui::Stroke::new(1.0, Color32::from_rgb(218, 204, 178));
                visuals.widgets.inactive.weak_bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(236, 224, 200);
                visuals.widgets.inactive.bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(236, 224, 200);
            }
            ReaderTheme::Mint => {
                visuals.widgets.hovered.fg_stroke.color = Color32::from_rgb(15, 30, 20);
                visuals.widgets.active.fg_stroke.color = Color32::from_rgb(0, 20, 10);
                visuals.widgets.noninteractive.bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(220, 230, 220);
                visuals.widgets.noninteractive.bg_stroke =
                    egui::Stroke::new(1.0, Color32::from_rgb(200, 215, 200));
                visuals.widgets.inactive.weak_bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(220, 230, 220);
                visuals.widgets.inactive.bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(220, 230, 220);
            }
            ReaderTheme::Light => {
                visuals.widgets.noninteractive.bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(244, 245, 248);
                visuals.widgets.noninteractive.bg_stroke =
                    egui::Stroke::new(1.0, Color32::from_rgb(220, 224, 230));
                visuals.widgets.inactive.weak_bg_fill = Color32::from_rgb(244, 245, 248);
            }
            ReaderTheme::Dark => {}
        }
        visuals
    }
}

use super::theme::ReaderTheme;
use crate::document::{Document, TocItem};
use crate::settings::ComicSettings;
use eframe::egui::{self, Color32, Panel, RichText, ScrollArea};

pub struct TocView;

impl TocView {
    pub fn render(
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        document: &Option<std::sync::Arc<dyn Document>>,
        comic_settings: &ComicSettings,
        current_chapter: &mut usize,
        current_page: &mut usize,
        show_toc: &mut bool,
        theme: &ReaderTheme,
    ) {
        if !*show_toc {
            return;
        }

        let doc = match document {
            Some(d) => d,
            None => return,
        };

        let available_w = ui.available_width();
        let max_toc_w = (available_w * 0.45).clamp(200.0, 480.0);
        let mut close_toc = false;

        Panel::left("toc_panel")
            .resizable(true)
            .show_separator_line(true)
            .min_size(140.0)
            .max_size(max_toc_w)
            .default_size(260.0)
            .show(ui, |ui| {
                ui.style_mut().interaction.resize_grab_radius_side = 8.0;
                ui.style_mut().wrap_mode = Some(egui::TextWrapMode::Wrap);

                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.heading("РћРіР»Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ");
                    ui.with_layout(egui::Layout::right_to_left(egui::Align::Center), |ui| {
                        if ui
                            .small_button("Г—")
                            .on_hover_text("РЎРєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РѕРіР»Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ")
                            .clicked()
                        {
                            close_toc = true;
                        }
                    });
                });
                ui.add_space(4.0);

                ScrollArea::vertical()
                    .auto_shrink([false, false])
                    .show(ui, |ui| {
                        ui.set_max_width(ui.available_width());
                        if let Some(page_content) = doc.page_content() {
                            let page_count = page_content.page_count();
                            let toc = doc.toc();

                            let has_custom_toc = doc.has_custom_toc();

                            if has_custom_toc {
                                for item in toc {
                                    render_toc_item(
                                        ui,
                                        item,
                                        *current_page,
                                        current_page,
                                        theme.accent_color(),
                                    );
                                }
                                ui.add_space(8.0);
                                ui.separator();
                                ui.add_space(4.0);
                            }

                            let is_double = comic_settings.page_layout == "Double"
                                && comic_settings.reading_mode != "Webtoon";
                            let step = if is_double { 2 } else { 1 };
                            let total_rows = page_count.div_ceil(step);
                            let row_height = ui.text_style_height(&egui::TextStyle::Button) + 6.0;

                            let mut render_pages_virtual = |ui: &mut egui::Ui| {
                                let mut new_page = None;
                                ScrollArea::vertical()
                                    .id_salt("pages_virtual_scroll")
                                    .auto_shrink([false, false])
                                    .show_rows(ui, row_height, total_rows, |ui, row_range| {
                                        for row_idx in row_range {
                                            let p = row_idx * step;
                                            if p >= page_count {
                                                break;
                                            }
                                            let is_current = if is_double {
                                                *current_page == p
                                                    || (p + 1 < page_count
                                                        && *current_page == p + 1)
                                            } else {
                                                *current_page == p
                                            };

                                            let page_name = page_content.page_name(p);
                                            let label = if is_double && p + 1 < page_count {
                                                if page_name.is_empty() {
                                                    format!("РЎС‚СЂ. {}-{}", p + 1, p + 2)
                                                } else {
                                                    format!(
                                                        "РЎС‚СЂ. {}-{} ({page_name})",
                                                        p + 1,
                                                        p + 2
                                                    )
                                                }
                                            } else if page_name.is_empty() {
                                                format!("РЎС‚СЂ. {}", p + 1)
                                            } else {
                                                format!("РЎС‚СЂ. {} ({page_name})", p + 1)
                                            };

                                            let mut rich = RichText::new(label);
                                            if is_current {
                                                rich = rich.strong().color(theme.accent_color());
                                            }

                                            let btn =
                                                egui::Button::selectable(is_current, rich).wrap();
                                            if ui.add(btn).clicked() {
                                                new_page = Some(p);
                                            }
                                        }
                                    });

                                if let Some(p) = new_page {
                                    *current_page = p;
                                }
                            };

                            if has_custom_toc {
                                egui::CollapsingHeader::new(format!("Р’СЃРµ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ ({page_count})"))
                                    .default_open(false)
                                    .show(ui, |ui| {
                                        render_pages_virtual(ui);
                                    });
                            } else {
                                ui.label(
                                    RichText::new(format!("РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ ({page_count}):")).strong(),
                                );
                                ui.add_space(4.0);
                                render_pages_virtual(ui);
                            }
                        } else {
                            let toc = doc.toc();
                            if toc.is_empty() {
                                ui.add_space(8.0);
                                ui.label(
                                    RichText::new("Р’ СЌС‚РѕР№ РєРЅРёРіРµ РЅРµС‚ РѕРіР»Р°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ")
                                        .italics()
                                        .color(Color32::from_gray(140)),
                                );
                            } else {
                                for item in toc {
                                    render_toc_item(
                                        ui,
                                        item,
                                        *current_chapter,
                                        current_chapter,
                                        theme.accent_color(),
                                    );
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    });
            });

        if close_toc {
            *show_toc = false;
        }
    }
}

pub fn render_toc_item(
    ui: &mut egui::Ui,
    item: &TocItem,
    current_chapter: usize,
    selected_chapter: &mut usize,
    accent_color: Color32,
) {
    render_toc_item_bounded(
        ui,
        item,
        current_chapter,
        selected_chapter,
        accent_color,
        0,
    );
}

fn render_toc_item_bounded(
    ui: &mut egui::Ui,
    item: &TocItem,
    current_chapter: usize,
    selected_chapter: &mut usize,
    accent_color: Color32,
    depth: usize,
) {
    if depth >= 32 {
        return;
    }
    let is_selected = current_chapter == item.chapter_index;
    let title = if item.title.trim().is_empty() {
        "вЂ”"
    } else {
        item.title.as_str()
    };

    if item.children.is_empty() {
        let mut rich = RichText::new(title);
        if is_selected {
            rich = rich.strong().color(accent_color);
        }
        let btn = egui::Button::selectable(is_selected, rich).wrap();
        if ui.add(btn).clicked() {
            *selected_chapter = item.chapter_index;
        }
    } else {
        let header_id = ui.make_persistent_id(format!(
            "toc_{}_{}_{}",
            depth, item.chapter_index, item.title
        ));
        egui::collapsing_header::CollapsingState::load_with_default_open(ui.ctx(), header_id, true)
            .show_header(ui, |ui| {
                let mut rich = RichText::new(title);
                if is_selected {
                    rich = rich.strong().color(accent_color);
                }
                let btn = egui::Button::selectable(is_selected, rich).wrap();
                if ui.add(btn).clicked() {
                    *selected_chapter = item.chapter_index;
                }
            })
            .body(|ui| {
                ui.indent(header_id.with("indent"), |ui| {
                    for child in &item.children {
                        render_toc_item_bounded(
                            ui,
                            child,
                            current_chapter,
                            selected_chapter,
                            accent_color,
                            depth + 1,
                        );
                    }
                });
            });
    }
}

use crate::document::Document;
use crate::settings::{AppSettings, ComicSettings};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Command {
    NextPage,
    PrevPage,
    PageDown,
    PageUp,
    LineDown,
    LineUp,
    NextChapter,
    PrevChapter,
    Home,
    End,
    ScrollBy(f32),
    GoToChapter(usize),
    GoToPage(usize),
    ToggleFullscreen,
    ExitFullscreen,
    ToggleAutoscroll,
    CopyChapterText,
    ZoomIn,
    ZoomOut,
    ResetZoom,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub struct Bookmark {
    pub book_path: PathBuf,
    pub chapter: usize,
    pub scroll_y: f32,
    pub label: String,
    pub timestamp: u64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct SearchHit {
    pub chapter_index: usize,
    pub paragraph_index: Option<usize>,
    pub snippet: String,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct ReaderCore {
    pub bookmarks: Vec<Bookmark>,
}

impl ReaderCore {
    pub fn add_bookmark(&mut self, bookmark: Bookmark) {
        if !self.bookmarks.iter().any(|b| {
            b.book_path == bookmark.book_path
                && b.chapter == bookmark.chapter
                && (b.scroll_y - bookmark.scroll_y).abs() < 1.0
        }) {
            self.bookmarks.push(bookmark);
        }
    }

    pub fn remove_bookmark(&mut self, book_path: &Path, chapter: usize) {
        self.bookmarks
            .retain(|b| !(b.book_path == book_path && b.chapter == chapter));
    }

    pub fn bookmarks_for_path<'a>(
        &'a self,
        book_path: &'a Path,
    ) -> impl Iterator<Item = &'a Bookmark> {
        self.bookmarks.iter().filter(move |b| b.book_path == book_path)
    }

    pub fn clear_bookmarks_for_path(&mut self, book_path: &Path) {
        self.bookmarks.retain(|b| b.book_path != book_path);
    }

    pub fn search_in_document(doc: &dyn Document, query: &str) -> Vec<SearchHit> {
        let q = query.trim().to_lowercase();
        if q.is_empty() {
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let mut hits = Vec::new();
        for (ch_idx, chapter) in doc.chapters().iter().enumerate() {
            for (p_idx, para) in chapter.paragraphs.iter().enumerate() {
                if let Some(pos) = para.to_lowercase().find(&q) {
                    let start = pos.saturating_sub(30);
                    let end = (pos + q.len() + 30).min(para.len());
                    let snippet = para[start..end].trim().to_string();
                    hits.push(SearchHit {
                        chapter_index: ch_idx,
                        paragraph_index: Some(p_idx),
                        snippet,
                    });
                    if hits.len() >= 100 {
                        return hits;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        hits
    }

    pub fn find_next_non_empty_chapter(
        doc: &dyn Document,
        current_chapter: usize,
    ) -> Option<usize> {
        let total = doc.chapters().len();
        ((current_chapter + 1)..total).find(|&idx| {
            doc.chapters()
                .get(idx)
                .map(|c| !c.is_empty())
                .unwrap_or(false)
        })
    }

    pub fn find_prev_non_empty_chapter(
        doc: &dyn Document,
        current_chapter: usize,
    ) -> Option<usize> {
        (0..current_chapter).rev().find(|&idx| {
            doc.chapters()
                .get(idx)
                .map(|c| !c.is_empty())
                .unwrap_or(false)
        })
    }

    pub fn extract_chapter_text(doc: &dyn Document, current_chapter: usize) -> Option<String> {
        if let Some(page_content) = doc.page_content()
            && let Some(text) = page_content.extract_page_text(current_chapter)
        {
            return Some(text);
        }
        let ch = doc.chapters().get(current_chapter)?;
        let mut s = String::new();
        if !ch.title.is_empty() {
            s.push_str(&ch.title);
            s.push_str("\n\n");
        }
        for (i, p) in ch.paragraphs.iter().enumerate() {
            if i > 0 {
                s.push_str("\n\n");
            }
            s.push_str(p);
        }
        Some(s)
    }

    pub fn apply_text_command(
        cmd: &Command,
        doc: &dyn Document,
        current_chapter: &mut usize,
        last_rendered_chapter: &mut usize,
        current_scroll_y: f32,
        max_scroll_y: f32,
        viewport_height: f32,
        pending_scroll_y: &mut Option<f32>,
    ) -> bool {
        let page_step = (viewport_height * 0.85).max(200.0);
        let line_step = 60.0;
        let next_non_empty = Self::find_next_non_empty_chapter(doc, *current_chapter);
        let prev_non_empty = Self::find_prev_non_empty_chapter(doc, *current_chapter);

        match cmd {
            Command::NextChapter => {
                if let Some(next_idx) = next_non_empty {
                    *current_chapter = next_idx;
                    *pending_scroll_y = Some(0.0);
                    *last_rendered_chapter = *current_chapter;
                    return true;
                }
            }
            Command::PrevChapter => {
                if let Some(prev_idx) = prev_non_empty {
                    *current_chapter = prev_idx;
                    *pending_scroll_y = Some(0.0);
                    *last_rendered_chapter = *current_chapter;
                    return true;
                }
            }
            Command::PageDown => {
                if current_scroll_y + 15.0 >= max_scroll_y {
                    if let Some(next_idx) = next_non_empty {
                        *current_chapter = next_idx;
                        *pending_scroll_y = Some(0.0);
                        *last_rendered_chapter = *current_chapter;
                        return true;
                    }
                } else {
                    *pending_scroll_y = Some((current_scroll_y + page_step).min(max_scroll_y));
                    return true;
                }
            }
            Command::PageUp => {
                if current_scroll_y <= 5.0 {
                    if let Some(prev_idx) = prev_non_empty {
                        *current_chapter = prev_idx;
                        *pending_scroll_y = Some(999_999.0);
                        *last_rendered_chapter = *current_chapter;
                        return true;
                    }
                } else {
                    *pending_scroll_y = Some((current_scroll_y - page_step).max(0.0));
                    return true;
                }
            }
            Command::LineDown => {
                *pending_scroll_y = Some((current_scroll_y + line_step).min(max_scroll_y));
                return true;
            }
            Command::LineUp => {
                *pending_scroll_y = Some((current_scroll_y - line_step).max(0.0));
                return true;
            }
            Command::Home => {
                *pending_scroll_y = Some(0.0);
                return true;
            }
            Command::End => {
                *pending_scroll_y = Some(max_scroll_y);
                return true;
            }
            Command::ScrollBy(delta) => {
                *pending_scroll_y = Some((current_scroll_y + delta).clamp(0.0, max_scroll_y));
                return true;
            }
            Command::GoToChapter(ch_idx) if *ch_idx < doc.chapters().len() => {
                *current_chapter = *ch_idx;
                *pending_scroll_y = Some(0.0);
                *last_rendered_chapter = *current_chapter;
                return true;
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        false
    }

    pub fn apply_paged_command(
        cmd: &Command,
        doc: &Option<Arc<dyn Document>>,
        comic_settings: &ComicSettings,
        current_page: &mut usize,
        current_scroll_y: f32,
        max_scroll_y: f32,
        viewport_height: f32,
        pending_scroll_y: &mut Option<f32>,
    ) -> bool {
        let page_count = doc
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|d| d.page_content())
            .map(|c| c.page_count())
            .unwrap_or(0);
        if page_count == 0 {
            return false;
        }

        let is_webtoon = comic_settings.reading_mode == "Webtoon";
        let page_step = (viewport_height * 0.85).max(200.0);
        let line_step = 60.0;

        if is_webtoon {
            match cmd {
                Command::PageDown => {
                    *pending_scroll_y = Some((current_scroll_y + page_step).min(max_scroll_y));
                    true
                }
                Command::PageUp => {
                    *pending_scroll_y = Some((current_scroll_y - page_step).max(0.0));
                    true
                }
                Command::LineDown => {
                    *pending_scroll_y = Some((current_scroll_y + line_step).min(max_scroll_y));
                    true
                }
                Command::LineUp => {
                    *pending_scroll_y = Some((current_scroll_y - line_step).max(0.0));
                    true
                }
                Command::Home => {
                    *pending_scroll_y = Some(0.0);
                    true
                }
                Command::End => {
                    *pending_scroll_y = Some(max_scroll_y);
                    true
                }
                _ => false,
            }
        } else {
            let is_double = comic_settings.page_layout == "Double";
            let step = if is_double { 2 } else { 1 };
            match cmd {
                Command::NextPage | Command::PageDown => {
                    *current_page = (*current_page + step).min(page_count.saturating_sub(1));
                    true
                }
                Command::PrevPage | Command::PageUp => {
                    *current_page = current_page.saturating_sub(step);
                    true
                }
                Command::Home => {
                    *current_page = 0;
                    true
                }
                Command::End => {
                    *current_page = page_count.saturating_sub(1);
                    true
                }
                Command::GoToPage(p) => {
                    *current_page = (*p).min(page_count.saturating_sub(1));
                    true
                }
                _ => false,
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn record_progress(
        settings: &mut AppSettings,
        document: &Option<Arc<dyn Document>>,
        current_file_path: &Option<PathBuf>,
        current_chapter: usize,
        current_page: usize,
        current_scroll_y: f32,
    ) {
        if let (Some(doc), Some(path)) = (document, current_file_path) {
            let meta = doc.metadata();
            let saved_ch = if doc.page_content().is_some() {
                current_page
            } else {
                current_chapter
            };
            settings.record_book_progress(
                path,
                &meta.title,
                &meta.author,
                saved_ch,
                current_scroll_y,
            );
        }
    }
}

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::fs::File;
use std::io::Read;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use crate::document::{
    BookChapter, BookMetadata, DecodedImage, Document, DocumentError, DocumentViewKind, HasToc,
    PageContent, PageInfo, PageTextSpan, TocItem,
};

pub struct DjvuDocument {
    metadata: BookMetadata,
    _path: PathBuf,
    doc: djvu_rs::DjVuDocument,
    page_count: usize,
    page_names: Vec<String>,
    chapters: Vec<BookChapter>,
    toc: Vec<TocItem>,
}

impl DjvuDocument {
    pub fn from_file<P: AsRef<Path>>(path: P) -> Result<Self, DocumentError> {
        let path_ref = path.as_ref();
        let mut file = File::open(path_ref).map_err(|e| {
            DocumentError::Io(format!(
                "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ DjVu С„Р°Р№Р» {}: {e}",
                path_ref.display()
            ))
        })?;

        let file_len = file.metadata().map(|m| m.len()).unwrap_or(0);
        const MAX_DJVU_SIZE: u64 = 500 * 1024 * 1024;
        if file_len > MAX_DJVU_SIZE {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                "Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ DjVu С„Р°Р№Р»Р° РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°РµС‚ РјР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ РґРѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёРјС‹Р№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ 500 РњР‘".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        let mut bytes = Vec::new();
        file.read_to_end(&mut bytes)
            .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Io(format!("РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РїСЂРѕС‡РёС‚Р°С‚СЊ DjVu С„Р°Р№Р»: {e}")))?;

        Self::from_bytes(bytes, path_ref)
    }

    pub fn from_bytes(bytes: Vec<u8>, path: &Path) -> Result<Self, DocumentError> {
        let offset = if bytes.len() >= 8 && bytes.starts_with(b"AT&TFORM") {
            0
        } else if let Some(pos) = bytes[..bytes.len().min(1024)]
            .windows(8)
            .position(|w| w == b"AT&TFORM")
        {
            pos
        } else if bytes.len() >= 4 && bytes.starts_with(b"FORM") {
            0
        } else {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(
                "Р¤Р°Р№Р» РЅРµ СЏРІР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РІР°Р»РёРґРЅС‹Рј DjVu РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚РѕРј (РѕС‚СЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє AT&TFORM)".to_string(),
            ));
        };

        let data = if offset > 0 { &bytes[offset..] } else { &bytes[..] };
        if data.len() < 12 {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(
                "Р¤Р°Р№Р» DjVu СЃР»РёС€РєРѕРј РєРѕСЂРѕС‚РєРёР№ РґР»СЏ С‡С‚РµРЅРёСЏ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєР°".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        let base_dir = path.parent().unwrap_or_else(|| Path::new("."));
        let base_dir_buf = base_dir.to_path_buf();
        let safe_resolver = move |name: &str| -> Result<Vec<u8>, djvu_rs::DocError> {
            let clean = name.strip_prefix("file://").unwrap_or(name);
            let p = Path::new(clean);
            if p.is_absolute() || !crate::document::is_safe_archive_path(clean) {
                return Err(djvu_rs::DocError::IndirectResolve(clean.to_string()));
            }
            let target = base_dir_buf.join(p);
            if let (Ok(canon_base), Ok(canon_target)) =
                (base_dir_buf.canonicalize(), target.canonicalize())
            {
                if !canon_target.starts_with(&canon_base) {
                    return Err(djvu_rs::DocError::IndirectResolve(clean.to_string()));
                }
            } else {
                return Err(djvu_rs::DocError::IndirectResolve(clean.to_string()));
            }
            let metadata = std::fs::metadata(&target)
                .map_err(|_| djvu_rs::DocError::IndirectResolve(clean.to_string()))?;
            if !metadata.is_file() || metadata.len() > crate::document::MAX_SINGLE_ENTRY_BYTES {
                return Err(djvu_rs::DocError::IndirectResolve(clean.to_string()));
            }
            std::fs::read(&target)
                .map_err(|_| djvu_rs::DocError::IndirectResolve(clean.to_string()))
        };

        let mut limits = djvu_rs::resource_limits::ResourceLimits::default();
        limits.max_file_bytes = Some(500 * 1024 * 1024);
        limits.max_pages = Some(10_000);
        limits.max_components = Some(10_000);
        limits.max_page_pixels = Some(25_000_000);
        limits.max_total_pixels = Some(500_000_000);
        limits.max_decoded_bytes = Some(150 * 1024 * 1024);
        limits.max_render_pixels = Some(25_000_000);
        let opts = djvu_rs::resource_limits::ParseOptions {
            limits: Some(limits),
        };

        let parse_result = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
            djvu_rs::DjVuDocument::parse_with_resolver_and_options(
                data,
                Some(safe_resolver),
                &opts,
            )
        }));

        let doc = match parse_result {
            Ok(Ok(d)) => d,
            Ok(Err(e)) => {
                return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!(
                    "РћС€РёР±РєР° РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРіР° DjVu РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°: {e:?}"
                )));
            }
            Err(_) => {
                return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(
                    "РђРІР°СЂРёР№РЅС‹Р№ СЃР±РѕР№ РїСЂРё СЂР°Р·Р±РѕСЂРµ DjVu СЃС‚СЂСѓРєС‚СѓСЂС‹".to_string(),
                ));
            }
        };

        let page_count = doc.page_count();
        if page_count == 0 {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(
                "DjVu РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚ РЅРµ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ РЅРё РѕРґРЅРѕР№ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        let default_title = path
            .file_stem()
            .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
            .unwrap_or("Р”РѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚ DjVu")
            .to_string();

        let meta_opt = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| doc.metadata()))
            .ok()
            .and_then(|r| r.ok())
            .flatten();

        let (title, author) = if let Some(meta) = meta_opt {
            let t = meta
                .title
                .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
                .unwrap_or(default_title);
            let a = meta
                .author
                .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ Р°РІС‚РѕСЂ".to_string());
            (t, a)
        } else {
            (default_title, "РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ Р°РІС‚РѕСЂ".to_string())
        };

        let mut page_names = Vec::with_capacity(page_count);
        let mut page_id_map = HashMap::new();
        if let Ok(dir) = doc.component_directory() {
            let mut page_idx = 0;
            for entry in dir {
                if entry.kind == 'P' {
                    let clean_id = entry.id.trim().to_string();
                    let lower = clean_id.to_lowercase();
                    page_id_map.insert(lower.clone(), page_idx);
                    if let Some(stripped) = lower
                        .strip_suffix(".djvu")
                        .or_else(|| lower.strip_suffix(".djv"))
                    {
                        page_id_map.insert(stripped.to_string(), page_idx);
                    }
                    let dec = percent_decode_djvu(&lower);
                    page_id_map.insert(dec.clone(), page_idx);
                    if let Some(stripped) = dec
                        .strip_suffix(".djvu")
                        .or_else(|| dec.strip_suffix(".djv"))
                    {
                        page_id_map.insert(stripped.to_string(), page_idx);
                    }
                    page_names.push(clean_id);
                    page_idx += 1;
                }
            }
        }

        let bookmarks = doc.bookmarks();
        let toc = convert_djvu_bookmarks(bookmarks, &page_id_map, page_count, 0);

        let chapters = if toc.is_empty() {
            (0..page_count)
                .map(|i| BookChapter {
                    id: Some(format!("page_{}", i + 1)),
                    title: format!("РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {}", i + 1),
                    paragraphs: vec![format!("РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {}", i + 1)],
                })
                .collect()
        } else {
            let mut list = Vec::with_capacity(toc.len());
            for (i, item) in toc.iter().enumerate() {
                list.push(BookChapter {
                    id: item.id.clone(),
                    title: item.title.clone(),
                    paragraphs: vec![format!(
                        "Р Р°Р·РґРµР» {} (СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {})",
                        i + 1,
                        item.chapter_index + 1
                    )],
                });
            }
            list
        };

        Ok(Self {
            metadata: BookMetadata { title, author },
            _path: path.to_path_buf(),
            doc,
            page_count,
            page_names,
            chapters,
            toc,
        })
    }
}

pub(crate) fn percent_decode_djvu(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut result = Vec::new();
    let bytes = s.as_bytes();
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < bytes.len() {
        if bytes[i] == b'%'
            && i + 2 < bytes.len()
            && let Ok(b_val) =
                u8::from_str_radix(std::str::from_utf8(&bytes[i + 1..i + 3]).unwrap_or(""), 16)
        {
            result.push(b_val);
            i += 3;
            continue;
        }
        result.push(bytes[i]);
        i += 1;
    }
    String::from_utf8_lossy(&result).to_string()
}

pub(crate) fn resolve_djvu_bookmark_page(
    url: &str,
    page_id_map: &HashMap<String, usize>,
    total_pages: usize,
) -> usize {
    if total_pages == 0 {
        return 0;
    }
    let trimmed = url.trim();
    if trimmed.is_empty() {
        return 0;
    }

    let decoded = percent_decode_djvu(trimmed);
    let s = decoded.trim();

    let target = s.strip_prefix('#').unwrap_or(s);
    let clean = target.split(['?', '&']).next().unwrap_or(target).trim();
    if clean.is_empty() {
        return 0;
    }

    let clean_lower = clean.to_lowercase();
    if let Some(&idx) = page_id_map.get(&clean_lower) {
        return idx.min(total_pages.saturating_sub(1));
    }
    if let Some(stripped) = clean_lower
        .strip_suffix(".djvu")
        .or_else(|| clean_lower.strip_suffix(".djv"))
    {
        if let Some(&idx) = page_id_map.get(stripped) {
            return idx.min(total_pages.saturating_sub(1));
        }
    }
    if let Some(&idx) = page_id_map
        .get(&format!("{clean_lower}.djvu"))
        .or_else(|| page_id_map.get(&format!("{clean_lower}.djv")))
    {
        return idx.min(total_pages.saturating_sub(1));
    }

    let num_candidate = clean_lower.strip_prefix('+').unwrap_or(&clean_lower);
    if let Ok(p) = num_candidate.parse::<usize>() {
        if p > 0 {
            return (p - 1).min(total_pages.saturating_sub(1));
        }
        return 0;
    }

    let prefixes = ["page", "pg", "p"];
    for prefix in prefixes {
        if let Some(rest) = clean_lower.strip_prefix(prefix) {
            let digits_candidate = rest.trim_start_matches(['_', '-', '+', '=']);
            if let Ok(p) = digits_candidate.parse::<usize>() {
                if p > 0 {
                    return (p - 1).min(total_pages.saturating_sub(1));
                }
                return 0;
            }
        }
    }

    0
}

fn convert_djvu_bookmarks(
    bookmarks: &[djvu_rs::DjVuBookmark],
    page_id_map: &HashMap<String, usize>,
    total_pages: usize,
    depth: usize,
) -> Vec<TocItem> {
    if depth >= 32 {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    bookmarks
        .iter()
        .map(|bm| {
            let chapter_index = resolve_djvu_bookmark_page(&bm.url, page_id_map, total_pages);
            TocItem {
                id: Some(bm.url.clone()),
                title: if bm.title.trim().is_empty() {
                    format!("РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {}", chapter_index + 1)
                } else {
                    bm.title.clone()
                },
                chapter_index,
                children: convert_djvu_bookmarks(
                    &bm.children,
                    page_id_map,
                    total_pages,
                    depth + 1,
                ),
            }
        })
        .collect()
}
