impl PageContent for DjvuDocument {
    fn page_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.page_count
    }

    fn page_name(&self, index: usize) -> &str {
        self.page_names.get(index).map(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("")
    }

    fn page_info(&self, _index: usize) -> Option<&PageInfo> {
        None
    }

    fn load_page_image(&self, index: usize) -> Result<DecodedImage, DocumentError> {
        self.load_page_image_scaled(index, 2.0)
    }

    fn load_page_image_scaled(
        &self,
        index: usize,
        scale: f32,
    ) -> Result<DecodedImage, DocumentError> {
        if index >= self.page_count {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!(
                "РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {} РІС‹С…РѕРґРёС‚ Р·Р° РїСЂРµРґРµР»С‹ РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°",
                index + 1
            )));
        }

        let render_result = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
            let page = self.doc.page(index).map_err(|e| {
                DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° РїРѕР»СѓС‡РµРЅРёСЏ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}: {e:?}", index + 1))
            })?;

            let page_w = page.width() as f32;
            let page_h = page.height() as f32;
            if page_w <= 0.0 || page_h <= 0.0 {
                return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!(
                    "РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {} РёРјРµРµС‚ РЅРµРєРѕСЂСЂРµРєС‚РЅС‹Рµ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂС‹ (0x0)",
                    index + 1
                )));
            }

            let mut render_scale = scale.clamp(0.1, 4.0);
            if page_w * render_scale > 4096.0 {
                render_scale = render_scale.min(4096.0 / page_w);
            }
            if page_h * render_scale > 4096.0 {
                render_scale = render_scale.min(4096.0 / page_h);
            }
            let total_pixels = (page_w * render_scale) * (page_h * render_scale);
            if total_pixels > 25_000_000.0 {
                render_scale = render_scale.min((25_000_000.0 / (page_w * page_h)).sqrt());
            }
            let render_scale = render_scale.clamp(0.05, 4.0);

            let width = ((page_w * render_scale).round() as u32).clamp(1, 4096);
            let height = ((page_h * render_scale).round() as u32).clamp(1, 4096);

            let opts = djvu_rs::djvu_render::RenderOptions {
                width,
                height,
                permissive: true,
                ..Default::default()
            };

            let pixmap = djvu_rs::djvu_render::render_pixmap(page, &opts).map_err(|e| {
                DocumentError::Corrupted(format!(
                    "РћС€РёР±РєР° РѕС‚СЂРёСЃРѕРІРєРё СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {} DjVu: {e:?}",
                    index + 1
                ))
            })?;

            if pixmap.width == 0 || pixmap.height == 0 {
                return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!(
                    "РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {} РёРјРµРµС‚ РЅСѓР»РµРІРѕР№ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ",
                    index + 1
                )));
            }

            let expected_len = (pixmap.width as usize)
                .saturating_mul(pixmap.height as usize)
                .saturating_mul(4);
            if pixmap.data.len() != expected_len {
                return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!(
                    "РќРµРєРѕСЂСЂРµРєС‚РЅС‹Р№ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ Р±СѓС„РµСЂР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}: РѕР¶РёРґР°РµС‚СЃСЏ {}, РїРѕР»СѓС‡РµРЅРѕ {}",
                    index + 1,
                    expected_len,
                    pixmap.data.len()
                )));
            }

            Ok(DecodedImage {
                width: pixmap.width,
                height: pixmap.height,
                rgba: pixmap.data,
            })
        }));

        self.doc.enforce_cache_budget(128 * 1024 * 1024, &[index]);

        match render_result {
            Ok(res) => res,
            Err(_) => Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!(
                "РђРІР°СЂРёР№РЅС‹Р№ СЃР±РѕР№ РґРµРєРѕРґРµСЂР° РїСЂРё РѕС‚СЂРёСЃРѕРІРєРµ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}",
                index + 1
            ))),
        }
    }

    fn extract_page_text(&self, index: usize) -> Option<String> {
        if index >= self.page_count {
            return None;
        }
        let res = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
            let page = self.doc.page(index).ok()?;
            page.text().ok().flatten()
        }));
        res.ok().flatten()
    }

    fn extract_page_text_spans(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Vec<PageTextSpan>> {
        if index >= self.page_count {
            return None;
        }
        let res = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
            let page = self.doc.page(index).ok()?;
            let layer = page.text_layer().ok().flatten()?;
            let page_w = page.width() as f32;
            let page_h = page.height() as f32;
            if page_w <= 0.0 || page_h <= 0.0 {
                return None;
            }

            let mut spans = Vec::new();
            fn collect_spans(
                zone: &djvu_rs::text::TextZone,
                page_w: f32,
                page_h: f32,
                depth: usize,
                spans: &mut Vec<PageTextSpan>,
            ) {
                if depth >= 32 {
                    return;
                }
                if matches!(
                    zone.kind,
                    djvu_rs::text::TextZoneKind::Word | djvu_rs::text::TextZoneKind::Character
                ) || (zone.children.is_empty() && !zone.text.trim().is_empty())
                {
                    let text = zone.text.trim();
                    if !text.is_empty() {
                        let min_x = (zone.rect.x as f32 / page_w).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
                        let min_y = (zone.rect.y as f32 / page_h).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
                        let max_x =
                            ((zone.rect.x + zone.rect.width) as f32 / page_w).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
                        let max_y =
                            ((zone.rect.y + zone.rect.height) as f32 / page_h).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
                        if max_x > min_x && max_y > min_y {
                            spans.push(PageTextSpan {
                                text: text.to_string(),
                                bbox: [min_x, min_y, max_x, max_y],
                            });
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    for child in &zone.children {
                        collect_spans(child, page_w, page_h, depth + 1, spans);
                    }
                }
            }

            for zone in &layer.zones {
                collect_spans(zone, page_w, page_h, 0, &mut spans);
            }

            if spans.is_empty() {
                None
            } else {
                Some(spans)
            }
        }));
        res.ok().flatten()
    }

    fn is_manga(&self) -> bool {
        false
    }
}

pub struct PdfDocument {

impl PdfDocument {
    pub fn from_file<P: AsRef<Path>>(path: P) -> Result<Self, DocumentError> {
        let path_ref = path.as_ref();
        let mut file = File::open(path_ref).map_err(|e| {
            DocumentError::Io(format!(
                "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ PDF С„Р°Р№Р» {}: {e}",
                path_ref.display()
            ))
        })?;

        let file_len = file.metadata().map(|m| m.len()).unwrap_or(0);
        const MAX_PDF_SIZE: u64 = 300 * 1024 * 1024;
        if file_len > MAX_PDF_SIZE {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                "Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ PDF С„Р°Р№Р»Р° РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°РµС‚ РјР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ РґРѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёРјС‹Р№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ 300 РњР‘".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        let mut bytes = Vec::new();
        file.read_to_end(&mut bytes)
            .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Io(format!("РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РїСЂРѕС‡РёС‚Р°С‚СЊ PDF С„Р°Р№Р»: {e}")))?;

        Self::from_bytes(bytes, path_ref)
    }

    pub fn from_bytes(bytes: Vec<u8>, path: &Path) -> Result<Self, DocumentError> {
        if bytes.len() < 4 || !bytes.starts_with(b"%PDF") {
            if let Some(pos) = bytes[..bytes.len().min(1024)]
                .windows(4)
                .position(|w| w == b"%PDF")
            {
                let _ = pos;
            } else {
                return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(
                    "Р¤Р°Р№Р» РЅРµ СЏРІР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РІР°Р»РёРґРЅС‹Рј PDF РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚РѕРј (РѕС‚СЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє %PDF)"
                        .to_string(),
                ));
            }
        }

        let data = Arc::new(bytes);

        let pdf = hayro::hayro_syntax::Pdf::new(data.clone()).map_err(|e| {
            DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРіР° PDF РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°: {e:?}"))
        })?;

        let page_count = pdf.pages().len();
        if page_count == 0 {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(
                "PDF РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚ РЅРµ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ РЅРё РѕРґРЅРѕР№ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        let default_title = path
            .file_stem()
            .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
            .unwrap_or("Р”РѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚ PDF")
            .to_string();

        let meta = pdf.metadata();
        let title = meta
            .title
            .as_ref()
            .map(|t| outline::decode_pdf_string(t))
            .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
            .unwrap_or(default_title);

        let author = meta
            .author
            .as_ref()
            .map(|a| outline::decode_pdf_string(a))
            .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ Р°РІС‚РѕСЂ".to_string());

        let toc = outline::extract_pdf_toc(&pdf, page_count);

        let mut chapters = Vec::with_capacity(toc.len());
        for (i, item) in toc.iter().enumerate() {
            chapters.push(BookChapter {
                id: item.id.clone(),
                title: item.title.clone(),
                paragraphs: vec![format!(
                    "Р Р°Р·РґРµР» {} (СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {})",
                    i + 1,
                    item.chapter_index + 1
                )],
            });
        }

        Ok(Self {
            metadata: BookMetadata { title, author },
            _path: path.to_path_buf(),
            data,
            page_count,
            chapters,
            toc,
        })
    }

impl PageContent for PdfDocument {
    fn page_count(&self) -> usize {

    fn page_name(&self, _index: usize) -> &str {

    }

    fn load_page_image_scaled(
        &self,
        index: usize,
        scale: f32,
    ) -> Result<DecodedImage, DocumentError> {
        if index >= self.page_count {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!(
                "РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {} РІС‹С…РѕРґРёС‚ Р·Р° РїСЂРµРґРµР»С‹ РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°",
                index + 1
            )));
        }

        let pdf = hayro::hayro_syntax::Pdf::new(self.data.clone())
            .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРіР° PDF: {e:?}")))?;

        let pages = pdf.pages();
        let page = pages.get(index).ok_or_else(|| {
            DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {} РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅР°", index + 1))
        })?;

        let interp_settings = hayro::hayro_interpret::InterpreterSettings::default();
        let render_settings = hayro::RenderSettings::default();

        let (page_w, page_h) = page.render_dimensions();
        let page_w = page_w.max(1.0);
        let page_h = page_h.max(1.0);

        let mut render_scale = scale.clamp(0.5, 4.0);
        if page_w * render_scale > 4096.0 {
            render_scale = render_scale.min(4096.0 / page_w);
        }
        if page_h * render_scale > 4096.0 {
            render_scale = render_scale.min(4096.0 / page_h);
        }
        let total_pixels = (page_w * render_scale) * (page_h * render_scale);
        if total_pixels > 25_000_000.0 {
            render_scale = render_scale.min((25_000_000.0 / (page_w * page_h)).sqrt());
        }
        let render_scale = render_scale.max(0.1);

        let pixmap_settings = hayro::PixmapSettings {
            x_scale: render_scale,
            y_scale: render_scale,
            bg_color: hayro::vello_cpu::color::palette::css::WHITE,
        };

        let cache = hayro::RenderCache::new();

        let pixmap = hayro::render(
            page,
            &cache,
            &interp_settings,
            &render_settings,
            &pixmap_settings,
        );

        let width = pixmap.width() as u32;
        let height = pixmap.height() as u32;

        if width == 0 || height == 0 {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!(
                "РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {} РёРјРµРµС‚ РЅСѓР»РµРІРѕР№ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ",
                index + 1
            )));
        }

        let raw_pixels = pixmap.data();
        let total_bytes = (width * height * 4) as usize;
        let mut rgba = vec![0u8; total_bytes];

        for (i, p) in raw_pixels.iter().enumerate() {
            let offset = i * 4;
            if p.a == 255 {
                rgba[offset] = p.r;
                rgba[offset + 1] = p.g;
                rgba[offset + 2] = p.b;
                rgba[offset + 3] = 255;
            } else if p.a == 0 {
                continue;
            } else {
                let inv_a = 255.0 / p.a as f32;
                rgba[offset] = (p.r as f32 * inv_a).min(255.0) as u8;
                rgba[offset + 1] = (p.g as f32 * inv_a).min(255.0) as u8;
                rgba[offset + 2] = (p.b as f32 * inv_a).min(255.0) as u8;
                rgba[offset + 3] = p.a;
            }
        }

        Ok(DecodedImage {
            width,
            height,
            rgba,
        })
    }

    fn extract_page_text(&self, index: usize) -> Option<String> {
        if index >= self.page_count {
            return None;
        }

        let pdf = hayro::hayro_syntax::Pdf::new(self.data.clone()).ok()?;
        let pages = pdf.pages();
        let page = pages.get(index)?;

        text::extract_page_text(page)
    }

    fn extract_page_text_spans(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Vec<PageTextSpan>> {
        if index >= self.page_count {
            return None;
        }

        let pdf = hayro::hayro_syntax::Pdf::new(self.data.clone()).ok()?;
        let pages = pdf.pages();
        let page = pages.get(index)?;

        text::extract_page_spans(page)
    }

    fn is_manga(&self) -> bool {
        false
    }
}

use crate::document::TocItem;
use hayro::hayro_syntax::Pdf;
use hayro::hayro_syntax::object::{Array, Dict, Object, ObjectIdentifier, String as PdfString};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

pub fn extract_pdf_toc(pdf: &Pdf, page_count: usize) -> Vec<TocItem> {
    if page_count == 0 {
        return Vec::new();
    }

    let xref = pdf.xref();
    let root = match xref.get::<Dict<'_>>(xref.root_id()) {
        Some(r) => r,
        None => return build_fallback_toc(page_count),
    };

    let outlines = match root.get::<Dict<'_>>("Outlines") {
        Some(o) => o,
        None => return build_fallback_toc(page_count),
    };

    let mut page_id_to_index: HashMap<ObjectIdentifier, usize> = HashMap::with_capacity(page_count);
    for (i, p) in pdf.pages().iter().enumerate() {
        if let Some(id) = p.raw().obj_id() {
            page_id_to_index.insert(id, i);
        }
    }

    let mut named_dests: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();
    if let Some(dests) = root.get::<Dict<'_>>("Dests") {
        for (name, _) in dests.entries() {
            let key = decode_pdf_string(name.as_ref());
            if let Some(arr) = dests.get::<Array<'_>>(name.as_ref()) {
                if let Some(page) = resolve_dest_array(&arr, &page_id_to_index, page_count) {
                    named_dests.insert(key, page);
                }
            } else if let Some(dict) = dests.get::<Dict<'_>>(name.as_ref()) {
                if let Some(arr) = dict.get::<Array<'_>>("D") {
                    if let Some(page) = resolve_dest_array(&arr, &page_id_to_index, page_count) {
                        named_dests.insert(key, page);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    let mut items = Vec::new();
    let mut cur = outlines.get::<Dict<'_>>("First");
    let mut visited = HashSet::new();

    while let Some(item_dict) = cur {
        if let Some(id) = item_dict.obj_id()
            && !visited.insert(id)
        {
            break;
        }

        if let Some(toc_item) = parse_outline_item(
            &item_dict,
            &page_id_to_index,
            &named_dests,
            page_count,
            &mut visited,
            0,
        ) {
            items.push(toc_item);
        }

        cur = item_dict.get::<Dict<'_>>("Next");
    }

    if items.is_empty() {
        build_fallback_toc(page_count)
    } else {
        items
    }
}

fn parse_outline_item(
    item_dict: &Dict<'_>,
    page_id_to_index: &HashMap<ObjectIdentifier, usize>,
    named_dests: &HashMap<String, usize>,
    page_count: usize,
    visited: &mut HashSet<ObjectIdentifier>,
    depth: usize,
) -> Option<TocItem> {
    if depth > 25 {
        return None;
    }

    let title_bytes = item_dict.get::<PdfString<'_>>("Title")?;
    let title = decode_pdf_string(title_bytes.as_bytes());
    if title.trim().is_empty() {
        return None;
    }

    let explicit_target = resolve_destination(item_dict, page_id_to_index, named_dests, page_count);

    let mut children = Vec::new();
    let mut cur_child = item_dict.get::<Dict<'_>>("First");
    while let Some(child_dict) = cur_child {
        if let Some(id) = child_dict.obj_id()
            && !visited.insert(id)
        {
            break;
        }
        if let Some(child_item) = parse_outline_item(
            &child_dict,
            page_id_to_index,
            named_dests,
            page_count,
            visited,
            depth + 1,
        ) {
            children.push(child_item);
        }
        cur_child = child_dict.get::<Dict<'_>>("Next");
    }

    let target_page = explicit_target
        .or_else(|| children.first().map(|c| c.chapter_index))
        .unwrap_or(0)
        .min(page_count.saturating_sub(1));

    Some(TocItem {
        id: Some(format!("page_{target_page}")),
        title,
        chapter_index: target_page,
        children,
    })
}

fn resolve_destination(
    item_dict: &Dict<'_>,
    page_id_to_index: &HashMap<ObjectIdentifier, usize>,
    named_dests: &HashMap<String, usize>,
    page_count: usize,
) -> Option<usize> {
    if let Some(dest_arr) = item_dict.get::<Array<'_>>("Dest")
        && let Some(page) = resolve_dest_array(&dest_arr, page_id_to_index, page_count)
    {
        return Some(page);
    }

    if let Some(dest_str) = item_dict.get::<PdfString<'_>>("Dest") {
        let key = decode_pdf_string(dest_str.as_bytes());
        if let Some(&page) = named_dests.get(&key) {
            return Some(page);
        }
    }

    if let Some(a_dict) = item_dict.get::<Dict<'_>>("A") {
        if let Some(d_arr) = a_dict.get::<Array<'_>>("D")
            && let Some(page) = resolve_dest_array(&d_arr, page_id_to_index, page_count)
        {
            return Some(page);
        }
        if let Some(d_str) = a_dict.get::<PdfString<'_>>("D") {
            let key = decode_pdf_string(d_str.as_bytes());
            if let Some(&page) = named_dests.get(&key) {
                return Some(page);
            }
        }
    }

    None
}

pub fn resolve_dest_array(
    arr: &Array<'_>,
    page_id_to_index: &HashMap<ObjectIdentifier, usize>,
    page_count: usize,
) -> Option<usize> {
    let first = arr.iter::<Object<'_>>().next()?;
    match first {
        Object::Dict(d) => {
            if let Some(id) = d.obj_id()
                && let Some(&page_idx) = page_id_to_index.get(&id)
            {
                return Some(page_idx.min(page_count.saturating_sub(1)));
            }
        }
        Object::Number(n) => {
            let i = n.as_i64();
            if i >= 0 {
                return Some((i as usize).min(page_count.saturating_sub(1)));
            }
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    None
}

pub fn build_fallback_toc(page_count: usize) -> Vec<TocItem> {
    (0..page_count)
        .map(|i| TocItem {
            id: Some(format!("page_{}", i + 1)),
            title: format!("РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {}", i + 1),
            chapter_index: i,
            children: Vec::new(),
        })
        .collect()
}

pub fn decode_pdf_string(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    if bytes.is_empty() {
        return String::new();
    }

    if bytes.len() >= 2 && bytes[0] == 0xFE && bytes[1] == 0xFF {
        let u16_chars: Vec<u16> = bytes[2..]
            .as_chunks::<2>()
            .0
            .iter()
            .map(|chunk| u16::from_be_bytes([chunk[0], chunk[1]]))
            .collect();
        if let Ok(s) = String::from_utf16(&u16_chars) {
            return s;
        }
    }

    if let Ok(s) = std::str::from_utf8(bytes)
        && s.chars().any(|c| c as u32 > 127)
    {
        return s.to_string();
    }

    encoding_rs::WINDOWS_1252
        .decode_without_bom_handling(bytes)
        .0
        .to_string()
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\Almas\Documents\QuietReader\src\document\pdf\text.rs ===
use crate::document::PageTextSpan;
use hayro::hayro_interpret::TransformExt;
use hayro::hayro_syntax::page::Page;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct GlyphEntry {
    pub c: char,
    pub x: f64,
    pub y: f64,
    pub font_size: f64,
    pub width: f64,
}

struct SpanExtractDevice {
    glyphs: Vec<GlyphEntry>,
}

impl<'a> hayro::hayro_interpret::Device<'a> for SpanExtractDevice {
    fn draw_path(
        &mut self,
        _: &hayro::kurbo::BezPath,
        _: hayro::hayro_interpret::DrawProps<'a>,
        _: &hayro::hayro_interpret::DrawMode,
    ) {
    }
    fn push_clip_path(&mut self, _: &hayro::hayro_interpret::ClipPath) {}
    fn push_transparency_group(
        &mut self,
        _: f32,
        _: Option<hayro::hayro_interpret::SoftMask<'a>>,
        _: hayro::hayro_interpret::BlendMode,
    ) {
    }
    fn draw_image(
        &mut self,
        _: hayro::hayro_interpret::Image<'a, '_>,
        _: hayro::hayro_interpret::ImageDrawProps<'a>,
    ) {
    }
    fn pop_clip(&mut self) {}
    fn pop_transparency_group(&mut self) {}

    fn draw_glyph_run(
        &mut self,
        glyph_run: &hayro::hayro_interpret::font::GlyphRun<'_, 'a>,
        props: hayro::hayro_interpret::DrawProps<'a>,
        _draw_mode: &hayro::hayro_interpret::DrawMode,
    ) {
        let run_transform = props.transform;
        let glyphs = glyph_run.glyphs();
        for (i, g) in glyphs.iter().enumerate() {
            let glyph_transform = run_transform * g.transform();
            let coeffs = glyph_transform.as_coeffs();
            let x = coeffs[4];
            let y = coeffs[5];

            if !x.is_finite() || !y.is_finite() {
                continue;
            }

            let scale_x = (coeffs[0] * coeffs[0] + coeffs[1] * coeffs[1]).sqrt();
            let scale_y = (coeffs[2] * coeffs[2] + coeffs[3] * coeffs[3]).sqrt();
            let basis_scale = if scale_y > 0.001 { scale_y } else { scale_x };
            let font_size = if basis_scale < 0.2 {
                basis_scale * 1000.0
            } else {
                basis_scale
            }
            .clamp(6.0, 140.0);

            let width = if i + 1 < glyphs.len() {
                let next_coeffs = (run_transform * glyphs[i + 1].transform()).as_coeffs();
                let dx = (next_coeffs[4] - x).abs();
                let dy = (next_coeffs[5] - y).abs();
                let step = if dx > dy { dx } else { dy };
                if step > 0.0 && step < font_size * 2.5 {
                    step
                } else {
                    font_size * 0.52
                }
            } else {
                font_size * 0.52
            };

            if let Some(unicode) = g.as_unicode() {
                match unicode {
                    hayro::hayro_interpret::hayro_cmap::BfString::Char(c) => {
                        if !c.is_control() || c == ' ' || c == '\t' {
                            self.glyphs.push(GlyphEntry {
                                c,
                                x,
                                y,
                                font_size,
                                width,
                            });
                        }
                    }
                    hayro::hayro_interpret::hayro_cmap::BfString::String(s) => {
                        for c in s.chars() {
                            if !c.is_control() || c == ' ' || c == '\t' {
                                self.glyphs.push(GlyphEntry {
                                    c,
                                    x,
                                    y,
                                    font_size,
                                    width,
                                });
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

pub fn extract_page_spans(page: &Page<'_>) -> Option<Vec<PageTextSpan>> {
    let (width, height) = page.render_dimensions();
    if width <= 0.0 || height <= 0.0 {
        return None;
    }

    let interp_cache = hayro::hayro_interpret::InterpreterCache::new();
    let interp_settings = hayro::hayro_interpret::InterpreterSettings::default();

    let mut state = hayro::hayro_interpret::Context::new(
        page.initial_transform(true).to_kurbo(),
        hayro::kurbo::Rect::new(0.0, 0.0, width as f64, height as f64),
        &interp_cache,
        page.xref(),
        interp_settings,
    );

    let mut device = SpanExtractDevice { glyphs: Vec::new() };
    hayro::hayro_interpret::interpret_page(page, &mut state, &mut device);

    let spans = group_and_sort_glyphs(device.glyphs, width, height);
    if spans.is_empty() {
        None
    } else {
        Some(spans)
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct RawSpan {
    pub text: String,
    pub min_x: f64,
    pub max_x: f64,
    pub min_y: f64,
    pub max_y: f64,
}

pub fn group_and_sort_glyphs(
    mut glyphs: Vec<GlyphEntry>,
    width: f32,
    height: f32,
) -> Vec<PageTextSpan> {
    if glyphs.is_empty() || width <= 0.0 || height <= 0.0 {
        return Vec::new();
    }

    glyphs.sort_by(|a, b| a.y.total_cmp(&b.y).then_with(|| a.x.total_cmp(&b.x)));

    let mut lines: Vec<Vec<GlyphEntry>> = Vec::new();
    let mut current_line: Vec<GlyphEntry> = Vec::new();
    let mut line_y = 0.0;
    let mut line_h = 12.0;

    for g in glyphs {
        if current_line.is_empty() {
            line_y = g.y;
            line_h = g.font_size;
            current_line.push(g);
        } else {
            let dy = (g.y - line_y).abs();
            let threshold = (line_h + g.font_size) * 0.45;
            if dy <= threshold {
                current_line.push(g);
            } else {
                lines.push(std::mem::take(&mut current_line));
                line_y = g.y;
                line_h = g.font_size;
                current_line.push(g);
            }
        }
    }
    if !current_line.is_empty() {
        lines.push(current_line);
    }

    let mut raw_spans: Vec<RawSpan> = Vec::new();

    for mut line in lines {
        line.sort_by(|a, b| a.x.total_cmp(&b.x));

        let mut seg_text = String::new();
        let mut min_x = f64::MAX;
        let mut max_x = f64::MIN;
        let mut min_y = f64::MAX;
        let mut max_y = f64::MIN;
        let mut prev_x = 0.0;
        let mut prev_w = 0.0;
        let mut first = true;

        for g in line {
            if first {
                first = false;
            } else {
                let gap = g.x - (prev_x + prev_w);
                if gap > (g.font_size * 3.5).max(30.0) {
                    let trimmed = seg_text.trim();
                    if !trimmed.is_empty() && max_x > min_x && max_y > min_y {
                        raw_spans.push(RawSpan {
                            text: trimmed.to_string(),
                            min_x,
                            max_x,
                            min_y,
                            max_y,
                        });
                    }
                    seg_text.clear();
                    min_x = f64::MAX;
                    max_x = f64::MIN;
                    min_y = f64::MAX;
                    max_y = f64::MIN;
                } else if gap > g.font_size * 0.22 && !seg_text.ends_with(' ') {
                    seg_text.push(' ');
                }
            }

            seg_text.push(g.c);
            min_x = min_x.min(g.x);
            max_x = max_x.max(g.x + g.width);
            min_y = min_y.min(g.y - g.font_size * 0.88);
            max_y = max_y.max(g.y + g.font_size * 0.24);

            prev_x = g.x;
            prev_w = g.width;
        }

        let trimmed = seg_text.trim();
        if !trimmed.is_empty() && max_x > min_x && max_y > min_y {
            raw_spans.push(RawSpan {
                text: trimmed.to_string(),
                min_x,
                max_x,
                min_y,
                max_y,
            });
        }
    }

    let ordered = order_spans_reading_flow(raw_spans, width as f64, height as f64);
    let mut spans = Vec::with_capacity(ordered.len());
    for s in ordered {
        let norm_x0 = (s.min_x / width as f64).clamp(0.0, 1.0) as f32;
        let norm_y0 = (s.min_y / height as f64).clamp(0.0, 1.0) as f32;
        let norm_x1 = (s.max_x / width as f64).clamp(0.0, 1.0) as f32;
        let norm_y1 = (s.max_y / height as f64).clamp(0.0, 1.0) as f32;
        if norm_x1 > norm_x0 && norm_y1 > norm_y0 {
            spans.push(PageTextSpan {
                text: s.text,
                bbox: [norm_x0, norm_y0, norm_x1, norm_y1],
            });
        }
    }

    spans
}

pub fn order_spans_reading_flow(
    mut raw_spans: Vec<RawSpan>,
    width: f64,
    height: f64,
) -> Vec<RawSpan> {
    if raw_spans.len() < 4 {
        raw_spans.sort_by(|a, b| a.min_y.total_cmp(&b.min_y).then_with(|| a.min_x.total_cmp(&b.min_x)));
        return raw_spans;
    }

    let mid_x = width * 0.5;
    let gutter_margin = width * 0.02;
    let header_limit = height * 0.18;
    let footer_limit = height * 0.85;

    let mut left_count = 0;
    let mut right_count = 0;
    let mut middle_crossing_count = 0;

    for s in &raw_spans {
        if s.min_y >= header_limit && s.max_y <= footer_limit {
            if s.max_x < mid_x - gutter_margin {
                left_count += 1;
            } else if s.min_x > mid_x + gutter_margin {
                right_count += 1;
            } else {
                middle_crossing_count += 1;
            }
        }
    }

    if left_count >= 3 && right_count >= 3 && middle_crossing_count == 0 {
        let mut headers = Vec::new();
        let mut col_left = Vec::new();
        let mut col_right = Vec::new();
        let mut footers = Vec::new();

        for s in raw_spans {
            if s.max_y <= header_limit {
                headers.push(s);
            } else if s.min_y >= footer_limit {
                footers.push(s);
            } else if s.max_x <= mid_x {
                col_left.push(s);
            } else {
                col_right.push(s);
            }
        }

        headers.sort_by(|a, b| a.min_y.total_cmp(&b.min_y).then_with(|| a.min_x.total_cmp(&b.min_x)));
        col_left.sort_by(|a, b| a.min_y.total_cmp(&b.min_y).then_with(|| a.min_x.total_cmp(&b.min_x)));
        col_right.sort_by(|a, b| a.min_y.total_cmp(&b.min_y).then_with(|| a.min_x.total_cmp(&b.min_x)));
        footers.sort_by(|a, b| a.min_y.total_cmp(&b.min_y).then_with(|| a.min_x.total_cmp(&b.min_x)));

        let mut result = Vec::with_capacity(headers.len() + col_left.len() + col_right.len() + footers.len());
        result.extend(headers);
        result.extend(col_left);
        result.extend(col_right);
        result.extend(footers);
        result
    } else {
        raw_spans.sort_by(|a, b| a.min_y.total_cmp(&b.min_y).then_with(|| a.min_x.total_cmp(&b.min_x)));
        raw_spans
    }
}

pub fn extract_page_text(page: &Page<'_>) -> Option<String> {
    let spans = extract_page_spans(page)?;
    let lines: Vec<String> = spans.into_iter().map(|s| s.text).collect();
    if lines.is_empty() {
        None
    } else {
        Some(lines.join("\n"))
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\Almas\Documents\QuietReader\src\document\comic.rs ===
use std::fs::File;
use std::io::Read;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use zip::ZipArchive;

use crate::document::{
    BookChapter, BookMetadata, DecodedImage, Document, DocumentError, DocumentViewKind, HasToc,
    PageContent, PageInfo, TocItem,
};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ComicArchiveKind {
    Zip,
    Rar,
    SevenZ,
    Folder,
}

pub struct ComicDocument {
    metadata: BookMetadata,
    path: PathBuf,
    kind: ComicArchiveKind,
    pages: Vec<PageInfo>,
    chapters: Vec<BookChapter>,
    toc: Vec<TocItem>,
    is_manga: bool,
}

impl ComicDocument {
    pub fn from_path<P: AsRef<Path>>(path: P) -> Result<Self, DocumentError> {
        let path_ref = path.as_ref();
        if !path_ref.exists() {
            return Err(DocumentError::Io(format!(
                "Р¤Р°Р№Р» РёР»Рё РєР°С‚Р°Р»РѕРі РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ: {}",
                path_ref.display()
            )));
        }

        let kind = if path_ref.is_dir() {
            ComicArchiveKind::Folder
        } else {
            let ext = path_ref
                .extension()
                .and_then(|e| e.to_str())
                .map(|e| e.to_lowercase())
                .unwrap_or_default();

            match ext.as_str() {
                "cbz" | "zip" => ComicArchiveKind::Zip,
                "cbr" | "rar" => ComicArchiveKind::Rar,
                "cb7" | "7z" => ComicArchiveKind::SevenZ,
                _ => {
                    let mut magic = [0u8; 8];
                    let n = File::open(path_ref)
                        .and_then(|mut f| f.read(&mut magic))
                        .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Io(format!("{}: {e}", path_ref.display())))?;
                    if n >= 4 && &magic[0..4] == b"PK\x03\x04" {
                        ComicArchiveKind::Zip
                    } else if n >= 7 && &magic[0..7] == b"Rar!\x1a\x07\x00" {
                        ComicArchiveKind::Rar
                    } else if n >= 6 && &magic[0..6] == b"7z\xbc\xaf\x27\x1c" {
                        ComicArchiveKind::SevenZ
                    } else {
                        return Err(DocumentError::Unsupported(format!(
                            "РќРµРїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РёРІР°РµРјС‹Р№ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ РєРѕРјРёРєСЃР°: {}",
                            path_ref.display()
                        )));
                    }
                }
            }
        };

        let (mut pages, comic_info_xml) = match kind {
            ComicArchiveKind::Folder => scan_folder(path_ref)?,
            ComicArchiveKind::Zip => scan_zip(path_ref)?,
            ComicArchiveKind::Rar => scan_rar(path_ref)?,
            ComicArchiveKind::SevenZ => scan_sevenz(path_ref)?,
        };

        if pages.is_empty() {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(
                "Р’ Р°СЂС…РёРІРµ/РїР°РїРєРµ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ РЅРё РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РёРІР°РµРјРѕРіРѕ РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ (JPEG, PNG, WEBP, GIF)"
                    .to_string(),
            ));
        }

        const MAX_PAGES_LIMIT: usize = 5000;
        if pages.len() > MAX_PAGES_LIMIT {
            pages.truncate(MAX_PAGES_LIMIT);
        }

        pages.sort_by(|a, b| natural_cmp(&a.archive_path, &b.archive_path));

        let default_title = path_ref
            .file_stem()
            .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
            .unwrap_or("РљРѕРјРёРєСЃ")
            .to_string();

        let mut title = default_title;
        let mut author = "РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ Р°РІС‚РѕСЂ".to_string();
        let mut is_manga = false;

        let path_lower = path_ref.to_string_lossy().to_lowercase();
        if path_lower.contains("manga") || path_lower.contains("РјР°РЅРіР°") {
            is_manga = true;
        }

        if let Some(xml_bytes) = comic_info_xml
            && let Ok(info) = parse_comic_info(&xml_bytes)
        {
            if let Some(t) = info.title
                && !t.trim().is_empty()
            {
                title = t;
            }
            if let Some(w) = info.writer
                && !w.trim().is_empty()
            {
                author = w;
            }
            if let Some(m) = info.is_manga {
                is_manga = m;
            }
        }

        let (chapters, toc) = build_comic_chapters(&pages, &title);

        Ok(Self {
            metadata: BookMetadata { title, author },
            path: path_ref.to_path_buf(),
            kind,
            pages,
            chapters,
            toc,
            is_manga,
        })
    }
}

impl HasToc for ComicDocument {
    fn toc(&self) -> &[TocItem] {
        &self.toc
    }
}

impl Document for ComicDocument {
    fn metadata(&self) -> &BookMetadata {
        &self.metadata
    }

    fn chapters(&self) -> &[BookChapter] {
        &self.chapters
    }

    fn toc(&self) -> &[TocItem] {
        &self.toc
    }

    fn format_name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "Comic"
    }

    fn has_custom_toc(&self) -> bool {
        self.toc.len() > 1
    }

    fn view_kind(&self) -> DocumentViewKind {
        DocumentViewKind::Comic
    }

    fn page_content(&self) -> Option<&dyn PageContent> {
        Some(self)
    }

    fn toc_capability(&self) -> Option<&dyn HasToc> {
        Some(self)
    }
}

impl PageContent for ComicDocument {
    fn page_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.pages.len()
    }

    fn page_name(&self, index: usize) -> &str {
        self.pages
            .get(index)
            .map(|p| p.display_name.as_str())
            .unwrap_or("")
    }

    fn page_info(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&PageInfo> {
        self.pages.get(index)
    }

    fn load_page_image(&self, index: usize) -> Result<DecodedImage, DocumentError> {
        let page = self.pages.get(index).ok_or_else(|| {
            DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° {} РІС‹С…РѕРґРёС‚ Р·Р° РїСЂРµРґРµР»С‹", index + 1))
        })?;
        let max_single = crate::document::MAX_SINGLE_ENTRY_BYTES;
        let max_total = crate::document::MAX_TOTAL_UNPACK_BYTES;

        let bytes = match self.kind {
            ComicArchiveKind::Folder => {
                if !is_safe_path(&page.archive_path) {
                    return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted("РќРµР±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅС‹Р№ РїСѓС‚СЊ".to_string()));
                }
                let full = self.path.join(&page.archive_path);
                let meta = std::fs::metadata(&full)?;
                if meta.len() > max_single {
                    return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                        "РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚ 50 РњР‘".to_string(),
                    ));
                }
                std::fs::read(&full)?
            }
            ComicArchiveKind::Zip => {
                let mut zip = ZipArchive::new(File::open(&self.path)?)
                    .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° ZIP: {e}")))?;
                let entry = zip
                    .by_name(&page.archive_path)
                    .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РќРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ РІ ZIP: {e}")))?;
                if entry.size() > max_single {
                    return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                        "РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚ 50 РњР‘".to_string(),
                    ));
                }
                let mut total = 0u64;
                crate::document::read_bounded(entry, max_single, &mut total, max_total)?
            }
            ComicArchiveKind::Rar => {
                let mut archive = unrar_rs::RarArchive::open(File::open(&self.path)?)
                    .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° RAR: {e}")))?;
                let handle = archive
                    .by_name(&page.archive_path)
                    .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РќРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ РІ RAR: {e}")))?;
                let mut total = 0u64;
                crate::document::read_bounded(handle, max_single, &mut total, max_total)?
            }
            ComicArchiveKind::SevenZ => {
                let (mut found_bytes, mut err_msg, mut total) = (None, None, 0u64);
                let target = &page.archive_path;
                sevenz_rust::decompress_file_with_extract_fn(&self.path, "", |entry, reader, _| {
                    if entry.name() == target {
                        match crate::document::read_bounded(
                            reader, max_single, &mut total, max_total,
                        ) {
                            Ok(buf) => found_bytes = Some(buf),
                            Err(e) => err_msg = Some(e),
                        }
                        Ok(false)
                    } else {
                        Ok(true)
                    }
                })
                .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° 7z: {e}")))?;
                if let Some(e) = err_msg {
                    return Err(e);
                }
                found_bytes.ok_or_else(|| DocumentError::Corrupted("РќРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ РІ 7z".to_string()))?
            }
        };

        decode_image_bytes(&bytes)
    }

    fn is_manga(&self) -> bool {
        self.is_manga
    }
}

fn decode_image_bytes(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<DecodedImage, DocumentError> {
    let dim_reader = image::ImageReader::new(std::io::Cursor::new(bytes))
        .with_guessed_format()
        .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚Р° РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ: {e}")))?;
    if let Ok((w, h)) = dim_reader.into_dimensions() {
        if w == 0 || h == 0 {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted("РР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёРµ РёРјРµРµС‚ РЅСѓР»РµРІРѕР№ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ".to_string()));
        }
        if w > 8192 || h > 16384 || (w as u64) * (h as u64) > 25_000_000 {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(format!(
                "Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂС‹ РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ ({w}x{h}) РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°СЋС‚ РґРѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёРјС‹Р№ Р»РёРјРёС‚"
            )));
        }
    }
    let mut reader = image::ImageReader::new(std::io::Cursor::new(bytes))
        .with_guessed_format()
        .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚Р° РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ: {e}")))?;
    let mut limits = image::Limits::default();
    limits.max_image_width = Some(8192);
    limits.max_image_height = Some(16384);
    limits.max_alloc = Some(150 * 1024 * 1024);
    reader.limits(limits);
    let img = reader.decode().map_err(|e| {
        DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РґРµРєРѕРґРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёРµ: {e}"))
    })?;
    let img = if img.width() > 4096 || img.height() > 4096 {
        img.thumbnail(4096, 4096)
    } else {
        img
    };
    let rgba = img.to_rgba8();
    Ok(DecodedImage {
        width: rgba.width(),
        height: rgba.height(),
        rgba: rgba.into_raw(),
    })
}

pub fn is_supported_image(name: &str) -> bool {
    let filename = Path::new(name)
        .file_name()
        .and_then(|f| f.to_str())
        .unwrap_or("");
    if filename.starts_with('.')
        || filename.eq_ignore_ascii_case("thumbs.db")
        || name.contains("__MACOSX")
    {
        return false;
    }
    let ext = Path::new(name)
        .extension()
        .and_then(|e| e.to_str())
        .map(|e| e.to_lowercase())
        .unwrap_or_default();

    matches!(
        ext.as_str(),
        "jpg" | "jpeg" | "png" | "webp" | "gif" | "bmp"
    )
}

fn is_safe_path(p: &str) -> bool {
    crate::document::is_safe_archive_path(p)
}

fn is_comic_info_xml(p: &Path) -> bool {
    p.file_name()
        .map(|f| f.to_string_lossy().eq_ignore_ascii_case("comicinfo.xml"))
        .unwrap_or(false)
}

fn make_page_info(archive_path: String, path_obj: &Path) -> PageInfo {
    let display_name = path_obj
        .file_name()
        .map(|f| f.to_string_lossy().to_string())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| archive_path.clone());
    let chapter_title = path_obj
        .parent()
        .and_then(|p| p.file_name())
        .map(|f| f.to_string_lossy().to_string());
    PageInfo {
        archive_path,
        display_name,
        chapter_title,
    }
}

fn scan_folder(folder: &Path) -> Result<(Vec<PageInfo>, Option<Vec<u8>>), DocumentError> {
    let mut pages = Vec::new();
    let mut comic_info = None;

    fn visit_dir(
        root: &Path,
        current: &Path,
        pages: &mut Vec<PageInfo>,
        comic_info: &mut Option<Vec<u8>>,
    ) {
        if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(current) {
            for entry in entries.flatten() {
                if pages.len() >= crate::document::MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES {
                    break;
                }
                let path = entry.path();
                if path.is_dir() {
                    visit_dir(root, &path, pages, comic_info);
                } else if path.is_file() {
                    let rel = path.strip_prefix(root).unwrap_or(&path);
                    let rel_str = rel.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/");

                    if is_comic_info_xml(rel) {
                        if let Ok(bytes) = std::fs::read(&path) {
                            *comic_info = Some(bytes);
                        }
                    } else if is_supported_image(&rel_str) && is_safe_path(&rel_str) {
                        pages.push(make_page_info(rel_str, rel));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    visit_dir(folder, folder, &mut pages, &mut comic_info);
    Ok((pages, comic_info))
}

fn scan_zip(path: &Path) -> Result<(Vec<PageInfo>, Option<Vec<u8>>), DocumentError> {
    let file =
        File::open(path).map_err(|e| DocumentError::Io(format!("РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ ZIP: {e}")))?;
    let mut zip = ZipArchive::new(file)
        .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРіР° ZIP: {e}")))?;

    if zip.len() > crate::document::MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES {
        return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
            "Р’ Р°СЂС…РёРІРµ РїСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚ РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІР° С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ (10 000)".to_string(),
        ));
    }

    let mut pages = Vec::new();
    let mut comic_info = None;
    let mut total_unpacked: u64 = 0;

    for i in 0..zip.len() {
        if let Ok(entry) = zip.by_index(i) {
            let name = entry.name().to_string();
            if !is_safe_path(&name) {
                return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!(
                    "РћР±РЅР°СЂСѓР¶РµРЅ РЅРµР±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅС‹Р№ РїСѓС‚СЊ РІ Р°СЂС…РёРІРµ: {name}"
                )));
            }
            if entry.size() > crate::document::MAX_SINGLE_ENTRY_BYTES {
                return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(format!(
                    "Р¤Р°Р№Р» {name} РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°РµС‚ Р»РёРјРёС‚ 50 РњР‘"
                )));
            }
            total_unpacked = total_unpacked.saturating_add(entry.size());
            if total_unpacked > crate::document::MAX_TOTAL_UNPACK_BYTES {
                return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                    "РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ РѕР±С‰РёР№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ СЂР°СЃРїР°РєРѕРІРєРё Р°СЂС…РёРІР° (500 РњР‘)".to_string(),
                ));
            }
            if name.ends_with('/') || name.ends_with('\\') {
                continue;
            }
            let path_obj = Path::new(&name);
            if is_comic_info_xml(path_obj) {
                let mut total_xml = 0u64;
                if let Ok(bytes) = crate::document::read_bounded(
                    entry,
                    5 * 1024 * 1024,
                    &mut total_xml,
                    5 * 1024 * 1024,
                ) {
                    comic_info = Some(bytes);
                }
            } else if is_supported_image(&name) {
                pages.push(make_page_info(name.clone(), path_obj));
            }
        }
    }

    Ok((pages, comic_info))
}

fn scan_rar(path: &Path) -> Result<(Vec<PageInfo>, Option<Vec<u8>>), DocumentError> {
    let file =
        File::open(path).map_err(|e| DocumentError::Io(format!("РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ RAR: {e}")))?;
    let mut archive = unrar_rs::RarArchive::open(file)
        .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРіР° RAR: {e}")))?;

    let mut pages = Vec::new();
    let mut comic_info_entry_name = None;

    let entry_names: Vec<String> = archive
        .entries()
        .take(crate::document::MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES + 1)
        .map(|e| e.name.clone())
        .collect();
    if entry_names.len() > crate::document::MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES {
        return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
            "Р’ RAR-Р°СЂС…РёРІРµ РїСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚ РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІР° С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ (10 000)".to_string(),
        ));
    }

    for raw_name in entry_names {
        let name = raw_name.replace('\\', "/");
        if !is_safe_path(&name) {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!(
                "РћР±РЅР°СЂСѓР¶РµРЅ РЅРµР±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅС‹Р№ РїСѓС‚СЊ РІ RAR-Р°СЂС…РёРІРµ: {name}"
            )));
        }

        let path_obj = Path::new(&name);
        if is_comic_info_xml(path_obj) {
            comic_info_entry_name = Some(raw_name);
        } else if is_supported_image(&name) {
            pages.push(make_page_info(raw_name, path_obj));
        }
    }

    let mut comic_info = None;
    if let Some(ci_name) = comic_info_entry_name
        && let Ok(handle) = archive.by_name(&ci_name)
    {
        let mut bytes = Vec::new();
        if handle.copy_to(&mut bytes).is_ok() && bytes.len() <= 5 * 1024 * 1024 {
            comic_info = Some(bytes);
        }
    }

    Ok((pages, comic_info))
}

fn scan_sevenz(path: &Path) -> Result<(Vec<PageInfo>, Option<Vec<u8>>), DocumentError> {
    let mut pages = Vec::new();
    let mut comic_info = None;
    let mut entry_count = 0usize;
    let mut total_unpacked = 0u64;
    let mut sec_err: Option<DocumentError> = None;

    sevenz_rust::decompress_file_with_extract_fn(path, "", |entry, reader, _| {
        entry_count += 1;
        if entry_count > crate::document::MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES {
            sec_err = Some(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                "Р’ 7z-Р°СЂС…РёРІРµ РїСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚ РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІР° С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ (10 000)".to_string(),
            ));
            return Ok(false);
        }
        let name = entry.name().replace('\\', "/");
        if !is_safe_path(&name) {
            sec_err = Some(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!(
                "РћР±РЅР°СЂСѓР¶РµРЅ РЅРµР±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅС‹Р№ РїСѓС‚СЊ РІ 7z-Р°СЂС…РёРІРµ: {name}"
            )));
            return Ok(false);
        }
        if entry.size() > crate::document::MAX_SINGLE_ENTRY_BYTES {
            sec_err = Some(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(format!(
                "Р¤Р°Р№Р» {name} РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°РµС‚ Р»РёРјРёС‚ 50 РњР‘"
            )));
            return Ok(false);
        }
        total_unpacked = total_unpacked.saturating_add(entry.size());
        if total_unpacked > crate::document::MAX_TOTAL_UNPACK_BYTES {
            sec_err = Some(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                "РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ РѕР±С‰РёР№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ СЂР°СЃРїР°РєРѕРІРєРё 7z-Р°СЂС…РёРІР° (500 РњР‘)".to_string(),
            ));
            return Ok(false);
        }

        let path_obj = Path::new(&name);
        if is_comic_info_xml(path_obj) {
            let mut total_xml = 0u64;
            let limit = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
            if let Ok(b) = crate::document::read_bounded(reader, limit, &mut total_xml, limit) {
                comic_info = Some(b);
            }
        } else if is_supported_image(&name) {
            pages.push(make_page_info(entry.name().to_string(), path_obj));
        }
        Ok(true)
    })
    .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° С‡С‚РµРЅРёСЏ 7z Р°СЂС…РёРІР°: {e}")))?;

    if let Some(err) = sec_err {
        return Err(err);
    }
    Ok((pages, comic_info))
}

fn build_comic_chapters(
    pages: &[PageInfo],
    default_title: &str,
) -> (Vec<BookChapter>, Vec<TocItem>) {
    let mut groups: Vec<(String, usize, usize)> = Vec::new();
    for (idx, page) in pages.iter().enumerate() {
        let name = page
            .chapter_title
            .clone()
            .unwrap_or_else(|| default_title.to_string());
        if let Some(last) = groups.last_mut()
            && last.0 == name
        {
            last.2 += 1;
        } else {
            groups.push((name, idx, 1));
        }
    }

    if groups.is_empty() {
        let ch = BookChapter {
            id: None,
            title: default_title.to_string(),
            paragraphs: vec![format!("Р’СЃРµРіРѕ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†: {}", pages.len())],
        };
        let t = TocItem {
            id: None,
            title: default_title.to_string(),
            chapter_index: 0,
            children: Vec::new(),
        };
        return (vec![ch], vec![t]);
    }

    let mut chapters = Vec::with_capacity(groups.len());
    let mut toc = Vec::with_capacity(groups.len());
    for (group_title, start, count) in groups {
        let id = Some(format!("ch_{start}"));
        let desc = format!("РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ {}вЂ“{} (РІСЃРµРіРѕ {count})", start + 1, start + count);
        chapters.push(BookChapter {
            id: id.clone(),
            title: group_title.clone(),
            paragraphs: vec![desc],
        });
        toc.push(TocItem {
            id,
            title: group_title,
            chapter_index: start,
            children: Vec::new(),
        });
    }
    (chapters, toc)
}

struct ParsedComicInfo {
    title: Option<String>,
    writer: Option<String>,
    is_manga: Option<bool>,
}

fn parse_comic_info(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<ParsedComicInfo, String> {
    let mut reader = quick_xml::Reader::from_reader(bytes);
    reader.config_mut().trim_text(true);
    let (mut title, mut series, mut number, mut writer, mut manga_tag) =
        (None, None, None, None, None);
    let mut current_tag = String::new();
    let mut buf = Vec::new();

    loop {
        match reader.read_event_into(&mut buf) {
            Ok(quick_xml::events::Event::Start(ref e)) => {
                current_tag = e.name().as_ref().to_ascii_lowercase();
            }
            Ok(quick_xml::events::Event::Text(ref e)) => {
                let raw = e.as_ref();
                let t = quick_xml::escape::unescape(raw)
                    .map(|c| c.into_owned())
                    .unwrap_or_else(|_| raw.to_string());
                match current_tag.as_str() {
                    "title" => title = Some(t),
                    "series" => series = Some(t),
                    "number" => number = Some(t),
                    "writer" => writer = Some(t),
                    "manga" => manga_tag = Some(t),
                    _ => {}
                }
            }
            Ok(quick_xml::events::Event::End(_)) => current_tag.clear(),
            Ok(quick_xml::events::Event::Eof) => break,
            Err(e) => return Err(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРіР° ComicInfo.xml: {e}")),
            _ => {}
        }
        buf.clear();
    }

    let full_title = match (series, number, title) {
        (Some(s), Some(n), Some(t)) if !t.is_empty() => Some(format!("{s} #{n}: {t}")),
        (Some(s), Some(n), _) => Some(format!("{s} #{n}")),
        (Some(s), None, Some(t)) if !t.is_empty() => Some(format!("{s}: {t}")),
        (Some(s), None, None) => Some(s),
        (None, _, Some(t)) => Some(t),
        _ => None,
    };
    let is_manga = manga_tag.map(|m| {
        let ml = m.to_ascii_lowercase();
        ml.contains("yes") || ml.contains("righttoleft")
    });
    Ok(ParsedComicInfo {
        title: full_title,
        writer,
        is_manga,
    })
}

fn consume_number(chars: &mut std::iter::Peekable<std::str::Chars<'_>>) -> u64 {
    let mut num: u64 = 0;
    while let Some(&d) = chars.peek() {
        if let Some(digit) = d.to_digit(10) {
            num = num.saturating_mul(10).saturating_add(digit as u64);
            chars.next();
        } else {
            break;
        }
    }
    num
}

pub fn natural_cmp(a: &str, b: &str) -> std::cmp::Ordering {
    let mut a_chars = a.chars().peekable();
    let mut b_chars = b.chars().peekable();

    loop {
        match (a_chars.peek(), b_chars.peek()) {
            (None, None) => return std::cmp::Ordering::Equal,
            (None, Some(_)) => return std::cmp::Ordering::Less,
            (Some(_), None) => return std::cmp::Ordering::Greater,
            (Some(&ca), Some(&cb)) => {
                if ca.is_ascii_digit() && cb.is_ascii_digit() {
                    let cmp = consume_number(&mut a_chars).cmp(&consume_number(&mut b_chars));
                    if cmp != std::cmp::Ordering::Equal {
                        return cmp;
                    }
                } else if ca.is_ascii_digit() {
                    return std::cmp::Ordering::Less;
                } else if cb.is_ascii_digit() {
                    return std::cmp::Ordering::Greater;
                } else {
                    let al = ca.to_lowercase().next().unwrap_or(ca);
                    let bl = cb.to_lowercase().next().unwrap_or(cb);
                    let cmp = al.cmp(&bl);
                    if cmp != std::cmp::Ordering::Equal {
                        return cmp;
                    }
                    a_chars.next();
                    b_chars.next();
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

use crate::document::{
    BookChapter, BookMetadata, Document, DocumentError, HasToc, TextContent, TocItem,

pub struct EpubDocument {
    metadata: BookMetadata,
    chapters: Vec<BookChapter>,
    toc: Vec<TocItem>,
}

impl TextContent for EpubDocument {
    fn chapters(&self) -> &[BookChapter] {
        &self.chapters
    }
}

impl HasToc for EpubDocument {
    fn toc(&self) -> &[TocItem] {
        &self.toc
    }

impl Document for EpubDocument {
    fn metadata(&self) -> &BookMetadata {
        &self.metadata
    }

    fn chapters(&self) -> &[BookChapter] {
        &self.chapters
    }

        &self.toc
    }

        "EPUB"

#[derive(Default, Debug)]
struct ManifestItem {
    id: String,
    href: String,
    media_type: String,
    properties: String,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct NavPoint {

    label: String,
