impl EpubDocument {
    pub const MAX_EPUB_SIZE: u64 = 200 * 1024 * 1024;
    pub const MAX_ENTRIES: usize = 10_000;
    pub const MAX_ENTRY_BYTES: u64 = 50 * 1024 * 1024;

    pub fn from_file<P: AsRef<Path>>(path: P) -> Result<Self, DocumentError> {
        let path_ref = path.as_ref();
        let meta = std::fs::metadata(path_ref).map_err(|e| {
            DocumentError::Io(format!("РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РёС‚СЊ РјРµС‚Р°РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ EPUB С„Р°Р№Р»Р°: {e}"))
        })?;
        if meta.len() > Self::MAX_EPUB_SIZE {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                "Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ С„Р°Р№Р»Р° EPUB РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°РµС‚ РґРѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёРјС‹Р№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ (200 РњР‘)".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        let file = std::fs::File::open(path_ref)
            .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Io(format!("РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ С„Р°Р№Р» EPUB: {e}")))?;
        let mut archive = ZipArchive::new(file)
            .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ ZIP-Р°СЂС…РёРІ EPUB: {e}")))?;
        if archive.len() > Self::MAX_ENTRIES {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                "РђСЂС…РёРІ EPUB СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ СЃР»РёС€РєРѕРј РјРЅРѕРіРѕ С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ (РїСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚ 10 000)".to_string(),
            ));
        }
        Self::from_archive(&mut archive)
    }

    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub fn from_bytes(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Self, DocumentError> {
        if bytes.len() as u64 > Self::MAX_EPUB_SIZE {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                "Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… EPUB РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°РµС‚ РґРѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёРјС‹Р№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ (200 РњР‘)".to_string(),
            ));
        }
        let cursor = std::io::Cursor::new(bytes);
        let mut archive = ZipArchive::new(cursor)
            .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ ZIP-Р°СЂС…РёРІ EPUB: {e}")))?;
        if archive.len() > Self::MAX_ENTRIES {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                "РђСЂС…РёРІ EPUB СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ СЃР»РёС€РєРѕРј РјРЅРѕРіРѕ С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ (РїСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚ 10 000)".to_string(),
            ));
        }
        Self::from_archive(&mut archive)
    }

    fn read_zip_entry<R: Read + std::io::Seek>(
        archive: &mut ZipArchive<R>,
        entry_name: &str,
        running_unpacked: &mut u64,
    ) -> Result<String, DocumentError> {
        let normalized = entry_name.replace('\\', "/");
        if !crate::document::is_safe_archive_path(&normalized) {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РќРµР±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅС‹Р№ РїСѓС‚СЊ РІ EPUB: '{entry_name}'")));
        }
        let decoded = percent_decode_str(&normalized);
        let mut matched = None;
        for i in 0..archive.len() {
            if let Ok(file) = archive.by_index(i) {
                let name = file.name().replace('\\', "/");
                if name == normalized || name == decoded {
                    matched = Some(i);
                    break;
                } else if matched.is_none() && (name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&normalized) || name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&decoded)) {
                    matched = Some(i);
                }
            }
        }

        let index = matched.ok_or_else(|| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("Р¤Р°Р№Р» '{entry_name}' РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ")))?;
        let file = archive.by_index(index).map_err(|e| DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° '{entry_name}': {e}")))?;
        if file.size() > Self::MAX_ENTRY_BYTES {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(format!("Р¤Р°Р№Р» '{entry_name}' РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°РµС‚ Р»РёРјРёС‚")));
        }

        let mut bytes = Vec::new();
        file.take(Self::MAX_ENTRY_BYTES.saturating_add(1))
            .read_to_end(&mut bytes)
            .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Io(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° С‡С‚РµРЅРёСЏ '{entry_name}': {e}")))?;

        if bytes.len() as u64 > Self::MAX_ENTRY_BYTES {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(format!("Р¤Р°Р№Р» '{entry_name}' РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°РµС‚ Р»РёРјРёС‚")));
        }

        *running_unpacked = running_unpacked.saturating_add(bytes.len() as u64);
        if *running_unpacked > crate::document::MAX_TOTAL_UNPACK_BYTES {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded("РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ РѕР±С‰РёР№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ СЂР°СЃРїР°РєРѕРІРєРё EPUB (500 РњР‘)".to_string()));
        }

        match String::from_utf8(bytes) {
            Ok(s) => Ok(s),
            Err(err) => {
                let raw = err.into_bytes();
                let (cow, _, _) = encoding_rs::WINDOWS_1251.decode(&raw);
                Ok(cow.into_owned())
            }
        }
    }

    fn find_opf_path<R: Read + std::io::Seek>(
        archive: &mut ZipArchive<R>,
        running_unpacked: &mut u64,
    ) -> Result<String, DocumentError> {
        let container_xml = match Self::read_zip_entry(archive, "META-INF/container.xml", running_unpacked) {
            Ok(c) => c,
            Err(_) => {
                for i in 0..archive.len() {
                    if let Ok(f) = archive.by_index(i) && f.name().ends_with(".opf") {
                        return Ok(f.name().to_string());
                    }
                }
                return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted("META-INF/container.xml РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ".to_string()));
            }
        };

        let mut reader = Reader::from_str(&container_xml);
        reader.config_mut().trim_text(true);
        let mut buf = Vec::new();

        loop {
            match reader.read_event_into(&mut buf) {
                Ok(Event::Start(ref e)) | Ok(Event::Empty(ref e)) => {
                    if local_name(e.name().as_ref()).eq_ignore_ascii_case("rootfile") {
                        for attr in e.attributes().flatten() {
                            if local_name(attr.key.as_ref()).eq_ignore_ascii_case("full-path") {
                                return Ok(attr.value.to_string());
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                Ok(Event::Eof) => break,
                Err(e) => return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° container.xml: {e}"))),
                _ => {}
            }
            buf.clear();
        }

        for i in 0..archive.len() {
            if let Ok(f) = archive.by_index(i) && f.name().ends_with(".opf") {
                return Ok(f.name().to_string());
            }
        }
        Err(DocumentError::Corrupted("РќРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ rootfile (OPF)".to_string()))
    }

    fn from_archive<R: Read + std::io::Seek>(
        archive: &mut ZipArchive<R>,
    ) -> Result<Self, DocumentError> {
        let mut total_unpacked: u64 = 0;
        for i in 0..archive.len() {
            if let Ok(entry) = archive.by_index_raw(i) {
                if !crate::document::is_safe_archive_path(entry.name()) {
                    return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РќРµР±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅС‹Р№ РїСѓС‚СЊ: {}", entry.name())));
                }
                total_unpacked = total_unpacked.saturating_add(entry.size());
                if total_unpacked > crate::document::MAX_TOTAL_UNPACK_BYTES {
                    return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded("РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ РѕР±С‰РёР№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ Р°СЂС…РёРІР° EPUB".to_string()));
                }
            }
        }

        let mut running_unpacked: u64 = 0;
        let opf_path = Self::find_opf_path(archive, &mut running_unpacked)?;
        let opf_dir = Path::new(&opf_path).parent().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/")).unwrap_or_default();
        let opf_content = Self::read_zip_entry(archive, &opf_path, &mut running_unpacked)?;
        let (metadata, manifest, spine_idrefs, ncx_id, nav_id) = Self::parse_opf(&opf_content)?;

        let manifest_map: HashMap<String, ManifestItem> = manifest.into_iter().map(|it| (it.id.clone(), it)).collect();
        let mut spine_files: Vec<(String, String)> = Vec::new();
        for idref in &spine_idrefs {
            if let Some(item) = manifest_map.get(idref) {
                spine_files.push((idref.clone(), resolve_path(&opf_dir, &item.href)));
            }
        }
        if spine_files.is_empty() {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted("EPUB spine РЅРµ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ СЂР°Р·РґРµР»РѕРІ".to_string()));
        }

        let mut chapters = Vec::new();
        let mut chapter_path_to_index: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();
        let mut anchor_to_chapter: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();

        for (ch_idx, (idref, path)) in spine_files.iter().enumerate() {
            let html_content = Self::read_zip_entry(archive, path, &mut running_unpacked)
                .or_else(|_| Self::read_zip_entry(archive, &percent_decode_str(path), &mut running_unpacked))
                .unwrap_or_default();

            let (parsed_title, paragraphs, anchors) = parse_html_chapter(&html_content);
            let final_title = if !parsed_title.is_empty() { parsed_title } else { format!("Р Р°Р·РґРµР» {}", ch_idx + 1) };

            let norm_path = normalize_zip_path(path);
            let dec_path = percent_decode_str(&norm_path);
            chapter_path_to_index.insert(norm_path.clone(), ch_idx);
            chapter_path_to_index.insert(dec_path.clone(), ch_idx);

            if let Some(fname) = Path::new(&norm_path).file_name().and_then(|n| n.to_str()) {
                chapter_path_to_index.entry(fname.to_string()).or_insert(ch_idx);
            }
            if let Some(fname) = Path::new(&dec_path).file_name().and_then(|n| n.to_str()) {
                chapter_path_to_index.entry(fname.to_string()).or_insert(ch_idx);
            }

            for anchor in anchors {
                let dec_anchor = percent_decode_str(&anchor);
                anchor_to_chapter.entry(anchor).or_insert(ch_idx);
                anchor_to_chapter.entry(dec_anchor).or_insert(ch_idx);
            }

            chapters.push(BookChapter {
                id: Some(idref.clone()),
                title: final_title,
                paragraphs,
            });
        }

        let mut toc = Vec::new();
        if let Some(ncx_id) = ncx_id && let Some(ncx_item) = manifest_map.get(&ncx_id) {
            let ncx_full_path = resolve_path(&opf_dir, &ncx_item.href);
            if let Ok(ncx_content) = Self::read_zip_entry(archive, &ncx_full_path, &mut running_unpacked) {
                toc = parse_ncx_toc(&ncx_content, &opf_dir, &chapter_path_to_index, &anchor_to_chapter);
            }
        }

        if toc.is_empty() && let Some(nav_id) = nav_id && let Some(nav_item) = manifest_map.get(&nav_id) {
            let nav_full_path = resolve_path(&opf_dir, &nav_item.href);
            if let Ok(nav_content) = Self::read_zip_entry(archive, &nav_full_path, &mut running_unpacked) {
                let nav_dir = Path::new(&nav_full_path).parent().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/")).unwrap_or_default();
                toc = parse_epub3_nav_toc(&nav_content, &nav_dir, &chapter_path_to_index, &anchor_to_chapter);
            }
        }

        if toc.is_empty() {
            toc = chapters.iter().enumerate().map(|(idx, ch)| TocItem {
                id: ch.id.clone(),
                title: ch.title.clone(),
                chapter_index: idx,
                children: Vec::new(),
            }).collect();
        }

        Ok(Self { metadata, chapters, toc })
    }

    fn parse_opf(
        opf: &str,
    ) -> Result<(BookMetadata, Vec<ManifestItem>, Vec<String>, Option<String>, Option<String>), DocumentError> {
        let mut reader = Reader::from_str(opf);
        reader.config_mut().trim_text(true);

        let mut title = String::new();
        let mut author = String::new();
        let mut manifest = Vec::new();
        let mut spine_idrefs = Vec::new();
        let mut ncx_id = None;
        let mut nav_id = None;
        let mut buf = Vec::new();
        let mut current_tag = String::new();

        loop {
            match reader.read_event_into(&mut buf) {
                ext @ (Ok(Event::Start(_)) | Ok(Event::Empty(_))) => {
                    let (e, is_start) = match &ext {
                        Ok(Event::Start(e)) => (e, true),
                        Ok(Event::Empty(e)) => (e, false),
                        _ => unreachable!(),
                    };
                    let tag_name = local_name(e.name().as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase();
                    if tag_name == "spine" {
                        for attr in e.attributes().flatten() {
                            if local_name(attr.key.as_ref()).eq_ignore_ascii_case("toc") {
                                ncx_id = Some(attr.value.to_string());
                            }
                        }
                    } else if tag_name == "item" {
                        let mut item = ManifestItem::default();
                        for attr in e.attributes().flatten() {
                            match local_name(attr.key.as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
                                "id" => item.id = attr.value.to_string(),
                                "href" => item.href = attr.value.to_string(),
                                "media-type" => item.media_type = attr.value.to_string(),
                                "properties" => item.properties = attr.value.to_string(),
                                _ => {}
                            }
                        }
                        if !item.id.is_empty() {
                            if item.media_type == "application/x-dtbncx+xml" && ncx_id.is_none() {
                                ncx_id = Some(item.id.clone());
                            }
                            if item.properties.split_whitespace().any(|p| p == "nav") && nav_id.is_none() {
                                nav_id = Some(item.id.clone());
                            }
                            manifest.push(item);
                        }
                    } else if tag_name == "itemref" {
                        for attr in e.attributes().flatten() {
                            if local_name(attr.key.as_ref()).eq_ignore_ascii_case("idref") {
                                spine_idrefs.push(attr.value.to_string());
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if is_start {
                        current_tag = tag_name;
                    }
                }
                Ok(Event::Text(ref e)) => {
                    let raw_str = e.as_ref();
                    let text = quick_xml::escape::unescape(raw_str).map(|c| c.into_owned()).unwrap_or_else(|_| raw_str.to_string());
                    let trimmed = text.trim();
                    if !trimmed.is_empty() {
                        match current_tag.as_str() {
                            "title" if title.is_empty() => title = trimmed.to_string(),
                            "creator" if author.is_empty() => author = trimmed.to_string(),
                            _ => {}
                        }
                    }
                }
                Ok(Event::End(_)) => current_tag.clear(),
                Ok(Event::Eof) => break,
                Err(e) => return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРіР° OPF: {e}"))),
                _ => {}
            }
            buf.clear();
        }

        if title.is_empty() {
            title = "Р‘РµР· РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЏ".to_string();
        }
        let metadata = BookMetadata { title, author };
        Ok((metadata, manifest, spine_idrefs, ncx_id, nav_id))
    }
}

pub fn resolve_path(base_dir: &str, href: &str) -> String {
    let clean_href = href.split('#').next().unwrap_or(href);
    if clean_href.contains(':') || clean_href.starts_with('/') || clean_href.starts_with('\\') {
        return clean_href.to_string();
    }
    let combined = if base_dir.is_empty() { clean_href.to_string() } else { format!("{base_dir}/{clean_href}") };
    let mut parts: Vec<&str> = Vec::new();
    for seg in combined.split('/') {
        if seg.is_empty() || seg == "." { continue; }
        if seg == ".." {
            if parts.is_empty() { return String::new(); }
            parts.pop();
        } else {
            parts.push(seg);
        }
    }
    parts.join("/")
}

fn normalize_zip_path(path: &str) -> String {
    path.replace('\\', "/").trim_start_matches('/').to_string()
}

pub fn percent_decode_str(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut result = Vec::new();
    let bytes = s.as_bytes();
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < bytes.len() {
        if bytes[i] == b'%' && i + 2 < bytes.len() && let Ok(b_val) = u8::from_str_radix(std::str::from_utf8(&bytes[i + 1..i + 3]).unwrap_or(""), 16) {
            result.push(b_val);
            i += 3;
            continue;
        }
        result.push(bytes[i]);
        i += 1;
    }
    String::from_utf8_lossy(&result).to_string()
}

pub fn sanitize_xml_entities(input: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(input.len());
    let mut chars = input.char_indices().peekable();
    while let Some((idx, c)) = chars.next() {
        if c == '&' {
            let rest = &input[idx + 1..];
            if let Some(semi_pos) = rest.find(';') && semi_pos <= 10 {
                let entity_name = &rest[..semi_pos];
                if matches!(entity_name, "amp" | "lt" | "gt" | "quot" | "apos") {
                    out.push('&');
                    out.push_str(entity_name);
                    out.push(';');
                    for _ in 0..=semi_pos { chars.next(); }
                    continue;
                }
                if entity_name.starts_with('#')
                    && (entity_name[1..].chars().all(|ch| ch.is_ascii_digit())
                        || (entity_name.starts_with("#x") || entity_name.starts_with("#X")) && entity_name[2..].chars().all(|ch| ch.is_ascii_hexdigit()))
                {
                    out.push('&');
                    out.push_str(entity_name);
                    out.push(';');
                    for _ in 0..=semi_pos { chars.next(); }
                    continue;
                }
                let repl = match entity_name {
                    "nbsp" => " ", "mdash" => "вЂ”", "ndash" => "вЂ“", "hellip" => "вЂ¦",
                    "laquo" => "В«", "raquo" => "В»", "ldquo" => "вЂњ", "rdquo" => "вЂќ",
                    "lsquo" => "вЂ", "rsquo" => "вЂ™", "copy" => "В©", "trade" => "в„ў",
                    "reg" => "В®", "bull" => "вЂў", "times" => "Г—", "divide" => "Г·",
                    "minus" => "в€’", "deg" => "В°", "euro" => "в‚¬", "pound" => "ВЈ",
                    "yen" => "ВҐ", "cent" => "Вў", "sect" => "В§", "para" => "В¶",
                    _ => "",
                };
                if !repl.is_empty() {
                    out.push_str(repl);
                    for _ in 0..=semi_pos { chars.next(); }
                    continue;
                }
            }
            out.push_str("&amp;");
        } else {
            out.push(c);
        }
    }
    out
}

fn flush_p(current_p: &mut String, paragraphs: &mut Vec<String>) {
    if !current_p.trim().is_empty() {
        paragraphs.push(current_p.trim().to_string());
        current_p.clear();
    }
}

pub fn extract_html_lenient(html: &str) -> (String, Vec<String>) {
    let mut title = String::new();
    let mut paragraphs = Vec::new();
    let mut current_p = String::new();
    let mut in_title = false;
    let mut skip = false;
    let mut i = 0;
    let bytes = html.as_bytes();

    while i < bytes.len() {
        if bytes[i] == b'<' {
            let rest = &html[i + 1..];
            if let Some(end_bracket) = rest.find('>') {
                let tag_raw = rest[..end_bracket].trim();
                let is_closing = tag_raw.starts_with('/');
                let tag_name = tag_raw.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("").trim_start_matches('/').to_ascii_lowercase();

                match tag_name.as_str() {
                    "script" | "style" | "head" => skip = !is_closing,
                    "title" => { in_title = !is_closing; skip = false; }
                    "p" | "div" | "blockquote" | "li" | "h1" | "h2" | "h3" | "h4" | "h5" | "h6" => flush_p(&mut current_p, &mut paragraphs),
                    "br" => current_p.push('\n'),
                    "hr" => { flush_p(&mut current_p, &mut paragraphs); paragraphs.push("* * *".to_string()); }
                    _ => {}
                }
                i += 1 + end_bracket + 1;
                continue;
            }
        }

        if !skip {
            let start = i;
            while i < bytes.len() && bytes[i] != b'<' { i += 1; }
            if let Ok(text_slice) = std::str::from_utf8(&bytes[start..i]) {
                let unescaped = quick_xml::escape::unescape(text_slice).map(|c| c.into_owned()).unwrap_or_else(|_| text_slice.to_string());
                let trimmed = unescaped.trim();
                if !trimmed.is_empty() {
                    if in_title && title.is_empty() {
                        title = trimmed.to_string();
                    } else {
                        if !current_p.is_empty() && !current_p.ends_with(' ') && !current_p.ends_with('\n') {
                            current_p.push(' ');
                        }
                        current_p.push_str(trimmed);
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            i += 1;
        }
    }
    flush_p(&mut current_p, &mut paragraphs);
    (title, paragraphs)
}

pub fn parse_html_chapter(html: &str) -> (String, Vec<String>, Vec<String>) {
    let sanitized = sanitize_xml_entities(html);
    let mut reader = Reader::from_str(&sanitized);
    reader.config_mut().trim_text(false);
    reader.config_mut().check_end_names = false;

    let mut title = String::new();
    let mut paragraphs = Vec::new();
    let mut anchors = Vec::new();
    let mut current_p = String::new();
    let (mut in_title_tag, mut in_heading_tag, mut skip_content) = (false, false, false);
    let mut buf = Vec::new();
    let mut had_error = false;

    loop {
        match reader.read_event_into(&mut buf) {
            Ok(Event::Start(ref e)) => {
                for attr in e.attributes().flatten() {
                    let k = local_name(attr.key.as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase();
                    if k == "id" || k == "name" { anchors.push(attr.value.to_string()); }
                }
                match local_name(e.name().as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
                    "script" | "style" | "head" => skip_content = true,
                    "title" => { skip_content = false; in_title_tag = true; }
                    "h1" | "h2" | "h3" | "h4" | "h5" | "h6" => { flush_p(&mut current_p, &mut paragraphs); in_heading_tag = true; }
                    "p" | "div" | "blockquote" | "li" => flush_p(&mut current_p, &mut paragraphs),
                    "br" => current_p.push('\n'),
                    _ => {}
                }
            }
            Ok(Event::Empty(ref e)) => {
                for attr in e.attributes().flatten() {
                    let k = local_name(attr.key.as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase();
                    if k == "id" || k == "name" { anchors.push(attr.value.to_string()); }
                }
                match local_name(e.name().as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
                    "br" => current_p.push('\n'),
                    "hr" => { flush_p(&mut current_p, &mut paragraphs); paragraphs.push("* * *".to_string()); }
                    _ => {}
                }
            }
            Ok(Event::Text(ref e)) if !skip_content => {
                let raw_str = e.as_ref();
                let text = quick_xml::escape::unescape(raw_str).map(|c| c.into_owned()).unwrap_or_else(|_| raw_str.to_string());
                if !text.is_empty() {
                    if in_title_tag && title.is_empty() {
                        title.push_str(text.trim());
                    } else {
                        current_p.push_str(&text);
                    }
                }
            }
            Ok(Event::Text(ref e)) if !skip_content => {
                let raw_str = e.as_ref();
                let text = quick_xml::escape::unescape(raw_str).map(|c| c.into_owned()).unwrap_or_else(|_| raw_str.to_string());
                if !text.is_empty() {
                    if in_title_tag && title.is_empty() {
                        title.push_str(text.trim());
                    } else {
                        current_p.push_str(&text);
                    }
                }
            }
            Ok(Event::GeneralRef(ref e)) if !skip_content => {
                let repl = match e.as_ref() {
                    "amp" => "&",
                    "lt" => "<",
                    "gt" => ">",
                    "quot" => "\"",
                    "apos" => "'",
                    _ => "",
                };
                if !repl.is_empty() {
                    current_p.push_str(repl);
                }
            }
            Ok(Event::End(ref e)) => {
                match local_name(e.name().as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
                    "script" | "style" | "head" => skip_content = false,
                    "title" => in_title_tag = false,
                    "h1" | "h2" | "h3" | "h4" | "h5" | "h6" => {
                        if in_heading_tag && title.is_empty() && !current_p.trim().is_empty() {
                            title = current_p.trim().to_string();
                        }
                        flush_p(&mut current_p, &mut paragraphs);
                        in_heading_tag = false;
                    }
                    "p" | "div" | "blockquote" | "li" => flush_p(&mut current_p, &mut paragraphs),
                    _ => {}
                }
            }
            Ok(Event::Eof) => break,
            Err(_) => { had_error = true; break; }
            _ => {}
        }
        buf.clear();
    }
    flush_p(&mut current_p, &mut paragraphs);

    if had_error || paragraphs.is_empty() {
        let (fb_title, fb_paras) = extract_html_lenient(html);
        if !fb_paras.is_empty() {
            if paragraphs.is_empty() || paragraphs.len() < fb_paras.len() / 2 {
                paragraphs = fb_paras;
            }
            if title.is_empty() { title = fb_title; }
        }
    }
    (title, paragraphs, anchors)
}

fn resolve_toc_destination(
    src: &str,
    opf_dir: &str,
    chapter_map: &HashMap<String, usize>,
    anchor_map: &HashMap<String, usize>,
) -> usize {
    let clean = src.trim();
    if clean.is_empty() { return 0; }

    let (file_part, anchor_part) = if let Some(pos) = clean.find('#') { (&clean[..pos], &clean[pos + 1..]) } else { (clean, "") };

    if !anchor_part.is_empty() {
        if let Some(&ch) = anchor_map.get(anchor_part) { return ch; }
        let dec_anchor = percent_decode_str(anchor_part);
        if let Some(&ch) = anchor_map.get(&dec_anchor) { return ch; }
    }
    if file_part.is_empty() { return 0; }

    let resolved = resolve_path(opf_dir, file_part);
    let norm = normalize_zip_path(&resolved);
    let dec = percent_decode_str(&norm);

    if let Some(&ch) = chapter_map.get(&norm) { return ch; }
    if let Some(&ch) = chapter_map.get(&dec) { return ch; }
    if let Some(fname) = Path::new(&norm).file_name().and_then(|n| n.to_str()) && let Some(&ch) = chapter_map.get(fname) { return ch; }
    if let Some(fname) = Path::new(&dec).file_name().and_then(|n| n.to_str()) && let Some(&ch) = chapter_map.get(fname) { return ch; }
    0
}

fn parse_ncx_toc(
    ncx_xml: &str,
    opf_dir: &str,
    chapter_map: &HashMap<String, usize>,
    anchor_map: &HashMap<String, usize>,
) -> Vec<TocItem> {
    let sanitized = sanitize_xml_entities(ncx_xml);
    let mut reader = Reader::from_str(&sanitized);
    reader.config_mut().trim_text(true);
    reader.config_mut().check_end_names = false;

    let mut root_nav_points: Vec<NavPoint> = Vec::new();
    let mut point_stack: Vec<NavPoint> = Vec::new();
    let mut in_text = false;
    let mut buf = Vec::new();

    loop {
        match reader.read_event_into(&mut buf) {
            ext @ (Ok(Event::Start(_)) | Ok(Event::Empty(_))) => {
                let e = match &ext { Ok(Event::Start(e)) | Ok(Event::Empty(e)) => e, _ => unreachable!() };
                let tag = local_name(e.name().as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase();
                if tag == "navpoint" && point_stack.len() < 32 {
                    let mut id = None;
                    for attr in e.attributes().flatten() {
                        if local_name(attr.key.as_ref()).eq_ignore_ascii_case("id") { id = Some(attr.value.to_string()); }
                    }
                    point_stack.push(NavPoint { id, label: String::new(), content_src: String::new(), children: Vec::new() });
                } else if tag == "content" {
                    for attr in e.attributes().flatten() {
                        if local_name(attr.key.as_ref()).eq_ignore_ascii_case("src") && let Some(last) = point_stack.last_mut() {
                            last.content_src = attr.value.to_string();
                        }
                    }
                } else if tag == "text" {
                    in_text = true;
                }
            }
            Ok(Event::Text(ref e)) if in_text => {
                let raw_str = e.as_ref();
                let text = quick_xml::escape::unescape(raw_str).map(|c| c.into_owned()).unwrap_or_else(|_| raw_str.to_string());
                let trimmed = text.trim();
                if !trimmed.is_empty() && let Some(last) = point_stack.last_mut() && last.label.is_empty() {
                    last.label = trimmed.to_string();
                }
            }
            Ok(Event::End(ref e)) => {
                let tag = local_name(e.name().as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase();
                if tag == "text" {
                    in_text = false;
                } else if tag == "navpoint" && let Some(finished_point) = point_stack.pop() {
                    if let Some(parent) = point_stack.last_mut() { parent.children.push(finished_point); } else { root_nav_points.push(finished_point); }
                }
            }
            Ok(Event::Eof) | Err(_) => break,
            _ => {}
        }
        buf.clear();
    }

    fn convert_nav(
        nav: &NavPoint,
        opf_dir: &str,
        chapter_map: &HashMap<String, usize>,
        anchor_map: &HashMap<String, usize>,
        depth: usize,
    ) -> TocItem {
        let ch_index = resolve_toc_destination(&nav.content_src, opf_dir, chapter_map, anchor_map);
        let children = if depth < 32 {
            nav.children.iter().map(|c| convert_nav(c, opf_dir, chapter_map, anchor_map, depth + 1)).collect()
        } else {
            Vec::new()
        };
        let title = if nav.label.is_empty() { "Р‘РµР· РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЏ".to_string() } else { nav.label.clone() };
        TocItem { id: nav.id.clone(), title, chapter_index: ch_index, children }
    }

    root_nav_points.iter().map(|nav| convert_nav(nav, opf_dir, chapter_map, anchor_map, 0)).collect()
}

fn parse_epub3_nav_toc(
    nav_html: &str,
    nav_dir: &str,
    chapter_map: &HashMap<String, usize>,
    anchor_map: &HashMap<String, usize>,
) -> Vec<TocItem> {
    let sanitized = sanitize_xml_entities(nav_html);
    let mut reader = Reader::from_str(&sanitized);
    reader.config_mut().trim_text(true);
    reader.config_mut().check_end_names = false;

    let mut in_toc_nav = false;
    let mut current_href = String::new();
    let mut current_title = String::new();
    let mut in_anchor = false;
    let mut stack: Vec<Vec<TocItem>> = vec![Vec::new()];
    let mut buf = Vec::new();

    loop {
        match reader.read_event_into(&mut buf) {
            Ok(Event::Start(ref e)) => {
                let tag = local_name(e.name().as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase();
                if tag == "nav" {
                    for attr in e.attributes().flatten() {
                        if attr.value.to_string().contains("toc") { in_toc_nav = true; }
                    }
                } else if in_toc_nav {
                    if (tag == "ol" || tag == "ul") && stack.len() < 32 {
                        stack.push(Vec::new());
                    } else if tag == "a" {
                        in_anchor = true;
                        current_title.clear();
                        current_href.clear();
                        for attr in e.attributes().flatten() {
                            if local_name(attr.key.as_ref()).eq_ignore_ascii_case("href") {
                                current_href = attr.value.to_string();
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            Ok(Event::Text(ref e)) if in_anchor => {
                let raw = e.as_ref();
                let text = quick_xml::escape::unescape(raw).map(|c| c.into_owned()).unwrap_or_else(|_| raw.to_string());
                current_title.push_str(text.trim());
            }
            Ok(Event::End(ref e)) => {
                let tag = local_name(e.name().as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase();
                if tag == "nav" {
                    in_toc_nav = false;
                } else if in_toc_nav {
                    if tag == "a" {
                        in_anchor = false;
                        if !current_href.is_empty() {
                            let ch = resolve_toc_destination(&current_href, nav_dir, chapter_map, anchor_map);
                            let title = if current_title.is_empty() { "Р Р°Р·РґРµР»".to_string() } else { current_title.clone() };
                            if let Some(cur_level) = stack.last_mut() {
                                cur_level.push(TocItem { id: None, title, chapter_index: ch, children: Vec::new() });
                            }
                        }
                    } else if (tag == "ol" || tag == "ul") && stack.len() > 1 && let Some(children) = stack.pop() {
                        if let Some(cur_level) = stack.last_mut() && let Some(last_item) = cur_level.last_mut() {
                            last_item.children = children;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            Ok(Event::Eof) | Err(_) => break,
            _ => {}
        }
        buf.clear();
    }
    stack.into_iter().next().unwrap_or_default()
}

fn local_name(name: &str) -> &str {
    name.rfind(':').map(|pos| &name[pos + 1..]).unwrap_or(name)
}

use crate::document::{
    BookChapter, BookMetadata, Document, DocumentError, HasToc, TextContent, TocItem,

use quick_xml::events::Event;

pub struct Fb2Document {
    metadata: BookMetadata,
    chapters: Vec<BookChapter>,
    toc: Vec<TocItem>,
}

    fn chapters(&self) -> &[BookChapter] {
        &self.chapters
    }

        &self.toc
    }

        &self.chapters

        &self.toc

        "FB2"

#[derive(Default, Debug)]
struct SectionNode {
    id: Option<String>,
    title: Option<String>,
    paragraphs: Vec<String>,
    subsections: Vec<SectionNode>,
}

#[derive(Default, Debug)]
struct BodyNode {
    name: Option<String>,
    title: Option<String>,
    direct_paragraphs: Vec<String>,
    sections: Vec<SectionNode>,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct TocLink {
    href: String,
    title: String,
}

impl Fb2Document {
    pub const MAX_FB2_SIZE: u64 = 150 * 1024 * 1024;

    pub fn from_file<P: AsRef<Path>>(path: P) -> Result<Self, DocumentError> {
        let path_ref = path.as_ref();
        let meta = std::fs::metadata(path_ref)
            .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Io(format!("Failed to read file metadata: {e}")))?;
        if meta.len() > Self::MAX_FB2_SIZE {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                "Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ С„Р°Р№Р»Р° FB2 РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°РµС‚ РјР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ РґРѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёРјС‹Р№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ (150 РњР‘)".to_string(),
            ));
        }
        let bytes = std::fs::read(path_ref)
            .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Io(format!("Failed to read file: {e}")))?;
        Self::from_bytes(&bytes)
    }

    pub fn from_bytes(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Self, DocumentError> {
        if bytes.len() as u64 > Self::MAX_FB2_SIZE {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                "Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… FB2 РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°РµС‚ РјР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ РґРѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёРјС‹Р№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ (150 РњР‘)".to_string(),
            ));
        }
        let text = decode_text(bytes);
        Self::parse_xml(&text)
    }

    fn parse_xml(xml: &str) -> Result<Self, DocumentError> {
        if !xml.to_ascii_lowercase().contains("<fictionbook") {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(
                "РћС‚СЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚ РєРѕСЂРЅРµРІРѕР№ С‚РµРі <FictionBook> РІ FB2 РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Рµ".to_string(),
            ));
        }
        let sanitized = crate::document::epub::sanitize_xml_entities(xml);
        let mut reader = Reader::from_str(&sanitized);
        reader.config_mut().trim_text(false);
        reader.config_mut().check_end_names = false;

        let mut book_title = String::new();
        let mut author_first = String::new();
        let mut author_middle = String::new();
        let mut author_last = String::new();

        let mut in_title_info = false;
        let mut in_author = false;
        let mut in_book_title = false;
        let mut in_first_name = false;
        let mut in_middle_name = false;
        let mut in_last_name = false;

        let mut bodies: Vec<BodyNode> = Vec::new();
        let mut current_body: Option<BodyNode> = None;
        let mut section_stack: Vec<SectionNode> = Vec::new();

        let mut explicit_toc_links: Vec<TocLink> = Vec::new();
        let mut current_link_href: Option<String> = None;
        let mut current_link_text = String::new();

        let mut tag_stack: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut in_title = false;
        let mut title_paragraphs: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut current_title_paragraph = String::new();

        let mut in_paragraph = false;
        let mut current_paragraph = String::new();
        let mut buf = Vec::new();
        let mut had_error = false;

        loop {
            match reader.read_event_into(&mut buf) {
                Ok(Event::Start(ref e)) => {
                    let tag_name = local_name(e.name().as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase();

                    match tag_name.as_str() {
                        "title-info" => in_title_info = true,
                        "author" if in_title_info => in_author = true,
                        "book-title" if in_title_info => in_book_title = true,
                        "first-name" if in_author => in_first_name = true,
                        "middle-name" if in_author => in_middle_name = true,
                        "last-name" if in_author => in_last_name = true,

                        "body" => {
                            let mut body_name = None;
                            for attr in e.attributes().flatten() {
                                let key = local_name(attr.key.as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase();
                                if key == "name" {
                                    body_name = Some(attr.value.to_string());
                                }
                            }
                            current_body = Some(BodyNode {
                                name: body_name,
                                title: None,
                                direct_paragraphs: Vec::new(),
                                sections: Vec::new(),
                            });
                            section_stack.clear();
                        }

                        "section" if current_body.is_some() => {
                            if section_stack.len() < 32 {
                                let mut sec_id = None;
                                for attr in e.attributes().flatten() {
                                    let key = local_name(attr.key.as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase();
                                    if key == "id" {
                                        sec_id = Some(attr.value.to_string());
                                    }
                                }
                                section_stack.push(SectionNode {
                                    id: sec_id,
                                    title: None,
                                    paragraphs: Vec::new(),
                                    subsections: Vec::new(),
                                });
                            }
                        }

                        "title" => {
                            let parent_tag = tag_stack.last().map(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
                            if parent_tag == "section" || parent_tag == "body" {
                                in_title = true;
                                title_paragraphs.clear();
                                current_title_paragraph.clear();
                            }
                        }

                        "p" | "v" | "subtitle" | "text-author" => {
                            if in_title {
                                current_title_paragraph.clear();
                            } else if current_body.is_some() {
                                in_paragraph = true;
                                current_paragraph.clear();
                            }
                        }

                        "a" => {
                            if let Some(ref body) = current_body
                                && body
                                    .name
                                    .as_deref()
                                    .unwrap_or("")
                                    .eq_ignore_ascii_case("toc")
                            {
                                for attr in e.attributes().flatten() {
                                    let key = local_name(attr.key.as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase();
                                    if key == "href" {
                                        current_link_href = Some(attr.value.to_string());
                                        current_link_text.clear();
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        _ => {}
                    }

                    if tag_stack.len() < 64 {
                        tag_stack.push(tag_name);
                    }
                }

                Ok(Event::End(ref e)) => {
                    let tag_name = local_name(e.name().as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase();
                    tag_stack.pop();

                    match tag_name.as_str() {
                        "title-info" => in_title_info = false,
                        "author" => in_author = false,
                        "book-title" => in_book_title = false,
                        "first-name" => in_first_name = false,
                        "middle-name" => in_middle_name = false,
                        "last-name" => in_last_name = false,

                        "a" => {
                            if let (Some(href), false) = (
                                current_link_href.take(),
                                current_link_text.trim().is_empty(),
                            ) {
                                explicit_toc_links.push(TocLink {
                                    href,
                                    title: normalize_whitespace(&current_link_text),
                                });
                            }
                        }

                        "p" | "v" | "subtitle" | "text-author" => {
                            if in_title {
                                let p_clean = normalize_whitespace(&current_title_paragraph);
                                if !p_clean.is_empty() {
                                    title_paragraphs.push(p_clean);
                                }
                                current_title_paragraph.clear();
                            } else if in_paragraph {
                                in_paragraph = false;
                                let p_clean = normalize_whitespace(&current_paragraph);
                                if !p_clean.is_empty() {
                                    if let Some(sec) = section_stack.last_mut() {
                                        sec.paragraphs.push(p_clean);
                                    } else if let Some(ref mut body) = current_body {
                                        body.direct_paragraphs.push(p_clean);
                                    }
                                }
                                current_paragraph.clear();
                            }
                        }

                        "title" => {
                            if in_title {
                                in_title = false;
                                if !current_title_paragraph.trim().is_empty() {
                                    title_paragraphs
                                        .push(current_title_paragraph.trim().to_string());
                                    current_title_paragraph.clear();
                                }
                                let title_text = title_paragraphs.join(" вЂ” ");
                                if !title_text.is_empty() {
                                    if let Some(sec) = section_stack.last_mut() {
                                        sec.title = Some(title_text);
                                    } else if let Some(ref mut body) = current_body {
                                        body.title = Some(title_text);
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        "section" => {
                            if let Some(completed_sec) = section_stack.pop() {
                                if let Some(parent_sec) = section_stack.last_mut() {
                                    parent_sec.subsections.push(completed_sec);
                                } else if let Some(ref mut body) = current_body {
                                    body.sections.push(completed_sec);
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        "body" => {
                            while let Some(sec) = section_stack.pop() {
                                if let Some(parent_sec) = section_stack.last_mut() {
                                    parent_sec.subsections.push(sec);
                                } else if let Some(ref mut body) = current_body {
                                    body.sections.push(sec);
                                }
                            }
                            if let Some(body) = current_body.take() {
                                bodies.push(body);
                            }
                        }

                        _ => {}
                    }
                }

                Ok(Event::Empty(ref e)) => {
                    let tag_name = local_name(e.name().as_ref()).to_ascii_lowercase();
                    if tag_name == "empty-line" && current_body.is_some() && !in_title {
                        if let Some(sec) = section_stack.last_mut() {
                            sec.paragraphs.push(String::new());
                        } else if let Some(ref mut body) = current_body {
                            body.direct_paragraphs.push(String::new());
                        }
                    }
                }

                Ok(Event::Text(ref e)) => {
                    let raw_str = e.as_ref();
                    let text = quick_xml::escape::unescape(raw_str)
                        .map(|c| c.into_owned())
                        .unwrap_or_else(|_| raw_str.to_string());

                    if in_book_title {
                        book_title.push_str(&text);
                    } else if in_first_name {
                        author_first.push_str(&text);
                    } else if in_middle_name {
                        author_middle.push_str(&text);
                    } else if in_last_name {
                        author_last.push_str(&text);
                    } else if in_title {
                        current_title_paragraph.push_str(&text);
                    } else if current_link_href.is_some() {
                        current_link_text.push_str(&text);
                    } else if in_paragraph {
                        current_paragraph.push_str(&text);
                    }
                }

                Ok(Event::GeneralRef(ref e)) => {
                    let repl = match e.as_ref() {
                        "amp" => "&",
                        "lt" => "<",
                        "gt" => ">",
                        "quot" => "\"",
                        "apos" => "'",
                        _ => "",
                    };
                    if !repl.is_empty() {
                        if in_first_name {
                            author_first.push_str(repl);
                        } else if in_middle_name {
                            author_middle.push_str(repl);
                        } else if in_last_name {
                            author_last.push_str(repl);
                        } else if in_title {
                            current_title_paragraph.push_str(repl);
                        } else if current_link_href.is_some() {
                            current_link_text.push_str(repl);
                        } else if in_paragraph {
                            current_paragraph.push_str(repl);
                        }
                    }
                }

                Ok(Event::Eof) => break,
                Err(_) => {
                    had_error = true;
                    break;
                }
                _ => {}
            }
            buf.clear();
        }

        if let Some(body) = current_body.take() {
            bodies.push(body);
        }

        let mut author_parts = Vec::new();
        if !author_first.trim().is_empty() {
            author_parts.push(author_first.trim());
        }
        if !author_middle.trim().is_empty() {
            author_parts.push(author_middle.trim());
        }
        if !author_last.trim().is_empty() {
            author_parts.push(author_last.trim());
        }
        let mut author = if author_parts.is_empty() {
            "РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ Р°РІС‚РѕСЂ".to_string()
        } else {
            author_parts.join(" ")
        };

        let mut title = if book_title.trim().is_empty() {
            "Р‘РµР· РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЏ".to_string()
        } else {
            book_title.trim().to_string()
        };

        let (mut chapters, mut toc, section_id_to_chapter) = build_chapters_and_toc(bodies);

        if chapters.is_empty() || chapters.iter().all(|c| c.is_empty()) {
            let (fb_title, fb_author, fb_chapters) = extract_fb2_lenient(xml);
            if !fb_chapters.is_empty() && fb_chapters.iter().any(|c| !c.is_empty()) {
                if title == "Р‘РµР· РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЏ" && !fb_title.is_empty() {
                    title = fb_title;
                }
                if author == "РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ Р°РІС‚РѕСЂ" && !fb_author.is_empty() {
                    author = fb_author;
                }
                chapters = fb_chapters;
                toc = chapters
                    .iter()
                    .enumerate()
                    .map(|(i, c)| TocItem {
                        id: c.id.clone(),
                        title: if c.title.is_empty() { format!("Р Р°Р·РґРµР» {}", i + 1) } else { c.title.clone() },
                        chapter_index: i,
                        children: Vec::new(),
                    })
                    .collect();
            } else {
                return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(
                    "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РёР·РІР»РµС‡СЊ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ РёР· РїРѕРІСЂРµР¶РґС‘РЅРЅРѕРіРѕ FB2 РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°".to_string(),
                ));
            }
        }

        if !explicit_toc_links.is_empty() {
            let mut custom_toc = Vec::new();
            for link in explicit_toc_links {
                let target_id = link.href.strip_prefix('#').unwrap_or(&link.href);
                let decoded_id = crate::document::epub::percent_decode_str(target_id);
                let chapter_idx = section_id_to_chapter
                    .get(target_id)
                    .or_else(|| section_id_to_chapter.get(&decoded_id))
                    .copied();

                if let Some(ch_idx) = chapter_idx {
                    custom_toc.push(TocItem {
                        id: Some(target_id.to_string()),
                        title: link.title,
                        chapter_index: ch_idx,
                        children: Vec::new(),
                    });
                }
            }
            if !custom_toc.is_empty() {
                toc = custom_toc;
            }
        }

        Ok(Fb2Document {
            metadata: BookMetadata { title, author },
            chapters,
            toc,
        })
    }
}

struct FlattenContext {
    chapters: Vec<BookChapter>,
    section_id_to_chapter: HashMap<String, usize>,
}

fn build_chapters_and_toc(bodies: Vec<BodyNode>) -> (Vec<BookChapter>, Vec<TocItem>, HashMap<String, usize>) {
    let mut ctx = FlattenContext {
        chapters: Vec::new(),
        section_id_to_chapter: HashMap::new(),
    };
    let mut root_toc: Vec<TocItem> = Vec::new();

    for body in bodies {
        if body
            .name
            .as_deref()
            .unwrap_or("")
            .eq_ignore_ascii_case("toc")
        {
            continue;
        }

        let body_has_direct_text = body.direct_paragraphs.iter().any(|p| !p.trim().is_empty());
        let body_has_title = body
            .title
            .as_ref()
            .map(|t| !t.trim().is_empty())
            .unwrap_or(false);

        let mut body_start_chapter: Option<usize> = None;

        if body_has_direct_text || (body.sections.is_empty() && body_has_title) {
            let idx = ctx.chapters.len();
            body_start_chapter = Some(idx);
            ctx.chapters.push(BookChapter {
                id: None,
                title: body.title.clone().unwrap_or_default(),
                paragraphs: body.direct_paragraphs,
            });
        }

        let mut body_sections_toc: Vec<TocItem> = Vec::new();

        for section in body.sections {
            let (sec_first_ch, sec_tocs) = flatten_section(section, &mut ctx, 0);
            if body_start_chapter.is_none() {
                body_start_chapter = sec_first_ch;
            }
            body_sections_toc.extend(sec_tocs);
        }

        if body_has_title {
            let title = body.title.unwrap_or_default();
            let chapter_index = body_start_chapter.unwrap_or(0);
            root_toc.push(TocItem {
                id: None,
                title,
                chapter_index,
                children: body_sections_toc,
            });
        } else {
            root_toc.extend(body_sections_toc);
        }
    }

    (ctx.chapters, root_toc, ctx.section_id_to_chapter)
}

fn flatten_section(
    mut section: SectionNode,
    ctx: &mut FlattenContext,
    depth: usize,
) -> (Option<usize>, Vec<TocItem>) {
    let has_own_title = section
        .title
        .as_ref()
        .map(|t| !t.trim().is_empty())
        .unwrap_or(false);

    if has_own_title && section.paragraphs.is_empty() && !section.subsections.is_empty() {
        while !section.subsections.is_empty() {
            let is_untitled_leaf = section.subsections[0]
                .title
                .as_ref()
                .map(|t| t.trim().is_empty())
                .unwrap_or(true)
                && section.subsections[0].subsections.is_empty();

            if is_untitled_leaf {
                let first_sub = section.subsections.remove(0);
                if section.id.is_none() {
                    section.id = first_sub.id;
                }
                section.paragraphs.extend(first_sub.paragraphs);
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    if depth >= 32 {
        let mut overflow_paragraphs = Vec::new();
        for sub in section.subsections.drain(..) {
            overflow_paragraphs.extend(sub.paragraphs);
            for s in sub.subsections {
                overflow_paragraphs.extend(s.paragraphs);
            }
        }
        section.paragraphs.extend(overflow_paragraphs);
    }

    let has_own_paragraphs = section.paragraphs.iter().any(|p| !p.trim().is_empty());
    let mut own_chapter_idx: Option<usize> = None;

    let should_create_chapter = if has_own_paragraphs {
        true
    } else if section.subsections.is_empty() {
        has_own_title
    } else {
        false
    };

    if should_create_chapter {
        let idx = ctx.chapters.len();
        own_chapter_idx = Some(idx);
        ctx.chapters.push(BookChapter {
            id: section.id.clone(),
            title: section.title.clone().unwrap_or_default(),
            paragraphs: section.paragraphs,
        });
    }

    let mut child_tocs: Vec<TocItem> = Vec::new();
    let mut first_child_chapter: Option<usize> = None;

    for sub in section.subsections {
        let (sub_first_ch, sub_tocs) = flatten_section(sub, ctx, depth + 1);
        if first_child_chapter.is_none() {
            first_child_chapter = sub_first_ch;
        }
        child_tocs.extend(sub_tocs);
    }

    let effective_chapter_idx = own_chapter_idx.or(first_child_chapter);

    if let (Some(id), Some(ch_idx)) = (&section.id, effective_chapter_idx) {
        ctx.section_id_to_chapter.entry(id.clone()).or_insert(ch_idx);
        ctx.section_id_to_chapter
            .entry(crate::document::epub::percent_decode_str(id))
            .or_insert(ch_idx);
    }

    if has_own_title {
        let title = section.title.unwrap_or_default();
        let chapter_index = effective_chapter_idx.unwrap_or(0);
        let toc_item = TocItem {
            id: section.id,
            title,
            chapter_index,
            children: child_tocs,
        };
        (effective_chapter_idx, vec![toc_item])
    } else {
        (effective_chapter_idx, child_tocs)
    }
}

pub fn extract_fb2_lenient(xml: &str) -> (String, String, Vec<BookChapter>) {
    let mut title = String::new();
    let mut author = String::new();
    let mut chapters = Vec::new();
    let mut current_paragraphs = Vec::new();
    let mut current_title = String::new();
    let mut in_title = false;
    let mut in_p = false;
    let mut current_p = String::new();

    let mut i = 0;
    let bytes = xml.as_bytes();
    while i < bytes.len() {
        if bytes[i] == b'<' {
            let rest = &xml[i + 1..];
            if let Some(end_bracket) = rest.find('>') {
                let tag_raw = rest[..end_bracket].trim();
                let is_closing = tag_raw.starts_with('/');
                let tag_name = tag_raw
                    .split_whitespace()
                    .next()
                    .unwrap_or("")
                    .trim_start_matches('/')
                    .to_ascii_lowercase();

                match tag_name.as_str() {
                    "book-title" => {
                        if is_closing && title.is_empty() {
                            title = current_p.trim().to_string();
                            current_p.clear();
                        }
                    }
                    "author" => {
                        if is_closing && author.is_empty() {
                            author = current_p.trim().to_string();
                            current_p.clear();
                        }
                    }
                    "title" => {
                        in_title = !is_closing;
                        if is_closing {
                            current_title = current_p.trim().to_string();
                            current_p.clear();
                        }
                    }
                    "section" if is_closing => {
                        if !current_paragraphs.is_empty() || !current_title.is_empty() {
                            chapters.push(BookChapter {
                                id: None,
                                title: std::mem::take(&mut current_title),
                                paragraphs: std::mem::take(&mut current_paragraphs),
                            });
                        }
                    }
                    "p" | "v" | "subtitle" => {
                        in_p = !is_closing;
                        if is_closing {
                            let p_clean = normalize_whitespace(&current_p);
                            if !p_clean.is_empty() {
                                if in_title {
                                    current_title = p_clean;
                                } else {
                                    current_paragraphs.push(p_clean);
                                }
                            }
                            current_p.clear();
                        }
                    }
                    _ => {}
                }
                i += 1 + end_bracket + 1;
                continue;
            }
        }

        if in_p || title.is_empty() || author.is_empty() {
            let start = i;
            while i < bytes.len() && bytes[i] != b'<' {
                i += 1;
            }
            if let Ok(text_slice) = std::str::from_utf8(&bytes[start..i]) {
                let unescaped = quick_xml::escape::unescape(text_slice)
                    .map(|c| c.into_owned())
                    .unwrap_or_else(|_| text_slice.to_string());
                current_p.push_str(&unescaped);
            }
        } else {
            i += 1;
        }
    }

    if !current_paragraphs.is_empty() || !current_title.is_empty() {
        chapters.push(BookChapter {
            id: None,
            title: current_title,
            paragraphs: current_paragraphs,
        });
    }

    (title, author, chapters)
}

fn local_name(name: &str) -> &str {
    if let Some(pos) = name.rfind(':') {
        &name[pos + 1..]
    } else {
        name
    }
}

fn decode_text(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    if bytes.starts_with(&[0xEF, 0xBB, 0xBF]) {
        return String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes[3..]).into_owned();
    }

    let probe_len = bytes.len().min(300);
    let probe_str = String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes[..probe_len]);

    if let Some(enc_label) = find_xml_encoding(&probe_str)
        && let Some(encoding) = encoding_rs::Encoding::for_label(enc_label.as_bytes())
    {
        let (decoded, _, _) = encoding.decode(bytes);
        return decoded.into_owned();
    }

    let (decoded, _, _) = encoding_rs::UTF_8.decode(bytes);
    decoded.into_owned()
}

fn find_xml_encoding(header: &str) -> Option<&str> {
    let lower = header.to_lowercase();
    let enc_idx = lower.find("encoding=")?;
    let remainder = &header[enc_idx + 9..];
    let quote = remainder.chars().next()?;
    if quote != '"' && quote != '\'' {
        return None;
    }
    let rest = &remainder[1..];
    let end_idx = rest.find(quote)?;
    Some(&rest[..end_idx])
}

fn normalize_whitespace(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut result = String::with_capacity(s.len());
    let mut last_was_space = false;
    for c in s.chars() {
        if c.is_whitespace() {
            if !last_was_space && !result.is_empty() {
                result.push(' ');
                last_was_space = true;
            }
        } else {
            result.push(c);
            last_was_space = false;
        }
    }
    result.trim().to_string()
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum DocumentError {
    Io(String),
    Unsupported(String),
    Corrupted(String),
    LimitExceeded(String),
}

impl std::fmt::Display for DocumentError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::Io(msg)
            | Self::Unsupported(msg)
            | Self::Corrupted(msg)
            | Self::LimitExceeded(msg) => write!(f, "{msg}"),
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for DocumentError {}

impl From<std::io::Error> for DocumentError {
    fn from(e: std::io::Error) -> Self {
        Self::Io(e.to_string())
    }
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct DecodedImage {
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
    pub rgba: Vec<u8>,
}

pub struct PageInfo {

    pub display_name: String,
    pub chapter_title: Option<String>,
}

    fn page_name(&self, index: usize) -> &str;
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    fn page_info(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&PageInfo> {
        let _ = index;
        None
    }
    fn load_page_image(&self, index: usize) -> Result<DecodedImage, DocumentError>;
    fn load_page_image_scaled(
        &self,
        index: usize,
        _scale: f32,
    ) -> Result<DecodedImage, DocumentError> {
        self.load_page_image(index)
    }
    fn extract_page_text(&self, _index: usize) -> Option<String> {
        None
    }
    fn extract_page_text_spans(&self, _index: usize) -> Option<Vec<PageTextSpan>> {
        None
    }
    fn is_manga(&self) -> bool {
        false
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct BookMetadata {
    pub title: String,
    pub author: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct BookChapter {
    pub id: Option<String>,
    pub title: String,
    pub paragraphs: Vec<String>,
}

impl BookChapter {
    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.title.trim().is_empty() && self.paragraphs.iter().all(|p| p.trim().is_empty())
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct TocItem {
    pub id: Option<String>,
    pub title: String,
    pub chapter_index: usize,
    pub children: Vec<TocItem>,
}

pub enum DocumentViewKind {
    Text,
    Pdf,
    Comic,
}

pub trait Document: Send + Sync {
    fn metadata(&self) -> &BookMetadata;
    fn chapters(&self) -> &[BookChapter];
    fn toc(&self) -> &[TocItem] {
        &[]
    }
    fn format_name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "Р”РѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚"
    }
    fn has_custom_toc(&self) -> bool {
        !self.toc().is_empty()
    }
    fn view_kind(&self) -> DocumentViewKind {
        DocumentViewKind::Text
    }
    fn page_content(&self) -> Option<&dyn PageContent> {
        None
    }
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    fn text_content(&self) -> Option<&dyn TextContent> {
        None
    }
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    fn toc_capability(&self) -> Option<&dyn HasToc> {
        None
    }
}

pub const MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES: usize = 10_000;
pub const MAX_SINGLE_ENTRY_BYTES: u64 = 50 * 1024 * 1024;
pub const MAX_TOTAL_UNPACK_BYTES: u64 = 500 * 1024 * 1024;

pub fn is_safe_archive_path(p: &str) -> bool {
    let trimmed = p.trim();
    if trimmed.is_empty() || trimmed.starts_with('/') || trimmed.starts_with('\\') || trimmed.contains(':') {
        return false;
    }
    let decoded = epub::percent_decode_str(trimmed);
    let dec_trimmed = decoded.trim();
    if dec_trimmed.is_empty() || dec_trimmed.starts_with('/') || dec_trimmed.starts_with('\\') || dec_trimmed.contains(':') {
        return false;
    }
    let normalized = dec_trimmed.replace('\\', "/");
    for seg in normalized.split('/') {
        if seg == ".." {
            return false;
        }
    }
    let path = Path::new(&normalized);
    !path.is_absolute()
        && !path.components().any(|c| {
            matches!(
                c,
                std::path::Component::ParentDir
                    | std::path::Component::RootDir
                    | std::path::Component::Prefix(_)
            )
        })
}

pub fn read_bounded<R: std::io::Read>(
    reader: R,
    max_entry_bytes: u64,
    total_unpacked: &mut u64,
    max_total_bytes: u64,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, DocumentError> {
    use std::io::Read;
    let mut buf = Vec::new();
    let n = reader
        .take(max_entry_bytes.saturating_add(1))
        .read_to_end(&mut buf)
        .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Io(format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° С‡С‚РµРЅРёСЏ РёР· Р°СЂС…РёРІР°: {e}")))?
        as u64;
    if n > max_entry_bytes {
        return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(format!(
            "РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂР° С„Р°Р№Р»Р° РІ Р°СЂС…РёРІРµ ({max_entry_bytes} Р±Р°Р№С‚)"
        )));
    }
    let Some(new_total) = total_unpacked.checked_add(n) else {
        return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(format!(
            "РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ РѕР±С‰РёР№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ СЂР°СЃРїР°РєРѕРІРєРё Р°СЂС…РёРІР° ({max_total_bytes} Р±Р°Р№С‚)"
        )));
    };
    if new_total > max_total_bytes {
        return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(format!(
            "РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ РѕР±С‰РёР№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ СЂР°СЃРїР°РєРѕРІРєРё Р°СЂС…РёРІР° ({max_total_bytes} Р±Р°Р№С‚)"
        )));
    }
    *total_unpacked = new_total;
    Ok(buf)
}

use crate::document::{
    ComicDocument, DjvuDocument, Document, DocumentError, EpubDocument, Fb2Document, PdfDocument,
    TxtDocument,
};
use std::path::Path;

pub trait DocumentFormat: Send + Sync {
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str;
    fn extensions(&self) -> &'static [&'static str];
    fn probe(&self, path: &Path) -> bool;
    fn open(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<Box<dyn Document>, DocumentError>;
}

pub struct PdfFormat;
impl DocumentFormat for PdfFormat {
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "PDF"
    }
    fn extensions(&self) -> &'static [&'static str] {
        &["pdf"]
    }
    fn probe(&self, path: &Path) -> bool {
        if !path.is_file() {
            return false;
        }
        use std::io::Read;
        if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::File::open(path) {
            let mut buf = [0u8; 64];
            if let Ok(n) = f.read(&mut buf) {
                return buf[..n].windows(4).any(|w| w == b"%PDF");
            }
        }
        false
    }
    fn open(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<Box<dyn Document>, DocumentError> {
        let doc = PdfDocument::from_file(path)?;
        Ok(Box::new(doc))
    }
}

pub struct DjvuFormat;
impl DocumentFormat for DjvuFormat {
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "DjVu"
    }
    fn extensions(&self) -> &'static [&'static str] {
        &["djvu", "djv"]
    }
    fn probe(&self, path: &Path) -> bool {
        if !path.is_file() {
            return false;
        }
        use std::io::Read;
        if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::File::open(path) {
            let mut buf = [0u8; 64];
            if let Ok(n) = f.read(&mut buf) {
                return buf[..n].windows(8).any(|w| w == b"AT&TFORM");
            }
        }
        false
    }
    fn open(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<Box<dyn Document>, DocumentError> {
        let doc = DjvuDocument::from_file(path)?;
        Ok(Box::new(doc))
    }
}

pub struct ComicFormat;
impl DocumentFormat for ComicFormat {
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "Comic / Manga"
    }
    fn extensions(&self) -> &'static [&'static str] {
        &["cbz", "cbr", "cb7"]
    }
    fn probe(&self, path: &Path) -> bool {
        if path.is_dir() {
            return true;
        }
        let ext = path
            .extension()
            .and_then(|e| e.to_str())
            .map(|e| e.to_lowercase())
            .unwrap_or_default();
        if matches!(ext.as_str(), "cbz" | "cbr" | "cb7" | "zip" | "rar" | "7z") {
            return true;
        }
        use std::io::Read;
        if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::File::open(path) {
            let mut magic = [0u8; 8];
            if let Ok(n) = f.read(&mut magic)
                && ((n >= 4 && &magic[0..4] == b"PK\x03\x04")
                    || (n >= 7 && &magic[0..7] == b"Rar!\x1a\x07\x00")
                    || (n >= 6 && &magic[0..6] == b"7z\xbc\xaf\x27\x1c"))
            {
                return true;
            }
        }
        false
    }
    fn open(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<Box<dyn Document>, DocumentError> {
        let doc = ComicDocument::from_path(path)?;
        Ok(Box::new(doc))
    }
}

pub struct EpubFormat;
impl DocumentFormat for EpubFormat {
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "EPUB"
    }
    fn extensions(&self) -> &'static [&'static str] {
        &["epub"]
    }
    fn probe(&self, path: &Path) -> bool {
        if !path.is_file() {
            return false;
        }
        use std::io::Read;
        if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::File::open(path) {
            let mut magic = [0u8; 4];
            if let Ok(4) = f.read(&mut magic) {
                return &magic == b"PK\x03\x04";
            }
        }
        false
    }
    fn open(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<Box<dyn Document>, DocumentError> {
        let doc = EpubDocument::from_file(path)?;
        Ok(Box::new(doc))
    }
}

pub struct Fb2Format;
impl DocumentFormat for Fb2Format {
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "FB2"
    }
    fn extensions(&self) -> &'static [&'static str] {
        &["fb2", "xml"]
    }
    fn probe(&self, path: &Path) -> bool {
        if !path.is_file() {
            return false;
        }
        use std::io::Read;
        if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::File::open(path) {
            let mut buf = [0u8; 512];
            if let Ok(n) = f.read(&mut buf) {
                let s = String::from_utf8_lossy(&buf[..n]).to_lowercase();
                return s.contains("<fictionbook") || s.contains("<?xml");
            }
        }
        false
    }
    fn open(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<Box<dyn Document>, DocumentError> {
        let doc = Fb2Document::from_file(path)?;
        Ok(Box::new(doc))
    }
}

pub struct TxtFormat;
impl DocumentFormat for TxtFormat {
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "TXT / MD / LOG"
    }
    fn extensions(&self) -> &'static [&'static str] {
        &["txt", "md", "markdown", "log"]
    }
    fn probe(&self, path: &Path) -> bool {
        if !path.is_file() {
            return false;
        }
        let ext = path
            .extension()
            .and_then(|e| e.to_str())
            .map(|e| e.to_lowercase())
            .unwrap_or_default();
        self.extensions().contains(&ext.as_str())
    }
    fn open(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<Box<dyn Document>, DocumentError> {
        let doc = TxtDocument::from_file(path)?;
        Ok(Box::new(doc))
    }
}

pub static FORMAT_REGISTRY: &[&dyn DocumentFormat] = &[
    &PdfFormat,
    &DjvuFormat,
    &ComicFormat,
    &EpubFormat,
    &Fb2Format,
    &TxtFormat,
];

pub fn open_with_registry(path: &Path) -> Result<Box<dyn Document>, DocumentError> {
    if path.is_dir() {
        return ComicFormat.open(path);
    }

    let ext = path
        .extension()
        .and_then(|e| e.to_str())
        .map(|e| e.to_lowercase())
        .unwrap_or_default();

    if !ext.is_empty() {
        for fmt in FORMAT_REGISTRY {
            if fmt.extensions().contains(&ext.as_str()) {
                return fmt.open(path);
            }
        }
    }

    let mut last_err = None;
    for fmt in FORMAT_REGISTRY {
        if fmt.probe(path) {
            match fmt.open(path) {
                Ok(doc) => return Ok(doc),
                Err(e) => last_err = Some(e),
            }
        }
    }

    Err(last_err.unwrap_or_else(|| {
        DocumentError::Unsupported(format!("РќРµРїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РёРІР°РµРјС‹Р№ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ С„Р°Р№Р»Р°: {}", path.display()))
    }))
}
