use crate::document::{
    BookChapter, BookMetadata, Document, DocumentError, HasToc, TextContent, TocItem,
};
use std::path::Path;

pub struct TxtDocument {
    metadata: BookMetadata,
    chapters: Vec<BookChapter>,
    toc: Vec<TocItem>,
}

impl TextContent for TxtDocument {
    fn chapters(&self) -> &[BookChapter] {
        &self.chapters
    }
}

impl HasToc for TxtDocument {
    fn toc(&self) -> &[TocItem] {
        &self.toc
    }
}

impl Document for TxtDocument {
    fn metadata(&self) -> &BookMetadata {
        &self.metadata
    }

    fn chapters(&self) -> &[BookChapter] {
        &self.chapters
    }

    fn toc(&self) -> &[TocItem] {
        &self.toc
    }

    fn format_name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "TXT"
    }

    fn has_custom_toc(&self) -> bool {
        self.toc.iter().any(|item| item.id.as_deref().unwrap_or("").starts_with("md_")) && self.toc.len() > 1
    }

    fn text_content(&self) -> Option<&dyn TextContent> {
        Some(self)
    }

    fn toc_capability(&self) -> Option<&dyn HasToc> {
        Some(self)
    }
}

impl TxtDocument {
    pub const MAX_TXT_SIZE: u64 = 100 * 1024 * 1024;

    pub fn from_text(text: &str, default_title: &str) -> Self {
        Self::from_bytes(text.as_bytes(), default_title, "txt").unwrap_or_else(|_| Self {
            metadata: BookMetadata {
                title: default_title.to_string(),
                author: String::new(),
            },
            chapters: Vec::new(),
            toc: Vec::new(),
        })
    }

    pub fn from_file<P: AsRef<Path>>(path: P) -> Result<Self, DocumentError> {
        let path_ref = path.as_ref();
        let meta = std::fs::metadata(path_ref).map_err(|e| {
            DocumentError::Io(format!(
                "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РїСЂРѕС‡РёС‚Р°С‚СЊ РјРµС‚Р°РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРІРѕРіРѕ С„Р°Р№Р»Р°: {e}"
            ))
        })?;
        if meta.len() > Self::MAX_TXT_SIZE {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                "Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРІРѕРіРѕ С„Р°Р№Р»Р° РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°РµС‚ РґРѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёРјС‹Р№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ (100 РњР‘)".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        let bytes = std::fs::read(path_ref)
            .map_err(|e| DocumentError::Io(format!("РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РїСЂРѕС‡РёС‚Р°С‚СЊ С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Р№ С„Р°Р№Р»: {e}")))?;

        let default_title = path_ref
            .file_stem()
            .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
            .unwrap_or("РўРµРєСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Р№ РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚")
            .to_string();

        let ext = path_ref
            .extension()
            .and_then(|e| e.to_str())
            .map(|e| e.to_lowercase())
            .unwrap_or_default();

        Self::from_bytes(&bytes, &default_title, &ext)
    }

    pub fn from_bytes(bytes: &[u8], default_title: &str, ext: &str) -> Result<Self, DocumentError> {
        if bytes.len() as u64 > Self::MAX_TXT_SIZE {
            return Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(
                "Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРІС‹С… РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°РµС‚ РґРѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёРјС‹Р№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ (100 РњР‘)".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        let sample = &bytes[..bytes.len().min(4096)];
        let is_utf16 = bytes.starts_with(&[0xFF, 0xFE]) || bytes.starts_with(&[0xFE, 0xFF]);
        if !is_utf16 && sample.contains(&0) {
            return Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(
                "Р¤Р°Р№Р» СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ Р±РёРЅР°СЂРЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ Рё РЅРµ СЏРІР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Рј РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚РѕРј".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        let text = decode_text_bytes(bytes);
        let is_markdown = ext == "md" || ext == "markdown";
        let is_log = ext == "log";

        let (chapters, toc) = if is_markdown {
            parse_markdown_chapters(&text, default_title)
        } else if is_log {
            parse_log_chapters(&text, default_title)
        } else {
            parse_plain_text_chapters(&text, default_title)
        };

        let title = toc
            .first()
            .map(|t| t.title.clone())
            .filter(|t| is_markdown && !t.is_empty())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| default_title.to_string());

        Ok(Self {
            metadata: BookMetadata {
                title,
                author: String::new(),
            },
            chapters,
            toc,
        })
    }
}

fn decode_text_bytes(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    if bytes.starts_with(&[0xEF, 0xBB, 0xBF]) {
        return String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes[3..]).into_owned();
    }
    if bytes.starts_with(&[0xFF, 0xFE]) {
        let (cow, _, _) = encoding_rs::UTF_16LE.decode(&bytes[2..]);
        return cow.into_owned();
    }
    if bytes.starts_with(&[0xFE, 0xFF]) {
        let (cow, _, _) = encoding_rs::UTF_16BE.decode(&bytes[2..]);
        return cow.into_owned();
    }
    if let Ok(s) = std::str::from_utf8(bytes) {
        return s.to_string();
    }
    let (cow, _, _) = encoding_rs::WINDOWS_1251.decode(bytes);
    cow.into_owned()
}

fn parse_markdown_chapters(text: &str, default_title: &str) -> (Vec<BookChapter>, Vec<TocItem>) {
    let mut chapters = Vec::new();
    let mut toc = Vec::new();
    let mut current_title = String::new();
    let mut current_paragraphs = Vec::new();
    let mut current_p = String::new();

    for line in text.lines() {
        let trimmed = line.trim();
        if let Some(heading) = trimmed
            .strip_prefix("# ")
            .or_else(|| trimmed.strip_prefix("## "))
            .or_else(|| trimmed.strip_prefix("### "))
        {
            if !current_p.trim().is_empty() {
                current_paragraphs.push(current_p.trim().to_string());
                current_p.clear();
            }
            if !current_title.is_empty() || !current_paragraphs.is_empty() {
                let idx = chapters.len();
                let ch_title = if current_title.is_empty() {
                    default_title.to_string()
                } else {
                    current_title.clone()
                };
                chapters.push(BookChapter {
                    id: Some(format!("md_{idx}")),
                    title: ch_title.clone(),
                    paragraphs: std::mem::take(&mut current_paragraphs),
                });
                toc.push(TocItem {
                    id: Some(format!("md_{idx}")),
                    title: ch_title,
                    chapter_index: idx,
                    children: Vec::new(),
                });
            }
            current_title = heading.trim().to_string();
        } else if trimmed.is_empty() {
            if !current_p.trim().is_empty() {
                current_paragraphs.push(current_p.trim().to_string());
                current_p.clear();
            }
        } else {
            if !current_p.is_empty() {
                current_p.push(' ');
            }
            current_p.push_str(trimmed);
        }
    }

    if !current_p.trim().is_empty() {
        current_paragraphs.push(current_p.trim().to_string());
    }
    if !current_title.is_empty() || !current_paragraphs.is_empty() || chapters.is_empty() {
        let idx = chapters.len();
        let ch_title = if current_title.is_empty() {
            default_title.to_string()
        } else {
            current_title
        };
        chapters.push(BookChapter {
            id: Some(format!("md_{idx}")),
            title: ch_title.clone(),
            paragraphs: current_paragraphs,
        });
        toc.push(TocItem {
            id: Some(format!("md_{idx}")),
            title: ch_title,
            chapter_index: idx,
            children: Vec::new(),
        });
    }

    (chapters, toc)
}

fn parse_log_chapters(text: &str, default_title: &str) -> (Vec<BookChapter>, Vec<TocItem>) {
    const LINES_PER_CHUNK: usize = 300;
    let lines: Vec<String> = text
        .lines()
        .filter(|l| !l.trim().is_empty())
        .map(|l| l.to_string())
        .collect();

    if lines.is_empty() {
        return (
            vec![BookChapter {
                id: Some("log_0".to_string()),
                title: default_title.to_string(),
                paragraphs: vec![String::new()],
            }],
            vec![TocItem {
                id: Some("log_0".to_string()),
                title: default_title.to_string(),
                chapter_index: 0,
                children: Vec::new(),
            }],
        );
    }

    let mut chapters = Vec::new();
    let mut toc = Vec::new();
    for (idx, chunk) in lines.chunks(LINES_PER_CHUNK).enumerate() {
        let start_line = idx * LINES_PER_CHUNK + 1;
        let end_line = start_line + chunk.len() - 1;
        let title = if lines.len() <= LINES_PER_CHUNK {
            default_title.to_string()
        } else {
            format!("{default_title} (СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё {start_line}вЂ“{end_line})")
        };
        chapters.push(BookChapter {
            id: Some(format!("log_{idx}")),
            title: title.clone(),
            paragraphs: chunk.to_vec(),
        });
        toc.push(TocItem {
            id: Some(format!("log_{idx}")),
            title,
            chapter_index: idx,
            children: Vec::new(),
        });
    }
    (chapters, toc)
}

fn parse_plain_text_chapters(text: &str, default_title: &str) -> (Vec<BookChapter>, Vec<TocItem>) {
    let mut paragraphs = Vec::new();
    let mut current_p = String::new();

    for line in text.lines() {
        let trimmed = line.trim();
        if trimmed.is_empty() {
            if !current_p.is_empty() {
                paragraphs.push(std::mem::take(&mut current_p));
            }
        } else {
            if !current_p.is_empty() {
                current_p.push(' ');
            }
            current_p.push_str(trimmed);
        }
    }
    if !current_p.is_empty() {
        paragraphs.push(current_p);
    }

    const PARAGRAPHS_PER_CHAPTER: usize = 250;
    if paragraphs.is_empty() {
        paragraphs.push(String::new());
    }

    let mut chapters = Vec::new();
    let mut toc = Vec::new();
    for (idx, chunk) in paragraphs.chunks(PARAGRAPHS_PER_CHAPTER).enumerate() {
        let title = if paragraphs.len() <= PARAGRAPHS_PER_CHAPTER {
            default_title.to_string()
        } else {
            format!("Р Р°Р·РґРµР» {}", idx + 1)
        };
        chapters.push(BookChapter {
            id: Some(format!("txt_{idx}")),
            title: title.clone(),
            paragraphs: chunk.to_vec(),
        });
        toc.push(TocItem {
            id: Some(format!("txt_{idx}")),
            title,
            chapter_index: idx,
            children: Vec::new(),
        });
    }
    (chapters, toc)
}

use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

fn get_ipc_dir() -> PathBuf {
    if let Some(parent) = crate::settings::AppSettings::get_settings_path().parent() {
        let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(parent);
        return parent.to_path_buf();
    }
    std::env::temp_dir()
}

fn get_ipc_request_path() -> PathBuf {
    get_ipc_dir().join("quietreader_ipc.req")
}

fn get_hwnd_file_path() -> PathBuf {
    get_ipc_dir().join("quietreader_hwnd.id")
}

pub struct SingleInstanceGuard {
    #[cfg(windows)]
    mutex_handle: isize,
    #[cfg(not(windows))]
    lock_file: PathBuf,
}

impl Drop for SingleInstanceGuard {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(get_hwnd_file_path());
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(get_ipc_request_path());
        #[cfg(windows)]
        unsafe {
            if self.mutex_handle != 0 {
                win32_ipc::CloseHandle(self.mutex_handle);
            }
        }
        #[cfg(not(windows))]
        {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&self.lock_file);
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(windows)]
mod win32_ipc {
    use super::{get_hwnd_file_path, get_ipc_request_path, SingleInstanceGuard};
    use std::path::PathBuf;
    use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicIsize, Ordering};

    const ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS: u32 = 183;
    const SW_RESTORE: i32 = 9;
    const SW_SHOW: i32 = 5;
    const ASFW_ANY: u32 = 0xFFFF_FFFF;
    const WM_NULL: u32 = 0x0000;

    static REGISTERED_HWND: AtomicIsize = AtomicIsize::new(0);

    #[link(name = "kernel32")]
    unsafe extern "system" {
        fn CreateMutexW(
            lp_mutex_attributes: *const core::ffi::c_void,
            b_initial_owner: i32,
            lp_name: *const u16,
        ) -> isize;
        fn GetLastError() -> u32;
        fn GetCurrentProcessId() -> u32;
        pub fn CloseHandle(h_object: isize) -> i32;
    }

    #[link(name = "user32")]
    unsafe extern "system" {
        fn GetActiveWindow() -> isize;
        fn GetForegroundWindow() -> isize;
        fn FindWindowW(lp_class_name: *const u16, lp_window_name: *const u16) -> isize;
        fn IsWindow(hwnd: isize) -> i32;
        fn IsIconic(hwnd: isize) -> i32;
        fn ShowWindow(hwnd: isize, n_cmd_show: i32) -> i32;
        fn SetForegroundWindow(hwnd: isize) -> i32;
        fn BringWindowToTop(hwnd: isize) -> i32;
        fn AllowSetForegroundWindow(dw_process_id: u32) -> i32;
        fn PostMessageW(hwnd: isize, msg: u32, wparam: usize, lparam: isize) -> i32;
        fn GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd: isize, lpdw_process_id: *mut u32) -> u32;
    }

    fn to_wide(s: &str) -> Vec<u16> {
        s.encode_utf16().chain(std::iter::once(0)).collect()
    }

    pub fn is_own_process_window(hwnd: isize) -> bool {
        if hwnd == 0 {
            return false;
        }
        let mut proc_id = 0u32;
        unsafe {
            GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, &mut proc_id);
            proc_id != 0 && proc_id == GetCurrentProcessId()
        }
    }

    fn find_primary_hwnd() -> isize {
        if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(get_hwnd_file_path())
            && let Ok(val) = text.trim().parse::<isize>()
            && val != 0
            && unsafe { IsWindow(val) != 0 }
        {
            return val;
        }
        let title = to_wide("QuietReader");
        let hwnd = unsafe { FindWindowW(std::ptr::null(), title.as_ptr()) };
        if is_own_process_window(hwnd) {
            hwnd
        } else {
            0
        }
    }

    pub fn focus_hwnd(hwnd: isize) {
        if hwnd == 0 {
            return;
        }
        unsafe {
            if IsIconic(hwnd) != 0 {
                ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_RESTORE);
            } else {
                ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_SHOW);
            }
            BringWindowToTop(hwnd);
            SetForegroundWindow(hwnd);
            PostMessageW(hwnd, WM_NULL, 0, 0);
        }
    }

    pub fn acquire_single_instance(initial_file: &Option<PathBuf>) -> Option<SingleInstanceGuard> {
        let mutex_name = to_wide("Local\\QuietReader_SingleInstance_Mutex_v1");
        unsafe {
            let handle = CreateMutexW(std::ptr::null(), 0, mutex_name.as_ptr());
            if handle == 0 {
                return Some(SingleInstanceGuard { mutex_handle: 0 });
            }

            if GetLastError() == ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS {
                CloseHandle(handle);

                let mut payload = String::from("ACTIVATE\n");
                if let Some(path) = initial_file {
                    if path.is_file() {
                        let abs = std::fs::canonicalize(path).unwrap_or_else(|_| path.clone());
                        payload.push_str(&abs.to_string_lossy());
                        payload.push('\n');
                    }
                }
                let _ = std::fs::write(get_ipc_request_path(), payload);

                AllowSetForegroundWindow(ASFW_ANY);
                let hwnd = find_primary_hwnd();
                focus_hwnd(hwnd);

                None
            } else {
                let _ = std::fs::remove_file(get_ipc_request_path());
                Some(SingleInstanceGuard {
                    mutex_handle: handle,
                })
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn register_primary_window_hwnd() {
        if REGISTERED_HWND.load(Ordering::Relaxed) != 0 {
            return;
        }
        unsafe {
            let mut hwnd = GetActiveWindow();
            if !is_own_process_window(hwnd) {
                hwnd = GetForegroundWindow();
            }
            if !is_own_process_window(hwnd) {
                let title = to_wide("QuietReader");
                hwnd = FindWindowW(std::ptr::null(), title.as_ptr());
            }
            if is_own_process_window(hwnd) {
                REGISTERED_HWND.store(hwnd, Ordering::Relaxed);
                let _ = std::fs::write(get_hwnd_file_path(), hwnd.to_string());
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn focus_current_process_window() {
        let mut hwnd = REGISTERED_HWND.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
        if hwnd == 0 {
            hwnd = find_primary_hwnd();
        }
        focus_hwnd(hwnd);
    }
}

pub fn acquire_single_instance(initial_file: &Option<PathBuf>) -> Option<SingleInstanceGuard> {
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        win32_ipc::acquire_single_instance(initial_file)
    }
    #[cfg(not(windows))]
    {
        let lock_file = get_ipc_dir().join("quietreader_instance.lock");
        if lock_file.exists()
            && let Ok(pid_str) = std::fs::read_to_string(&lock_file)
            && let Ok(pid) = pid_str.trim().parse::<u32>()
        {
            let proc_alive = std::path::Path::new(&format!("/proc/{pid}")).exists()
                || std::process::Command::new("kill")
                    .args(["-0", &pid.to_string()])
                    .output()
                    .map(|o| o.status.success())
                    .unwrap_or(false);
            if proc_alive {
                let mut payload = String::from("ACTIVATE\n");
                if let Some(path) = initial_file {
                    if path.is_file() {
                        let abs = std::fs::canonicalize(path).unwrap_or_else(|_| path.clone());
                        payload.push_str(&abs.to_string_lossy());
                        payload.push('\n');
                    }
                }
                let _ = std::fs::write(get_ipc_request_path(), payload);
                return None;
            }
        }
        let _ = std::fs::write(&lock_file, std::process::id().to_string());
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(get_ipc_request_path());
        Some(SingleInstanceGuard { lock_file })
    }
}

pub fn register_primary_window_hwnd() {
    #[cfg(windows)]
    win32_ipc::register_primary_window_hwnd();
}

static LAST_IPC_POLL: Mutex<Option<Instant>> = Mutex::new(None);

pub fn poll_single_instance_requests() -> (bool, Vec<PathBuf>) {
    if let Ok(mut guard) = LAST_IPC_POLL.lock() {
        let now = Instant::now();
        if let Some(last) = *guard
            && now.duration_since(last) < Duration::from_millis(200)
        {
            return (false, Vec::new());
        }
        *guard = Some(now);
    }

    let req_path = get_ipc_request_path();
    if !req_path.exists() {
        return (false, Vec::new());
    }

    let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&req_path).unwrap_or_default();
    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&req_path);

    let mut files = Vec::new();
    for line in content.lines() {
        let trimmed = line.trim();
        if !trimmed.is_empty() && trimmed != "ACTIVATE" {
            let p = PathBuf::from(trimmed);
            if p.is_file() {
                files.push(p);
            }
        }
    }

    #[cfg(windows)]
    win32_ipc::focus_current_process_window();

    (true, files)
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\Almas\Documents\QuietReader\src\platform\window.rs ===
pub fn is_window_accessible_in_work_area(
    x: i32,
    y: i32,
    width: i32,
    height: i32,
    work_left: i32,
    work_top: i32,
    work_right: i32,
    work_bottom: i32,
) -> bool {
    if width <= 0 || height <= 0 || work_right <= work_left || work_bottom <= work_top {
        return false;
    }

    let grab_left = x + 16;
    let grab_right = (x + width - 16).max(grab_left + 64);
    let grab_top = y;
    let grab_bottom = y + 40;

    let grab_overlap_w = grab_right.min(work_right) - grab_left.max(work_left);
    let grab_overlap_h = grab_bottom.min(work_bottom) - grab_top.max(work_top);

    let body_overlap_w = (x + width).min(work_right) - x.max(work_left);
    let body_overlap_h = (y + height).min(work_bottom) - y.max(work_top);

    y >= work_top - 8
        && grab_overlap_w >= 80
        && grab_overlap_h >= 20
        && body_overlap_w >= 120
        && body_overlap_h >= 80
}

pub fn reposition_window_into_work_area(
    x: i32,
    y: i32,
    width: i32,
    height: i32,
    work_left: i32,
    work_top: i32,
    work_right: i32,
    work_bottom: i32,
) -> (i32, i32, i32, i32) {
    let work_w = (work_right - work_left).max(400);
    let work_h = (work_bottom - work_top).max(300);

    let new_w = width.clamp(400.min(work_w), work_w);
    let new_h = height.clamp(300.min(work_h), work_h);

    let completely_outside =
        x + new_w <= work_left || x >= work_right || y + new_h <= work_top || y >= work_bottom;

    if completely_outside {
        let centered_x = work_left + (work_w - new_w) / 2;
        let centered_y = work_top + (work_h - new_h) / 2;
        (centered_x, centered_y, new_w, new_h)
    } else {
        let clamped_x = x.clamp(work_left, (work_right - new_w).max(work_left));
        let clamped_y = y.clamp(work_top, (work_bottom - new_h).max(work_top));
        (clamped_x, clamped_y, new_w, new_h)
    }
}

#[cfg(windows)]
mod windows_fullscreen {
    use super::{is_window_accessible_in_work_area, reposition_window_into_work_area};
    use std::sync::Mutex;

    const GWL_STYLE: i32 = -16;
    const GWL_EXSTYLE: i32 = -20;
    const WS_CAPTION: i32 = 0x00C00000;
    const WS_THICKFRAME: i32 = 0x00040000;
    const WS_EX_DLGMODALFRAME: i32 = 0x00000001;
    const WS_EX_WINDOWEDGE: i32 = 0x00000100;
    const WS_EX_CLIENTEDGE: i32 = 0x00000200;
    const WS_EX_STATICEDGE: i32 = 0x00020000;
    const MONITOR_DEFAULTTONULL: u32 = 0;
    const MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST: u32 = 2;
    const HWND_TOP: isize = 0;
    const SWP_NOSIZE: u32 = 0x0001;
    const SWP_NOMOVE: u32 = 0x0002;
    const SWP_NOZORDER: u32 = 0x0004;
    const SWP_NOACTIVATE: u32 = 0x0010;
    const SWP_FRAMECHANGED: u32 = 0x0020;
    const SWP_NOOWNERZORDER: u32 = 0x0200;

    #[repr(C)]
    #[derive(Clone, Copy, Default)]
    struct Rect {
        left: i32,
        top: i32,
        right: i32,
        bottom: i32,
    }

    #[repr(C)]
    #[derive(Clone, Copy, Default)]
    struct Point {
        x: i32,
        y: i32,
    }

    #[repr(C)]
    #[derive(Clone, Copy, Default)]
    struct WindowPlacement {
        length: u32,
        flags: u32,
        show_cmd: u32,
        pt_min_position: Point,
        pt_max_position: Point,
        rc_normal_position: Rect,
    }

    #[repr(C)]
    #[derive(Clone, Copy, Default)]
    struct MonitorInfo {
        cb_size: u32,
        rc_monitor: Rect,
        rc_work: Rect,
        dw_flags: u32,
    }

    #[derive(Clone, Copy)]
    struct SavedWindowState {
        hwnd: isize,
        style: i32,
        ex_style: i32,
        placement: WindowPlacement,
    }

    static SAVED_STATE: Mutex<Option<SavedWindowState>> = Mutex::new(None);

    #[link(name = "user32")]
    unsafe extern "system" {
        fn GetActiveWindow() -> isize;
        fn GetForegroundWindow() -> isize;
        fn FindWindowW(lp_class_name: *const u16, lp_window_name: *const u16) -> isize;
        fn GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd: isize, lpdw_process_id: *mut u32) -> u32;
        fn IsIconic(hwnd: isize) -> i32;
        fn IsZoomed(hwnd: isize) -> i32;
        fn GetWindowRect(hwnd: isize, lprect: *mut Rect) -> i32;
        fn GetWindowLongW(hwnd: isize, n_index: i32) -> i32;
        fn SetWindowLongW(hwnd: isize, n_index: i32, dw_new_long: i32) -> i32;
        fn GetWindowPlacement(hwnd: isize, lpwndpl: *mut WindowPlacement) -> i32;
        fn SetWindowPlacement(hwnd: isize, lpwndpl: *const WindowPlacement) -> i32;
        fn MonitorFromWindow(hwnd: isize, dw_flags: u32) -> isize;
        fn MonitorFromRect(lprc: *const Rect, dw_flags: u32) -> isize;
        fn GetMonitorInfoW(hmonitor: isize, lpmi: *mut MonitorInfo) -> i32;
        fn SetWindowPos(
            hwnd: isize,
            hwnd_insert_after: isize,
            x: i32,
            y: i32,
            cx: i32,
            cy: i32,
            u_flags: u32,
        ) -> i32;
    }

    #[link(name = "kernel32")]
    unsafe extern "system" {
        fn GetCurrentProcessId() -> u32;
    }

    fn to_wide(s: &str) -> Vec<u16> {
        s.encode_utf16().chain(std::iter::once(0)).collect()
    }

    fn is_own_process_window(hwnd: isize) -> bool {
        if hwnd == 0 {
            return false;
        }
        let mut proc_id = 0u32;
        unsafe {
            GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, &mut proc_id);
            proc_id != 0 && proc_id == GetCurrentProcessId()
        }
    }

    fn get_own_window_hwnd() -> isize {
        unsafe {
            let mut hwnd = GetActiveWindow();
            if is_own_process_window(hwnd) {
                return hwnd;
            }
            hwnd = GetForegroundWindow();
            if is_own_process_window(hwnd) {
                return hwnd;
            }
            let title = to_wide("QuietReader");
            hwnd = FindWindowW(std::ptr::null(), title.as_ptr());
            if is_own_process_window(hwnd) {
                return hwnd;
            }
            0
        }
    }

    pub fn sanitize_window_geometry(
        x: Option<f32>,
        y: Option<f32>,
        width: f32,
        height: f32,
    ) -> (Option<f32>, Option<f32>, f32, f32) {
        let w = if width.is_finite() {
            width.clamp(600.0, 7680.0)
        } else {
            1024.0
        };
        let h = if height.is_finite() {
            height.clamp(400.0, 4320.0)
        } else {
            768.0
        };

        let (Some(px), Some(py)) = (x, y) else {
            return (None, None, w, h);
        };
        if !px.is_finite() || !py.is_finite() || px <= -15000.0 || py <= -15000.0 {
            return (None, None, w, h);
        }

        let ix = px.round() as i32;
        let iy = py.round() as i32;
        let iw = w.round() as i32;
        let ih = h.round() as i32;

        unsafe {
            let grab_rect = Rect {
                left: ix + 16,
                top: iy,
                right: (ix + iw - 16).max(ix + 80),
                bottom: iy + 40,
            };
            let mon = MonitorFromRect(&grab_rect, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONULL);
            if mon != 0 {
                let mut mi = MonitorInfo {
                    cb_size: std::mem::size_of::<MonitorInfo>() as u32,
                    ..Default::default()
                };
                if GetMonitorInfoW(mon, &mut mi) != 0
                    && is_window_accessible_in_work_area(
                        ix,
                        iy,
                        iw,
                        ih,
                        mi.rc_work.left,
                        mi.rc_work.top,
                        mi.rc_work.right,
                        mi.rc_work.bottom,
                    )
                {
                    let work_w = (mi.rc_work.right - mi.rc_work.left).max(600) as f32;
                    let work_h = (mi.rc_work.bottom - mi.rc_work.top).max(400) as f32;
                    return (Some(px), Some(py), w.min(work_w), h.min(work_h));
                }
            }

            let win_rect = Rect {
                left: ix,
                top: iy,
                right: ix + iw,
                bottom: iy + ih,
            };
            let nearest = MonitorFromRect(&win_rect, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST);
            if nearest != 0 {
                let mut mi = MonitorInfo {
                    cb_size: std::mem::size_of::<MonitorInfo>() as u32,
                    ..Default::default()
                };
                if GetMonitorInfoW(nearest, &mut mi) != 0 {
                    let (nx, ny, nw, nh) = reposition_window_into_work_area(
                        ix,
                        iy,
                        iw,
                        ih,
                        mi.rc_work.left,
                        mi.rc_work.top,
                        mi.rc_work.right,
                        mi.rc_work.bottom,
                    );
                    return (
                        Some(nx as f32),
                        Some(ny as f32),
                        (nw as f32).max(600.0),
                        (nh as f32).max(400.0),
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        (None, None, w, h)
    }

    pub fn ensure_window_on_screen() -> bool {
        let hwnd = get_own_window_hwnd();
        if hwnd == 0 {
            return false;
        }
        unsafe {
            if IsIconic(hwnd) != 0 || IsZoomed(hwnd) != 0 {
                return false;
            }

            let mut rect = Rect::default();
            if GetWindowRect(hwnd, &mut rect) == 0 {
                return false;
            }

            let ix = rect.left;
            let iy = rect.top;
            let iw = rect.right - rect.left;
            let ih = rect.bottom - rect.top;
            if iw <= 0 || ih <= 0 {
                return false;
            }

            let grab_rect = Rect {
                left: ix + 16,
                top: iy,
                right: (ix + iw - 16).max(ix + 80),
                bottom: iy + 40,
            };
            let mon = MonitorFromRect(&grab_rect, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONULL);
            if mon != 0 {
                let mut mi = MonitorInfo {
                    cb_size: std::mem::size_of::<MonitorInfo>() as u32,
                    ..Default::default()
                };
                if GetMonitorInfoW(mon, &mut mi) != 0
                    && is_window_accessible_in_work_area(
                        ix,
                        iy,
                        iw,
                        ih,
                        mi.rc_work.left,
                        mi.rc_work.top,
                        mi.rc_work.right,
                        mi.rc_work.bottom,
                    )
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            let nearest = MonitorFromRect(&rect, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST);
            if nearest != 0 {
                let mut mi = MonitorInfo {
                    cb_size: std::mem::size_of::<MonitorInfo>() as u32,
                    ..Default::default()
                };
                if GetMonitorInfoW(nearest, &mut mi) != 0 {
                    let (nx, ny, nw, nh) = reposition_window_into_work_area(
                        ix,
                        iy,
                        iw,
                        ih,
                        mi.rc_work.left,
                        mi.rc_work.top,
                        mi.rc_work.right,
                        mi.rc_work.bottom,
                    );
                    SetWindowPos(
                        hwnd,
                        0,
                        nx,
                        ny,
                        nw,
                        nh,
                        SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOOWNERZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE,
                    );
                    return true;
                }
            }
            false
        }
    }

    pub fn set_window_fullscreen(fullscreen: bool) -> bool {
        unsafe {
            if fullscreen {
                let hwnd = get_own_window_hwnd();
                if hwnd == 0 {
                    return false;
                }

                let style = GetWindowLongW(hwnd, GWL_STYLE);
                let ex_style = GetWindowLongW(hwnd, GWL_EXSTYLE);
                let mut placement = WindowPlacement {
                    length: std::mem::size_of::<WindowPlacement>() as u32,
                    ..Default::default()
                };
                GetWindowPlacement(hwnd, &mut placement);

                if let Ok(mut guard) = SAVED_STATE.lock() {
                    *guard = Some(SavedWindowState {
                        hwnd,
                        style,
                        ex_style,
                        placement,
                    });
                }

                let new_style = style & !(WS_CAPTION | WS_THICKFRAME);
                let new_ex_style = ex_style
                    & !(WS_EX_DLGMODALFRAME
                        | WS_EX_WINDOWEDGE
                        | WS_EX_CLIENTEDGE
                        | WS_EX_STATICEDGE);

                SetWindowLongW(hwnd, GWL_STYLE, new_style);
                SetWindowLongW(hwnd, GWL_EXSTYLE, new_ex_style);

                let monitor = MonitorFromWindow(hwnd, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST);
                let mut mi = MonitorInfo {
                    cb_size: std::mem::size_of::<MonitorInfo>() as u32,
                    ..Default::default()
                };
                if monitor != 0 && GetMonitorInfoW(monitor, &mut mi) != 0 {
                    let x = mi.rc_monitor.left;
                    let y = mi.rc_monitor.top;
                    let w = mi.rc_monitor.right - mi.rc_monitor.left;
                    let h = (mi.rc_monitor.bottom - mi.rc_monitor.top) + 1;
                    SetWindowPos(
                        hwnd,
                        HWND_TOP,
                        x,
                        y,
                        w,
                        h,
                        SWP_NOOWNERZORDER | SWP_FRAMECHANGED,
                    );
                    return true;
                }
                false
            } else {
                let saved = SAVED_STATE.lock().ok().and_then(|mut g| g.take());
                if let Some(saved) = saved {
                    SetWindowLongW(saved.hwnd, GWL_STYLE, saved.style);
                    SetWindowLongW(saved.hwnd, GWL_EXSTYLE, saved.ex_style);
                    SetWindowPlacement(saved.hwnd, &saved.placement);
                    SetWindowPos(
                        saved.hwnd,
                        0,
                        0,
                        0,
                        0,
                        0,
                        SWP_NOMOVE
                            | SWP_NOSIZE
                            | SWP_NOZORDER
                            | SWP_NOOWNERZORDER
                            | SWP_FRAMECHANGED,
                    );
                    let _ = ensure_window_on_screen();
                    return true;
                }
                false
            }
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(windows)]
pub fn sanitize_window_geometry(
    x: Option<f32>,
    y: Option<f32>,
    width: f32,
    height: f32,
) -> (Option<f32>, Option<f32>, f32, f32) {
    windows_fullscreen::sanitize_window_geometry(x, y, width, height)
}

#[cfg(not(windows))]
pub fn sanitize_window_geometry(
    x: Option<f32>,
    y: Option<f32>,
    width: f32,
    height: f32,
) -> (Option<f32>, Option<f32>, f32, f32) {
    let w = if width.is_finite() {
        width.clamp(600.0, 7680.0)
    } else {
        1024.0
    };
    let h = if height.is_finite() {
        height.clamp(400.0, 4320.0)
    } else {
        768.0
    };
    if let (Some(px), Some(py)) = (x, y)
        && px.is_finite()
        && py.is_finite()
        && (-10000.0..=10000.0).contains(&px)
        && (-10000.0..=10000.0).contains(&py)
    {
        (Some(px), Some(py), w, h)
    } else {
        (None, None, w, h)
    }
}

#[cfg(windows)]
pub fn ensure_window_on_screen() -> bool {
    windows_fullscreen::ensure_window_on_screen()
}

#[cfg(not(windows))]
pub fn ensure_window_on_screen() -> bool {
    false
}

#[cfg(windows)]
pub fn set_window_fullscreen(fullscreen: bool) -> bool {
    windows_fullscreen::set_window_fullscreen(fullscreen)
}

#[cfg(not(windows))]
pub fn set_window_fullscreen(_fullscreen: bool) -> bool {
    false
}

mod platform;
mod settings;

use app::QuietReaderApp;
use settings::AppSettings;

fn main() -> eframe::Result<()> {
    let initial_file = std::env::args_os().nth(1).map(std::path::PathBuf::from);
    let Some(_instance_guard) = platform::acquire_single_instance(&initial_file) else {
        return Ok(());
    };

    let settings = AppSettings::load();

    let mut viewport = eframe::egui::ViewportBuilder::default()
        .with_min_inner_size([600.0, 400.0])
        .with_title("QuietReader");

    let (pos_x, pos_y, w, h) = platform::sanitize_window_geometry(
        settings.window.x,
        settings.window.y,
        settings.window.width,
        settings.window.height,
    );
    viewport = viewport.with_inner_size([w, h]);

    if let (Some(x), Some(y)) = (pos_x, pos_y) {
        viewport = viewport.with_position([x, y]);
    }

    if settings.window.maximized {
        viewport = viewport.with_maximized(true);
    }

    if let Ok(icon) = eframe::icon_data::from_png_bytes(include_bytes!("../assets/app_64.png")) {
        viewport = viewport.with_icon(icon);
    }

    let native_options = eframe::NativeOptions {
        viewport,
        ..Default::default()
    };

    eframe::run_native(
        "QuietReader",
        native_options,
        Box::new(move |cc| Ok(Box::new(QuietReaderApp::new(cc, initial_file, settings)))),
    )
}

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Clone, Copy)]
pub enum AssociationStatus {
    Default,
    Registered,
    NotRegistered,
}

pub fn supports_file_association() -> bool {
    cfg!(windows)
}

#[cfg(windows)]
pub fn set_keep_screen_on(keep_on: bool) {
    const ES_CONTINUOUS: u32 = 0x80000000;
    const ES_DISPLAY_REQUIRED: u32 = 0x00000002;

    unsafe extern "system" {
        fn SetThreadExecutionState(es_flags: u32) -> u32;
    }

    unsafe {
        if keep_on {
            SetThreadExecutionState(ES_CONTINUOUS | ES_DISPLAY_REQUIRED);
        } else {
            SetThreadExecutionState(ES_CONTINUOUS);
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(not(windows))]
pub fn set_keep_screen_on(_keep_on: bool) {}

#[cfg(windows)]
mod windows_registry {
    use super::AssociationStatus;
    use std::ffi::c_void;

    const HKEY_CURRENT_USER: *mut c_void = 0x80000001u32 as usize as *mut c_void;
    const KEY_READ: u32 = 0x20019;
    const KEY_ALL_ACCESS: u32 = 0xF003F;
    const REG_SZ: u32 = 1;
    const REG_EXPAND_SZ: u32 = 2;
    const ERROR_SUCCESS: i32 = 0;
    const SHCNE_ASSOCCHANGED: i32 = 0x08000000;
    const SHCNF_IDLIST: u32 = 0x0000;

    #[link(name = "advapi32")]
    #[link(name = "shell32")]
    unsafe extern "system" {
        fn RegOpenKeyExW(
            hKey: *mut c_void,
            lpSubKey: *const u16,
            ulOptions: u32,
            samDesired: u32,
            phkResult: *mut *mut c_void,
        ) -> i32;

        fn RegCreateKeyExW(
            hKey: *mut c_void,
            lpSubKey: *const u16,
            Reserved: u32,
            lpClass: *mut u16,
            dwOptions: u32,
            samDesired: u32,
            lpSecurityAttributes: *mut c_void,
            phkResult: *mut *mut c_void,
            lpdwDisposition: *mut u32,
        ) -> i32;

        fn RegSetValueExW(
            hKey: *mut c_void,
            lpValueName: *const u16,
            Reserved: u32,
            dwType: u32,
            lpData: *const u8,
            cbData: u32,
        ) -> i32;

        fn RegQueryValueExW(
            hKey: *mut c_void,
            lpValueName: *const u16,
            lpReserved: *mut u32,
            lpType: *mut u32,
            lpData: *mut u8,
            lpcbData: *mut u32,
        ) -> i32;

        fn RegCloseKey(hKey: *mut c_void) -> i32;

        fn SHChangeNotify(
            wEventId: i32,
            uFlags: u32,
            dwItem1: *const c_void,
            dwItem2: *const c_void,
        );
    }

    struct RegKey(*mut c_void);

    impl Drop for RegKey {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            if !self.0.is_null() {
                unsafe { RegCloseKey(self.0) };
            }
        }
    }

    fn to_wide(s: &str) -> Vec<u16> {
        s.encode_utf16().chain(std::iter::once(0)).collect()
    }

    fn reg_open_key(root: *mut c_void, sub_key: &str, access: u32) -> Option<RegKey> {
        let wide = to_wide(sub_key);
        let mut hkey: *mut c_void = std::ptr::null_mut();
        let res = unsafe { RegOpenKeyExW(root, wide.as_ptr(), 0, access, &mut hkey) };
        if res == ERROR_SUCCESS && !hkey.is_null() {
            Some(RegKey(hkey))
        } else {
            None
        }
    }

    fn reg_create_key(root: *mut c_void, sub_key: &str) -> Result<RegKey, String> {
        let wide = to_wide(sub_key);
        let mut hkey: *mut c_void = std::ptr::null_mut();
        let mut disp: u32 = 0;
        let res = unsafe {
            RegCreateKeyExW(
                root,
                wide.as_ptr(),
                0,
                std::ptr::null_mut(),
                0,
                KEY_ALL_ACCESS,
                std::ptr::null_mut(),
                &mut hkey,
                &mut disp,
            )
        };
        if res == ERROR_SUCCESS && !hkey.is_null() {
            Ok(RegKey(hkey))
        } else {
            Err(format!(
                "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ СЃРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ РєР»СЋС‡ СЂРµРµСЃС‚СЂР° '{sub_key}': РєРѕРґ {res}"
            ))
        }
    }

    fn reg_set_string(key: &RegKey, name: Option<&str>, val: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let wide_name = name.map(to_wide);
        let name_ptr = wide_name
            .as_ref()
            .map(|w| w.as_ptr())
            .unwrap_or(std::ptr::null());
        let wide_val = to_wide(val);
        let byte_len = (wide_val.len() * 2) as u32;
        let res = unsafe {
            RegSetValueExW(
                key.0,
                name_ptr,
                0,
                REG_SZ,
                wide_val.as_ptr() as *const u8,
                byte_len,
            )
        };
        if res == ERROR_SUCCESS {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(format!("РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ Р·Р°РїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ РІ СЂРµРµСЃС‚СЂ: РєРѕРґ {res}"))
        }
    }

    fn reg_get_string(key: &RegKey, name: Option<&str>) -> Option<String> {
        let wide_name = name.map(to_wide);
        let name_ptr = wide_name
            .as_ref()
            .map(|w| w.as_ptr())
            .unwrap_or(std::ptr::null());
        let mut data_type: u32 = 0;
        let mut data_len: u32 = 0;

        let res = unsafe {
            RegQueryValueExW(
                key.0,
                name_ptr,
                std::ptr::null_mut(),
                &mut data_type,
                std::ptr::null_mut(),
                &mut data_len,
            )
        };
        if res != ERROR_SUCCESS || data_len == 0 {
            return None;
        }

        let mut buf = vec![0u8; data_len as usize];
        let res = unsafe {
            RegQueryValueExW(
                key.0,
                name_ptr,
                std::ptr::null_mut(),
                &mut data_type,
                buf.as_mut_ptr(),
                &mut data_len,
            )
        };
        if res != ERROR_SUCCESS || (data_type != REG_SZ && data_type != REG_EXPAND_SZ) {
            return None;
        }

        let u16_len = (data_len as usize) / 2;
        if u16_len == 0 {
            return Some(String::new());
        }
        let u16_slice = unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(buf.as_ptr() as *const u16, u16_len) };
        let s = String::from_utf16_lossy(u16_slice);
        Some(s.trim_end_matches('\0').to_string())
    }

    fn notify_shell_assoc_changed() {
        unsafe {
            SHChangeNotify(
                SHCNE_ASSOCCHANGED,
                SHCNF_IDLIST,
                std::ptr::null(),
                std::ptr::null(),
            );
        }
    }

    pub fn check_fb2_association_status() -> AssociationStatus {
        let current_exe = match std::env::current_exe() {
            Ok(p) => p.to_string_lossy().to_lowercase(),
            Err(_) => return AssociationStatus::NotRegistered,
        };

        let is_our_progid_registered = reg_open_key(
            HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\Classes\QuietReader.fb2\shell\open\command",
            KEY_READ,
        )
        .and_then(|k| reg_get_string(&k, None))
        .map(|cmd| cmd.to_lowercase().contains(&current_exe))
        .unwrap_or(false);

        if !is_our_progid_registered {
            return AssociationStatus::NotRegistered;
        }

        if let Some(user_choice_key) = reg_open_key(
            HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\FileExts\.fb2\UserChoice",
            KEY_READ,
        ) {
            if let Some(progid) = reg_get_string(&user_choice_key, Some("ProgId"))
                && progid.eq_ignore_ascii_case("QuietReader.fb2")
            {
                return AssociationStatus::Default;
            }
            return AssociationStatus::Registered;
        }

        if let Some(dot_fb2_key) =
            reg_open_key(HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"Software\Classes\.fb2", KEY_READ)
            && let Some(progid) = reg_get_string(&dot_fb2_key, None)
            && progid.eq_ignore_ascii_case("QuietReader.fb2")
        {
            return AssociationStatus::Default;
        }

        AssociationStatus::Registered
    }

    pub fn register_fb2_association() -> Result<String, String> {
        let exe_path = std::env::current_exe()
            .map_err(|e| format!("РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РёС‚СЊ РїСѓС‚СЊ Рє РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРёРјРѕРјСѓ С„Р°Р№Р»Сѓ: {e}"))?;
        let exe_str = exe_path.to_string_lossy();
        let open_cmd = format!("\"{exe_str}\" \"%1\"");
        let doc_icon_cmd = format!("\"{exe_str}\",1");
        let app_icon_cmd = format!("\"{exe_str}\",0");

        let dot_fb2 = reg_create_key(HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"Software\Classes\.fb2")?;
        let _ = reg_set_string(&dot_fb2, None, "QuietReader.fb2");
        let _ = reg_set_string(
            &dot_fb2,
            Some("Content Type"),
            "application/x-fictionbook+xml",
        );

        let open_with =
            reg_create_key(HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"Software\Classes\.fb2\OpenWithProgids")?;
        let _ = reg_set_string(&open_with, Some("QuietReader.fb2"), "");

        let progid_key = reg_create_key(HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"Software\Classes\QuietReader.fb2")?;
        let _ = reg_set_string(&progid_key, None, "РљРЅРёРіР° FictionBook (FB2)");
        let _ = reg_set_string(
            &progid_key,
            Some("FriendlyTypeName"),
            "РљРЅРёРіР° FictionBook (FB2)",
        );

        let icon_key = reg_create_key(
            HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\Classes\QuietReader.fb2\DefaultIcon",
        )?;
        let _ = reg_set_string(&icon_key, None, &doc_icon_cmd);

        let cmd_key = reg_create_key(
            HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\Classes\QuietReader.fb2\shell\open\command",
        )?;
        let _ = reg_set_string(&cmd_key, None, &open_cmd);

        let app_key = reg_create_key(
            HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\Classes\Applications\QuietReader.exe",
        )?;
        let _ = reg_set_string(&app_key, Some("FriendlyAppName"), "QuietReader");

        let app_icon_key = reg_create_key(
            HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\Classes\Applications\QuietReader.exe\DefaultIcon",
        )?;
        let _ = reg_set_string(&app_icon_key, None, &app_icon_cmd);

        let supported = reg_create_key(
            HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\Classes\Applications\QuietReader.exe\SupportedTypes",
        )?;
        let _ = reg_set_string(&supported, Some(".fb2"), "");

        let app_cmd = reg_create_key(
            HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\Classes\Applications\QuietReader.exe\shell\open\command",
        )?;
        let _ = reg_set_string(&app_cmd, None, &open_cmd);

        let caps = reg_create_key(HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"Software\QuietReader\Capabilities")?;
        let _ = reg_set_string(&caps, Some("ApplicationName"), "QuietReader");
        let _ = reg_set_string(
            &caps,
            Some("ApplicationDescription"),
            "РњРёРЅРёРјР°Р»РёСЃС‚РёС‡РЅР°СЏ С‡РёС‚Р°Р»РєР° РєРЅРёРі FB2",
        );

        let file_assoc = reg_create_key(
            HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\QuietReader\Capabilities\FileAssociations",
        )?;
        let _ = reg_set_string(&file_assoc, Some(".fb2"), "QuietReader.fb2");

        let reg_apps = reg_create_key(HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"Software\RegisteredApplications")?;
        let _ = reg_set_string(
            &reg_apps,
            Some("QuietReader"),
            r"Software\QuietReader\Capabilities",
        );

        notify_shell_assoc_changed();

        Ok(
            "QuietReader СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ Р·Р°СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ РІ СЂРµРµСЃС‚СЂРµ Windows (HKCU) РґР»СЏ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚Р° .fb2."
                .to_string(),
        )
    }

    pub fn is_association_prompt_dismissed() -> bool {
        reg_open_key(
            HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\QuietReader\Settings",
            KEY_READ,
        )
        .and_then(|k| reg_get_string(&k, Some("DismissedDefaultPrompt")))
        .map(|v| v == "1")
        .unwrap_or(false)
    }

    pub fn set_association_prompt_dismissed(dismissed: bool) {
        if let Ok(key) = reg_create_key(HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"Software\QuietReader\Settings") {
            let val = if dismissed { "1" } else { "0" };
            let _ = reg_set_string(&key, Some("DismissedDefaultPrompt"), val);
        }
    }
}

pub fn load_system_font_bytes(font_name: &str) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    platform_fonts::load_font(font_name)
}

#[cfg(windows)]
mod platform_fonts {
    use std::path::PathBuf;

    pub fn load_font(font_name: &str) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
        let filename = match font_name {
            "Georgia" => "georgia.ttf",
            "Palatino Linotype" => "pala.ttf",
            "Calibri" => "calibri.ttf",
            "Segoe UI" => "segoeui.ttf",
            "Verdana" => "verdana.ttf",
            _ => "georgia.ttf",
        };

        if let Some(windir) = std::env::var_os("WINDIR") {
            let p = PathBuf::from(windir).join("Fonts").join(filename);
            if let Ok(bytes) = std::fs::read(&p) {
                return Some(bytes);
            }
        }
        let fallback = PathBuf::from(r"C:\Windows\Fonts").join(filename);
        std::fs::read(fallback).ok()
    }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
mod platform_fonts {
    use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

    fn find_font_in_dir(dir: &Path, candidates: &[&str], depth: usize) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
        if depth > 4 || !dir.is_dir() {
            return None;
        }
        let entries = std::fs::read_dir(dir).ok()?;
        for entry in entries.flatten() {
            let path = entry.path();
            if path.is_dir() {
                if let Some(bytes) = find_font_in_dir(&path, candidates, depth + 1) {
                    return Some(bytes);
                }
            } else if let Some(fname) = path.file_name().and_then(|n| n.to_str()) {
                for &cand in candidates {
                    if fname.eq_ignore_ascii_case(cand) {
                        if let Ok(bytes) = std::fs::read(&path) {
                            return Some(bytes);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        None
    }

    pub fn load_font(font_name: &str) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
        let candidates: &[&str] = match font_name {
            "Georgia" => &[
                "georgia.ttf",
                "LiberationSerif-Regular.ttf",
                "DejaVuSerif.ttf",
            ],
            "Palatino Linotype" => &["pala.ttf", "P052-Roman.otf", "DejaVuSerif.ttf"],
            "Calibri" => &["calibri.ttf", "Carlito-Regular.ttf", "DejaVuSans.ttf"],
            "Segoe UI" => &["segoeui.ttf", "Cantarell-Regular.otf", "DejaVuSans.ttf"],
            "Verdana" => &["verdana.ttf", "DejaVuSans.ttf"],
            _ => &["georgia.ttf", "DejaVuSerif.ttf"],
        };

        let mut dirs = vec![
            PathBuf::from("/usr/share/fonts"),
            PathBuf::from("/usr/local/share/fonts"),
        ];
        if let Some(home) = std::env::var_os("HOME") {
            dirs.push(PathBuf::from(&home).join(".local/share/fonts"));
            dirs.push(PathBuf::from(home).join(".fonts"));
        }

        for dir in dirs {
            if let Some(bytes) = find_font_in_dir(&dir, candidates, 0) {
                return Some(bytes);
            }
        }
        None
    }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
mod platform_fonts {
    use std::path::PathBuf;

    pub fn load_font(font_name: &str) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
        let candidates: &[&str] = match font_name {
            "Georgia" => &["Georgia.ttf", "georgia.ttf"],
            "Palatino Linotype" => &["Palatino.ttc", "pala.ttf"],
            "Calibri" => &["Calibri.ttf", "calibri.ttf", "HelveticaNeue.ttc"],
            "Segoe UI" => &["SFNS.ttf", "Helvetica.ttc", "Arial.ttf"],
            "Verdana" => &["Verdana.ttf", "verdana.ttf"],
            _ => &["Georgia.ttf", "georgia.ttf"],
        };

        let mut dirs = vec![
            PathBuf::from("/Library/Fonts"),
            PathBuf::from("/System/Library/Fonts"),
            PathBuf::from("/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental"),
        ];
        if let Some(home) = std::env::var_os("HOME") {
            dirs.push(PathBuf::from(home).join("Library/Fonts"));
        }

        for dir in dirs {
            for &cand in candidates {
                let p = dir.join(cand);
                if let Ok(bytes) = std::fs::read(&p) {
                    return Some(bytes);
                }
            }
        }
        None
    }
}

#[cfg(not(any(windows, target_os = "linux", target_os = "macos")))]
mod platform_fonts {
    pub fn load_font(_font_name: &str) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
        None
    }
}

            maximized: false,

}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct PdfSettings {
    pub layout: String,
    pub fit_mode: String,
    pub zoom_percent: f32,
    pub rotation: i32,
}

impl Default for PdfSettings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            layout: "Single".to_string(),
            fit_mode: "FitWindow".to_string(),
            zoom_percent: 100.0,
            rotation: 0,
        }
    }
}

pub const CURRENT_SETTINGS_SCHEMA_VERSION: u32 = 2;

fn default_schema_version() -> u32 {
    0
}

fn default_font_family() -> String {
    "Georgia".to_string()
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct AppSettings {
    #[serde(default = "default_schema_version")]
    pub schema_version: u32,
    pub window: WindowState,
    pub recent_books: Vec<RecentBook>,
    pub reading_progress: HashMap<PathBuf, BookProgress>,
    pub last_opened_book: Option<PathBuf>,
    pub open_tabs: Vec<PathBuf>,
    pub active_tab_index: usize,
    pub font_size: f32,
    pub font_family: String,
    pub theme: String,
    pub keep_screen_on: bool,
    pub show_toc: bool,
    pub auto_scroll: AutoScrollSettings,
    pub dismiss_association_prompt: bool,
    pub comic: ComicSettings,
    pub pdf: PdfSettings,
}

impl Default for AppSettings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            schema_version: CURRENT_SETTINGS_SCHEMA_VERSION,
            window: WindowState::default(),
            recent_books: Vec::new(),
            reading_progress: HashMap::new(),
            last_opened_book: None,
            open_tabs: Vec::new(),
            active_tab_index: 0,
            font_size: 19.0,
            font_family: default_font_family(),
            theme: "Sepia".to_string(),
            keep_screen_on: true,
            show_toc: true,
            auto_scroll: AutoScrollSettings::default(),
            dismiss_association_prompt: false,
            comic: ComicSettings::default(),
            pdf: PdfSettings::default(),
        }
    }
}

impl AppSettings {
    pub fn last_save_error() -> Option<String> {
        LAST_SAVE_ERROR.lock().ok().and_then(|guard| guard.clone())
    }

    pub fn clear_save_error() {
        if let Ok(mut guard) = LAST_SAVE_ERROR.lock() {
            *guard = None;
        }
    }

    pub fn get_settings_path() -> PathBuf {
        if let Ok(exe_path) = std::env::current_exe()
            && let Some(parent) = exe_path.parent()
        {
            let local_settings = parent.join("quietreader_settings.json");
            if local_settings.exists() {
                return local_settings;
            }
        }

        if let Some(app_data) = std::env::var_os("APPDATA") {
            let dir = PathBuf::from(app_data).join("QuietReader");
            let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir);
            return dir.join("settings.json");
        }

        if let Some(xdg) = std::env::var_os("XDG_CONFIG_HOME") {
            let dir = PathBuf::from(xdg).join("QuietReader");
            let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir);
            return dir.join("settings.json");
        }
        if let Some(home) = std::env::var_os("HOME") {
            let dir = if cfg!(target_os = "macos") {
                PathBuf::from(home).join("Library/Application Support/QuietReader")
            } else {
                PathBuf::from(home).join(".config/QuietReader")
            };
            let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir);
            return dir.join("settings.json");
        }

        PathBuf::from("quietreader_settings.json")
    }

    pub fn load() -> Self {
        let path = Self::get_settings_path();
        Self::load_from_path(&path)
    }

    fn make_backup_path(path: &Path) -> PathBuf {
        let mut bak_path = path.to_path_buf();
        let ext = bak_path
            .extension()
            .and_then(|e| e.to_str())
            .map(|e| format!("{e}.bak"))
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "bak".to_string());
        bak_path.set_extension(ext);
        bak_path
    }

    pub fn migrate_from_file(&mut self, path: &Path) {
        if self.schema_version < CURRENT_SETTINGS_SCHEMA_VERSION {
            let bak_path = Self::make_backup_path(path);
            if path.exists() {
                let _ = std::fs::copy(path, &bak_path);
            }

            for book in &self.recent_books {
                self.reading_progress
                    .entry(book.path.clone())
                    .or_insert(BookProgress {
                        chapter: book.chapter,
                        scroll_y: book.scroll_y,
                        timestamp: book.timestamp,
                    });
            }

            if self.font_family.trim().is_empty() {
                self.font_family = default_font_family();
            }
            self.font_size = self.font_size.clamp(10.0, 48.0);
            self.schema_version = CURRENT_SETTINGS_SCHEMA_VERSION;

            let _ = self.save_to_path(path);
        } else {
            let mut need_save = false;
            if self.reading_progress.is_empty() && !self.recent_books.is_empty() {
                for book in &self.recent_books {
                    self.reading_progress
                        .entry(book.path.clone())
                        .or_insert(BookProgress {
                            chapter: book.chapter,
                            scroll_y: book.scroll_y,
                            timestamp: book.timestamp,
                        });
                }
                need_save = true;
            }
            if need_save {
                let _ = self.save_to_path(path);
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn load_from_path(path: &Path) -> Self {
        let bak_path = Self::make_backup_path(path);

        if path.exists() {
            if let Ok(content) = std::fs::read_to_string(path) {
                match serde_json::from_str::<AppSettings>(&content) {
                    Ok(mut settings) => {
                        settings.migrate_from_file(path);
                        return settings;
                    }
                    Err(_) => {
                        if !bak_path.exists() {
                            let _ = std::fs::copy(path, &bak_path);
                        }
                        let corrupted_path = path.with_extension("corrupted");
                        let _ = std::fs::copy(path, &corrupted_path);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                let corrupted_path = path.with_extension("corrupted");
                let _ = std::fs::copy(path, &corrupted_path);
            }
        }

        if bak_path.exists()
            && let Ok(bak_content) = std::fs::read_to_string(&bak_path)
            && let Ok(mut bak_settings) = serde_json::from_str::<AppSettings>(&bak_content)
        {
            bak_settings.migrate_from_file(path);
            let _ = bak_settings.save_to_path(path);
            return bak_settings;
        }

        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn save(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
        let path = Self::get_settings_path();
        self.save_to_path(&path)
    }

    pub fn save_to_path(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(parent);
        }
        let content = serde_json::to_string_pretty(self).map_err(|e| {
            let err = format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµСЂРёР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёРё РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРµРє: {e}");
            if let Ok(mut lock) = LAST_SAVE_ERROR.lock() {
                *lock = Some(err.clone());
            }
            err
        })?;

        let tmp_path = path.with_extension(format!("tmp.{}", std::process::id()));

        let write_res = (|| -> std::io::Result<()> {
            use std::io::Write;
            let mut file = std::fs::File::create(&tmp_path)?;
            file.write_all(content.as_bytes())?;
            file.sync_all()?;
            Ok(())
        })();

        if let Err(e) = write_res {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&tmp_path);
            let err = format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РїРёСЃРё РІСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ С„Р°Р№Р»Р° РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРµРє: {e}");
            if let Ok(mut lock) = LAST_SAVE_ERROR.lock() {
                *lock = Some(err.clone());
            }
            return Err(err);
        }

        let mut rename_err = None;
        for _ in 0..5 {
            match std::fs::rename(&tmp_path, path) {
                Ok(()) => {
                    rename_err = None;
                    break;
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    rename_err = Some(e);
                    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(15));
                }
            }
        }

        if let Some(e) = rename_err {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&tmp_path);
            let err = format!("РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёСЏ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРµРє {}: {e}", path.display());
            if let Ok(mut lock) = LAST_SAVE_ERROR.lock() {
                *lock = Some(err.clone());
            }
            return Err(err);
        }

        if let Ok(mut lock) = LAST_SAVE_ERROR.lock() {
            *lock = None;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn record_book_progress(
        &mut self,
        path: &Path,
        title: &str,
        author: &str,
        chapter: usize,
        scroll_y: f32,
    ) {
        let now = std::time::SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
            .map(|d| d.as_secs())
            .unwrap_or(0);

        self.reading_progress.insert(
            path.to_path_buf(),
            BookProgress {
                chapter,
                scroll_y,
                timestamp: now,
            },
        );

        if self.reading_progress.len() > 10_000 {
            let mut entries: Vec<_> = self
                .reading_progress
                .iter()
                .map(|(p, b)| (p.clone(), b.timestamp))
                .collect();
            entries.sort_by_key(|&(_, t)| t);
            for (old_path, _) in entries.into_iter().take(2_000) {
                self.reading_progress.remove(&old_path);
            }
        }

        self.recent_books.retain(|b| b.path != path);
        self.recent_books.insert(
            0,
            RecentBook {
                path: path.to_path_buf(),
                title: if title.is_empty() {
                    path.file_name()
                        .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
                        .unwrap_or_else(|| "Р‘РµР· РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЏ".to_string())
                } else {
                    title.to_string()
                },
                author: author.to_string(),
                chapter,
                scroll_y,
                timestamp: now,
            },
        );

        if self.recent_books.len() > 25 {
            self.recent_books.truncate(25);
        }

        self.last_opened_book = Some(path.to_path_buf());
    }

    pub fn get_book_progress(&self, path: &Path) -> Option<(usize, f32)> {
        if let Some(prog) = self.reading_progress.get(path) {
            return Some((prog.chapter, prog.scroll_y));
        }
        let path_str = path.to_string_lossy();
        for (p, prog) in &self.reading_progress {
            if p.to_string_lossy().eq_ignore_ascii_case(&path_str) {
                return Some((prog.chapter, prog.scroll_y));
            }
        }
        self.recent_books
            .iter()
            .find(|b| b.path == path || b.path.to_string_lossy().eq_ignore_ascii_case(&path_str))
            .map(|b| (b.chapter, b.scroll_y))
    }

    pub fn update_window_state(
        &mut self,
        x: Option<f32>,
        y: Option<f32>,
        width: f32,
        height: f32,
        maximized: bool,
    ) {
        if !maximized {
            if let (Some(px), Some(py)) = (x, y)
                && px.is_finite()
                && py.is_finite()
                && px > -15000.0
                && py > -15000.0
            {
                self.window.x = Some(px);
                self.window.y = Some(py);
            }
            if width.is_finite() && height.is_finite() && width >= 400.0 && height >= 300.0 {
                self.window.width = width;
                self.window.height = height;
            }
        }
        self.window.maximized = maximized;
    }
}

use super::*;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};

fn unique_test_dir(prefix: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
    static COUNTER: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(1);
    let id = COUNTER.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    let pid = std::process::id();
    let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("qr_test_{prefix}_{pid}_{id}"));
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
    dir
}

#[test]
fn test_comic_natural_sort_ordering() {
    use document::comic::natural_cmp;
    use std::cmp::Ordering;

    assert_eq!(natural_cmp("page1.jpg", "page2.jpg"), Ordering::Less);
    assert_eq!(natural_cmp("page2.jpg", "page10.jpg"), Ordering::Less);
    assert_eq!(natural_cmp("page10.jpg", "page2.jpg"), Ordering::Greater);
    assert_eq!(natural_cmp("ch1_p09.png", "ch1_p10.png"), Ordering::Less);
    assert_eq!(natural_cmp("page5.webp", "page5.webp"), Ordering::Equal);
}

#[test]
fn test_comic_cbz_archive_and_metadata_parsing() {
    use std::io::Write;
    let test_dir = unique_test_dir("comic_cbz");
    let cbz_path = test_dir.join("test_manga.cbz");

    let mut sample_png = Vec::new();
    let img = image::RgbaImage::new(10, 10);
    img.write_to(
        &mut std::io::Cursor::new(&mut sample_png),
        image::ImageFormat::Png,
    )
    .unwrap();

    {
        let file = std::fs::File::create(&cbz_path).unwrap();
        let mut zip = zip::ZipWriter::new(file);
        let options = zip::write::SimpleFileOptions::default()
            .compression_method(zip::CompressionMethod::Deflated);

        let comic_info_xml = r#"<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<ComicInfo xmlns:xsd="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema">
  <Title>Р’Р°РЅ-РџРёСЃ: Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1</Title>
  <Series>One Piece</Series>
  <Number>1</Number>
  <Writer>Р­Р№РёС‚РёСЂРѕ РћРґР°</Writer>
  <Manga>YesAndRightToLeft</Manga>
</ComicInfo>"#;
        zip.start_file("ComicInfo.xml", options).unwrap();
        zip.write_all(comic_info_xml.as_bytes()).unwrap();

        zip.start_file("ch01/page_10.png", options).unwrap();
        zip.write_all(&sample_png).unwrap();

        zip.start_file("ch01/page_01.png", options).unwrap();
        zip.write_all(&sample_png).unwrap();

        zip.start_file("ch01/page_02.png", options).unwrap();
        zip.write_all(&sample_png).unwrap();

        zip.finish().unwrap();
    }

    let doc = document::open_document(&cbz_path).expect("Р”РѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ CBZ Р°СЂС…РёРІ");
    assert_eq!(doc.metadata().title, "One Piece #1: Р’Р°РЅ-РџРёСЃ: Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1");
    assert_eq!(doc.metadata().author, "Р­Р№РёС‚РёСЂРѕ РћРґР°");

    let comic = doc
        .page_content()
        .expect("Р”РѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚ РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СЂРµР°Р»РёР·РѕРІР°С‚СЊ ComicDocumentTrait");
    assert!(comic.is_manga(), "Р”РѕР»Р¶РµРЅ РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РёС‚СЊСЃСЏ РєР°Рє РјР°РЅРіР°");
    assert_eq!(comic.page_count(), 3, "Р”РѕР»Р¶РЅРѕ Р±С‹С‚СЊ 3 СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹");

    assert_eq!(comic.page_name(0), "page_01.png");
    assert_eq!(comic.page_name(1), "page_02.png");
    assert_eq!(comic.page_name(2), "page_10.png");

    let decoded = comic
        .load_page_image(0)
        .expect("РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° 0 РґРѕР»Р¶РЅР° СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ РґРµРєРѕРґРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊСЃСЏ");
    assert_eq!(decoded.width, 10);
    assert_eq!(decoded.height, 10);
    assert_eq!(decoded.rgba.len(), 10 * 10 * 4);

    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&test_dir);
}

#[test]
fn test_comic_folder_scan() {
    let test_dir = unique_test_dir("comic_folder");
    let folder_path = test_dir.join("MangaFolder");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&folder_path).unwrap();

    let mut sample_png = Vec::new();
    let img = image::RgbaImage::new(4, 4);
    img.write_to(
        &mut std::io::Cursor::new(&mut sample_png),
        image::ImageFormat::Png,
    )
    .unwrap();

    std::fs::write(folder_path.join("2.png"), &sample_png).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(folder_path.join("1.png"), &sample_png).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(folder_path.join("10.png"), &sample_png).unwrap();

    let doc = document::open_document(&folder_path).expect("Р”РѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РїР°РїРєСѓ СЃ РјР°РЅРіРѕР№");
    let comic = doc
        .page_content()
        .expect("РџР°РїРєР° РґРѕР»Р¶РЅР° РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РёС‚СЊСЃСЏ РєР°Рє РєРѕРјРёРєСЃ");
    assert_eq!(comic.page_count(), 3);
    assert_eq!(comic.page_name(0), "1.png");
    assert_eq!(comic.page_name(1), "2.png");
    assert_eq!(comic.page_name(2), "10.png");

    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&test_dir);
}

#[test]
fn test_pdf_document_parsing_and_rasterization() {
    let minimal_pdf = b"%PDF-1.4
1 0 obj
<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>
endobj
2 0 obj
<< /Type /Pages /Kids [3 0 R] /Count 1 >>
endobj
3 0 obj
<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 200 300] /Resources <<>> >>
endobj
xref
0 4
0000000000 65535 f 
0000000009 00000 n 
0000000058 00000 n 
0000000115 00000 n 
trailer
<< /Size 4 /Root 1 0 R >>
startxref
206
%%EOF";

    let temp_dir = unique_test_dir("pdf");
    let pdf_path = temp_dir.join("test_sample.pdf");
    std::fs::write(&pdf_path, minimal_pdf).unwrap();

    let doc = document::open_document(&pdf_path).expect("Р”РѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РІР°Р»РёРґРЅС‹Р№ PDF");
    assert_eq!(doc.chapters().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(doc.toc().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(doc.toc()[0].title, "РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° 1");

    let comic = doc
        .page_content()
        .expect("PDF РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СЂРµР°Р»РёР·РѕРІС‹РІР°С‚СЊ ComicDocumentTrait РґР»СЏ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС‡РЅРѕРіРѕ РѕС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ");
    assert_eq!(comic.page_count(), 1);
    assert!(!comic.is_manga());

    let page_img = comic
        .load_page_image(0)
        .expect("РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° PDF РґРѕР»Р¶РЅР° СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ СЂР°СЃС‚РµСЂРёР·РѕРІР°С‚СЊСЃСЏ");
    assert!(page_img.width > 0);
    assert!(page_img.height > 0);
    assert_eq!(
        page_img.rgba.len(),
        (page_img.width * page_img.height * 4) as usize
    );

    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&temp_dir);
}

#[test]
fn test_font_family_settings_and_serialization() {
    let default_settings = AppSettings::default();
    assert_eq!(default_settings.font_family, "Georgia");

    let old_json = r#"{"window":{"width":800.0,"height":600.0,"maximized":false},"recent_books":[],"font_size":18.0,"theme":"Sepia","keep_screen_on":true,"show_toc":true}"#;
    let loaded: AppSettings =
        serde_json::from_str(old_json).expect("Р”РѕР»Р¶РµРЅ РґРµСЃРµСЂРёР°Р»РёР·РѕРІР°С‚СЊСЃСЏ СЃРѕ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµРј РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ");
    assert_eq!(loaded.font_family, "Georgia");

    let custom_settings = AppSettings {
        font_family: "Palatino Linotype".to_string(),
        ..Default::default()
    };
    let serialized = serde_json::to_string(&custom_settings).unwrap();
    let deserialized: AppSettings = serde_json::from_str(&serialized).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(deserialized.font_family, "Palatino Linotype");

    let font_ids: Vec<&str> = crate::app::fonts::AVAILABLE_FONTS
        .iter()
        .map(|f| f.id)
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        font_ids,
        vec![
            "Georgia",
            "Palatino Linotype",
            "Calibri",
            "Segoe UI",
            "Verdana"
        ]
    );
}
