#[test]
fn test_comic_settings_auto_reading_mode() {
    let default_settings = AppSettings::default();
    assert_eq!(default_settings.comic.reading_mode, "Auto");

    let is_manga_doc = true;
    let effective = match default_settings.comic.reading_mode.as_str() {
        "Manga" => true,
        "Comic" => false,
        "Webtoon" => false,
        _ => is_manga_doc,
    };
    assert!(effective);

    let not_manga_doc = false;
    let effective_regular = match default_settings.comic.reading_mode.as_str() {
        "Manga" => true,
        "Comic" => false,
        "Webtoon" => false,
        _ => not_manga_doc,
    };
    assert!(!effective_regular);
}

#[test]
fn test_archive_path_traversal_protection() {
    use crate::document::{ComicDocument, DocumentError, is_safe_archive_path};
    use std::io::Write;

    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("../evil.png"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("ch1/../../evil.png"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("/etc/passwd"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path(r"\Windows\System32\cmd.exe"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("C:/evil.png"));
    assert!(is_safe_archive_path("ch01/page_01.png"));

    let test_dir = unique_test_dir("traversal");
    let cbz_path = test_dir.join("traversal.cbz");

    {
        let file = std::fs::File::create(&cbz_path).unwrap();
        let mut zip = zip::ZipWriter::new(file);
        let options = zip::write::SimpleFileOptions::default();
        zip.start_file("../evil.png", options).unwrap();
        zip.write_all(b"fake_image").unwrap();
        zip.finish().unwrap();
    }

    let res = ComicDocument::from_path(&cbz_path);
    assert!(matches!(res, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&test_dir);
}

#[test]
fn test_zip_bomb_and_bounded_read_protection() {
    use crate::document::{DocumentError, read_bounded};

    let data = vec![0u8; 2048];
    let mut total_unpacked = 0u64;

    let res_single = read_bounded(&data[..], 1024, &mut total_unpacked, 10_000);
    assert!(matches!(res_single, Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(_))));

    let mut total_unpacked = 9500u64;
    let res_total = read_bounded(&data[..1000], 2048, &mut total_unpacked, 10_000);
    assert!(matches!(res_total, Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(_))));
}

#[test]
fn test_archive_excessive_entries_protection() {
    use crate::document::{DocumentError, EpubDocument, MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES};

    let mut zip_buf = std::io::Cursor::new(Vec::new());
    {
        let mut zip = zip::ZipWriter::new(&mut zip_buf);
        let options = zip::write::SimpleFileOptions::default()
            .compression_method(zip::CompressionMethod::Stored);
        for i in 0..=MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES {
            zip.start_file(format!("f_{i}.txt"), options).unwrap();
        }
        zip.finish().unwrap();
    }

    let res = EpubDocument::from_bytes(&zip_buf.into_inner());
    assert!(matches!(res, Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(_))));
}

#[test]
fn test_corrupted_pdf_epub_fb2_handling() {
    use crate::document::{DocumentError, EpubDocument, Fb2Document, PdfDocument};
    use std::path::Path;

    let bad_pdf = PdfDocument::from_bytes(b"NOT_A_VALID_PDF_FILE".to_vec(), Path::new("bad.pdf"));
    assert!(matches!(bad_pdf, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));

    let bad_pdf_header =
        PdfDocument::from_bytes(b"%PDF-1.4\nBROKEN_GARBAGE".to_vec(), Path::new("bad2.pdf"));
    assert!(matches!(bad_pdf_header, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));

    let bad_epub = EpubDocument::from_bytes(b"NOT_A_ZIP_ARCHIVE");
    assert!(matches!(bad_epub, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));

    let bad_fb2 = Fb2Document::from_bytes(b"THIS IS NOT XML OR FICTIONBOOK AT ALL");
    assert!(matches!(bad_fb2, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));
}

#[test]
fn test_corrupted_settings_json_backup_and_recovery() {
    let temp_dir = unique_test_dir("corrupted_settings");
    let settings_path = temp_dir.join("settings.json");
    let bak_path = temp_dir.join("settings.json.bak");
    let corrupted_content = "{ \"font_size\": BROKEN_JSON !!!";
    std::fs::write(&settings_path, corrupted_content).unwrap();

    let loaded = AppSettings::load_from_path(&settings_path);
    assert_eq!(loaded, AppSettings::default());
    assert!(bak_path.exists());
    assert_eq!(
        std::fs::read_to_string(&bak_path).unwrap(),
        corrupted_content
    );

    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&temp_dir);
}

#[test]
fn test_txt_md_log_format_via_registry() {
    let temp_dir = unique_test_dir("txt_md_log");
    let md_path = temp_dir.join("notes.md");
    std::fs::write(
        &md_path,
        "# Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1. Р’РІРµРґРµРЅРёРµ\n\nРџРµСЂРІС‹Р№ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†.\n\n## Р“Р»Р°РІР° 2. Р”РµС‚Р°Р»Рё\n\nР’С‚РѕСЂРѕР№ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†.",
    )
    .unwrap();

    let doc = document::open_document(&md_path).expect("Р”РѕР»Р¶РµРЅ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ MD С‡РµСЂРµР· СЂРµРµСЃС‚СЂ");
    assert_eq!(doc.chapters().len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(doc.toc().len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(doc.toc()[0].title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1. Р’РІРµРґРµРЅРёРµ");
    assert_eq!(doc.toc()[1].title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° 2. Р”РµС‚Р°Р»Рё");

    let txt_path = temp_dir.join("sample.txt");
    std::fs::write(&txt_path, "РђР±Р·Р°С† РїРµСЂРІС‹Р№.\nР’С‚РѕСЂР°СЏ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР°.\n\nРђР±Р·Р°С† РІС‚РѕСЂРѕР№.").unwrap();
    let txt_doc = document::open_document(&txt_path).expect("Р”РѕР»Р¶РµРЅ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ TXT С‡РµСЂРµР· СЂРµРµСЃС‚СЂ");
    assert_eq!(txt_doc.chapters().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(txt_doc.chapters()[0].paragraphs.len(), 2);

    let log_path = temp_dir.join("system.log");
    std::fs::write(&log_path, "[INFO] Started\n[WARN] Low memory\n").unwrap();
    let log_doc = document::open_document(&log_path).expect("Р”РѕР»Р¶РµРЅ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ LOG С‡РµСЂРµР· СЂРµРµСЃС‚СЂ");
    assert_eq!(log_doc.chapters().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(log_doc.chapters()[0].paragraphs.len(), 2);

    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&temp_dir);
}

#[test]
fn test_settings_schema_version_migration_with_backup() {
    let temp_dir = unique_test_dir("schema_migration");
    let settings_path = temp_dir.join("settings.json");
    let bak_path = temp_dir.join("settings.json.bak");
    let legacy_json = r#"{"window":{"width":900.0,"height":700.0,"maximized":false},"recent_books":[],"font_size":21.0,"theme":"Dark","keep_screen_on":false,"show_toc":true}"#;
    std::fs::write(&settings_path, legacy_json).unwrap();

    let migrated = AppSettings::load_from_path(&settings_path);
    assert_eq!(
        migrated.schema_version,
        settings::CURRENT_SETTINGS_SCHEMA_VERSION
    );
    assert_eq!(migrated.font_size, 21.0);
    assert_eq!(migrated.theme, "Dark");
    assert!(bak_path.exists());
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(&bak_path).unwrap(), legacy_json);

    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&temp_dir);
}

#[test]
fn test_utf16_txt_decoding_and_pageup_prev_chapter() {
    use crate::document::Document;
    let text = "РџСЂРёРІРµС‚, РјРёСЂ!\n\nР’С‚РѕСЂРѕР№ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†.";
    let mut utf16le_bytes = vec![0xFF, 0xFE];
    for code_unit in text.encode_utf16() {
        utf16le_bytes.extend_from_slice(&code_unit.to_le_bytes());
    }
    let doc = document::TxtDocument::from_bytes(&utf16le_bytes, "UTF16Test", "txt")
        .expect("Р”РѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ РґРµРєРѕРґРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ UTF-16LE");
    assert_eq!(doc.chapters().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(doc.chapters()[0].paragraphs[0], "РџСЂРёРІРµС‚, РјРёСЂ!");
    assert_eq!(doc.chapters()[0].paragraphs[1], "Р’С‚РѕСЂРѕР№ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†.");

    let md_bytes = b"# Ch1\n\nText 1\n\n# Ch2\n\nText 2";
    let md_doc = document::TxtDocument::from_bytes(md_bytes, "MdBook", "md").unwrap();
    let mut ch = 1usize;
    let mut last_rendered = 1usize;
    let mut pending_scroll = None;
    let changed = crate::core::ReaderCore::apply_text_command(
        &crate::core::Command::PageUp,
        &md_doc,
        &mut ch,
        &mut last_rendered,
        0.0,
        500.0,
        600.0,
        &mut pending_scroll,
    );
    assert!(changed);
    assert_eq!(ch, 0);
    assert_eq!(pending_scroll, Some(999_999.0));
}

#[test]
fn test_safe_archive_path_checks() {
    use crate::document::is_safe_archive_path;
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path(""));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("../secret"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("foo/../../secret"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("foo/../secret"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("/etc/passwd"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("C:\\windows\\system32"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("C:foo"));
    assert!(is_safe_archive_path("page01.png"));
    assert!(is_safe_archive_path("sub/folder/page01.png"));
}

    assert!(is_supported_image("test.png"));
    assert!(is_supported_image("folder/test.jpg"));
    assert!(is_supported_image("test.webp"));
    assert!(!is_supported_image(".hidden.png"));
    assert!(!is_supported_image("test.exe"));
    assert!(!is_supported_image("__MACOSX/page.png"));
}

#[test]
fn test_fb2_deep_section_recursion_bounded() {
    use crate::document::Document;
    use crate::document::Fb2Document;
    let mut xml = String::from("<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"utf-8\"?><FictionBook xmlns=\"http://www.gribuser.ru/xml/fictionbook/2.0\"><body>");
    for i in 0..50 {
        xml.push_str(&format!("<section><title><p>Р“Р»Р°РІР° {}</p></title><p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ {}</p>", i, i));
    }
    for _ in 0..50 {
        xml.push_str("</section>");
    }
    xml.push_str("</body></FictionBook>");

    let doc = Fb2Document::from_bytes(xml.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    assert!(!doc.chapters().is_empty());
    assert!(!doc.toc().is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn test_settings_corrupted_backup_protection() {
    let temp_dir = unique_test_dir("bak_protection");
    let settings_path = temp_dir.join("settings.json");
    let bak_path = temp_dir.join("settings.json.bak");

    let valid_backup_json = r#"{"schema_version":2,"font_size":27.0,"theme":"Oled"}"#;
    std::fs::write(&bak_path, valid_backup_json).unwrap();

    let broken_json = "{ truncated invalid json ...";
    std::fs::write(&settings_path, broken_json).unwrap();

    let restored = AppSettings::load_from_path(&settings_path);
    assert_eq!(restored.font_size, 27.0);
    assert_eq!(restored.theme, "Oled");

    let bak_content = std::fs::read_to_string(&bak_path).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(bak_content, valid_backup_json);

    let corrupted_path = temp_dir.join("settings.corrupted");
    assert!(corrupted_path.exists());

    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&temp_dir);
}

#[test]
fn test_reading_progress_separation_from_recent_books() {
    let mut settings = AppSettings::default();

    for i in 1..=30 {
        let p = std::path::PathBuf::from(format!("C:\\Books\\book_{i}.epub"));
        settings.record_book_progress(&p, &format!("РљРЅРёРіР° {i}"), "РђРІС‚РѕСЂ", i, (i * 10) as f32);
    }

    assert_eq!(settings.recent_books.len(), 25);
    assert_eq!(settings.reading_progress.len(), 30);

    let old_book_path = std::path::PathBuf::from("C:\\Books\\book_1.epub");
    assert!(settings.recent_books.iter().all(|b| b.path != old_book_path));
    assert_eq!(settings.get_book_progress(&old_book_path), Some((1, 10.0)));

    settings.recent_books.clear();
    assert_eq!(settings.recent_books.len(), 0);
    assert_eq!(settings.get_book_progress(&old_book_path), Some((1, 10.0)));
}

#[test]

    let minimal_json = r#"{"font_size": 22.0}"#;

    assert_eq!(settings.font_size, 22.0);

    assert_eq!(settings.theme, "Sepia");
    assert!(settings.keep_screen_on);
    assert!(settings.show_toc);
    assert_eq!(settings.comic.reading_mode, "Auto");
    assert_eq!(settings.pdf.layout, "Single");
    assert!(settings.reading_progress.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn test_pdf_transitive_glyph_sorting_and_line_clustering() {
    use crate::document::pdf::text::{group_and_sort_glyphs, GlyphEntry};

    let glyphs = vec![
        GlyphEntry { c: 'l', x: 20.0, y: 10.2, font_size: 10.0, width: 5.0 },
        GlyphEntry { c: 'H', x: 10.0, y: 10.0, font_size: 10.0, width: 5.0 },
        GlyphEntry { c: 'e', x: 15.0, y: 9.8, font_size: 10.0, width: 5.0 },
        GlyphEntry { c: 'o', x: 25.0, y: 10.1, font_size: 10.0, width: 5.0 },
        GlyphEntry { c: 'W', x: 10.0, y: 30.0, font_size: 10.0, width: 5.0 },
        GlyphEntry { c: 'o', x: 15.0, y: 30.2, font_size: 10.0, width: 5.0 },
        GlyphEntry { c: 'r', x: 20.0, y: 29.9, font_size: 10.0, width: 5.0 },
        GlyphEntry { c: 'l', x: 25.0, y: 30.0, font_size: 10.0, width: 5.0 },
        GlyphEntry { c: 'd', x: 30.0, y: 30.1, font_size: 10.0, width: 5.0 },
    ];

    let spans = group_and_sort_glyphs(glyphs, 200.0, 200.0);
    assert_eq!(spans.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(spans[0].text, "Helo");
    assert_eq!(spans[1].text, "World");
}

#[test]
fn test_keyboard_input_suppressed_when_wants_keyboard_input() {
    use crate::app::input::process_keyboard_events;
    use eframe::egui::{Key, Modifiers};

    let dummy_modifiers = Modifiers::default();
    let cmds = process_keyboard_events(
        true,
        |k| matches!(k, Key::A | Key::Plus | Key::Space | Key::ArrowRight | Key::F),
        dummy_modifiers,
        false,
    );
    assert!(cmds.is_empty());

    let cmds_not_focused = process_keyboard_events(
        false,
        |k| matches!(k, Key::F),
        dummy_modifiers,
        false,
    );
    assert_eq!(cmds_not_focused, vec![crate::core::Command::ToggleFullscreen]);
}

#[test]
fn test_keyboard_manga_vs_comic_navigation_direction() {
    use crate::app::input::process_keyboard_events;
    use eframe::egui::{Key, Modifiers};

    let dummy_modifiers = Modifiers::default();

    let manga_left = process_keyboard_events(
        false,
        |k| k == Key::ArrowLeft,
        dummy_modifiers,
        true,
    );
    assert_eq!(manga_left, vec![crate::core::Command::NextPage]);

    let manga_right = process_keyboard_events(
        false,
        |k| k == Key::ArrowRight,
        dummy_modifiers,
        true,
    );
    assert_eq!(manga_right, vec![crate::core::Command::PrevPage]);

    let comic_left = process_keyboard_events(
        false,
        |k| k == Key::ArrowLeft,
        dummy_modifiers,
        false,
    );
    assert_eq!(comic_left, vec![crate::core::Command::PrevPage]);

    let comic_right = process_keyboard_events(
        false,
        |k| k == Key::ArrowRight,
        dummy_modifiers,
        false,
    );
    assert_eq!(comic_right, vec![crate::core::Command::NextPage]);
}

#[test]
fn test_autoscroll_controller_state_and_paged_advance() {
    use crate::app::autoscroll::AutoscrollController;

    let mut controller = AutoscrollController::default();
    assert!(!controller.is_active());

    controller.toggle(100.0, 500.0, 60.0);
    assert!(controller.is_active());
    assert_eq!(controller.step_start_scroll_y, 100.0);
    assert_eq!(controller.target_step_scroll_y, 160.0);

    controller.stop();
    assert!(!controller.is_active());
}

        panic!("Simulated decoder fatal error");
    }));
    assert!(result.is_err());
}

#[test]
fn test_document_loader_async_request_and_response() {
    use crate::app::document_loader::DocumentLoader;
    use std::path::PathBuf;
    use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

    let mut loader = DocumentLoader::default();
    let test_path = PathBuf::from("nonexistent_test_document_12345.epub");
    let req_id = loader.request_load(test_path.clone());
    assert!(req_id > 0);

    let start = Instant::now();
    let mut received = None;
    while start.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(3) {
        let responses = loader.poll_responses();
        if let Some(resp) = responses.into_iter().find(|r| r.request_id == req_id) {
            received = Some(resp);
            break;
        }
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(15));
    }

    let resp = received.expect("Background thread should return a load response");
    assert_eq!(resp.request_id, req_id);
    assert_eq!(resp.path, test_path);
    assert!(resp.result.is_err());
}

#[test]
fn test_book_tab_loading_and_update_lifecycle() {
    use crate::app::tabs::BookTab;
    use std::path::PathBuf;

    let path = PathBuf::from("sample_book.pdf");
    let tab = BookTab::new_loading(path.clone(), 77);
    assert!(tab.is_loading);
    assert!(tab.document.is_none());
    assert!(tab.load_error.is_none());
    assert_eq!(tab.request_id, 77);
    assert_eq!(tab.format_badge, "PDF");
    assert_eq!(tab.file_path, path);
}

#[test]
fn test_obsolete_response_discard_on_tab_close_and_reopen() {
    use crate::app::tabs::BookTab;
    use std::path::PathBuf;

    let path = PathBuf::from("chapter1.fb2");
    let mut tabs = vec![BookTab::new_loading(path.clone(), 1)];

    tabs.clear();
    let stale_resp_id = 1u64;
    let matching = tabs
        .iter()
        .position(|t| t.file_path == path && t.request_id == stale_resp_id);
    assert_eq!(matching, None);

    tabs.push(BookTab::new_loading(path.clone(), 2));

    let matching_stale = tabs
        .iter()
        .position(|t| t.file_path == path && t.request_id == stale_resp_id);
    assert_eq!(matching_stale, None);

    let matching_fresh = tabs
        .iter()
        .position(|t| t.file_path == path && t.request_id == 2);
    assert_eq!(matching_fresh, Some(0));
}

#[test]
fn test_document_load_error_formatting_variants() {
    use crate::app::document_loader::format_document_error;
    use crate::document::DocumentError;

    let io_err = format_document_error(&DocumentError::Io("file missing".to_string()));
    assert!(io_err.contains("РћС€РёР±РєР° РІРІРѕРґР°-РІС‹РІРѕРґР°"));

    let unsup_err = format_document_error(&DocumentError::Unsupported("raw".to_string()));
    assert!(unsup_err.contains("РќРµРїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РёРІР°РµРјС‹Р№ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚"));

    let corr_err = format_document_error(&DocumentError::Corrupted("invalid header".to_string()));
    assert!(corr_err.contains("РџРѕРІСЂРµР¶РґС‘РЅРЅС‹Р№ РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚"));

    let lim_err = format_document_error(&DocumentError::LimitExceeded("size limit".to_string()));
    assert!(lim_err.contains("РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚ Р±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё"));
}

#[test]
fn test_pdf_view_scale_discretization() {
    use crate::app::pdf_view::PdfViewState;
    use crate::settings::PdfSettings;

    let state = PdfViewState::default();
    let mut settings = PdfSettings::default();

    settings.fit_mode = "Original".to_string();
    assert_eq!(state.compute_target_scale(&settings), 2.0);

    settings.fit_mode = "FitWidth".to_string();
    assert_eq!(state.compute_target_scale(&settings), 2.5);

    settings.fit_mode = "FitWindow".to_string();
    assert_eq!(state.compute_target_scale(&settings), 2.25);

    settings.fit_mode = "Custom".to_string();
    settings.zoom_percent = 100.0;
    let s1 = state.compute_target_scale(&settings);
    assert!((s1 * 4.0).fract() < 1e-4);

    settings.zoom_percent = 104.0;
    let s2 = state.compute_target_scale(&settings);
    assert_eq!(s1, s2);
}

#[test]
fn test_pdf_view_clear_cache_and_document_generation_isolation() {
    use crate::app::pdf_view::PdfViewState;

    let mut state = PdfViewState::default();
    assert_eq!(state.doc_generation, 0);

    state.page_input = "42".to_string();
    state.clear_cache();
    assert_eq!(state.doc_generation, 1);
    assert_eq!(state.total_texture_bytes, 0);
    assert!(state.textures.is_empty());
    assert!(state.pending_pages.is_empty());
    assert!(state.page_text_spans.is_empty());
    assert!(!state.selection_dirty);
    assert!(state.selected_text.is_empty());

    state.clear_cache();
    assert_eq!(state.doc_generation, 2);
}

#[test]
fn test_pdf_selection_state_and_dirty_flag() {
    use crate::app::pdf_view::PdfViewState;
    use eframe::egui::Pos2;

    let mut state = PdfViewState::default();
    assert!(!state.selection_dirty);
    assert!(state.selected_text.is_empty());

    state.selection_start = Some(Pos2::new(10.0, 10.0));
    state.selection_current = Some(Pos2::new(50.0, 50.0));
    state.selection_dirty = true;
    state.selected_text = "Sample extracted text".to_string();

    state.clear_cache();
    assert!(state.selection_start.is_none());
    assert!(state.selection_current.is_none());
    assert!(!state.selection_dirty);
    assert!(state.selected_text.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn test_settings_save_debounce_state_and_flush() {
    use crate::app::QuietReaderApp;
    use crate::settings::AppSettings;

    let mut app = QuietReaderApp::from_settings(AppSettings::default());
    assert!(!app.settings_dirty);

    app.mark_settings_dirty();
    assert!(app.settings_dirty);

    app.flush_debounced_settings();
    assert!(app.settings_dirty);

    app.flush_settings_now();
    assert!(!app.settings_dirty);
}

#[test]
fn test_text_view_virtualization_bounds_estimation() {
    let font_size = 19.0f32;
    let line_height = font_size * 1.45;
    let para_spacing = font_size * 0.4;
    let chars_per_line = (760.0 / (font_size * 0.52)).max(10.0);

    let short_text = "Short single line.";
    let char_count = short_text.chars().count();
    let num_lines = (char_count as f32 / chars_per_line).ceil().max(1.0);
    assert_eq!(num_lines, 1.0);
    let est_height = num_lines * line_height + para_spacing;
    assert!(est_height > 20.0 && est_height < 50.0);

    let view_top = 100.0f32;
    let view_bot = 800.0f32;

    let top_y_offscreen_above = 0.0f32;
    let bot_y_offscreen_above = top_y_offscreen_above + est_height;
    assert!(bot_y_offscreen_above < view_top);

    let top_y_visible = 300.0f32;
    let bot_y_visible = top_y_visible + est_height;
    assert!(!(bot_y_visible < view_top || top_y_visible > view_bot));

    let top_y_offscreen_below = 1200.0f32;
    assert!(top_y_offscreen_below > view_bot);
}

#[test]
fn test_pdf_transform_span_bbox_all_rotations() {
    use crate::app::pdf_view::transform_span_bbox;
    use eframe::egui;
    let rect = egui::Rect::from_min_max(egui::Pos2::new(100.0, 200.0), egui::Pos2::new(300.0, 600.0));
    let bbox = [0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 0.4];

    let r0 = transform_span_bbox(bbox, 0, rect);
    assert!((r0.min.x - 120.0).abs() < 1e-3);
    assert!((r0.min.y - 280.0).abs() < 1e-3);
    assert!((r0.max.x - 200.0).abs() < 1e-3);
    assert!((r0.max.y - 360.0).abs() < 1e-3);

    let r90 = transform_span_bbox(bbox, 90, rect);
    assert!((r90.min.x - 220.0).abs() < 1e-3);
    assert!((r90.max.x - 260.0).abs() < 1e-3);
    assert!((r90.min.y - 240.0).abs() < 1e-3);
    assert!((r90.max.y - 400.0).abs() < 1e-3);

    let r180 = transform_span_bbox(bbox, 180, rect);
    assert!((r180.min.x - 200.0).abs() < 1e-3);
    assert!((r180.max.x - 280.0).abs() < 1e-3);
    assert!((r180.min.y - 440.0).abs() < 1e-3);
    assert!((r180.max.y - 520.0).abs() < 1e-3);

    let r270 = transform_span_bbox(bbox, 270, rect);
    assert!((r270.min.x - 140.0).abs() < 1e-3);
    assert!((r270.max.x - 180.0).abs() < 1e-3);
    assert!((r270.min.y - 400.0).abs() < 1e-3);
    assert!((r270.max.y - 560.0).abs() < 1e-3);
}

#[test]
fn test_pdf_reading_order_two_columns_and_gutter() {
    use crate::document::pdf::text::{group_and_sort_glyphs, GlyphEntry};
    let mut glyphs = Vec::new();
    for (i, c) in "Left1".chars().enumerate() {
        glyphs.push(GlyphEntry { c, x: 50.0 + i as f64 * 10.0, y: 300.0, font_size: 14.0, width: 10.0 });
    }
    for (i, c) in "Right1".chars().enumerate() {
        glyphs.push(GlyphEntry { c, x: 450.0 + i as f64 * 10.0, y: 300.0, font_size: 14.0, width: 10.0 });
    }
    for (i, c) in "Left2".chars().enumerate() {
        glyphs.push(GlyphEntry { c, x: 50.0 + i as f64 * 10.0, y: 350.0, font_size: 14.0, width: 10.0 });
    }
    for (i, c) in "Right2".chars().enumerate() {
        glyphs.push(GlyphEntry { c, x: 450.0 + i as f64 * 10.0, y: 350.0, font_size: 14.0, width: 10.0 });
    }
    for (i, c) in "Left3".chars().enumerate() {
        glyphs.push(GlyphEntry { c, x: 50.0 + i as f64 * 10.0, y: 400.0, font_size: 14.0, width: 10.0 });
    }
    for (i, c) in "Right3".chars().enumerate() {
        glyphs.push(GlyphEntry { c, x: 450.0 + i as f64 * 10.0, y: 400.0, font_size: 14.0, width: 10.0 });
    }

    let spans = group_and_sort_glyphs(glyphs, 800.0, 1000.0);
    assert_eq!(spans.len(), 6);
    let texts: Vec<&str> = spans.iter().map(|s| s.text.as_str()).collect();
    assert_eq!(texts, vec!["Left1", "Left2", "Left3", "Right1", "Right2", "Right3"]);
}

#[test]
fn test_pdf_outline_resolve_dest_clamping() {
    use hayro::hayro_syntax::object::{Array, FromBytes};
    use std::collections::HashMap;

    let dest_data = b"[ 5 /XYZ 0 792 1.0 ]";
    if let Some(arr) = Array::from_bytes(dest_data) {
        let page_map = HashMap::new();
        let page_count = 3;
        let resolved = crate::document::pdf::outline::resolve_dest_array(&arr, &page_map, page_count);
        assert_eq!(resolved, Some(2));
    }
}

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
use crate::app::document_loader::DocumentLoader;
use crate::document::{
    is_safe_archive_path, read_bounded, DjvuDocument, DocumentError, EpubDocument,
    Fb2Document, PdfDocument, MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES, MAX_SINGLE_ENTRY_BYTES, MAX_TOTAL_UNPACK_BYTES,
};
use crate::settings::AppSettings;

fn unique_test_dir(prefix: &str) -> PathBuf {
    static COUNTER: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);
    let id = COUNTER.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    let pid = std::process::id();
    let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("quietreader_crit_{prefix}_{pid}_{id}"));
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
    let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir);
    dir
}

#[test]
fn test_read_bounded_limits_and_overflow_protection() {
    let data = vec![0xAAu8; 4096];
    let mut total_unpacked = 0u64;

    let ok_read = read_bounded(&data[..100], 500, &mut total_unpacked, 1000);
    assert!(ok_read.is_ok());
    assert_eq!(ok_read.unwrap().len(), 100);
    assert_eq!(total_unpacked, 100);

    let err_single = read_bounded(&data[..], 200, &mut total_unpacked, 10000);
    assert!(matches!(err_single, Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(_))));

    let mut total_unpacked = 900u64;
    let err_total = read_bounded(&data[..200], 1000, &mut total_unpacked, 1000);
    assert!(matches!(err_total, Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(_))));

    let mut total_unpacked = u64::MAX - 5;
    let err_overflow = read_bounded(&data[..10], 100, &mut total_unpacked, u64::MAX);
    assert!(matches!(err_overflow, Err(DocumentError::LimitExceeded(_))));
}

#[test]
fn test_is_safe_archive_path_comprehensive() {
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path(""));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path(" "));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path(".."));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("../test.txt"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("..\\test.txt"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("foo/../bar"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("foo/..\\bar"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("/etc/shadow"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("\\Windows\\System32"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("C:\\autoexec.bat"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("D:file.txt"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("\\\\server\\share\\file"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("//server/share/file"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("%2e%2e/evil.txt"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("..%2fevil.txt"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("%2e%2e%5cevil.txt"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("%2fetc/passwd"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("%5cWindows"));

    assert!(is_safe_archive_path("chapter1.xhtml"));
    assert!(is_safe_archive_path("images/cover.jpg"));
    assert!(is_safe_archive_path("OEBPS/text/part1.xhtml"));
    assert!(is_safe_archive_path("a/b/c/d/e.png"));
}

#[test]
fn test_corrupted_and_truncated_djvu_handling() {
    let dummy_path = Path::new("test.djvu");

    let res_empty = DjvuDocument::from_bytes(Vec::new(), dummy_path);
    assert!(res_empty.is_err());

    let res_short = DjvuDocument::from_bytes(vec![0x41, 0x54], dummy_path);
    assert!(res_short.is_err());

    let res_garbage = DjvuDocument::from_bytes(b"RANDOM_NON_DJVU_DATA_BYTES".to_vec(), dummy_path);
    assert!(res_garbage.is_err());

    let res_bad_form = DjvuDocument::from_bytes(b"AT&TXXXX\x00\x00\x00\x04DJVU".to_vec(), dummy_path);
    assert!(res_bad_form.is_err());

    let res_trunc = DjvuDocument::from_bytes(b"AT&TFORM".to_vec(), dummy_path);
    assert!(res_trunc.is_err());
}

#[test]
fn test_corrupted_and_truncated_pdf_handling() {
    let dummy_path = Path::new("test.pdf");

    let res_empty = PdfDocument::from_bytes(Vec::new(), dummy_path);
    assert!(matches!(res_empty, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));

    let res_short = PdfDocument::from_bytes(vec![b'%', b'P'], dummy_path);
    assert!(matches!(res_short, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));

    let res_garbage = PdfDocument::from_bytes(b"THIS_IS_DEFINITELY_NOT_A_PDF".to_vec(), dummy_path);
    assert!(matches!(res_garbage, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));

    let res_header_only = PdfDocument::from_bytes(b"%PDF-1.7\n%%EOF".to_vec(), dummy_path);
    assert!(matches!(res_header_only, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));
}

#[test]
fn test_corrupted_and_malformed_epub_handling() {
    let res_empty = EpubDocument::from_bytes(&[]);
    assert!(matches!(res_empty, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));

    let res_garbage = EpubDocument::from_bytes(b"PK\x03\x04\x00\x00BROKEN_ZIP_DATA");
    assert!(matches!(res_garbage, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));

    let mut zip_buf = std::io::Cursor::new(Vec::new());
    {
        let mut zip = zip::ZipWriter::new(&mut zip_buf);
        let options = zip::write::SimpleFileOptions::default()
            .compression_method(zip::CompressionMethod::Stored);
        zip.start_file("unrelated.txt", options).unwrap();
        std::io::Write::write_all(&mut zip, b"no container xml").unwrap();
        zip.finish().unwrap();
    }
    let res_no_container = EpubDocument::from_bytes(&zip_buf.into_inner());
    assert!(matches!(res_no_container, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));
}

#[test]
fn test_corrupted_and_malformed_fb2_handling() {
    let res_empty = Fb2Document::from_bytes(&[]);
    assert!(matches!(res_empty, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));

    let res_garbage = Fb2Document::from_bytes(b"PLAIN TEXT WITHOUT ANY XML TAGS");
    assert!(matches!(res_garbage, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));

    let res_invalid_xml = Fb2Document::from_bytes(b"<FictionBook><body><section><title><p>Broken");
    assert!(matches!(res_invalid_xml, Err(DocumentError::Corrupted(_))));
}

#[test]
fn test_reading_progress_persistence_and_normalization() {
    let mut settings = AppSettings::default();
    let p1 = PathBuf::from("C:\\Books\\Library\\Novel.epub");
    let p1_lower = PathBuf::from("c:\\books\\library\\novel.epub");
    let p2 = PathBuf::from("D:\\Comics\\Manga_Vol1.cbz");

    settings.record_book_progress(&p1, "Novel Title", "Author Name", 4, 320.0);
    assert_eq!(settings.get_book_progress(&p1), Some((4, 320.0)));
    assert_eq!(settings.get_book_progress(&p1_lower), Some((4, 320.0)));

    settings.record_book_progress(&p2, "Manga Title", "Mangaka", 12, 0.0);
    assert_eq!(settings.get_book_progress(&p2), Some((12, 0.0)));

    assert_eq!(settings.reading_progress.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(settings.recent_books.len(), 2);

    settings.recent_books.clear();
    assert_eq!(settings.recent_books.len(), 0);
    assert_eq!(settings.get_book_progress(&p1), Some((4, 320.0)));
    assert_eq!(settings.get_book_progress(&p2), Some((12, 0.0)));
}

#[test]
fn test_atomic_settings_save_and_backup_safety() {
    let temp_dir = unique_test_dir("save_safety");
    let settings_path = temp_dir.join("settings.json");
    let bak_path = temp_dir.join("settings.json.bak");
    let corrupted_path = temp_dir.join("settings.corrupted");

    let mut settings = AppSettings::default();
    settings.font_size = 28.0;
    settings.theme = "Sepia".to_string();

    let save_res = settings.save_to_path(&settings_path);
    assert!(save_res.is_ok());
    assert!(settings_path.exists());

    let loaded = AppSettings::load_from_path(&settings_path);
    assert_eq!(loaded.font_size, 28.0);
    assert_eq!(loaded.theme, "Sepia");

    std::fs::write(&bak_path, r#"{"schema_version":2,"font_size":25.0,"theme":"Dark"}"#).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(&settings_path, "{ broken invalid json...").unwrap();

    let recovered = AppSettings::load_from_path(&settings_path);
    assert_eq!(recovered.font_size, 25.0);
    assert_eq!(recovered.theme, "Dark");
    assert!(corrupted_path.exists());

    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&temp_dir);
}

#[test]
fn test_async_loader_cancellation_and_generation_switch() {
    let mut loader = DocumentLoader::default();
    let path_a = PathBuf::from("nonexistent_book_a.epub");
    let path_b = PathBuf::from("nonexistent_book_b.pdf");

    let req_a = loader.request_load(path_a.clone());
    let req_b = loader.request_load(path_b.clone());

    assert!(req_a > 0);
    assert!(req_b > req_a);

    let start = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut finished = Vec::new();
    while start.elapsed() < std::time::Duration::from_secs(3) && finished.len() < 2 {
        for resp in loader.poll_responses() {
            finished.push(resp);
        }
        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(20));
    }

    assert_eq!(finished.len(), 2);
    let resp_a = finished.iter().find(|r| r.request_id == req_a).unwrap();
    let resp_b = finished.iter().find(|r| r.request_id == req_b).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(resp_a.path, path_a);
    assert_eq!(resp_b.path, path_b);
    assert!(resp_a.result.is_err());
    assert!(resp_b.result.is_err());
}

#[test]
fn test_resource_limits_constants() {
    assert!(MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES >= 1000);
    assert!(MAX_SINGLE_ENTRY_BYTES >= 10 * 1024 * 1024);
    assert!(MAX_TOTAL_UNPACK_BYTES >= 50 * 1024 * 1024);
    assert!(MAX_TOTAL_UNPACK_BYTES > MAX_SINGLE_ENTRY_BYTES);
}

        ch7.paragraphs

    );

fn test_section_with_untitled_preamble_subsection() {

                <title>

    assert_eq!(ch0.paragraphs.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(ch0.paragraphs[0], "Р’РІРѕРґРЅС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚ РіР»Р°РІС‹ 7, РґРёРєС‚РѕРІРєР°.");

    let ch1 = &doc.chapters()[toc[0].children[0].chapter_index];
    assert_eq!(ch1.title, "РЎРµР°РЅСЃ 631");
    assert_eq!(ch1.paragraphs[0], "РўРµРєСЃС‚ СЃРµР°РЅСЃР° 631.");
}

#[test]
fn test_multiple_top_level_chapters() {
    let xml = r#"<FictionBook><body>
            <section><title><p>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1</p></title><p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ 1</p></section>
            <section><title><p>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 2</p></title><p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ 2</p></section>
            <section><title><p>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 3</p></title><p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ 3</p></section>
        </body></FictionBook>"#;

    let doc = Fb2Document::from_bytes(xml.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    let toc = doc.toc();
    assert_eq!(toc.len(), 3);
    assert_eq!(toc[0].title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1");
    assert_eq!(toc[1].title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° 2");
    assert_eq!(toc[2].title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° 3");
    assert!(toc[0].children.is_empty());
    assert!(toc[1].children.is_empty());
    assert!(toc[2].children.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn test_nested_subsections_multiple_levels() {
    let xml = r#"<FictionBook><body>
            <section>
                <title><p>Р§Р°СЃС‚СЊ 1</p></title>
                <p>РџСЂРµРґРёСЃР»РѕРІРёРµ С‡Р°СЃС‚Рё 1</p>
                <section>
                    <title><p>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1.1</p></title>
                    <p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ РіР»Р°РІС‹ 1.1</p>
                    <section>
                        <title><p>РџРѕРґСЂР°Р·РґРµР» 1.1.1</p></title>
                        <p>Р“Р»СѓР±РѕРєРёР№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚</p>
                    </section>
                </section>
            </section>
        </body></FictionBook>"#;

    let doc = Fb2Document::from_bytes(xml.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    let toc = doc.toc();
    assert_eq!(toc.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(toc[0].title, "Р§Р°СЃС‚СЊ 1");
    assert_eq!(toc[0].children.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(toc[0].children[0].title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1.1");
    assert_eq!(toc[0].children[0].children.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(toc[0].children[0].children[0].title, "РџРѕРґСЂР°Р·РґРµР» 1.1.1");
}

#[test]
fn test_sections_without_titles_with_nested_titled_sections() {
    let xml = r#"<FictionBook><body>
            <section>
                <p>Р’РІРѕРґРЅС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚ СЃРµРєС†РёРё Р±РµР· Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєР°</p>
                <section>
                    <title><p>Р’Р»РѕР¶РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє Рђ</p></title>
                    <p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ Рђ</p>
                </section>
                <section>
                    <title><p>Р’Р»РѕР¶РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє Р‘</p></title>
                    <p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ Р‘</p>
                </section>
            </section>
        </body></FictionBook>"#;

    let doc = Fb2Document::from_bytes(xml.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    let toc = doc.toc();
    assert_eq!(toc.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(toc[0].title, "Р’Р»РѕР¶РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє Рђ");
    assert_eq!(toc[1].title, "Р’Р»РѕР¶РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє Р‘");
}

#[test]
fn test_titles_with_nested_xml_elements() {
    let xml = r#"<FictionBook><body>
            <section>
                <title>
                    <p><strong>Р§Р°СЃС‚СЊ I.</strong> <em>РќР°С‡Р°Р»Рѕ</em></p>
                    <p>Р’ <style name="highlight">РіРѕСЂРѕРґРµ</style> N</p>
                </title>
                <p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ РіР»Р°РІС‹...</p>
            </section>
        </body></FictionBook>"#;

    let doc = Fb2Document::from_bytes(xml.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    let toc = doc.toc();
    assert_eq!(toc.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(toc[0].title, "Р§Р°СЃС‚СЊ I. РќР°С‡Р°Р»Рѕ вЂ” Р’ РіРѕСЂРѕРґРµ N");
}

#[test]
fn test_multiple_bodies() {
    let xml = r#"<FictionBook>
            <body>
                <title><p>РљРЅРёРіР° РїРµСЂРІР°СЏ</p></title>
                <section><title><p>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1</p></title><p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ 1</p></section>
            </body>
            <body>
                <title><p>РџСЂРёРјРµС‡Р°РЅРёСЏ</p></title>
                <section><title><p>РџСЂРёРјРµС‡Р°РЅРёРµ 1</p></title><p>РџРѕСЏСЃРЅРµРЅРёРµ</p></section>
            </body>
        </FictionBook>"#;

    let doc = Fb2Document::from_bytes(xml.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    let toc = doc.toc();
    assert_eq!(toc.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(toc[0].title, "РљРЅРёРіР° РїРµСЂРІР°СЏ");
    assert_eq!(toc[0].children.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(toc[0].children[0].title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1");

    assert_eq!(toc[1].title, "РџСЂРёРјРµС‡Р°РЅРёСЏ");
    assert_eq!(toc[1].children.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(toc[1].children[0].title, "РџСЂРёРјРµС‡Р°РЅРёРµ 1");
}

#[test]
fn test_book_without_titles() {
    let xml = r#"<FictionBook><body>
            <section><p>РџСЂРѕСЃС‚Рѕ С‚РµРєСЃС‚ Р±РµР· Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєРѕРІ.</p></section>
            <section><p>Р’С‚РѕСЂРѕР№ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†.</p></section>
        </body></FictionBook>"#;

    let doc = Fb2Document::from_bytes(xml.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    assert!(doc.toc().is_empty());
    assert_eq!(doc.chapters().len(), 2);
}

#[test]
fn test_toc_navigation_to_correct_section() {
    let xml = r#"<FictionBook><body>
            <section id="sec_intro">
                <title><p>Р’РІРµРґРµРЅРёРµ</p></title>
                <p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ РІРІРµРґРµРЅРёСЏ</p>
            </section>
            <section id="sec_part1">
                <title><p>Р§Р°СЃС‚СЊ 1</p></title>
                <p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ С‡Р°СЃС‚Рё 1</p>
                <section id="sec_sub1">
                    <title><p>РџРѕРґСЂР°Р·РґРµР» 1.1</p></title>
                    <p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ РїРѕРґСЂР°Р·РґРµР»Р° 1.1</p>
                </section>
            </section>
        </body></FictionBook>"#;

    let doc = Fb2Document::from_bytes(xml.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    let toc = doc.toc();

    let intro_item = &toc[0];
    assert_eq!(intro_item.title, "Р’РІРµРґРµРЅРёРµ");
    assert_eq!(
        doc.chapters()[intro_item.chapter_index].paragraphs[0],
        "РўРµРєСЃС‚ РІРІРµРґРµРЅРёСЏ"
    );

    let part1_item = &toc[1];
    assert_eq!(part1_item.title, "Р§Р°СЃС‚СЊ 1");
    assert_eq!(
        doc.chapters()[part1_item.chapter_index].paragraphs[0],
        "РўРµРєСЃС‚ С‡Р°СЃС‚Рё 1"
    );

    let sub1_item = &part1_item.children[0];
    assert_eq!(sub1_item.title, "РџРѕРґСЂР°Р·РґРµР» 1.1");
    assert_eq!(
        doc.chapters()[sub1_item.chapter_index].paragraphs[0],
        "РўРµРєСЃС‚ РїРѕРґСЂР°Р·РґРµР»Р° 1.1"
    );
}

#[test]
fn test_repeated_chapter_titles() {
    let xml = r#"<FictionBook><body>
            <section>
                <title><p>РўРѕРј 1</p></title>
                <section><title><p>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1</p></title><p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ С‚РѕРјР° 1 РіР»Р°РІС‹ 1</p></section>
            </section>
            <section>
                <title><p>РўРѕРј 2</p></title>
                <section><title><p>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1</p></title><p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ С‚РѕРјР° 2 РіР»Р°РІС‹ 1</p></section>
            </section>
        </body></FictionBook>"#;

    let doc = Fb2Document::from_bytes(xml.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    let toc = doc.toc();

    let ch1_tom1 = &toc[0].children[0];
    let ch1_tom2 = &toc[1].children[0];

    assert_eq!(ch1_tom1.title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1");
    assert_eq!(ch1_tom2.title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1");
    assert_ne!(ch1_tom1.chapter_index, ch1_tom2.chapter_index);

    assert_eq!(
        doc.chapters()[ch1_tom1.chapter_index].paragraphs[0],
        "РўРµРєСЃС‚ С‚РѕРјР° 1 РіР»Р°РІС‹ 1"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        doc.chapters()[ch1_tom2.chapter_index].paragraphs[0],
        "РўРµРєСЃС‚ С‚РѕРјР° 2 РіР»Р°РІС‹ 1"
    );
}

#[test]
fn test_explicit_body_name_toc() {
    let xml = r##"<FictionBook>
            <body name="toc">
                <title><p>РћРіР»Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ</p></title>
                <section>
                    <p><a href="#ch1">Р“Р»Р°РІР° РїРµСЂРІР°СЏ</a></p>
                    <p><a href="#ch2">Р“Р»Р°РІР° РІС‚РѕСЂР°СЏ</a></p>
                </section>
            </body>
            <body>
                <section id="ch1">
                    <p>РЎРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ РїРµСЂРІРѕР№ РіР»Р°РІС‹</p>
                </section>
                <section id="ch2">
                    <p>РЎРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ РІС‚РѕСЂРѕР№ РіР»Р°РІС‹</p>
                </section>
            </body>
        </FictionBook>"##;

    let doc = Fb2Document::from_bytes(xml.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    let toc = doc.toc();

    assert_eq!(toc.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(toc[0].title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° РїРµСЂРІР°СЏ");
    assert_eq!(toc[1].title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° РІС‚РѕСЂР°СЏ");

    assert_eq!(
        doc.chapters()[toc[0].chapter_index].paragraphs[0],
        "РЎРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ РїРµСЂРІРѕР№ РіР»Р°РІС‹"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        doc.chapters()[toc[1].chapter_index].paragraphs[0],
        "РЎРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ РІС‚РѕСЂРѕР№ РіР»Р°РІС‹"
    );

    assert_eq!(doc.chapters().len(), 2);
}

#[test]
fn test_platform_keep_screen_on() {
    crate::platform::set_keep_screen_on(true);
    crate::platform::set_keep_screen_on(false);
}

#[test]
fn test_platform_file_association_query_and_dismiss() {
    let status = crate::platform::check_fb2_association_status();
    assert!(matches!(
        status,
        crate::platform::AssociationStatus::Default
            | crate::platform::AssociationStatus::Registered
            | crate::platform::AssociationStatus::NotRegistered
    ));

    crate::platform::set_association_prompt_dismissed(true);
    assert!(crate::platform::is_association_prompt_dismissed());
    crate::platform::set_association_prompt_dismissed(false);
    assert!(!crate::platform::is_association_prompt_dismissed());
}

#[test]
#[ignore = "РР·РјРµРЅСЏРµС‚ СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ СЃРёСЃС‚РµРјРЅС‹Рµ Р°СЃСЃРѕС†РёР°С†РёРё С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ РІ СЂРµРµСЃС‚СЂРµ РћРЎ"]
fn test_platform_register_association() {
    let result = crate::platform::register_fb2_association();
    assert!(result.is_ok());

    let status = crate::platform::check_fb2_association_status();
    assert!(
        status == crate::platform::AssociationStatus::Registered
            || status == crate::platform::AssociationStatus::Default
    );
}

#[test]
fn test_command_line_path_handling() {
    let temp_file = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
        "test_spaces_{}_{}.fb2",
        std::process::id(),
        std::time::SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
            .map(|d| d.as_nanos())
            .unwrap_or(0)
    ));

    let xml = r#"<FictionBook><body><section><title><p>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1</p></title><p>РўРµРєСЃС‚</p></section></body></FictionBook>"#;
    std::fs::write(&temp_file, xml).unwrap();

    let doc = Fb2Document::from_file(&temp_file).expect("Р”РѕР»Р¶РµРЅ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ С„Р°Р№Р» РїРѕ РїСѓС‚Рё СЃ РїСЂРѕР±РµР»Р°РјРё");
    assert_eq!(doc.toc().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(doc.toc()[0].title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1");

    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(temp_file);
}

#[test]
fn test_settings_window_state_and_recent_books() {
    let mut settings = AppSettings::default();

    settings.update_window_state(Some(120.0), Some(80.0), 1200.0, 800.0, false);
    assert_eq!(settings.window.x, Some(120.0));
    assert_eq!(settings.window.y, Some(80.0));
    assert_eq!(settings.window.width, 1200.0);
    assert_eq!(settings.window.height, 800.0);
    assert!(!settings.window.maximized);

    let path1 = std::path::PathBuf::from(r"C:\Books\WarAndPeace.fb2");
    let path2 = std::path::PathBuf::from(r"C:\Books\EugeneOnegin.fb2");

    settings.record_book_progress(&path1, "Р’РѕР№РЅР° Рё РјРёСЂ", "Р›РµРІ РўРѕР»СЃС‚РѕР№", 2, 450.0);
    assert_eq!(settings.recent_books.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(settings.last_opened_book, Some(path1.clone()));
    assert_eq!(settings.get_book_progress(&path1), Some((2, 450.0)));

    settings.record_book_progress(&path2, "Р•РІРіРµРЅРёР№ РћРЅРµРіРёРЅ", "РђР»РµРєСЃР°РЅРґСЂ РџСѓС€РєРёРЅ", 0, 100.0);
    assert_eq!(settings.recent_books.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(settings.last_opened_book, Some(path2.clone()));
    assert_eq!(settings.recent_books[0].path, path2);

    settings.record_book_progress(&path1, "Р’РѕР№РЅР° Рё РјРёСЂ", "Р›РµРІ РўРѕР»СЃС‚РѕР№", 3, 600.0);
    assert_eq!(settings.recent_books.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(settings.recent_books[0].path, path1);
    assert_eq!(settings.get_book_progress(&path1), Some((3, 600.0)));

    let json = serde_json::to_string(&settings).expect("РЎРµСЂРёР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ РґРѕР»Р¶РЅР° СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°С‚СЊ");
    let loaded: AppSettings = serde_json::from_str(&json).expect("Р”РµСЃРµСЂРёР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ РґРѕР»Р¶РЅР° СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°С‚СЊ");
    assert_eq!(loaded, settings);
}

#[test]
fn test_parse_epub_in_memory() {
    use std::io::Write;
    use zip::write::SimpleFileOptions;

    let mut zip_buf = std::io::Cursor::new(Vec::new());
    {
        let mut zip = zip::ZipWriter::new(&mut zip_buf);
        let options =
            SimpleFileOptions::default().compression_method(zip::CompressionMethod::Stored);

        let container_xml = r#"<?xml version="1.0"?>
<container version="1.0" xmlns="urn:oasis:names:tc:opendocument:xmlns:container">
  <rootfiles>
    <rootfile full-path="OEBPS/content.opf" media-type="application/oebps-package+xml"/>
  </rootfiles>
</container>"#;
        zip.start_file("META-INF/container.xml", options).unwrap();
        zip.write_all(container_xml.as_bytes()).unwrap();

        let content_opf = r#"<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<package xmlns="http://www.idpf.org/2007/opf" version="2.0" unique-identifier="BookId">
  <metadata xmlns:dc="http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/">
    <dc:title>РњР°СЃС‚РµСЂ Рё РњР°СЂРіР°СЂРёС‚Р°</dc:title>
    <dc:creator>РњРёС…Р°РёР» Р‘СѓР»РіР°РєРѕРІ</dc:creator>
  </metadata>
  <manifest>
    <item id="ncx" href="toc.ncx" media-type="application/x-dtbncx+xml"/>
    <item id="ch1" href="chapter1.xhtml" media-type="application/xhtml+xml"/>
    <item id="ch2" href="chapter2.xhtml" media-type="application/xhtml+xml"/>
  </manifest>
  <spine toc="ncx">
    <itemref idref="ch1"/>
    <itemref idref="ch2"/>
  </spine>
</package>"#;
        zip.start_file("OEBPS/content.opf", options).unwrap();
        zip.write_all(content_opf.as_bytes()).unwrap();

        let toc_ncx = r#"<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<ncx xmlns="http://www.daisy.org/z3986/2005/ncx/" version="2005-1">
  <navMap>
    <navPoint id="np-1" playOrder="1">
      <navLabel><text>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1. РќРёРєРѕРіРґР° РЅРµ СЂР°Р·РіРѕРІР°СЂРёРІР°Р№С‚Рµ СЃ РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹РјРё</text></navLabel>
      <content src="chapter1.xhtml"/>
    </navPoint>
    <navPoint id="np-2" playOrder="2">
      <navLabel><text>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 2. РџРѕРЅС‚РёР№ РџРёР»Р°С‚</text></navLabel>
      <content src="chapter2.xhtml"/>
    </navPoint>
  </navMap>
</ncx>"#;
        zip.start_file("OEBPS/toc.ncx", options).unwrap();
        zip.write_all(toc_ncx.as_bytes()).unwrap();

        let ch1_xhtml = r#"<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml">
<head><title>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1</title></head>
<body>
  <h1>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1. РќРёРєРѕРіРґР° РЅРµ СЂР°Р·РіРѕРІР°СЂРёРІР°Р№С‚Рµ СЃ РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹РјРё</h1>
  <p>РћРґРЅР°Р¶РґС‹ РІРµСЃРЅРѕСЋ, РІ С‡Р°СЃ РЅРµР±С‹РІР°Р»Рѕ Р¶Р°СЂРєРѕРіРѕ Р·Р°РєР°С‚Р°, РІ РњРѕСЃРєРІРµ, РЅР° РџР°С‚СЂРёР°СЂС€РёС… РїСЂСѓРґР°С…, РїРѕСЏРІРёР»РёСЃСЊ РґРІР° РіСЂР°Р¶РґР°РЅРёРЅР°.</p>
  <p>РџРµСЂРІС‹Р№ РёР· РЅРёС… вЂ” РїСЂРёР±Р»РёР·РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ СЃРѕСЂРѕРєР°Р»РµС‚РЅРёР№, РѕРґРµС‚С‹Р№ РІ СЃРµСЂРµРЅСЊРєСѓСЋ Р»РµС‚РЅСЋСЋ РїР°СЂСѓ...</p>
</body>
</html>"#;
        zip.start_file("OEBPS/chapter1.xhtml", options).unwrap();
        zip.write_all(ch1_xhtml.as_bytes()).unwrap();

        let ch2_xhtml = r#"<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml">
<head><title>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 2</title></head>
<body>
  <h1>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 2. РџРѕРЅС‚РёР№ РџРёР»Р°С‚</h1>
  <p>Р’ Р±РµР»РѕРј РїР»Р°С‰Рµ СЃ РєСЂРѕРІР°РІС‹Рј РїРѕРґР±РѕРµРј, С€Р°СЂРєР°СЋС‰РµР№ РєР°РІР°Р»РµСЂРёР№СЃРєРѕР№ РїРѕС…РѕРґРєРѕР№...</p>
</body>
</html>"#;
        zip.start_file("OEBPS/chapter2.xhtml", options).unwrap();
        zip.write_all(ch2_xhtml.as_bytes()).unwrap();

        zip.finish().unwrap();
    }

    let zip_bytes = zip_buf.into_inner();
    let doc =
        document::EpubDocument::from_bytes(&zip_bytes).expect("Р”РѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ СЂР°СЃРїР°СЂСЃРёС‚СЊ EPUB");

    assert_eq!(doc.metadata().title, "РњР°СЃС‚РµСЂ Рё РњР°СЂРіР°СЂРёС‚Р°");
    assert_eq!(doc.metadata().author, "РњРёС…Р°РёР» Р‘СѓР»РіР°РєРѕРІ");
    assert_eq!(doc.chapters().len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(doc.chapters()[0].paragraphs.len(), 3);
    assert!(doc.chapters()[0].paragraphs[0].contains("Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1"));
    assert!(doc.chapters()[0].paragraphs[1].contains("РџР°С‚СЂРёР°СЂС€РёС… РїСЂСѓРґР°С…"));
    assert_eq!(doc.chapters()[1].paragraphs.len(), 2);
    assert!(doc.chapters()[1].paragraphs[0].contains("Р“Р»Р°РІР° 2"));
    assert!(doc.chapters()[1].paragraphs[1].contains("Р±РµР»РѕРј РїР»Р°С‰Рµ"));

    assert_eq!(doc.toc().len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(
        doc.toc()[0].title,
        "Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1. РќРёРєРѕРіРґР° РЅРµ СЂР°Р·РіРѕРІР°СЂРёРІР°Р№С‚Рµ СЃ РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹РјРё"
    );
    assert_eq!(doc.toc()[0].chapter_index, 0);
    assert_eq!(doc.toc()[1].title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° 2. РџРѕРЅС‚РёР№ РџРёР»Р°С‚");
    assert_eq!(doc.toc()[1].chapter_index, 1);
}

#[test]
fn test_window_state_persistence_and_multimonitor_recovery() {
    let mut settings = AppSettings::default();

    settings.update_window_state(Some(320.0), Some(180.0), 650.0, 450.0, false);
    assert_eq!(settings.window.x, Some(320.0));
    assert_eq!(settings.window.y, Some(180.0));
    assert_eq!(settings.window.width, 650.0);
    assert_eq!(settings.window.height, 450.0);
    assert!(!settings.window.maximized);

    settings.update_window_state(Some(-8.0), Some(-8.0), 1920.0, 1080.0, true);
    assert_eq!(settings.window.x, Some(320.0));
    assert_eq!(settings.window.y, Some(180.0));
    assert_eq!(settings.window.width, 650.0);
    assert_eq!(settings.window.height, 450.0);
    assert!(settings.window.maximized);

    settings.update_window_state(Some(-32000.0), Some(-32000.0), 650.0, 450.0, false);
    assert_eq!(settings.window.x, Some(320.0));
    assert_eq!(settings.window.y, Some(180.0));

    assert!(!crate::platform::is_window_accessible_in_work_area(
        2200, 150, 700, 500, 0, 0, 1920, 1040
    ));
    let (rx, ry, rw, rh) =
        crate::platform::reposition_window_into_work_area(2200, 150, 700, 500, 0, 0, 1920, 1040);
    assert_eq!((rw, rh), (700, 500));
    assert!(crate::platform::is_window_accessible_in_work_area(
        rx, ry, rw, rh, 0, 0, 1920, 1040
    ));

    assert!(!crate::platform::is_window_accessible_in_work_area(
        -1500, 100, 680, 480, 0, 0, 1920, 1040
    ));
    let (lx, ly, lw, lh) =
        crate::platform::reposition_window_into_work_area(-1500, 100, 680, 480, 0, 0, 1920, 1040);
    assert_eq!((lw, lh), (680, 480));
    assert!(crate::platform::is_window_accessible_in_work_area(
        lx, ly, lw, lh, 0, 0, 1920, 1040
    ));

    let (sx, sy, sw, sh) =
        crate::platform::sanitize_window_geometry(Some(9500.0), Some(9500.0), 650.0, 450.0);
    assert!(sw <= 650.0 && sw >= 400.0);
    assert!(sh <= 450.0 && sh >= 300.0);
    if let (Some(px), Some(py)) = (sx, sy) {
        assert!(px < 9000.0 && py < 9000.0);
    }
}

#[test]
fn test_epub_percent_decode_and_path_resolution() {
    use document::epub::{percent_decode_str, resolve_path};
    assert_eq!(percent_decode_str("Chapter%201.xhtml"), "Chapter 1.xhtml");
    assert_eq!(percent_decode_str("%D1%82%D0%B5%D1%81%D1%82"), "С‚РµСЃС‚");
    assert_eq!(percent_decode_str("no_percent.html"), "no_percent.html");

    assert_eq!(resolve_path("OEBPS", "ch1.xhtml"), "OEBPS/ch1.xhtml");
    assert_eq!(resolve_path("OEBPS/text", "../ch1.xhtml"), "OEBPS/ch1.xhtml");
    assert_eq!(resolve_path("OEBPS", "../../outside.xhtml"), "");
    assert_eq!(resolve_path("OEBPS", "http://evil.com/x.html"), "http://evil.com/x.html");
}

#[test]
fn test_epub_entity_sanitization_and_lenient_html() {
    use document::epub::{parse_html_chapter, sanitize_xml_entities};
    let raw_html = "<html><body><h1>Title</h1><p>AT&T and Barnes & Noble &nbsp; &mdash; 5 &lt; 10</p><img src=\"broken.jpg\"><p>Second paragraph after unclosed img</p></body></html>";
    let sanitized = sanitize_xml_entities(raw_html);
    assert!(sanitized.contains("&amp;"));
    assert!(sanitized.contains("вЂ”"));

    let (title, paragraphs, _) = parse_html_chapter(raw_html);
    assert_eq!(title, "Title");
    assert_eq!(paragraphs.len(), 3);
    assert_eq!(paragraphs[0], "Title");
    assert!(paragraphs[1].contains("AT&T"));
    assert!(paragraphs[1].contains("Barnes & Noble"));
    assert!(paragraphs[1].contains("вЂ”"));
    assert!(paragraphs[1].contains("5 < 10"));
    assert!(paragraphs[2].contains("Second paragraph"));
}

#[test]
fn test_fb2_explicit_toc_links_with_percent_decoding() {
    use document::Fb2Document;
    let sample = r##"<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<FictionBook>
  <description>
    <title-info>
      <author><first-name>Author</first-name></author>
      <book-title>Test Book</book-title>
    </title-info>
  </description>
  <body name="toc">
    <title><p>РћРіР»Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ</p></title>
    <p><a href="#%D0%B3%D0%BB%D0%B0%D0%B2%D0%B0_1">Р“Р»Р°РІР° РїРµСЂРІР°СЏ</a></p>
  </body>
  <body>
    <section id="РіР»Р°РІР°_1">
      <title><p>Р“Р»Р°РІР° 1</p></title>
      <p>РўРµРєСЃС‚ РіР»Р°РІС‹ 1</p>
    </section>
  </body>
</FictionBook>"##;

    let doc = Fb2Document::from_bytes(sample.as_bytes()).expect("Should parse");
    assert_eq!(doc.toc().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(doc.toc()[0].title, "Р“Р»Р°РІР° РїРµСЂРІР°СЏ");
    assert_eq!(doc.toc()[0].chapter_index, 0);
}

#[test]
fn test_is_safe_archive_path_percent_encoding_traversal() {
    use document::is_safe_archive_path;
    assert!(is_safe_archive_path("OEBPS/ch1.xhtml"));
    assert!(is_safe_archive_path("images/cover.jpg"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("../escape.xhtml"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("%2e%2e/escape.xhtml"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("..%2fescape.xhtml"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("/absolute/path.xhtml"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("C:/Windows/system.ini"));
    assert!(!is_safe_archive_path("%2fetc/passwd"));
}

#[test]
fn test_reading_progress_case_insensitive_lookup() {
    use std::path::PathBuf;
    let mut settings = settings::AppSettings::default();
    let path_upper = PathBuf::from("C:\\Books\\MyBook.epub");
    let path_lower = PathBuf::from("c:\\books\\mybook.epub");

    settings.record_book_progress(&path_upper, "My Book", "Author", 3, 145.5);
    let prog = settings.get_book_progress(&path_lower);
    assert_eq!(prog, Some((3, 145.5)));
}

#[test]
fn test_djvu_corrupted_header_and_short_bytes() {
    use document::DjvuDocument;
    use std::path::Path;

    let res_empty = DjvuDocument::from_bytes(vec![], Path::new("dummy.djvu"));
    assert!(res_empty.is_err());

    let res_short = DjvuDocument::from_bytes(vec![1, 2, 3], Path::new("dummy.djvu"));
    assert!(res_short.is_err());

    let res_garbage = DjvuDocument::from_bytes(b"NOT_A_DJVU_FILE_AT_ALL".to_vec(), Path::new("dummy.djvu"));
    assert!(res_garbage.is_err());

    let res_trunc = DjvuDocument::from_bytes(b"AT&TFORM\x00\x00\x00\x04DJVU".to_vec(), Path::new("dummy.djvu"));
    assert!(res_trunc.is_err());
}

#[test]
fn test_djvu_bookmark_page_resolution() {
    use document::djvu::resolve_djvu_bookmark_page;
    use std::collections::HashMap;

    let mut map = HashMap::new();
    map.insert("intro.djvu".to_string(), 0);
    map.insert("ch1.djvu".to_string(), 1);
    map.insert("ch2.djvu".to_string(), 2);
    map.insert("РіР»Р°РІР°.djvu".to_string(), 3);

    assert_eq!(resolve_djvu_bookmark_page("#intro.djvu", &map, 10), 0);
    assert_eq!(resolve_djvu_bookmark_page("#ch1.djvu", &map, 10), 1);
    assert_eq!(resolve_djvu_bookmark_page("#ch2", &map, 10), 2);
    assert_eq!(resolve_djvu_bookmark_page("#%D0%B3%D0%BB%D0%B0%D0%B2%D0%B0.djvu", &map, 10), 3);
    assert_eq!(resolve_djvu_bookmark_page("#1", &map, 10), 0);
    assert_eq!(resolve_djvu_bookmark_page("#+5", &map, 10), 4);
    assert_eq!(resolve_djvu_bookmark_page("#page0006", &map, 10), 5);
    assert_eq!(resolve_djvu_bookmark_page("#pg_8", &map, 10), 7);
    assert_eq!(resolve_djvu_bookmark_page("#p3", &map, 10), 2);
    assert_eq!(resolve_djvu_bookmark_page("#9999", &map, 10), 9);
    assert_eq!(resolve_djvu_bookmark_page("#0", &map, 10), 0);
    assert_eq!(resolve_djvu_bookmark_page("", &map, 10), 0);
    assert_eq!(resolve_djvu_bookmark_page("#unknown_target", &map, 10), 0);
}

}

#[test]
fn test_document_format_name_and_custom_toc() {
    use document::{Document, TxtDocument, Fb2Document};
    let txt = TxtDocument::from_text("Sample text", "title");
    assert_eq!(txt.format_name(), "TXT");
    assert!(!txt.has_custom_toc());

    let fb2_sample = r##"<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<FictionBook>
  <description><title-info><book-title>Test</book-title></title-info></description>
  <body name="toc"><title><p>TOC</p></title><p><a href="#ch1">Ch 1</a></p></body>
  <body><section id="ch1"><p>Text</p></section></body>
</FictionBook>"##;
    let fb2 = Fb2Document::from_bytes(fb2_sample.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(fb2.format_name(), "FB2");
    assert!(fb2.has_custom_toc());
}

#[test]
fn test_reader_core_bookmarks_and_search() {
    use crate::core::{Bookmark, ReaderCore};
    use crate::document::TxtDocument;
    use std::path::PathBuf;

    let mut core = ReaderCore::default();
    let path = PathBuf::from("test_book.txt");
    let bm1 = Bookmark {
        book_path: path.clone(),
        chapter: 0,
        scroll_y: 100.0,
        label: "Marker 1".to_string(),
        timestamp: 123456,
    };
    let bm2 = Bookmark {
        book_path: path.clone(),
        chapter: 1,
        scroll_y: 200.0,
        label: "Marker 2".to_string(),
        timestamp: 123457,
    };

    core.add_bookmark(bm1.clone());
    core.add_bookmark(bm2.clone());
    core.add_bookmark(bm1.clone());

    assert_eq!(core.bookmarks_for_path(&path).count(), 2);

    core.remove_bookmark(&path, 0);
    assert_eq!(core.bookmarks_for_path(&path).count(), 1);

    core.clear_bookmarks_for_path(&path);
    assert_eq!(core.bookmarks_for_path(&path).count(), 0);

    let doc = TxtDocument::from_text("First chapter with keyword needle inside it\nSecond chapter without it", "Doc");
    let hits = ReaderCore::search_in_document(&doc, "needle");
    assert_eq!(hits.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(hits[0].chapter_index, 0);
    assert!(hits[0].snippet.contains("needle"));
}

#[test]
fn test_paged_common_helpers() {
    use crate::app::paged_common::{calculate_paged_dimensions, parse_page_input, zoom_in, zoom_out, zoom_reset};
    use eframe::egui::Vec2;

    assert_eq!(parse_page_input("1", 10), Some(0));
    assert_eq!(parse_page_input("5", 10), Some(4));
    assert_eq!(parse_page_input("10", 10), Some(9));
    assert_eq!(parse_page_input("99", 10), Some(9));
    assert_eq!(parse_page_input("0", 10), Some(0));
    assert_eq!(parse_page_input("abc", 10), None);

    let mut zoom = 100.0;
    let mut fit = "FitWidth".to_string();
    let mut pan = Vec2::new(10.0, 20.0);

    zoom_in(&mut zoom, &mut fit);
    assert_eq!(zoom, 115.0);
    assert_eq!(fit, "Custom");

    zoom_out(&mut zoom, &mut fit);
    assert_eq!(zoom, 100.0);

    zoom_reset(&mut zoom, &mut fit, &mut pan);
    assert_eq!(zoom, 100.0);
    assert_eq!(fit, "Original");
    assert_eq!(pan, Vec2::ZERO);

    let (w_fit_w, _) = calculate_paged_dimensions(800.0, 1200.0, 1000.0, 800.0, "FitWidth", 100.0);
    assert_eq!(w_fit_w, 984.0);

    let (_, h_fit_h) = calculate_paged_dimensions(800.0, 1200.0, 1000.0, 800.0, "FitHeight", 100.0);
    assert_eq!(h_fit_h, 784.0);
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\Almas\Documents\QuietReader\AGENTS.md ===
1. Project Goal

QuietReader is a modular, cross-platform ebook reader for Windows, Linux, and macOS.

The architecture must support multiple document formats, independent UI development, maintainability, and future expansion without unnecessary refactoring.

All development must follow the principles and rules defined in this document.

2. Core Principles

SOLID

SOLID principles must guide architectural decisions:

SRP: Each module has one clear responsibility.

OCP: New formats and features must require minimal changes to existing code.

LSP: Implementations of the same interface must honor its contract.

ISP: Modules must not depend on capabilities they do not need.

DIP: High-level logic must depend on abstractions, not concrete implementations.

Do not introduce unnecessary abstractions merely to satisfy SOLID formally.

General Rules

Prefer modularity, clear boundaries, and low coupling.

Reuse existing functionality instead of duplicating it.

Preserve existing behavior unless a change is explicitly required.

Keep changes focused on the requested task.

Avoid monolithic modules and unrelated responsibilities.

3. Modular Architecture

The project must maintain clear separation between four areas.

3.1. Application Core

Contains application state, navigation, reading progress, bookmarks, history, search coordination, and other reader functionality.

Must not depend on a specific UI framework.

Must not contain format-specific parsing or platform-specific implementation details.

Coordinates document backends and application services through well-defined interfaces.

Exposes document operations and supported capabilities through stable abstractions. The UI must not need to know which concrete backend implements them.

3.2. Document Backends

Each document format must be handled by a backend exposed through an appropriate interface. Related formats may share an implementation when this keeps the architecture clear and maintainable.

Supported and planned formats:

Flowable text: EPUB, TXT, MD, LOG, FB2.

Fixed-layout documents: PDF, DJVU.

Comics and manga: CBZ, CBR, CB7, and image folders.

Rules:

The listed formats define project targets, not proof of existing support. Inspect the current implementation before assuming a format or capability is available. Do not claim support unless it is implemented and appropriately verified.

Adding a format must not require rewriting existing backends or format-specific UI components. Updating a central registry is acceptable.

Format-specific parsing, metadata extraction, and content access belong in document backends.

Use shared interfaces for common capabilities and separate interfaces for format-specific capabilities.

Optional features, such as table of contents, text search, and cover extraction, must not be mandatory for every format.

Related formats may share implementations and dependencies where appropriate.

Keep format-specific dependencies isolated wherever practical.

3.3. Presentation and UI

Contains the library, reader interface, navigation panels, toolbars, themes, settings screens, fullscreen behavior, and viewing modes.

Must not parse document files or implement format-specific decoding.

Must communicate with the application core through defined interfaces.

Must not depend directly on concrete document backends.

Keep visual styling and UI-specific resources separate from document processing and business logic.

Format-specific viewing modes may control presentation but must not implement document decoding.

3.4. Platform Services

Contains operating-system-specific functionality, including file associations, configuration paths, system integration, and screen-sleep prevention.

Hide platform-specific implementation details behind common interfaces.

Keep Windows, Linux, and macOS implementations isolated.

Use conditional compilation where appropriate.

Platform-specific code must not leak into document backends or general UI logic.

4. Document and Rendering Contracts

Common document operations must have consistent behavior and error handling.

Document types must expose only the capabilities they support.

Separate document decoding, content preparation, layout, and UI presentation into clear responsibilities.

Document backends must not depend on GUI frameworks or GUI-specific resources.

Expensive operations must not unnecessarily block the UI.

Handle missing, corrupted, and unsupported content gracefully.

5. Cross-Platform Requirements

Windows, Linux, and macOS are first-class target platforms.

Do not introduce platform-specific assumptions into shared application logic.

Avoid hardcoded paths and dependencies that unnecessarily restrict platform support.

Evaluate dependency maintenance, licensing, binary size, and cross-platform compatibility before adoption.

Isolate and document necessary platform-specific exceptions.

6. Reliability and Data Safety

Never silently discard user settings, bookmarks, reading progress, or history during routine changes.

Preserve existing data through appropriate migrations when persistent data formats change.

Failed migrations must not silently destroy user data; preserve recoverability whenever persistent data is modified.

Handle file and operating-system errors without uncontrolled application crashes.

Avoid loading entire large documents into memory when incremental processing or caching is appropriate.

Validate document and archive contents and apply reasonable resource limits.

Archive processing must prevent path traversal and account for decompression bombs, excessive entry counts, and unreasonable resource consumption.

Do not use panics as normal error handling for document parsing or I/O.

7. Development Rules for AI Agents

Before Changes

Inspect the existing project structure and relevant implementations.

Follow established architecture and reuse existing interfaces where appropriate.

Prefer the smallest correct change.

Do not perform unrelated refactoring or introduce unnecessary dependencies.

Do not weaken module boundaries for short-term convenience.

After Changes

Run appropriate formatting, compilation, and tests when available.

Add or update tests for new functionality and important bug fixes.

Report what changed and which checks were actually completed.

Explicitly mention unresolved issues and unverified platform compatibility.

If a relevant check cannot be run, state why and identify what remains unverified.

Make broader architectural changes only when necessary to satisfy requirements, fix a fundamental design issue, or prevent recurring problems. Explain the justification and keep the scope controlled. Do not undertake a large-scale migration without a clear need.

8. Architecture Decision Rule

Maintainability, modularity, cross-platform compatibility, and correctness take priority over premature optimization and unnecessary abstraction.

New formats and features must fit the architecture without coupling document processing, application logic, UI design, and operating-system integration.

9. Binary Size Optimization

Use release builds for production.

Apply stripping and compiler optimizations when supported and beneficial.

Evaluate binary size, build time, and runtime performance before adopting additional profile settings.

Avoid unnecessary dependencies and disable unused default features (default-features = false) where appropriate.

Do not use panic = "abort" solely for size reduction without considering its consequences.

Verify that optimizations preserve functionality and cross-platform compatibility.

Always use the verified release profile settings `opt-level = "s"` and `lto = "thin"`, as this configuration reduces binary size by ~10% (1.56 MB saved) and builds twice as fast compared to `opt-level = 3` with `lto = "fat"`. After building a release, copy the final binary to the project root (`QuietReader.exe`).

10. Workspace Hygiene and Cleanup

If the project folder size exceeds 3 GB (`> 3 GB`), clean up the workspace (e.g., `target/` build artifacts and temporary files), keeping only the source files, project configuration, and the root `QuietReader.exe` binary, and deleting everything unnecessary.

File line count checks must enforce a limit of 1000 lines per file (not 700). If a file exceeds 1000 lines, refactor it, remove redundant comments and duplicate code, and decompose it into separate modules whenever possible.

11. Immutability of AGENTS.md

`AGENTS.md` is strictly inviolable. Editing or modifying `AGENTS.md` is forbidden without direct, explicit permission given personally by the project owner (user).

12. Code Comments Policy

Do not write or leave comments in the code. Writing comments in code is strictly prohibited. If comments are present in the code, they must be removed.

fn main() {
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        let out_dir = std::env::var("OUT_DIR").unwrap();
        let obj_path = format!("{out_dir}/resources.o");

        let windres_cmd = if std::process::Command::new("windres")
            .arg("--version")
            .output()
            .map(|o| o.status.success())
            .unwrap_or(false)
        {
            "windres".to_string()
        } else {
            "C:\\Users\\Almas\\AppData\\Local\\Microsoft\\WinGet\\Packages\\BrechtSanders.WinLibs.POSIX.UCRT_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe\\mingw64\\bin\\windres.exe".to_string()
        };

        let status = std::process::Command::new(&windres_cmd)
            .args(["resources.rc", "-o", &obj_path])
            .status()
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ Р·Р°РїСѓСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ {windres_cmd}: {e}"));

        assert!(status.success(), "РљРѕРјР°РЅРґР° windres Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€РёР»Р°СЃСЊ СЃ РѕС€РёР±РєРѕР№");

        println!("cargo:rustc-link-arg={obj_path}");
        println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=resources.rc");
        println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=assets/app.ico");
        println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=assets/fb2_doc.ico");
    }
}
